local State = {
	aimbot = true, silentAim = false, target = "Head",
	smoothing = true, smoothness = 5.0,
	drawFov = false, fovRadius = 120, fovColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 64, 80),
	visibleCheck = true, teamCheck = false, aliveCheck = true,
	openAnim = "fade", dim = 20, uiTransparency = 0,
}

State.brand = { a = "NAURU", b = "HUB", name = "Nauru Hub", tag = "beta" }
local PAD = 16
local capturing = false

State.reg, State.regN = {}, {}
function State.regAdd(parent, id, get, set)
	local key = parent.Name .. ":" .. id
	if State.reg[key] then
		State.regN[key] = (State.regN[key] or 1) + 1
		key = key .. "#" .. State.regN[key]
	end
	State.reg[key] = { get = get, set = set, page = parent.Name }
end

State.icons = {}
local liveTw = {}
local COLOR_PROPS = { "BackgroundColor3", "TextColor3", "Color", "ImageColor3", "ScrollBarImageColor3", "PlaceholderColor3" }
State.activeTw = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
function State.stopTweens(obj)
	local a = State.activeTw[obj]
	if not a then return end
	State.activeTw[obj] = nil
	for tw in pairs(a) do pcall(function() tw:Cancel() end) end
end
local function tween(obj, t, props, style, dir)
	local tw = TweenService:Create(obj, TweenInfo.new(t, style or Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, dir or Enum.EasingDirection.Out), props)
	local act = State.activeTw[obj]
	if not act then act = {} State.activeTw[obj] = act end
	act[tw] = true
	tw.Completed:Connect(function() if State.activeTw[obj] == act then act[tw] = nil end end)
	tw:Play()
	for _, k in ipairs(COLOR_PROPS) do
		if props[k] ~= nil then
			liveTw[tw] = { obj = obj, t = t, props = props, style = style, dir = dir }
			tw.Completed:Connect(function() liveTw[tw] = nil end)
			break
		end
	end
	return tw
end

local function fadeBar(sf)
	local thick = sf.ScrollBarThickness
	sf.ScrollBarImageTransparency = 1
	sf.ScrollBarThickness = 0
	local tok = 0
	sf:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CanvasPosition"):Connect(function()
		tok += 1
		local my = tok
		sf.ScrollBarThickness = thick
		TweenService:Create(sf, TweenInfo.new(0.15), { ScrollBarImageTransparency = 0.2 }):Play()
		task.delay(0.8, function()
			if my ~= tok then return end
			local tw = TweenService:Create(sf, TweenInfo.new(0.4), { ScrollBarImageTransparency = 1 })
			tw.Completed:Connect(function() if my == tok then sf.ScrollBarThickness = 0 end end)
			tw:Play()
		end)
	end)
end

State.fadeBar = fadeBar

local function create(class, props, parent)
	local o = Instance.new(class)
	for k, v in pairs(props) do o[k] = v end
	o.Parent = parent
	if class == "ScrollingFrame" and (props.ScrollBarThickness or 0) > 0 then fadeBar(o) end
	return o
end

local function corner(o, r) return create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, r) }, o) end

local function stroke(o, color, th, tr)
	return create("UIStroke", { Color = color, Thickness = th or 1, Transparency = tr or 0, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border }, o)
end

local function createFrame(parent, props)
	if props.BackgroundColor3 == nil and props.BackgroundTransparency == nil then props.BackgroundTransparency = 1 end
	props.BorderSizePixel = 0
	return create("Frame", props, parent)
end

function State.titleCase(s)
	if type(s) ~= "string" or s:sub(1, 1) == "@" then return s end
	return (s:gsub("(%a)([%w']*)", function(a, b)
		local w = a .. b
		local lw = w:lower()
		if lw == "ui" then return "UI" end
		if lw == "esp" then return "ESP" end
		return a:upper() .. b
	end))
end

local function createLabel(parent, text, size, color, font, props)
	local noTitle = props and props.NoTitle
	if props and props.NoTitle ~= nil then props = table.clone(props) props.NoTitle = nil end
	if not noTitle and not (props and props.RichText) then text = State.titleCase(text) end
	local p = {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Text = text, TextSize = size, TextColor3 = color,
		Font = font or FONT, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
	}
	for k, v in pairs(props or {}) do p[k] = v end
	if p.FontFace then p.Font = nil end
	return create("TextLabel", p, parent)
end

P+Jv8iABcB8wGoAi0DewOVA30DPQPgAnIC/QGKASABwwBwACcA4/+e/1X/Bv+w/lf+AP6z/Xb9U/1P/XD9tP0b/p7+Nf/V/3EAAgF8AdgBEwIs
AiYCBQLQAY4BRwEBAb8AhQBSACYAAADa/7P/iv9f/zH/Bf/d/r3+qv6m/rP+0v4B/z7/hP/O/xYAWACQALkA0wDeANsAzAC2AJoAfQBgAEcAMA
AeABAABAD7//L/6v/j/93/2f/Y/9n/3v/l/+7/+P8AAA==
]],
	}
	local VOL = { click = 0.55, on = 0.6, off = 0.6 }
	local ALPHA = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/"
	local function b64decode(str)
		local lut = {}
		for n = 1, 64 do lut[ALPHA:byte(n)] = n - 1 end
		local out, buf, bits = {}, 0, 0
		for n = 1, #str do
			local v = lut[str:byte(n)]
			if v then
				buf = buf * 64 + v
				bits += 6
				if bits >= 8 then
					bits -= 8
					local div = 2 ^ bits
					out[#out + 1] = string.char(math.floor(buf / div) % 256)
					buf = buf % div
				end
			end
		end
		return table.concat(out)
	end
	local ready = {}
	task.spawn(function()
		pcall(function()
			if not (writefile and getcustomasset) then return end
			if isfolder and not isfolder("NauruHub") then makefolder("NauruHub") end
			for kind, data in pairs(DATA) do
				local path = "NauruHub/sfx_" .. kind .. ".wav"
				local ok = pcall(function()
					if not (isfile and isfile(path)) or #readfile(path) < 100 then writefile(path, b64decode(data)) end
					local s = Instance.new("Sound")
					s.Name = "NauruSfx_" .. kind
					s.SoundId = getcustomasset(path)
					s.Volume = VOL[kind]
					s.Parent = SoundService
					ready[kind] = s
				end)
			end
		end)
	end)
	function State.sfx(kind)
		if not State.sounds then return end
		local src = ready[kind] or ready.click
		if not src then return end
		local c = src:Clone()
		c.Parent = SoundService
		c:Play()
		Debris:AddItem(c, 1)
	end
end

local function createButton(parent, props)
	local p = {
		AutoButtonColor = false, Text = "", BorderSizePixel = 0, BackgroundTransparency = 1,
		Font = FONT, TextSize = 12, TextColor3 = C.text,
	}
	for k, v in pairs(props) do p[k] = v end
	local wantSound = p.Sound
	p.Sound = nil
	if not p.RichText then p.Text = State.titleCase(p.Text) end
	local btn = create("TextButton", p, parent)
	if wantSound then btn.Activated:Connect(function() State.sfx("click") end) end
	return btn
end

local function isInside(gui, pos)
	local a, s = gui.AbsolutePosition, gui.AbsoluteSize
	return pos.X >= a.X and pos.X <= a.X + s.X and pos.Y >= a.Y and pos.Y <= a.Y + s.Y
end

local function isPress(input)
	return input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
end

local function isMove(input)
	return input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
end

function State.fitText(obj, width, maxSize, minSize, font)
	local TextService = game:GetService("TextService")
	local function fit()
		local size = maxSize
		pcall(function()
			while size > minSize do
				if TextService:GetTextSize(obj.Text, size, obj:GetAttribute("DynFont") and obj.Font or font, Vector2.new(10000, 100)).X <= width - 10 then break end
				size -= 1
			end
		end)
		obj.TextSize = size
		obj.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
	end
	obj:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(fit)
	fit()
end

local function bindDrag(hit, onDrag)
	local dragging = false
	local function update(input)
		local a, s = hit.AbsolutePosition, hit.AbsoluteSize
		onDrag(math.clamp((input.Position.X - a.X) / s.X, 0, 1), math.clamp((input.Position.Y - a.Y) / s.Y, 0, 1))
	end
	hit.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if isPress(input) then dragging = true update(input) end
	end)
	UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if dragging and isMove(input) then update(input) end
	end)
	UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if isPress(input) then dragging = false end
	end)
end

State.iconFallback = {}
do
	local URL = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eeeiqjjj/icon.github.io/refs/heads/main/lucide.lua"
	local Lucide, IconCache = nil, {}
	local SIZE_KEYS = { "48px", "64px", "256px", "24px", 48, 64, 24 }
	local SHEET_KEYS = { "Spritesheets", "SpriteSheets", "Spritesheet", "Sheets", "Assets", "assets" }

	local function fetch(url)
		local body
		pcall(function() body = game:HttpGet(url) end)
		if not body or body == "" then
			local req = (syn and syn.request) or (http and http.request) or http_request or request
			if req then
				pcall(function()
					local r = req({ Url = url, Method = "GET" })
					body = r and r.Body
				end)
			end
		end
		return body
	end

	local lastErr
	for attempt = 1, 3 do
		local ok, res = pcall(function()
			local body = fetch(URL)
			assert(body and body ~= "", "empty response")
			return assert(loadstring(body))()
		end)
		if ok and res ~= nil then Lucide = res break end
		lastErr = ok and "pack returned nothing" or tostring(res)
		if attempt < 3 then task.wait(1) end
	end
	if not Lucide then warn("[Nauru Hub] Lucide icons failed to load (" .. tostring(lastErr) .. "); using built-in icons.") end

	local function toVec2(v)
		if typeof(v) == "Vector2" then return v end
		if type(v) == "table" then
			local x, y = v[1] or v.X or v.x, v[2] or v.Y or v.y
			if type(x) == "number" and type(y) == "number" then return Vector2.new(x, y) end
		end
		return nil
	end
	local function toImageString(v)
		if type(v) == "number" then return "rbxassetid://" .. v end
		if type(v) == "string" and v ~= "" then
			if v:match("^%d+$") then return "rbxassetid://" .. v end
			return v
		end
		return nil
	end
	local function lookupSheet(container, idx)
		if type(container) ~= "table" then return nil end
		for _, k in ipairs(SHEET_KEYS) do
			local sh = container[k]
			if type(sh) == "table" then
				local v = sh[idx] or sh[tostring(idx)] or (tonumber(idx) and sh[tonumber(idx)])
				if v then return v end
			end
		end
		return nil
	end
	local function parseEntry(entry, containers)
		if entry == nil then return nil end
		if type(entry) == "string" or type(entry) == "number" then return toImageString(entry), nil, nil end
		if type(entry) ~= "table" then return nil end
		local raw = entry.Image or entry.image or entry.Url or entry.URL or entry.url or entry.id or entry.Id or entry.ImageId
		local size = toVec2(entry.ImageRectSize or entry.imageRectSize or entry.RectSize or entry.size or entry.Size)
		local offset = toVec2(entry.ImageRectOffset or entry.ImageRectPosition or entry.imageRectOffset
			or entry.imageRectPosition or entry.RectOffset or entry.offset or entry.Position)
		if raw == nil and entry[1] ~= nil then
			raw = entry[1]
			local second, third = entry[2], entry[3]
			if type(second) == "table" and (second.ImageRectSize or second.ImageRectPosition or second.ImageRectOffset
				or second.imageRectSize or second.imageRectOffset or second.imageRectPosition) then
				size = toVec2(second.ImageRectSize or second.imageRectSize)
				offset = toVec2(second.ImageRectPosition or second.ImageRectOffset or second.imageRectPosition or second.imageRectOffset)
			else
				size, offset = toVec2(second), toVec2(third)
			end
		end
		local image
		if type(raw) == "number" or (type(raw) == "string" and raw:match("^%d+$")) then
			for _, c in ipairs(containers) do
				local sheet = lookupSheet(c, raw)
				if sheet then image = toImageString(sheet) break end
			end
		end
		image = image or toImageString(raw)
		if not image then return nil end
		return image, size, offset
	end
	local function nameVariants(name)
		local v = { name }
		local u = name:gsub("%-", "_")
		if u ~= name then table.insert(v, u) end
		table.insert(v, "lucide-" .. name)
		table.insert(v, "lucide_" .. name)
		return v
	end
	local function findIn(tbl, name)
		if type(tbl) ~= "table" then return nil end
		for _, n in ipairs(nameVariants(name)) do
			if tbl[n] ~= nil then return tbl[n] end
		end
		for _, sk in ipairs(SIZE_KEYS) do
			local sub = tbl[sk]
			if type(sub) == "table" then
				for _, n in ipairs(nameVariants(name)) do
					if sub[n] ~= nil then return sub[n] end
				end
			end
		end
		return nil
	end
	local function resolve(name)
		if not Lucide then return nil end
		if IconCache[name] ~= nil then return IconCache[name] or nil end
		local mod, result = Lucide, nil
		local callers = {}
		if type(mod) == "function" then
			table.insert(callers, function() return mod(name) end)
		elseif type(mod) == "table" then
			for _, fname in ipairs({ "Icon", "GetIcon", "getIcon", "Get", "get", "GetAsset", "getAsset", "Lookup" }) do
				local f = mod[fname]
				if type(f) == "function" then
					table.insert(callers, function() return f(name) end)
					table.insert(callers, function() return f(mod, name) end)
					table.insert(callers, function() return f(name, "lucide") end)
				end
			end
		end
		for _, call in ipairs(callers) do
			local ok, data = pcall(call)
			if ok and data then
				local image, size, offset = parseEntry(data, { mod })
				if image then result = { image, size, offset } break end
			end
		end
		if not result and type(mod) == "table" then
			local holders = {}
			for _, key in ipairs({ "Icons", "icons", "lucide", "Lucide", "Data", "data" }) do
				if type(mod[key]) == "table" then table.insert(holders, mod[key]) end
			end
			table.insert(holders, mod)
			for _, h in ipairs(holders) do
				local entry = findIn(h, name)
				if entry ~= nil then
					local image, size, offset = parseEntry(entry, { mod, h })
					if image then result = { image, size, offset } break end
				end
			end
		end
		IconCache[name] = result or false
		return result
	end

	function State.setLucide(img, name, color)
		local r = resolve(name)
		if not r then return false end
		img.Image = r[1]
		img.ImageRectSize = r[2] or Vector2.zero
		img.ImageRectOffset = r[3] or Vector2.zero
		img.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
		img.ImageTransparency = 0
		if color then img.ImageColor3 = color end
		return true
	end
end

local function newIcon(parent, size, builder)
	local k = size / 20
	local holder = createFrame(parent, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5) })
	local inks, strokes = {}, {}
	local img, lname = nil, nil
	if type(builder) == "string" then
		lname = (builder:gsub("^lucide:", ""))
		img = create("ImageLabel", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ImageColor3 = C.sub }, holder)
		if State.setLucide(img, lname, C.sub) then
			builder = function() end
		else
			img:Destroy()
			img = nil
			builder = State.iconFallback[lname] or function() end
		end
	end

	local function bar(cx, cy, w, h, rot, par)
		local f = createFrame(par or holder, {
			AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(cx * k, cy * k),
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w * k, h * k), Rotation = rot or 0, BackgroundColor3 = C.sub,
		})
		corner(f, 1)
		table.insert(inks, f)
		return f
	end

	local function ring(cx, cy, w, h, o)
		o = o or {}
		local f = createFrame(o.parent or holder, {
			AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(cx * k, cy * k),
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w * k, h * k), BackgroundColor3 = o.fill, BackgroundTransparency = o.fill and 0 or 1,
		})
		corner(f, o.r or 0)
		table.insert(strokes, stroke(f, C.sub, math.max(1, (o.th or 1.5) * k)))
		return f
	end

	builder(bar, ring, holder, k)

	local icon = { Holder = holder, cur = C.sub, img = img, inks = inks }
	function icon.tint(color, t)
		icon.cur = color
		if img then tween(img, t or 0, { ImageColor3 = color }) end
		for _, f in inks do tween(f, t or 0, { BackgroundColor3 = color }) end
		for _, s in strokes do tween(s, t or 0, { Color = color }) end
	end
	function icon.paint(color)
		icon.cur = color
		if img then img.ImageColor3 = color end
		for _, f in inks do f.BackgroundColor3 = color end
	end
	if img then
		icon.name = lname
		function icon.setIcon(name)
			if name == icon.name then return end
			if State.setLucide(img, name) then icon.name = name end
		end
	end
	table.insert(State.icons, icon)
	return icon
end

local Icons = {}

function Icons.crosshair(bar)
	for _, sx in { -1, 1 } do
		for _, sy in { -1, 1 } do
			local cx, cy = 10 + sx * 8, 10 + sy * 8
			bar(cx - sx * 2.5, cy, 5, 1.6)
			bar(cx, cy - sy * 2.5, 1.6, 5)
		end
	end
	bar(10, 10, 7, 1.6)
	bar(10, 10, 1.6, 7)
end

function Icons.eye(_, ring)
	ring(10, 10, 18, 11, { r = 999 })
	ring(10, 10, 6, 6, { r = 999 })
end

function Icons.sparkle(bar)
	bar(8, 12, 1.6, 12) bar(8, 12, 12, 1.6)
	bar(8, 12, 1.4, 6, 45) bar(8, 12, 1.4, 6, -45)
	bar(15.5, 5, 1.4, 6) bar(15.5, 5, 6, 1.4)
end

function Icons.person(_, ring, holder, k)
	k = k or 1
	ring(10, 6, 8, 8, { r = 999 })
	local clip = createFrame(holder, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(2 * k, 13 * k), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16 * k, 6 * k), ClipsDescendants = true })
	ring(8, 6.5, 14, 14, { r = 999, parent = clip })
end

function Icons.players(_, ring)
	ring(10, 6, 7, 7, { r = 999 })
	ring(10, 15.5, 14, 7, { r = 3.5 })
end

function Icons.sliders(bar, ring)
	for i, y in { 4, 10, 16 } do
		bar(10, y, 16, 1.5)
		ring(({ 7, 13, 8 })[i], y, 5, 5, { r = 999, fill = C.bg })
	end
end

end

end

end

end

	bar(13, 15.5, 5.5, 1.8) bar(15.5, 13, 1.8, 5.5)
	bar(7, 4.5, 5.5, 1.8) bar(4.5, 7, 1.8, 5.5)
end

function Icons.shuffle(bar)
	bar(10, 10, 18, 1.7, -36) bar(10, 10, 18, 1.7, 36)
	bar(15.2, 4.5, 5, 1.7) bar(17.5, 6.8, 1.7, 5)
	bar(15.2, 15.5, 5, 1.7) bar(17.5, 13.2, 1.7, 5)
end

function Icons.loop(bar, ring)
	ring(10, 10, 15, 10, { r = 4 })
	bar(12.6, 4.2, 4, 1.7, 40) bar(12.6, 5.9, 4, 1.7, -40)
end

function Icons.replay(bar)
	local r, steps = 7.5, 12
	local from, to = 180, -137
	local d = (from - to) / steps
	for i = 0, steps - 1 do
		local mid = from - (i + 0.5) * d
		local rad = math.rad(mid)
		bar(10 + r * math.cos(rad), 10 + r * math.sin(rad), 2 * r * math.sin(math.rad(d / 2)) + 1.1, 1.7, mid + 90)
	end
	bar(3.45, 5.75, 2.9, 1.7, -45)
	bar(2.5, 4.6, 1.7, 4.4)
	bar(4.6, 6.7, 4.4, 1.7)
end

function Icons.translate(bar)
	local k = 20 / 24
	local function line(x1, y1, x2, y2)
		local dx, dy = x2 - x1, y2 - y1
		bar((x1 + x2) / 2 * k, (y1 + y2) / 2 * k, math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) * k + 1.2, 1.8, math.deg(math.atan2(dy, dx)))
	end
	line(5, 8, 11, 14)
	line(4, 14, 10, 8)
	line(10, 8, 12, 5)
	line(2, 5, 14, 5)
	line(7, 2, 8, 2)
	line(22, 22, 17, 12)
	line(17, 12, 12, 22)
	line(14, 18, 20, 18)
end

	bar(3.5, 10, 5, 1.8)
	bar(7.2, 7.5, 6, 1.8, -65)
	bar(10, 10, 6, 1.8, 65)
	bar(12.8, 12.5, 6, 1.8, 65)
	bar(16, 10, 5, 1.8, -65)
	bar(18, 10, 3, 1.8)
end

function Icons.music(bar)
	bar(8.3, 10.25, 1.6, 9.5)
	bar(16.3, 8.5, 1.6, 9)
	bar(12.3, 4.75, 8.4, 2.2, -10.6)
	for _, h in ipairs({ bar(6, 15, 5.4, 4, -20), bar(14, 13, 5.4, 4, -20) }) do
		local c = h:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner")
		if c then c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) end
	end
end

function Icons.volume(bar)
	local P = { w1 = {}, w2 = {} }
	bar(4.5, 10, 3.5, 5.5)
	for x = 7, 11 do bar(x, 10, 1.3, 5.5 + (x - 6.25) / 4.75 * 6.5) end
	for i, r in ipairs({ 6.5, 10 }) do
		for _, a in ipairs({ -40, -20, 0, 20, 40 }) do
			local rad = math.rad(a)

			table.insert(i == 1 and P.w1 or P.w2, bar(8 + r * math.cos(rad), 10 + r * math.sin(rad), 2.4, 1.6, a + 90))
		end
	end
	P.slash = bar(10, 10, 1.7, 19, -45)
	Icons.volumeParts = P
end

function Icons.wifi(bar)
	local lv = { {}, {}, {} }
	local dot = bar(10, 16.5, 3, 3)
	local c = dot:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner")
	if c then c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) end
	table.insert(lv[1], dot)
	for i, r in ipairs({ 5.5, 9.5, 13.5 }) do
		for a = -45, 45, 15 do
			local chord = 2 * r * math.sin(math.rad(7.5)) + 0.8
			table.insert(lv[i], bar(10 + r * math.sin(math.rad(a)), 16.5 - r * math.cos(math.rad(a)), chord, 1.8, a))
		end
	end
	Icons.wifiLevels = lv
end

local PanelFrames, PanelShadows = {}, {}
local function createPanel(parent, name, x, y, w, h, radius)
	local container = createFrame(parent, { Name = name .. "Container", Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, h) })
	for i = 1, 4 do
		local s = createFrame(container, {
			Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-i * 3, -i * 3 + 5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w + i * 6, h + i * 6),
			BackgroundColor3 = C.black, BackgroundTransparency = 0.93, ZIndex = 0,
		})
		corner(s, radius + i * 3)
		table.insert(PanelShadows, s)
	end
	local panel = createFrame(container, { Name = name, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = C.bg })
	corner(panel, radius)
	stroke(panel, C.accent, 1, 0.8)
	table.insert(PanelFrames, panel)

	local gfx = createFrame(panel, { Name = "ThemeGradient", Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 0, ZIndex = 0, Visible = false })
	gfx:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true)
	gfx:SetAttribute("NoTranslate", true)
	corner(gfx, radius)
	local gg = create("UIGradient", { Rotation = 35 }, gfx)
	gg:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true)
	table.insert(PanelFrames, gfx)
	State.panelGrads = State.panelGrads or {}
	table.insert(State.panelGrads, { gfx, gg })
	return panel, container
end

local function applyTransparency()
	local t = State.uiTransparency / 100
	for _, f in ipairs(PanelFrames) do f.BackgroundTransparency = t end
	for _, f in ipairs(PanelShadows) do f.BackgroundTransparency = 0.93 + 0.07 * t end
end

local function createSection(parent, x, y, w, text)
	local l = createLabel(parent, text, 11, C.secTitle, FONT_BOLD, {
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 16),
		AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None,
	})
	local line = createFrame(parent, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x + 40, y), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(0, w - 40), 1), BackgroundColor3 = C.line })
	local function fit()
		local tw = 40
		pcall(function() tw = game:GetService("TextService"):GetTextSize(l.Text, 11, FONT_BOLD, Vector2.new(1000, 16)).X + 8 end)
		line.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x + tw, y)
		line.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(0, w - tw), 1)
	end
	l:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(fit)
	fit()
end

local function createInfoRow(parent, x, y, w, key, value, iconBuilder)
	local kx = x
	if iconBuilder then
		local ic = newIcon(parent, 13, iconBuilder)
		ic.Holder.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x + 6.5, y)
		kx = x + 19
	end
	createLabel(parent, key, 11, C.sub, FONT, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(kx, y), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w * 0.5, 16) })
	local vl = createLabel(parent, value, 11, C.text, FONT_BOLD, {
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x + w, y), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w * 0.5, 16),
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, NoTitle = true,
	})
	vl:SetAttribute("NoTranslate", true)
	return vl
end

local function createCheckbox(parent, x, y, w, text, default, onToggle)
	local row = createButton(parent, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, 20) })
	local box = createFrame(row, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 3), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14), BackgroundColor3 = C.bg })
	corner(box, 3)
	local boxStroke = stroke(box, C.line, 1)
	local label = createLabel(row, text, 12, C.dim, FONT, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(24, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 1, 0) })

	local check = create("CanvasGroup", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, GroupTransparency = 1 }, box)
	local checkScale = create("UIScale", { Scale = 0.5 }, check)
	local marks = {}
	for _, b in { { 5.2, 8.2, 3.6, 45 }, { 8.6, 6.6, 7.5, -52 } } do
		local f = createFrame(check, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(b[1], b[2]), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(b[3], 1.8), Rotation = b[4], BackgroundColor3 = C.bg })
		corner(f, 1)
		table.insert(marks, f)
	end

	local state = default and true or false
	local api = {}
	local function render(animate)
		local t = animate and 0.16 or 0
		tween(box, t, { BackgroundColor3 = state and C.accent or C.bg })
		tween(boxStroke, t, { Color = state and C.accent or C.line })
		tween(label, t, { TextColor3 = state and C.text or C.dim })
		tween(check, t, { GroupTransparency = state and 0 or 1 })
		tween(checkScale, t, { Scale = state and 1 or 0.5 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
	end
	function api.Set(v, silent)
		state = v
		render(true)
		if not silent then State.sfx(state and "on" or "off") end
		if not silent and onToggle then onToggle(state) end
		if not silent and State.touch then State.touch() end
	end
	function api.Get() return state end
	State.regAdd(parent, "cb:" .. text, function() return state end, function(v) if state ~= (v and true or false) then api.Set(v and true or false) end end)

	row.Activated:Connect(function() api.Set(not state) end)
	row.MouseEnter:Connect(function() if not state then tween(boxStroke, 0.1, { Color = C.hoverLine }) end end)
	row.MouseLeave:Connect(function() if not state then tween(boxStroke, 0.1, { Color = C.line }) end end)
	render(false)

	table.insert(C.hooks, function()
		for _, f in ipairs(marks) do f.BackgroundColor3 = C.bg end
		render(false)
	end)
	return api
end

local function createKeybind(parent, x, y, text, onSet)
	local btn = createButton(parent, { Sound = true,
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(38, 16),
		BackgroundColor3 = C.field, BackgroundTransparency = 0, Text = text, TextSize = 9, Font = FONT_BOLD,
		TextColor3 = text == "NONE" and C.sub or C.text,
	})
	corner(btn, 4)
	local s = stroke(btn, C.line, 1)
	State.fitText(btn, 38, 9, 7, FONT_BOLD)
	local listening = false
	local shown = text

	local function set(name)
		shown = name
		btn.Text = name
		tween(btn, 0.12, { TextColor3 = name == "NONE" and C.sub or C.text })
		tween(s, 0.12, { Color = C.line })
	end

	btn.Activated:Connect(function()
		if listening then return end
		listening, capturing = true, true
		btn.Text = "..."
		btn.TextColor3 = C.accent
		tween(s, 0.12, { Color = C.accent })
		local conn
		conn = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
			local name
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
				name = input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape and "NONE" or string.sub(string.upper(input.KeyCode.Name), 1, 5)
			elseif input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then name = "M1"
			elseif input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 then name = "M2"
			elseif input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton3 then name = "M3" end
			if not name then return end
			conn:Disconnect()
			listening = false
			task.defer(function() capturing = false end)
			set(name)
			if onSet then onSet(input, name) end
		end)
	end)
	btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() if not listening then tween(s, 0.1, { Color = C.hoverLine2 }) end end)
	btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function() if not listening then tween(s, 0.1, { Color = C.line }) end end)
	State.regAdd(parent, "kb:" .. x .. ":" .. y, function() return shown end, function(n) if type(n) == "string" and not listening then set(n) end end)
	return btn
end

local function createSlider(parent, x, y, w, cfg)
	local min, max, dec = cfg.min, cfg.max, cfg.decimals or 0
	createLabel(parent, cfg.label, 11, C.text, FONT, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w - 50, 16) })
	local valueLabel = createLabel(parent, "", 11, C.sub, FONT, {
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x + w, y), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(50, 16), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,
	})
	local track = createFrame(parent, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y + 10), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, 3), BackgroundColor3 = C.track })
	corner(track, 2)
	local fill = createFrame(track, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent })
	corner(fill, 2)
	local handle = createFrame(track, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(9, 9), BackgroundColor3 = C.white })
	corner(handle, 999)
	local hit = createButton(parent, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y + 2), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, 16) })

	local cur = cfg.value
	local function set(v, instant, silent)
		local m = 10 ^ dec
		v = math.clamp(math.floor(v * m + 0.5) / m, min, max)
		cur = v
		local r = (v - min) / (max - min)
		local t = instant and 0 or 0.08
		tween(fill, t, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(r, 1) })
		tween(handle, t, { Position = UDim2.fromScale(r, 0.5) })
		valueLabel.Text = string.format("%." .. dec .. "f", v) .. (cfg.suffix or "")
		if cfg.onChange and not silent then cfg.onChange(v) end
		if not silent and State.touch then State.touch() end
	end

	bindDrag(hit, function(rx) set(min + rx * (max - min)) end)
	hit.MouseEnter:Connect(function() tween(handle, 0.12, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(11, 11) }) end)
	hit.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tween(handle, 0.12, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(9, 9) }) end)
	set(cfg.value, true)
	State.regAdd(parent, "sl:" .. tostring(cfg.label), function() return cur end, function(v) if type(v) == "number" then set(v, true) end end)
	return { Set = function(v, instant) set(v, instant, true) end, Get = function() return cur end }
end

local function createDropdown(parent, x, y, w, options, default, onChange, isData)

	local H = 24
	local btn = createButton(parent, { Sound = true, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, H), BackgroundColor3 = C.field, BackgroundTransparency = 0 })
	corner(btn, 6)
	local bs = stroke(btn, C.line, 1)
	local label = createLabel(btn, default, 12, C.text, FONT, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -30, 1, 0) })
	if isData then label:SetAttribute("NoTranslate", true) end
	local chev = newIcon(btn, 10, Icons.chevron)
	chev.Holder.Position = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0.5, 0)

	local MAXROWS = 6
	local listH = math.min(#options, MAXROWS) * 24 + 8
	local list = createFrame(parent, {
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y + H + 4), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, 0), BackgroundColor3 = C.field,
		ClipsDescendants = true, Visible = false, ZIndex = 40,
	})
	corner(list, 6)
	stroke(list, C.line, 1)
	local sf = create("ScrollingFrame", {
		Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ScrollBarThickness = 2,
		ScrollBarImageColor3 = C.accent, CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y,
	}, list)

	local optionButtons, current, open = {}, default, false
	local function setOpen(v)
		open = v
		if v then list.Visible = true end
		tween(list, 0.18, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, v and listH or 0) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
		tween(chev.Holder, 0.18, { Rotation = v and 180 or 0 })
		tween(bs, 0.15, { Color = v and C.accent or C.line })
		if not v then task.delay(0.19, function() if not open then list.Visible = false end end) end
	end

	local function choose(opt, silent)
		current = opt
		label.Text = opt
		for name, b in pairs(optionButtons) do b.TextColor3 = name == opt and C.accent or C.sub end
		setOpen(false)
		if not silent then
			if onChange then onChange(opt) end
			if State.touch then State.touch() end
		end
	end

	local function build(opts, sel)
		for _, b in pairs(optionButtons) do b:Destroy() end
		optionButtons = {}
		listH = math.min(#opts, MAXROWS) * 24 + 8
		sf.CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0, #opts * 24 + 8)
		for i, opt in ipairs(opts) do
			local ob = createButton(sf, { Sound = true,
				Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 4 + (i - 1) * 24), Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 22), Text = opt, TextSize = 12,
				TextColor3 = opt == sel and C.accent or C.sub, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, BackgroundColor3 = C.accent, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
			})
			corner(ob, 4)
			create("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8) }, ob)
			if isData then ob:SetAttribute("NoTranslate", true) end
			optionButtons[opt] = ob
			ob.MouseEnter:Connect(function() tween(ob, 0.1, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.88 }) end)
			ob.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tween(ob, 0.1, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }) end)
			ob.Activated:Connect(function() choose(opt) end)
		end
		current = sel
		label.Text = sel
		if open then tween(list, 0.18, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, listH) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint) end
	end
	build(options, default)

	btn.Activated:Connect(function() setOpen(not open) end)
	UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if open and isPress(input) and not isInside(list, input.Position) and not isInside(btn, input.Position) then setOpen(false) end
	end)

	if not isData then
		State.regAdd(parent, "dd:" .. x .. ":" .. y, function() return current end, function(opt) if optionButtons[opt] and opt ~= current then choose(opt) end end)
	end
	return {
		SetOptions = function(opts, sel) build(opts, sel or current) end,
		Set = function(opt, silent) if optionButtons[opt] then choose(opt, silent) end end,
		Get = function() return current end,
	}
end

local function createColorPicker(parent, x, y, initial, onChange)
	local swatch = createButton(parent, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(24, 14), BackgroundColor3 = initial, BackgroundTransparency = 0 })
	corner(swatch, 4)
	local h, s, v = initial:ToHSV()

	local popup = createFrame(parent, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x + 24 - 162, y + 13), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(162, 0), BackgroundColor3 = C.field, ClipsDescendants = true, Visible = false, ZIndex = 40 })
	corner(popup, 8)
	stroke(popup, C.line, 1)

	local sv = createFrame(popup, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 8), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(146, 74), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromHSV(h, 1, 1) })
	corner(sv, 4)
	local white = createFrame(sv, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = C.white })
	corner(white, 4)
	create("UIGradient", { Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0, 1) }, white)
	local dark = createFrame(sv, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = C.black })
	corner(dark, 4)
	create("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Transparency = NumberSequence.new(1, 0) }, dark)
	local marker = createFrame(sv, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(9, 9) })
	corner(marker, 999)
	stroke(marker, C.white, 1.5)

	local hue = createFrame(popup, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 90), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(146, 10), BackgroundColor3 = C.white })
	corner(hue, 5)
	local keys = {}
	for i = 0, 6 do table.insert(keys, ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(i / 6, Color3.fromHSV(i / 6 % 1, 1, 1))) end
	create("UIGradient", { Color = ColorSequence.new(keys) }, hue)
	local hueMarker = createFrame(hue, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(h, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 14), BackgroundColor3 = C.white })
	corner(hueMarker, 2)

	local function apply()
		local color = Color3.fromHSV(h, s, v)
		sv.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromHSV(h, 1, 1)
		marker.Position = UDim2.fromScale(s, 1 - v)
		hueMarker.Position = UDim2.fromScale(h, 0.5)
		swatch.BackgroundColor3 = color
		if onChange then onChange(color) end
		if State.touch then State.touch() end
	end
	bindDrag(createButton(sv, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 5 }), function(rx, ry) s, v = rx, 1 - ry apply() end)
	bindDrag(createButton(hue, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 5 }), function(rx) h = math.min(rx, 0.999) apply() end)

	local open = false
	local function setOpen(o)
		open = o
		if o then popup.Visible = true end
		tween(popup, 0.18, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(162, o and 108 or 0) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
		if not o then task.delay(0.19, function() if not open then popup.Visible = false end end) end
	end
	swatch.Activated:Connect(function() setOpen(not open) end)
	UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if open and isPress(input) and not isInside(popup, input.Position) and not isInside(swatch, input.Position) then setOpen(false) end
	end)
	apply()
	State.regAdd(parent, "cp:" .. x .. ":" .. y, function()
		local c = Color3.fromHSV(h, s, v)
		return { math.floor(c.R * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(c.G * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(c.B * 255 + 0.5) }
	end, function(t)
		if type(t) == "table" and #t == 3 then
			h, s, v = Color3.fromRGB(t[1], t[2], t[3]):ToHSV()
			apply()
		end
	end)
	return {
		Set = function(c) if typeof(c) == "Color3" then h, s, v = c:ToHSV() apply() end end,
		Get = function() return Color3.fromHSV(h, s, v) end,
	}
end

local function createActionButton(parent, x, y, w, text, onClick, filled)
	local function base() return filled and C.accent or C.field end
	local function hover() return filled and C.accentHover or C.hoverField end
	local btn = createButton(parent, { Sound = true,
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, 24),
		BackgroundColor3 = base(), BackgroundTransparency = 0, Text = text, TextSize = 12, Font = FONT_BOLD,
		TextColor3 = filled and C.bg or C.text,
	})
	corner(btn, 6)
	local s = stroke(btn, filled and C.accent or C.line, 1)
	State.fitText(btn, w, 12, 7, FONT_BOLD)
	local press = create("UIScale", {}, btn)
	btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		tween(btn, 0.1, { BackgroundColor3 = hover() })
		if not filled then tween(s, 0.1, { Color = C.hoverLine2 }) end
	end)
	btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		tween(btn, 0.1, { BackgroundColor3 = base() })
		tween(press, 0.1, { Scale = 1 })
		if not filled then tween(s, 0.1, { Color = C.line }) end
	end)
	btn.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function() tween(press, 0.07, { Scale = 0.96 }) end)
	btn.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function() tween(press, 0.14, { Scale = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back) end)
	btn.Activated:Connect(function() if onClick then onClick() end end)
	table.insert(C.hooks, function()
		btn.BackgroundColor3 = base()
		s.Color = filled and C.accent or C.line
		btn.TextColor3 = filled and C.bg or C.text
	end)
	return btn
end

local function createTextInput(parent, x, y, w, placeholder, onSubmit)
	local frame = createFrame(parent, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, 24), BackgroundColor3 = C.field })
	corner(frame, 6)
	local s = stroke(frame, C.line, 1)
	local box = create("TextBox", {
		BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 1, 0), Text = "", PlaceholderText = placeholder,
		PlaceholderColor3 = C.dim, TextColor3 = C.text, Font = FONT, TextSize = 12, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, ClearTextOnFocus = false,
	}, frame)
	box.Focused:Connect(function() tween(s, 0.12, { Color = C.accent }) end)
	box.FocusLost:Connect(function(enter)
		tween(s, 0.12, { Color = C.line })
		if onSubmit then onSubmit(box.Text, enter) end
	end)
	return box
end

local old = playerGui:FindFirstChild("NauruHubUI")
if old then old:Destroy() end

local gui = create("ScreenGui", { Name = "NauruHubUI", ResetOnSpawn = false, ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling, IgnoreGuiInset = true }, playerGui)
pcall(function() gui.ScreenInsets = Enum.ScreenInsets.DeviceSafeInsets end)
for _ = 1, 60 do
	if gui.AbsoluteSize.X > 0 then break end
	task.wait()
end

local dimFrame = createFrame(gui, { Name = "Dim", Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = C.black, BackgroundTransparency = 1 })

local holder = createFrame(gui, { Name = "Holder", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0) })
local responsiveScale = create("UIScale", {}, holder)
local root = create("CanvasGroup", { Name = "Root", Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, holder)
local popScale = create("UIScale", {}, root)
popScale.Scale = 1
local layoutFrame = createFrame(root, { Name = "Layout", Position = UDim2.fromOffset(PAD, PAD), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(460, 555) })

local view = { scale = CONFIG.defaultScale, x = 0, y = 0, w = 492, h = 587 }
local scaleSlider
local gripBtn
local gripConn
local viewportValue
local layoutMode = "wide"

State.mobileFull = UserInputService.TouchEnabled and not UserInputService.KeyboardEnabled
local function maxScale()
	local vp = gui.AbsoluteSize

	return math.min(vp.X / (view.w - PAD * 2), vp.Y / (view.h - PAD * 2))
end

local function applyView()
	local vp = gui.AbsoluteSize
	if vp.X < 1 or vp.Y < 1 then return end
	view.scale = math.min(math.max(view.scale, CONFIG.minScale), maxScale())
	responsiveScale.Scale = view.scale
	if State.pinSync then State.pinSync() end
	holder.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(view.w, view.h)
	local pad = PAD * view.scale
	view.x = math.clamp(view.x, -pad, math.max(-pad, vp.X - view.w * view.scale + pad))
	view.y = math.clamp(view.y, -pad, math.max(-pad, vp.Y - view.h * view.scale + pad))
	holder.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(view.x, view.y)
	if scaleSlider then scaleSlider.Set(view.scale, true) end
end

function State.autoScale()
	local vp = gui.AbsoluteSize
	if vp.X < 1 or vp.Y < 1 then return CONFIG.defaultScale end
	if State.mobileFull then return math.clamp(maxScale() * 0.85, CONFIG.minScale, maxScale()) end
	return math.clamp(vp.Y / (895 + (State.extH or 0)), CONFIG.defaultScale, 1.6)
end

local function centerView()
	local vp = gui.AbsoluteSize
	view.x = (vp.X - view.w * view.scale) / 2
	view.y = (vp.Y - view.h * view.scale) / 2
	applyView()
end

local function bindWindowDrag(hit)
	local dragging, startMouse, startX, startY, active = false, nil, 0, 0, nil
	hit.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if isPress(input) then
			active = input
			dragging, startMouse, startX, startY = true, input.Position, view.x, view.y
		end
	end)
	UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if dragging and isMove(input) and (input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch or input == active) then
			local d = input.Position - startMouse
			view.x, view.y = startX + d.X, startY + d.Y
			applyView()
		end
	end)
	UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if isPress(input) and (input == active or input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then dragging, active = false, nil end
	end)
end

do
	local grip = createButton(layoutFrame, { Name = "ResizeGrip", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(466, 445), Size = State.mobileFull and UDim2.fromOffset(48, 48) or UDim2.fromOffset(30, 30), ZIndex = 20 })
	gripBtn = grip
	for _, sh in ipairs({ { 1, 3, 0.8 }, { 0, 2, 0.5 } }) do
		local g = create("CanvasGroup", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, sh[1], 0.5, sh[2]), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18), BackgroundTransparency = 1, GroupTransparency = sh[3] }, grip)
		local si = newIcon(g, 16, Icons.grip)
		si.Holder.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
		si.tint(C.black)
	end
	local gripIcon = newIcon(grip, 16, Icons.grip)
	gripIcon.Holder.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
	gripIcon.Holder.ZIndex = 22
	gripIcon.tint(C.sub)

	local resizing, startMouse, startScale, active = false, nil, 1, nil
	grip.MouseEnter:Connect(function() if not resizing then gripIcon.tint(C.text, 0.12) end end)
	grip.MouseLeave:Connect(function() if not resizing then gripIcon.tint(C.sub, 0.12) end end)
	grip.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if isPress(input) then
			active = input
			resizing, startMouse, startScale = true, input.Position, view.scale
			gripIcon.tint(C.accent, 0.1)
		end
	end)
	UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if resizing and isMove(input) and (input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch or input == active) then
			local d = input.Position - startMouse
			view.scale = startScale + (d.X / view.w + d.Y / view.h) / 2
			applyView()
		end
	end)
	UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if isPress(input) and resizing and (input == active or input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
			resizing, active = false, nil
			gripIcon.tint(C.sub, 0.15)
		end
	end)
end

local toastHolder = createFrame(gui, { Name = "Toasts", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 1), Position = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, -16), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(250, 320) })
create("UIListLayout", { VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Bottom, HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Right, Padding = UDim.new(0, 8), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, toastHolder)
local toastId = 0

local function notify(title, text)
	toastId += 1
	local toast = create("CanvasGroup", { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(240, 46), BackgroundColor3 = C.bg, BorderSizePixel = 0, GroupTransparency = 1, LayoutOrder = toastId }, toastHolder)
	corner(toast, 8)
	local toastStroke = stroke(toast, C.accent, 1, 1)
	createFrame(toast, { Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 1, 0), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent })
	createLabel(toast, title, 12, C.white, FONT_BOLD, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 9), Size = UDim2.new(1, -22, 0, 14), TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd })
	createLabel(toast, text, 11, C.sub, FONT, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 25), Size = UDim2.new(1, -22, 0, 14), TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd })
	tween(toast, 0.25, { GroupTransparency = 0 })
	tween(toastStroke, 0.25, { Transparency = 0.6 })
	task.delay(2.6, function()
		tween(toast, 0.3, { GroupTransparency = 1 })
		tween(toastStroke, 0.3, { Transparency = 1 })
		task.wait(0.32)
		toast:Destroy()
	end)
end

local topBar, topBarContainer = createPanel(layoutFrame, "TopBar", 0, 0, 296, 28, 7)
bindWindowDrag(createButton(topBar, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1) }))

local topAccent = createFrame(topBar, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(7, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -14, 0, 2), BackgroundColor3 = C.white })
corner(topAccent, 1)
State.topLine = create("UIGradient", { Color = ColorSequence.new({ ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, C.accent), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, C.red), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, C.accent) }) }, topAccent)
State.topLine.Offset = Vector2.new(-1, 0)
TweenService:Create(State.topLine, TweenInfo.new(2.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.Out, -1), { Offset = Vector2.new(1, 0) }):Play()
local topRow = createFrame(topBar, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1) })
create("UIListLayout", { FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal, Padding = UDim.new(0, 7), VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center, HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center, SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, topRow)

local function topDivider(order) createFrame(topRow, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(1, 12), BackgroundColor3 = C.divider, LayoutOrder = order }) end
function State.measureW(text, font)
	local plain = string.gsub(text, "<[^>]+>", "")
	local ok, v = pcall(function() return game:GetService("TextService"):GetTextSize(plain, 11, font, Vector2.new(10000, 100)) end)
	return math.ceil(ok and v.X or #plain * 7) + 4
end
local function autoLabel(text, color, order, font)
	font = font or FONT_BOLD
	local l = createLabel(topRow, text, 11, color, font, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(State.measureW(text, font), 28), LayoutOrder = order, RichText = true, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center })
	l:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
		l.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(State.measureW(l.Text, font), 28)
		if State.fitBar then State.fitBar() end
	end)
	return l
end
local brandLabel = autoLabel('Nauru Hub <font color="rgb(135,125,121)" face="GothamMedium">[beta]</font>', C.white, 1)
topDivider(2)

State.clockOffset = nil
function State.clockText()
	if State.clockOffset then
		local okT, utc = pcall(function() return DateTime.now().UnixTimestamp end)
		return os.date("!%H:%M:%S", (okT and utc or os.time()) + math.floor(State.clockOffset * 3600 + 0.5))
	end
	local ok, t = pcall(function() return DateTime.now():FormatLocalTime("HH:mm:ss", "en-us") end)
	if ok and type(t) == "string" then return t end
	return os.date("%H:%M:%S")
end
State.clockSources = {
	{ url = "https://ipwho.is/", parse = function(b)
		local sec = string.match(b, '"timezone"%s*:%s*{.-"offset"%s*:%s*(-?%d+)')
		return sec and tonumber(sec) / 3600
	end },
	{ url = "https://worldtimeapi.org/api/ip", parse = function(b)
		local sign, hh, mm = string.match(b, '"utc_offset"%s*:%s*"([+-])(%d%d):?(%d%d)"')
		return sign and (tonumber(hh) + tonumber(mm) / 60) * (sign == "-" and -1 or 1)
	end },
	{ url = "https://ipapi.co/json/", parse = function(b)
		local sign, hh, mm = string.match(b, '"utc_offset"%s*:%s*"([+-])(%d%d):?(%d%d)"')
		return sign and (tonumber(hh) + tonumber(mm) / 60) * (sign == "-" and -1 or 1)
	end },
	{ url = "http://ip-api.com/json/?fields=status,offset", parse = function(b)
		local sec = string.match(b, '"offset"%s*:%s*(-?%d+)')
		return sec and tonumber(sec) / 3600
	end },
}
function State.clockLookup()
	local req = request or http_request or (syn and syn.request) or (http and http.request)
	for _, src in ipairs(State.clockSources) do
		local body
		if req then
			local ok, res = pcall(function() return req({ Url = src.url, Method = "GET" }) end)
			body = ok and res and res.Body
		end
		if not body then
			local ok, res = pcall(function() return game:HttpGet(src.url) end)
			body = ok and res
		end
		if type(body) == "string" then
			local ok, off = pcall(src.parse, body)
			if ok and type(off) == "number" and off >= -14 and off <= 14 then
				State.clockOffset = off
				return true
			end
		end
	end
	return false
end
task.spawn(function()
	while gui.Parent do
		local ok = State.clockLookup()
		task.wait(ok and 1800 or 30)
	end
end)
local clockLabel = autoLabel(State.clockText(), C.topText, 7)
topDivider(8)

State.execFile = "NauruHub/.exec.dat"
function State.execHash(n)
	local str, h1, h2 = n .. ":nauru-exec-7f3a91", 5381, 52711
	for i = 1, #str do
		local c = string.byte(str, i)
		h1 = (h1 * 33 + c) % 4294967291
		h2 = (h2 * 131 + c * 7 + h1) % 4294967279
	end
	return string.format("%08x%08x", h1, h2)
end
State.execCount = 1
pcall(function()
	if readfile and writefile and isfile and isfolder and makefolder then
		local prev = 0
		if isfile(State.execFile) then
			local n, sum = string.match(readfile(State.execFile), "^(%d+)|(%x+)$")
			if n and sum == State.execHash(n) then prev = tonumber(n) end
		end
		State.execCount = math.floor(prev) + 1
		if not isfolder("NauruHub") then makefolder("NauruHub") end
		writefile(State.execFile, State.execCount .. "|" .. State.execHash(State.execCount))
	end
end)
local execLabel = autoLabel(State.execCount .. (State.execCount == 1 and " Execution" or " Executions"), C.topText, 9)

do
	local function ntag(o) o:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true) o:SetAttribute("NoTranslate", true) return o end
	local GOOD = C.green

	local dot = ntag(createFrame(topBar, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(9, 14), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(5, 5), BackgroundColor3 = GOOD, BackgroundTransparency = 0 }))
	corner(dot, 999)
	local ring = ntag(createFrame(topBar, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(9, 14), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(5, 5), BackgroundColor3 = GOOD, BackgroundTransparency = 0.3 }))
	corner(ring, 999)
	task.spawn(function()
		while gui.Parent do
			ring.Size, ring.BackgroundTransparency = UDim2.fromOffset(5, 5), 0.3
			tween(ring, 1.3, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(13, 13), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
			task.wait(1.9)
		end
	end)

end

do
	local div = createFrame(topRow, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(1, 12), BackgroundColor3 = C.divider, LayoutOrder = 10 })
	local function short(n, cap)
		if utf8.len(n) and utf8.len(n) > cap then return string.sub(n, 1, (utf8.offset(n, cap) or cap + 1) - 1) .. "..." end
		return n
	end
	local fullName, userShow, fitting = game.Name, true, false
	local lbl = autoLabel(short(fullName, 24), C.topText, 11)
	State.gameTitle = { label = lbl, div = div }
	local MAXW = 476
	local function fitBar()
		if fitting then return end
		fitting = true
		local sum, n = 0, 0
		for _, ch in ipairs(topRow:GetChildren()) do
			if ch:IsA("GuiObject") and ch.Visible and ch ~= lbl and ch ~= div and ch ~= execLabel then sum += ch.Size.X.Offset n += 1 end
		end
		local function totalWith(exW, titleW)
			local tsum, tn = sum + exW, n + 1
			if titleW then tsum += titleW + 1 tn += 2 end
			return tsum + 7 * math.max(tn - 1, 0) + 28
		end
		local execFull = State.execCount .. (State.execCount == 1 and " Execution" or " Executions")
		local execShort = State.execCount .. " Exec"
		local exText, text, shown = execFull, lbl.Text, false
		local found = false
		for _, ex in ipairs({ execFull, execShort }) do
			local exW = State.measureW(ex, FONT_BOLD)
			if not userShow then
				if totalWith(exW, nil) <= MAXW then exText, found = ex, true break end
			else
				for _, cap in ipairs({ 24, 18, 14, 11, 8 }) do
					local t = short(fullName, cap)
					if totalWith(exW, State.measureW(t, FONT_BOLD)) <= MAXW then exText, text, shown, found = ex, t, true, true break end
				end
				if found then break end
			end
		end
		if not found then exText = execShort end
		if execLabel.Text ~= exText then execLabel.Text = exText end
		local exW = State.measureW(exText, FONT_BOLD)
		if execLabel.Size.X.Offset ~= exW then execLabel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(exW, 28) end
		if shown then
			if lbl.Text ~= text then lbl.Text = text end
			local wpx = State.measureW(text, FONT_BOLD)
			if lbl.Size.X.Offset ~= wpx then lbl.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(wpx, 28) end
		end
		if lbl.Visible ~= shown then lbl.Visible = shown end
		if div.Visible ~= shown then div.Visible = shown end
		local w = totalWith(exW, shown and lbl.Size.X.Offset or nil)
		topBarContainer.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(math.min(0, math.floor((460 - w) / 2)), 0)
		if topBarContainer.Size.X.Offset ~= w then
			topBarContainer.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, 28)
			local i = 0
			for _, ch in ipairs(topBarContainer:GetChildren()) do
				if ch ~= topBar and ch:IsA("Frame") then
					i += 1
					ch.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w + i * 6, 28 + i * 6)
				end
			end
		end
		fitting = false
	end
	State.fitBar = fitBar
	function State.gameTitle.set(v) userShow = v fitBar() end
	fitBar()
	task.spawn(function()
		local ok, info = pcall(function() return game:GetService("MarketplaceService"):GetProductInfo(game.PlaceId) end)
		if ok and info and info.Name and info.Name ~= "" then fullName = info.Name fitBar() end
	end)
end

local mainWindow = createPanel(layoutFrame, "MainWindow", 0, 38, 460, 401, 10)

local sidebar = createFrame(mainWindow, { Name = "Sidebar", Size = UDim2.fromOffset(76, 401) })
createFrame(mainWindow, { Name = "Divider", Position = UDim2.fromOffset(76, 0), Size = UDim2.new(0, 1, 1, 0), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent, BackgroundTransparency = 0.3 })
local content = createFrame(mainWindow, { Name = "Content", Position = UDim2.fromOffset(77, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(383, 401) })

local pages = {}
local currentPage
local PAGE_BASE = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
local descLabel
local DESCRIPTIONS = {
	main = "Movement, Camera, Teleportation",
	visuals = "ESP for Monsters, Players and Items",
	settings = "Window scale and size presets, menu keybind, viewport info and about.",
}

local function createPage(name)
	local p = create("CanvasGroup", { Name = name, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, GroupTransparency = 1, Visible = false }, content)
	pages[name] = p
	return p
end

local function switchPage(name)
	if name == currentPage or not pages[name] then return end
	local old, new = pages[currentPage], pages[name]
	local oldName = currentPage
	currentPage = name
	if descLabel then
		descLabel.Text = DESCRIPTIONS[name] or ""
		descLabel.TextTransparency = 1
		tween(descLabel, 0.25, { TextTransparency = 0 })
	end
	if old then
		tween(old, 0.14, { GroupTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-10, 0) })
		task.delay(0.15, function() if currentPage ~= oldName then old.Visible = false end end)
	end
	new.Visible = true
	new.GroupTransparency = 1
	new.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 0)
	tween(new, 0.24, { GroupTransparency = 0, Position = PAGE_BASE }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
end

local tabs, currentTab = {}, "main"
local function selectTab(name)
	currentTab = name
	for n, b in pairs(tabs) do tween(b, 0.15, { TextColor3 = n == name and C.accent or C.sub }) end
	switchPage(name)
end
for i, name in ipairs({ "main", "visuals", "effects", "player", "config" }) do
	local b = createButton(tabBar, { Sound = true, Text = name, TextSize = 12, Font = FONT_BOLD, TextColor3 = C.sub, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 20), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, LayoutOrder = 3 + i })
	create("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 5), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 5) }, b)
	tabs[name] = b
	b.Activated:Connect(function() selectTab(name) end)
	b.MouseEnter:Connect(function() if currentTab ~= name then tween(b, 0.1, { TextColor3 = C.text }) end end)
	b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() if currentTab ~= name then tween(b, 0.1, { TextColor3 = C.sub }) end end)
end

local sections = {
	{ name = "features", icon = Icons.sparkle, y = 34, pages = { "main", "visuals" } },
	{ name = "ui", icon = Icons.config, y = 76, pages = { "effects", "player", "config" } },
	{ name = "themes", icon = Icons.palette, y = 118, pages = { "themes" } },
	{ name = "settings", icon = "settings", y = 322, pages = { "settings" }, pinned = true },
}
local navs = {}

local function setSection(i)
	local sec = sections[i]
	if not sec then return end
	for j, n in ipairs(navs) do
		n.selected = (j == i)
		n.icon.tint(n.selected and C.accent or C.sub, 0.18)
		tween(n.btn, 0.18, { BackgroundTransparency = n.selected and 0.9 or 1 })
	end
	local multi = #sec.pages > 1
	local inSec = {}
	for _, n in ipairs(sec.pages) do inSec[n] = true end
	crumbLabel.Text = State.titleCase(sec.name)
	crumbSep.Visible = multi
	for n, b in pairs(tabs) do b.Visible = multi and inSec[n] == true end
	if multi then
		if not inSec[currentTab] then currentTab = sec.pages[1] end
		selectTab(currentTab)
	else
		switchPage(sec.pages[1])
	end
end

function State.makeNav(sec)
	local btn = createButton(sidebar, { Sound = true, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(38, sec.y), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(44, 40), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent })
	corner(btn, 9)
	local icon = newIcon(btn, 20, sec.icon)
	icon.Holder.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
	local sc = create("UIScale", {}, icon.Holder)
	local nav = { btn = btn, icon = icon, selected = false, sc = sc }
	btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		tween(sc, 0.15, { Scale = 1.15 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
		if not nav.selected then icon.tint(C.text, 0.12) tween(btn, 0.12, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.95 }) end
	end)
	btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		tween(sc, 0.15, { Scale = 1 })
		if not nav.selected then icon.tint(C.sub, 0.12) tween(btn, 0.12, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }) end
	end)
	btn.Activated:Connect(function() setSection(table.find(sections, sec)) end)
	return nav
end

function State.layoutNavs(animate)
	local count = 0
	for _, sec in ipairs(sections) do if not sec.pinned then count += 1 end end
	local step = count <= 6 and 42 or 36
	local y = 34
	for i, sec in ipairs(sections) do
		if not sec.pinned then sec.y = y y += step end
		local pos = UDim2.fromOffset(38, sec.y)
		if animate then tween(navs[i].btn, 0.25, { Position = pos }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint) else navs[i].btn.Position = pos end
	end
end

function State.hideSection(name)
	for i, sec in ipairs(sections) do
		if sec.name == name and not sec.pinned then
			local wasSelected = navs[i].selected
			navs[i].btn:Destroy()
			table.remove(sections, i)
			table.remove(navs, i)
			State.layoutNavs(true)
			if wasSelected then setSection(1) end
			return true
		end
	end
	return false
end

for i, sec in ipairs(sections) do navs[i] = State.makeNav(sec) end

local avatar = createButton(sidebar, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(38, 366), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(34, 34), BackgroundColor3 = C.field, BackgroundTransparency = 0 })
corner(avatar, 999)
stroke(avatar, C.accent, 1.5)

corner(avatarImg, 999)

avatar.MouseEnter:Connect(function() tween(avatarScale, 0.15, { Scale = 1.08 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back) end)
avatar.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tween(avatarScale, 0.15, { Scale = 1 }) end)
avatar.Activated:Connect(function()
	for i, sec in ipairs(sections) do if sec.name == "settings" then setSection(i) end end
end)

function State.dismissBtn(parent, key)
	local b = createButton(parent, {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 9), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 16),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1, ZIndex = 10,
	})
	corner(b, 4)
	local st = stroke(b, C.line, 1, 1)
	local bars = {}
	for _, rot in { 45, -45 } do
		local xb = createFrame(b, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(9, 1.6), Rotation = rot, BackgroundColor3 = C.sub, ZIndex = 11 })
		corner(xb, 1)
		table.insert(bars, xb)
	end
	b.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		for _, xb in ipairs(bars) do tween(xb, 0.1, { BackgroundColor3 = C.red }) end
		tween(st, 0.1, { Color = C.red })
	end)
	b.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		for _, xb in ipairs(bars) do tween(xb, 0.1, { BackgroundColor3 = C.sub }) end
		tween(st, 0.1, { Color = C.line })
	end)
	b.Activated:Connect(function() State.dock.set(key, false) end)
	return b
end

function State.headGlow(parent, z)
	local f = createFrame(parent, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent, BackgroundTransparency = 0.86, ZIndex = z or 1 })
	create("UIGradient", { Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.55, 0.55), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1) }) }, f)
	return f
end

function State.cardHeader(panel, icon, title)
	local clip = createFrame(panel, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22), ClipsDescendants = true, ZIndex = 1 })
	local g = State.headGlow(clip, 1)
	g.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40)
	corner(g, 10)
	createFrame(panel, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 22), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1), BackgroundColor3 = C.divider })
	local lbl
	if icon then
		local ic = newIcon(panel, 12, icon)
		ic.Holder.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 11)
		ic.tint(C.accent)
	end
	if title then
		lbl = createLabel(panel, title, 10, C.sub, FONT_BOLD, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -110, 0, 22) })
	end
	return lbl
end

function State.pill(lbl)
	lbl.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
	lbl.Position = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 4)
	lbl.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X
	lbl.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 14)
	lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
	lbl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None
	lbl.TextSize = 9
	lbl.TextColor3 = C.accent
	lbl.BackgroundColor3 = C.accent
	lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 0.84
	create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0) }, lbl)
	create("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 7), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 7) }, lbl)
	stroke(lbl, C.accent, 1, 0.7)
	lbl.Visible = lbl.Text ~= ""
	lbl:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function() lbl.Visible = lbl.Text ~= "" end)
	return lbl
end

function State.makeSheen(parent, radius, z, strength)
	local f = createFrame(parent, { Name = "Sheen", Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 0, ZIndex = z or 60, Visible = false, Active = false })
	f:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true)
	if radius then corner(f, radius):SetAttribute("NoTheme", true) end
	local peak = strength or 0.88
	local K = NumberSequenceKeypoint.new
	local g = create("UIGradient", { Rotation = 20, Offset = Vector2.new(-1, 0), Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
		K(0, 1), K(0.26, 1), K(0.36, 0.985), K(0.43, 0.95), K(0.5, peak), K(0.57, 0.95), K(0.64, 0.985), K(0.74, 1), K(1, 1),
	}) }, f)
	local tok = 0
	local api = {}
	function api.play(dur, delay)
		tok += 1
		local my = tok
		g.Offset = Vector2.new(-1, 0)
		task.delay(delay or 0, function()
			if my ~= tok then return end
			f.Visible = true
			local tw = tween(g, dur or 0.9, { Offset = Vector2.new(1, 0) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut)
			tw.Completed:Connect(function() if my == tok then f.Visible = false end end)
		end)
	end
	return api
end

State.popFxList = {}
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
	for _, e in ipairs(State.popFxList) do
		if e.S.slot.Visible then e.g.Rotation = (e.g.Rotation + dt * 80) % 360 end
	end
end)
function State.popFx(S, popup, st, rowsFn)
	local wash = createFrame(popup, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent, BackgroundTransparency = 0, ZIndex = 0 })
	create("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
		NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.8), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0.94), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
	}) }, wash)
	st.Color = C.white
	local g = create("UIGradient", { Color = ColorSequence.new({
		ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, C.accent), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, C.line), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, C.accent),
	}) }, st)
	table.insert(State.popFxList, { S = S, g = g })
	local sheen = State.makeSheen(popup, nil, 60, 0.9)
	function S.fx()
		sheen.play(1.0)
		if rowsFn then
			local n = 0
			for _, r in ipairs(rowsFn()) do
				if r:IsA("GuiButton") and r.Visible then
					n += 1
					if n > 9 then break end
					local us = r:FindFirstChild("PopScale") or create("UIScale", { Name = "PopScale" }, r)
					us.Scale = 0.86
					task.delay(0.06 + n * 0.035, function()
						if us.Parent then tween(us, 0.35, { Scale = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back) end
					end)
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

function State.slide(popup, parent, card)
	local S = { card = card, popup = popup, open = false, busyUntil = 0, tok = 0, rest = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 6), hide = UDim2.fromOffset(3, -200) }
	S.slot = create("Frame", {
		Name = popup.Name .. "Slot", BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ClipsDescendants = true,
		Visible = false, ZIndex = popup.ZIndex, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0),
	}, parent)
	popup.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
	popup.Parent = S.slot

	function S.sliding() return os.clock() < S.busyUntil end

	function S.raise()
		local c = S.card
		if c and not S.raisedTo and c.ZIndex <= S.slot.ZIndex then
			S.cardZ = c.ZIndex
			c.ZIndex = S.slot.ZIndex + 1
			S.raisedTo = c.ZIndex
		end
	end
	function S.lower()
		local c = S.card
		if c and S.raisedTo and c.ZIndex == S.raisedTo then c.ZIndex = S.cardZ end
		S.raisedTo = nil
	end

	function S.set(x, y, w, h, below, dur)
		local gap, M = 6 + 28, 3
		local sw, sh = w + M * 2, h + gap + M
		S.rest = UDim2.fromOffset(M, below and gap or M)
		S.hide = UDim2.fromOffset(M, below and -(h + 4) or (sh + 4))
		local sp = UDim2.fromOffset(x - M, below and (y - gap) or (y - M))
		local ss, ps = UDim2.fromOffset(sw, sh), UDim2.fromOffset(w, h)
		local goal = S.open and S.rest or S.hide
		local flipped = S.below ~= nil and S.below ~= below
		S.below = below
		if S.slot.Visible and not flipped and ((dur or 0) > 0 or S.sliding()) then
			local t = (dur or 0) > 0 and dur or 0.22
			tween(S.slot, t, { Position = sp, Size = ss }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
			tween(S.popup, t, { Size = ps, Position = goal }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
		else
			State.stopTweens(S.slot)
			State.stopTweens(S.popup)
			S.slot.Position, S.slot.Size = sp, ss
			S.popup.Size, S.popup.Position = ps, goal
		end
	end

	function S.show()
		S.tok += 1
		S.open = true
		S.raise()
		if not S.slot.Visible then
			State.stopTweens(S.popup)
			S.popup.Position = S.hide
			S.slot.Visible, S.popup.Visible = true, true
		end
		S.popup.Visible = true
		if S.popup:IsA("CanvasGroup") then S.popup.GroupTransparency = 0 end
		if S.fx then S.fx() end
		S.busyUntil = os.clock() + 0.45
		tween(S.popup, 0.45, { Position = S.rest }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
	end

	function S.close(onDone)
		if not S.slot.Visible then if onDone then onDone() end return end
		S.tok += 1
		local t = S.tok
		S.open = false
		S.busyUntil = os.clock() + 0.3
		tween(S.popup, 0.3, { Position = S.hide }, Enum.EasingStyle.Cubic, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
		task.delay(0.31, function()
			if S.tok == t and not S.open then
				S.slot.Visible, S.popup.Visible = false, false
				S.lower()
				if onDone then onDone() end
			end
		end)
	end

	function S.snap()
		S.tok += 1
		S.open = false
		State.stopTweens(S.popup)
		S.slot.Visible, S.popup.Visible = false, false
		S.popup.Position = S.hide
		S.lower()
	end
	return S
end

State.pinPop = { music = {}, lang = {}, appearance = {}, players = {} }
State.pinArrows = {}
function State.pinBtn(parent, key)
	local b = createButton(parent, {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, -30, 0, 9), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 16),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1, ZIndex = 10,
	})
	corner(b, 4)
	local pinIcon = newIcon(b, 12, "arrow-up-right")
	pinIcon.Holder.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
	pinIcon.Holder.ZIndex = 11
	pinIcon.tint(C.sub)
	State.pinArrows[key] = pinIcon.Holder
	b.MouseEnter:Connect(function() pinIcon.tint(C.accent, 0.1) end)
	b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() pinIcon.tint(C.sub, 0.1) end)
	b.Activated:Connect(function() State.pin.toggle(key) end)
	return b
end

function State.popBelow(wc, h, below)
	if wc.AbsoluteSize.Y <= 0 then return below end
	local sy = wc.Size.Y.Offset > 0 and wc.AbsoluteSize.Y / wc.Size.Y.Offset or 1
	local need = (h + 10) * sy
	local roomAbove = wc.AbsolutePosition.Y
	local roomBelow = gui.AbsoluteSize.Y - (wc.AbsolutePosition.Y + wc.AbsoluteSize.Y)
	if below and need > roomBelow and roomAbove > roomBelow then return false end
	if not below and need > roomAbove and roomBelow > roomAbove then return true end
	return below
end

function State.popSide(wc, h, pref)
	local top, ch = wc.Position.Y.Offset, wc.Size.Y.Offset
	local lh = layoutFrame.Size.Y.Offset
	local roomBelow = lh + PAD - 4 - (top + ch + 6)
	local roomAbove = top + PAD - 4 - 6
	if pref and roomBelow >= h then return true end
	if not pref and roomAbove >= h then return false end
	if pref and roomAbove >= h then return false end
	if not pref and roomBelow >= h then return true end
	return roomBelow > roomAbove
end

local overlayH = 58
function placeOverlay()
	local wp = widgetContainer.Position
	local pref = State.popBelow(widgetContainer, 190, layoutMode == "side" and not (State.pin and State.pin.cur == "music"))
	if not (State.pin and State.pin.cur == "music") then pref = State.popSide(widgetContainer, 190, pref) end
	if pref then
		ovSlide.set(wp.X.Offset, wp.Y.Offset + 143 + 6, 215, overlayH, true)
	else
		ovSlide.set(wp.X.Offset, wp.Y.Offset - 6 - overlayH, 215, overlayH, false)
	end
end
overlayMsg:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
	overlayMsg.Visible = overlayMsg.Text ~= ""
	if overlayMsg.Visible then overlayH = 58 placeOverlay() end
end)
placeOverlay()

local titleLabel = createLabel(widget, "not playing", 14, C.white, FONT_BOLD, {
	Position = UDim2.fromOffset(58, 14), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(104, 20), TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd,
})

do
	local TS = game:GetService("TextService")
	local W, H = 104, 22
	local busy = false
	titleLabel.ClipsDescendants = true
	local function fit()
		if busy then return end
		busy = true

		titleLabel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W, H)
		titleLabel.TextSize, titleLabel.TextWrapped, titleLabel.TextTruncate = 9, true, Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
		pcall(function()
			local txt, font = titleLabel.Text, titleLabel.Font
			for size = 14, 9, -1 do
				if TS:GetTextSize(txt, size, font, Vector2.new(10000, 100)).X <= W then
					titleLabel.TextSize, titleLabel.TextWrapped, titleLabel.TextTruncate = size, false, Enum.TextTruncate.None
					return
				end
			end
			for size = 10, 8, -1 do
				if TS:GetTextSize(txt, size, font, Vector2.new(W, 10000)).Y <= H then
					titleLabel.TextSize, titleLabel.TextWrapped, titleLabel.TextTruncate = size, true, Enum.TextTruncate.None
					return
				end
			end
			titleLabel.TextSize, titleLabel.TextWrapped, titleLabel.TextTruncate = 8, true, Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
		end)
		busy = false
	end
	titleLabel:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(fit)
	fit()
end
