do
	local mw = { kick = 0 }
	State.mw = mw
	local coverScale = create("UIScale", {}, cover)
	local spotScale = create("UIScale", {}, spot)
	local coverStroke = cover:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
	local waveStroke = wave:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
	function mw.chosen(title, author)
		titleLabel.Text = title
		trackLabel.Text = (author and author ~= "") and author or "unknown artist"
		titleLabel.TextTransparency, trackLabel.TextTransparency = 1, 1
		titleLabel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(74, 14)
		trackLabel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(99, 32)
		tween(titleLabel, 0.4, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(58, 14), TextTransparency = 0 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
		tween(trackLabel, 0.4, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(83, 32), TextTransparency = 0 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
		tween(coverStroke, 0.2, { Color = C.accent })
		tween(waveStroke, 0.2, { Color = C.hoverLine })
	end
	function mw.started()
		tween(coverStroke, 0.25, { Color = C.accent })
		tween(waveStroke, 0.25, { Color = C.accent })
	end
	function mw.stopped()
		tween(coverStroke, 0.3, { Color = C.line })
		tween(waveStroke, 0.3, { Color = C.line })
	end
	function mw.resumed()
		tween(coverStroke, 0.25, { Color = C.accent })
		tween(waveStroke, 0.25, { Color = C.accent })
	end
end

do
	local TX, TW = 99, 71
	local function fmt(t) t = math.max(0, math.floor(t or 0)) return string.format("%d:%02d", t // 60, t % 60) end
	local timeL = createLabel(widget, "0:00", 9, C.sub, FONT_BOLD, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 75), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(30, 12) })
	local timeR = createLabel(widget, "0:00", 9, C.sub, FONT_BOLD, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(173, 75), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(30, 12), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right })
	local track = createFrame(widget, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(TX, 81), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(TW, 4), BackgroundColor3 = C.track })
	corner(track, 999)
	local fill = createFrame(track, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent })
	corner(fill, 999)
	local knob = createFrame(track, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 10), BackgroundColor3 = C.white })
	corner(knob, 999)
	local knobScale = create("UIScale", { Scale = 0 }, knob)
	local hit = createButton(widget, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(TX - 4, 72), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(TW + 8, 18) })

	local dragging, frac, hovered, lastLen = false, 0, false, 0
	local function paint(f)
		frac = math.clamp(f, 0, 1)
		fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(frac, 1)
		knob.Position = UDim2.fromScale(frac, 0.5)
	end
	local function fromInput(input)
		return (input.Position.X - track.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(track.AbsoluteSize.X, 1)
	end
	local function look()
		local on = lastLen > 0
		tween(knobScale, 0.18, { Scale = on and ((hovered or dragging) and 1.25 or 1) or 0 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
		tween(track, 0.15, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(TW, (on and (hovered or dragging)) and 6 or 4) })
	end

	hit.MouseEnter:Connect(function() hovered = true look() end)
	hit.MouseLeave:Connect(function() hovered = false look() end)
	hit.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if not isPress(input) or ympSound.TimeLength <= 0 then return end
		dragging = true
		paint(fromInput(input))
		timeL.Text = fmt(frac * ympSound.TimeLength)
		look()
	end)
	UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if dragging and isMove(input) then
			paint(fromInput(input))
			timeL.Text = fmt(frac * ympSound.TimeLength)
		end
	end)
	UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if not dragging or not isPress(input) then return end
		dragging = false
		local len = ympSound.TimeLength
		if len > 0 then
			local t = math.clamp(frac * len, 0, math.max(len - 0.1, 0))
			if not ympSound.IsPlaying and not ympSound.IsPaused then
				ympSound:Play()
			end
			ympSound.TimePosition = t
		end
		look()
	end)

	task.spawn(function()
		while gui.Parent do
			task.wait(0.1)
			local len = ympSound.TimeLength
			if (len > 0) ~= (lastLen > 0) then lastLen = len look() end
			lastLen = len
			if not dragging then
				local pos = ympSound.TimePosition
				paint(len > 0 and pos / len or 0)
				timeL.Text = fmt(pos)
			end
			timeR.Text = fmt(len)
		end
	end)
end

local waveT, waveLvl, wavePeak = 0, 0, 120
waveConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
	if not holder.Visible and not (State.pin and State.pin.cur == "music") then return end
	waveT += dt
	local mw = State.mw
	mw.kick = math.max(0, mw.kick - dt * 1.6)

	local raw = 0
	if playing then pcall(function() raw = ympSound.PlaybackLoudness end) end
	wavePeak = math.max(120, wavePeak * (1 - dt * 0.15), raw)
	local want = 0
	if playing then
		want = raw > 1 and math.clamp(raw / wavePeak, 0, 1) or (0.5 + 0.3 * math.sin(waveT * 3.1) * math.sin(waveT * 1.3))
	end
	waveLvl += (want - waveLvl) * math.min(1, dt * (want > waveLvl and 18 or 5))
	local prog = 0
	pcall(function() if ympSound.TimeLength > 0 then prog = ympSound.TimePosition / ympSound.TimeLength end end)
	local maxH = 15
	for i, b in ipairs(bars) do
		local x = (i - 0.5) / WAVE_N
		local env = 0.42 + 0.58 * math.sin(x * math.pi) ^ 0.8
		local flow = math.clamp(math.noise(i * 0.29 - waveT * 2.3, waveT * 0.6, 3) + 0.5, 0, 1)
		local sheen = 0.5 + 0.5 * math.sin(i * 0.55 - waveT * 5.2)
		local target
		if playing then
			local amp = math.clamp(waveLvl * (0.35 + 0.65 * flow) * (0.75 + 0.25 * sheen) * (1 + mw.kick * 0.6), 0, 1)
			target = 2 + (maxH - 2) * env * amp ^ 0.85
		else
			target = 1.6 + 0.8 * (0.5 + 0.5 * math.sin(waveT * 1.6 + i * 0.35))
		end
		local rate = target > heights[i] and 26 or 9
		heights[i] += (target - heights[i]) * math.min(1, dt * rate)
		local h = math.max(2, heights[i])
		b.Size = UDim2.new(0.6 / WAVE_N, 0, 0, math.floor(h + 0.5))
		b.BackgroundTransparency = (x <= prog or not playing and prog <= 0) and 0 or 0.55
	end
end)

local ympResultOrder = 0

local function ympClearResults()
	for _, c in overlayScroll:GetChildren() do
		if c:IsA("TextButton") or (c:IsA("Frame") and c ~= overlayMsg) then c:Destroy() end
	end
	overlayMsg.Text = ""
	ovx.count.Text = ""
	ympResultOrder = 0
	overlayScroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
end

local overlayToken, overlayShown = 0, false
local function ympShowOverlay()
	overlayToken += 1
	if not searchOverlay.Visible then placeOverlay() end
	ovSlide.show()
	overlayShown = true
end
local function ympCloseOverlay()
	overlayToken += 1
	local t = overlayToken
	if not searchOverlay.Visible then ympClearResults() return end
	overlayShown = false
	ovSlide.close(function()
		if overlayToken == t then ympClearResults() end
	end)
end
overlayClose.Activated:Connect(ympCloseOverlay)

local pendingQueue, queue, qIndex = {}, {}, 0
local shuffleOn = false
local lastManual = 0
local ended = false
local ympPlayTrack

ympPlayTrack = function(id, title, author, dlOnly, onDone)
	if not dlOnly then
		lastManual = tick()
		State.mpos, State.mlen = 0, 0
	end
	if ympBusy then
		notify("music", "already downloading, please wait")
		if onDone then pcall(onDone, false, "busy") end
		return
	end
	if not dlOnly then
		ympCloseOverlay()
		ended, endBadge.Visible = false, false
		searchInput.Text = ""
		playing = false
		State.mw.stopped()
		State.mw.chosen(title, author)
		ympSound:Stop()
	end
	notify("downloading", title)
	ympBusy = true
	task.spawn(function()
		local ok = false
		local mpath = "YouTube Music Player/Musics/" .. id .. ".mp3"
		pcall(function()

			if isfile and isfile(mpath) then
				if State.lib and not State.lib.meta[id] then State.lib.remember(id, title, author) end
				if not dlOnly then
					ympSound.SoundId = getcustomasset(mpath)
					ympSound:Play()
				end
				ok = true
				return
			end
			local req = request or http_request or (syn and syn.request) or nil
			if not req then return end
			local locale = Players.LocalPlayer.LocaleId:lower()

			local K256 = {
				1116352408,1899447441,3049323471,3921009573,961987163,1508970993,
				2453635748,2870763221,3624381080,310598401,607225278,1426881987,
				1925078388,2162078206,2614888103,3248222580,3835390401,4022224774,
				264347078,604807628,770255983,1249150122,1555081692,1996064986,
				2554220882,2821834349,2952996808,3210313671,3336571891,3584528711,
				113926993,338241895,666307205,773529912,1294757372,1396182291,
				1695183700,1986661051,2177026350,2456956037,2730485921,2820302411,
				3259730800,3345764771,3516065817,3600352804,4094571909,275423344,
				430227734,506948616,659060556,883997877,958139571,1322822218,
				1537002063,1747873779,1955562222,2024104815,2227730452,2361852424,
				2428436474,2756734187,3204031479,3329325298,
			}
			local function yAdd(...)
				local n = 0
				for i = 1, select("#",...) do n = bit32.band(n + select(i,...), 4294967295) end
				return n
			end
			local function sha256(msg)
				local bytes = { string.byte(msg, 1, #msg) }
				local mlen = #bytes * 8
				table.insert(bytes, 128)
				while #bytes % 64 ~= 56 do table.insert(bytes, 0) end
				local hi = math.floor(mlen / 4294967296)
				local lo = mlen % 4294967296
				for i = 24,0,-8 do table.insert(bytes, bit32.band(bit32.rshift(hi,i),255)) end
				for i = 24,0,-8 do table.insert(bytes, bit32.band(bit32.rshift(lo,i),255)) end
				local H = {1779033703,3144134277,1013904242,2773480762,1359893119,2600822924,528734635,1541459225}
				for i = 1, #bytes, 64 do
					local W = {}
					for j = 0,15 do
						local n = i + j*4
						W[j+1] = bit32.bor(bit32.lshift(bytes[n],24),bit32.lshift(bytes[n+1],16),bit32.lshift(bytes[n+2],8),bytes[n+3])
					end
					for j = 17,64 do
						local s0 = bit32.bxor(bit32.rrotate(W[j-15],7),bit32.rrotate(W[j-15],18),bit32.rshift(W[j-15],3))
						local s1 = bit32.bxor(bit32.rrotate(W[j-2],17),bit32.rrotate(W[j-2],19),bit32.rshift(W[j-2],10))
						W[j] = yAdd(W[j-16],s0,W[j-7],s1)
					end
					local a,b,c,d,e,f,g,h = H[1],H[2],H[3],H[4],H[5],H[6],H[7],H[8]
					for j = 1,64 do
						local S1 = bit32.bxor(bit32.rrotate(e,6),bit32.rrotate(e,11),bit32.rrotate(e,25))
						local ch = bit32.bxor(bit32.band(e,f),bit32.band(bit32.bnot(e),g))
						local t1 = yAdd(h,S1,ch,K256[j],W[j])
						local S0 = bit32.bxor(bit32.rrotate(a,2),bit32.rrotate(a,13),bit32.rrotate(a,22))
						local maj = bit32.bxor(bit32.band(a,b),bit32.band(a,c),bit32.band(b,c))
						local t2 = yAdd(S0,maj)
						h=g g=f f=e e=yAdd(d,t1) d=c c=b b=a a=yAdd(t1,t2)
					end
					H[1]=yAdd(H[1],a) H[2]=yAdd(H[2],b) H[3]=yAdd(H[3],c) H[4]=yAdd(H[4],d)
					H[5]=yAdd(H[5],e) H[6]=yAdd(H[6],f) H[7]=yAdd(H[7],g) H[8]=yAdd(H[8],h)
				end
				local hex = ""
				for i = 1,8 do hex ..= string.format("%08x", H[i]) end
				return hex
			end

			local function findNonce(salt, difficulty)
				local target = string.rep("0", difficulty)
				for i = 0, math.huge do
					if sha256(tostring(salt) .. ":" .. tostring(i)):sub(1, difficulty) == target then
						return tostring(i)
					end
					if i % 1000 == 0 then task.wait() end
				end
			end

			local function getToken()
				local lang = locale:gsub("^(%a+)%-(%a+)$", function(a,b) return a.."-"..b:upper() end)
					.. "," .. (locale:match("^(%a+)") or "en") .. ";q=0.9,en-US;q=0.8,en;q=0.7"
				local hdrs = {
					accept = "*/*", ["accept-language"] = lang,
					referer = "https://en.loader.to/",
					["sec-ch-ua"] = "\"Chromium\";v=\"137\", \"Not/A)Brand\";v=\"24\"",
					["sec-ch-ua-mobile"] = "?1", ["sec-ch-ua-platform"] = "\"Android\"",
					["sec-fetch-dest"] = "empty", ["sec-fetch-mode"] = "cors", ["sec-fetch-site"] = "cross-site",
				}
				local ok1, r1 = pcall(function() return req({ Url = "https://p.savenow.to/api/pow/challenge", Method = "GET", Headers = hdrs }) end)
				if not ok1 or not r1 then return nil end
				local body1 = r1.Body or r1.body
				if type(body1) ~= "string" then return nil end
				local ok2, ch = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(body1) end)
				if not ok2 or type(ch) ~= "table" then return nil end
				if ch.salt == nil or ch.difficulty == nil or ch.expires_at == nil or ch.signature == nil then return nil end
				local nonce = findNonce(ch.salt, tonumber(ch.difficulty))
				if not nonce then return nil end
				local verHdrs = {
					accept = "*/*", ["accept-language"] = lang, ["content-type"] = "application/json",
					referer = "https://en.loader.to/",
					["sec-fetch-dest"] = "empty", ["sec-fetch-mode"] = "cors", ["sec-fetch-site"] = "cross-site",
				}
				local ok3, r3 = pcall(function()
					return req({ Url = "https://p.savenow.to/api/pow/verify", Method = "POST", Headers = verHdrs,
						Body = HttpService:JSONEncode({ salt = ch.salt, difficulty = ch.difficulty,
							expires_at = ch.expires_at, signature = ch.signature, nonce = nonce }) })
					end)
				if not ok3 or not r3 then return nil end
				local body3 = r3.Body or r3.body
				if type(body3) ~= "string" then return nil end
				local ok4, vr = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(body3) end)
				if not ok4 or type(vr) ~= "table" or vr.success ~= true or type(vr.token) ~= "string" then return nil end
				return vr.token
			end

			local lang = locale:gsub("^(%a+)%-(%a+)$", function(a,b) return a.."-"..b:upper() end)
				.. "," .. (locale:match("^(%a+)") or "en") .. ";q=0.9,en-US;q=0.8,en;q=0.7"
			local token = getToken()
			local dlHdrs = {
				accept = "*/*", ["accept-language"] = lang,
				referer = "https://en.loader.to/",
				["sec-ch-ua"] = "\"Chromium\";v=\"137\", \"Not/A)Brand\";v=\"24\"",
				["sec-ch-ua-mobile"] = "?1", ["sec-ch-ua-platform"] = "\"Android\"",
				["sec-fetch-dest"] = "empty", ["sec-fetch-mode"] = "cors", ["sec-fetch-site"] = "cross-site",
			}
			if token then dlHdrs.authorization = "Bearer " .. token end
			local dlUrl = "https://p.savenow.to/api/v2/download?format=mp3&url="
				.. HttpService:UrlEncode("https://youtu.be/" .. id)
				.. "&apikey=dfcb6d76f2f6a9894gjkege8a4ab232222"
			local ok5, r5 = pcall(function() return req({ Url = dlUrl, Method = "GET", Headers = dlHdrs }) end)
			if not ok5 or not r5 then return end
			local body5 = r5.Body or r5.body
			if type(body5) ~= "string" then return end
			local ok6, dlData = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(body5) end)
			if not ok6 or type(dlData) ~= "table" or type(dlData.progress_url) ~= "string" then return end

			local startT = tick()
			local finalData
			task.wait(5)
			while tick() - startT <= 200 do
				task.wait(2)
				local ok7, r7 = pcall(function() return req({ Url = dlData.progress_url, Method = "GET", Headers = dlHdrs }) end)
				if ok7 and r7 then
					local pb = r7.Body or r7.body
					if type(pb) == "string" then
						local ok8, pd = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(pb) end)
						if ok8 and type(pd) == "table" and pd.progress == 1000 and type(pd.download_url) == "string" then
							finalData = pd
							break
						end
					end
				end
			end
			if not finalData then return end

			local urls = {}
			if type(finalData.download_url) == "string" then table.insert(urls, finalData.download_url) end
			if type(finalData.alternative_download_urls) == "table" then
				for _, alt in ipairs(finalData.alternative_download_urls) do
					if #urls >= 4 then break end
					if type(alt) == "table" and type(alt.url) == "string" then table.insert(urls, alt.url) end
				end
			end
			if #urls == 0 then return end

			local fileData
			for _, url in ipairs(urls) do
				local dok, dres = pcall(function() return game:HttpGet(url) end)
				if dok and dres and #dres > 100 then fileData = dres break end
				task.wait(0.5)
			end
			if not fileData then return end

			pcall(function()
				if not isfolder("YouTube Music Player") then makefolder("YouTube Music Player") end
				if not isfolder("YouTube Music Player/Musics") then makefolder("YouTube Music Player/Musics") end
				writefile(mpath, fileData)
			end)

			if State.lib then State.lib.remember(id, title, author) end
			if dlOnly then
				task.wait(0.3)
				ok = (isfile and isfile(mpath)) and true or false
				return
			end
			task.wait(1)
			if not (isfile and isfile(mpath)) then return end
			ympSound.SoundId = getcustomasset(mpath)
			ympSound:Play()
			ok = true
		end)
		ympBusy = false
		if dlOnly then
			notify(ok and "saved" or "music error", ok and title or "couldn't download")
			if onDone then pcall(onDone, ok) end
			return
		end
		if ok then
			playing = true
			notify("now playing", title)
			if State.lib then State.lib.remember(id, title, author) end
			pcall(function()
				local cpath = "YouTube Music Player/Internal/Cache/Search_" .. id .. ".jpg"
				if isfile(cpath) then coverImg.Image = getcustomasset(cpath) end
			end)
		else
			State.mw.stopped()
			titleLabel.Text = title trackLabel.Text = "load failed"
			notify("music error", "couldn't load audio")
		end
	end)
end

local function ympAddResult(id, title, author, thumb)
	local btn = createButton(overlayScroll, {
		Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 38), BackgroundColor3 = C.field,
		BackgroundTransparency = 0, LayoutOrder = ympResultOrder, ZIndex = 42,
		Text = "", BorderSizePixel = 0,
	})
	local rowIdx = ympResultOrder
	ympResultOrder += 1
	ovx.count.Text = tostring(ympResultOrder)
	corner(btn, 6)
	local rowStroke = stroke(btn, C.accent, 1, 1)
	local rowBar = createFrame(btn, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 0), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent, ZIndex = 46 })
	corner(rowBar, 2)

	local thumbImg = create("ImageLabel", {
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(54, 30), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 4),
		BackgroundColor3 = C.bg, BackgroundTransparency = 0,
		Image = "", ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop, ZIndex = 43,
	}, btn)
	corner(thumbImg, 4)
	local thumbStroke = stroke(thumbImg, C.line, 1, 0.4)
	local thumbDim = createFrame(thumbImg, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ZIndex = 44 })
	corner(thumbDim, 4)
	local thumbPlay = newIcon(thumbImg, 14, "play")
	thumbPlay.Holder.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
	thumbPlay.Holder.ZIndex = 45
	thumbPlay.Holder.Visible = false
	thumbPlay.tint(Color3.new(1, 1, 1))
	if type(thumb) == "string" and #thumb > 4 then
		task.spawn(function()
			pcall(function()
				local dir = "YouTube Music Player/Internal/Cache"
				if not isfolder(dir) then makefolder(dir) end
				local cpath = dir .. "/Search_" .. id .. ".jpg"
				if not isfile(cpath) then writefile(cpath, game:HttpGet(thumb)) end
				thumbImg.Image = getcustomasset(cpath)
				thumbImg.ImageRectOffset = Vector2.new(0, 45)
				thumbImg.ImageRectSize   = Vector2.new(480, 270)
			end)
		end)
	end

	local titleLbl = createLabel(btn, title, 10, C.text, FONT_BOLD, {
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(62, 5), Size = UDim2.new(1, -94, 0, 14),
		TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, ZIndex = 43,
	})
	local authorLbl = createLabel(btn, author, 9, C.sub, FONT, {
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(62, 20), Size = UDim2.new(1, -94, 0, 12),
		TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd, ZIndex = 43,
	})

	local idxLbl = createLabel(btn, string.format("%02d", rowIdx + 1), 9, C.dim, FONT_BOLD, {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -9, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 14),
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, ZIndex = 43, NoTitle = true,
	})
	idxLbl:SetAttribute("NoTranslate", true)
	local playPill = createFrame(btn, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -19, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(22, 22), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent, ZIndex = 46 })
	corner(playPill, 999)
	local ppScale = create("UIScale", { Scale = 0 }, playPill)
	local ppIcon = newIcon(playPill, 11, "play")
	ppIcon.Holder.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
	ppIcon.Holder.ZIndex = 47
	ppIcon.tint(C.white)
	btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		tween(btn, 0.08, { BackgroundColor3 = C.line })
		tween(rowBar, 0.18, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 22) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
		tween(titleLbl, 0.1, { TextColor3 = C.accent })
		tween(thumbDim, 0.12, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.45 })
		tween(rowStroke, 0.12, { Transparency = 0.5 })
		tween(thumbStroke, 0.12, { Color = C.accent, Transparency = 0.2 })
		tween(ppScale, 0.22, { Scale = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
		tween(idxLbl, 0.1, { TextTransparency = 1 })
		thumbPlay.Holder.Visible = true
	end)
	btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		tween(btn, 0.08, { BackgroundColor3 = C.field })
		tween(rowBar, 0.15, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 0) })
		tween(titleLbl, 0.1, { TextColor3 = C.text })
		tween(thumbDim, 0.12, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
		tween(rowStroke, 0.15, { Transparency = 1 })
		tween(thumbStroke, 0.15, { Color = C.line, Transparency = 0.4 })
		tween(ppScale, 0.15, { Scale = 0 })
		tween(idxLbl, 0.15, { TextTransparency = 0.15 })
		thumbPlay.Holder.Visible = false
	end)

	btn.BackgroundTransparency, thumbImg.BackgroundTransparency, thumbImg.ImageTransparency = 1, 1, 1
	titleLbl.TextTransparency, authorLbl.TextTransparency, idxLbl.TextTransparency, thumbStroke.Transparency = 1, 1, 1, 1
	task.delay(math.min(rowIdx, 8) * 0.04, function()
		if not btn.Parent then return end
		tween(btn, 0.25, { BackgroundTransparency = 0 })
		tween(thumbImg, 0.25, { BackgroundTransparency = 0, ImageTransparency = 0 })
		tween(titleLbl, 0.25, { TextTransparency = 0 })
		tween(authorLbl, 0.25, { TextTransparency = 0 })
		tween(idxLbl, 0.25, { TextTransparency = 0.15 })
		tween(thumbStroke, 0.25, { Transparency = 0.4 })
	end)

	local myIndex = #pendingQueue + 1
	pendingQueue[myIndex] = { id = id, title = title, author = author }
	btn.Activated:Connect(function()
		if ympBusy then
			notify("music", "already downloading, please wait")
			return
		end
		queue, qIndex = pendingQueue, myIndex
		ympPlayTrack(id, title, author)
	end)

	overlayScroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, ympResultOrder * 42 + 10)
	overlayH = math.clamp(ympResultOrder * 42 + 32, 58, 190)
	placeOverlay()
end

local function ympSearch(query)
	query = query:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
	if query == "" then ympCloseOverlay() return end
	State.lastQuery = query
	ympClearResults()
	overlayMsg.Text = "searching."
	ympShowOverlay()
	task.spawn(function()
		local n, k = 1, 0
		ovx.loadBar.Visible = true
		while overlayMsg.Text:sub(1, 9) == "searching" do
			overlayMsg.Text = "searching" .. string.rep(".", n)
			n = n % 3 + 1
			if k % 2 == 0 then
				ovx.loadBar.Position = UDim2.new(-0.3, 0, 0, 22)
				tween(ovx.loadBar, 0.7, { Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut)
			end
			k += 1
			task.wait(0.35)
		end
		ovx.loadBar.Visible = false
	end)
	task.spawn(function()
		local ok, res = pcall(function()
			return game:HttpGet(YMP_API .. "/search/youtube/free/" .. HttpService:UrlEncode(query))
		end)
		if not ok or not res then
			overlayMsg.Text = "search failed – check http settings"
			return
		end
		local low = res:lower()
		if low:find("too many requests") then
			overlayMsg.Text = "rate limited – try again in a moment"
			return
		end
		local jok, data = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(res) end)
		if not jok or not data or not data.results then
			overlayMsg.Text = low:find("no results") and "no results found" or "no results found"
			return
		end
		overlayMsg.Text = ""
		pendingQueue = {}
		for _, item in ipairs(data.results) do
			ympAddResult(item.id or "", item.name or "Unknown", item.author or "", item.thumbnail)
		end
		if ympResultOrder == 0 then overlayMsg.Text = "no results found" end
	end)
end

local history = {}
local pauseBtn
local function showEnded()
	ended = true
	playing = false
	State.mw.stopped()
	endBadge.Visible = true
	if pauseBtn then pauseBtn.Text = "▶" end
	notify("music", "finished playing")
end
local function ympGo(i)
	qIndex = i
	local t = queue[i]
	ympPlayTrack(t.id, t.title, t.author)
end

		"Michael Jackson Billie Jean",

function RND.queueNext(auto)
	local n = #queue
	if n == 0 then
		if auto then showEnded() else notify("music", "search for a song first") end
		return
	end
	if ympBusy then
		if not auto then notify("music", "still loading, please wait") end
		return
	end
	local nextI
	if shuffleOn then
		if n == 1 then nextI = 1 else
			repeat nextI = math.random(n) until nextI ~= qIndex
		end
		table.insert(history, qIndex)
		if #history > 30 then table.remove(history, 1) end
	else
		nextI = qIndex + 1
		if nextI > n then
			if auto then showEnded() return end
			nextI = 1
		end
	end
	ympGo(nextI)
end

function RND.go(auto)
	if ympBusy or RND.busy then
		if not auto then notify("music", "still loading, please wait") end
		return
	end
	RND.busy = true
	if not auto then notify("music", "picking a random song...") end
	task.spawn(function()
		local pick
		for _ = 1, 3 do
			local q = RND.pool[math.random(#RND.pool)]
			local ok, res = pcall(function() return game:HttpGet(YMP_API .. "/search/youtube/free/" .. HttpService:UrlEncode(q)) end)
			if ok and type(res) == "string" and not res:lower():find("too many requests") then
				local jok, data = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(res) end)
				if jok and type(data) == "table" and type(data.results) == "table" and #data.results > 0 then
					local fresh, any = {}, {}
					for i, it in ipairs(data.results) do
						if i > 8 then break end
						if type(it.id) == "string" and it.id ~= "" then
							table.insert(any, it)
							if not RND.recent[it.id] then table.insert(fresh, it) end
						end
					end
					local from = #fresh > 0 and fresh or any
					if #from > 0 then pick = from[math.random(#from)] break end
				end
			end
		end
		RND.busy = false
		if not pick then
			notify("music", "couldn't find a random song")
			RND.queueNext(auto)
			return
		end

		if type(pick.thumbnail) == "string" and #pick.thumbnail > 4 and writefile and isfile then
			pcall(function()
				local dir = "YouTube Music Player/Internal/Cache"
				if not isfolder(dir) then makefolder(dir) end
				local cpath = dir .. "/Search_" .. pick.id .. ".jpg"
				if not isfile(cpath) then writefile(cpath, game:HttpGet(pick.thumbnail)) end
			end)
		end
		local t = { id = pick.id, title = pick.name or "Unknown", author = pick.author or "" }
		RND.recent[t.id] = true
		RND.recentN += 1
		if RND.recentN > 40 then RND.recent, RND.recentN = { [t.id] = true }, 1 end
		table.insert(RND.hist, t)
		if #RND.hist > 30 then table.remove(RND.hist, 1) end
		queue, qIndex = { t }, 1
		ympPlayTrack(t.id, t.title, t.author)
	end)
end
local function ympAdvance(auto) RND.go(auto) end

local function ympPrevious()
	if ympBusy then notify("music", "still loading, please wait") return end
	if (ympSound.IsPlaying or ympSound.IsPaused) and ympSound.TimePosition > 3 then
		ympSound.TimePosition = 0
		return
	end
	local top = RND.hist[#RND.hist]
	if top and #queue == 1 and queue[1].id == top.id and #RND.hist >= 2 then
		table.remove(RND.hist)
		local pt = RND.hist[#RND.hist]
		queue, qIndex = { { id = pt.id, title = pt.title, author = pt.author } }, 1
		ympPlayTrack(pt.id, pt.title, pt.author)
		return
	end
	if #queue == 0 then notify("music", "search for a song first") return end
	local prev
	if shuffleOn and #history > 0 then
		prev = table.remove(history)
	else
		prev = qIndex - 1
		if prev < 1 then prev = #queue end
	end
	ympGo(prev)
end

local function transport(x, text, cb)
	local b = createButton(widget, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, 81), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 16), Text = text, TextSize = 9, Font = FONT_BOLD, TextColor3 = C.sub })
	b.MouseEnter:Connect(function() tween(b, 0.1, { TextColor3 = C.text }) end)
	b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tween(b, 0.1, { TextColor3 = C.sub }) end)
	b.Activated:Connect(cb)
	return b
end
local prevBtn = transport(48, "|◀", ympPrevious)
pauseBtn = transport(66, "||", function()
	if ended then
		ended, endBadge.Visible = false, false
		ympSound.TimePosition = 0
		ympSound:Play()
	elseif ympSound.IsPlaying then
		ympSound:Pause()
	elseif ympSound.IsPaused then
		ympSound:Resume()
	else
		notify("music", "nothing is playing")
	end
end)
State.replayBtn.Activated:Connect(function()
	ended, endBadge.Visible = false, false
	ympSound.TimePosition = 0
	ympSound:Play()
end)
local skipBtn = transport(84, "▶|", function() ympAdvance(false) end)

local function toggleButton(x, builder, onToggle)
	local btn = createButton(widget, { Sound = true, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, 91), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(22, 22), BackgroundColor3 = C.field, BackgroundTransparency = 0 })
	corner(btn, 6)
	local st = stroke(btn, C.line, 1)
	local glow = createFrame(btn, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent, BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
	corner(glow, 6)
	local icon = newIcon(btn, 15, builder)
	icon.Holder.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, -1)
	icon.tint(C.sub)
	local iconScale = create("UIScale", {}, icon.Holder)
	local dot = createFrame(btn, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -2), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 3), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent, BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
	corner(dot, 999)
	local active = false
	local api = {}
	function api.Set(v)
		active = v
		tween(glow, 0.16, { BackgroundTransparency = v and 0.82 or 1 })
		tween(st, 0.16, { Color = v and C.accent or C.line })
		tween(dot, 0.16, { BackgroundTransparency = v and 0 or 1, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(v and 9 or 3, 3) })
		icon.tint(v and C.accent or C.sub, 0.16)
		iconScale.Scale = 0.75
		tween(iconScale, 0.28, { Scale = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
	end
	btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		if not active then icon.tint(C.text, 0.1) tween(st, 0.1, { Color = C.hoverLine }) end
	end)
	btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		if not active then icon.tint(C.sub, 0.1) tween(st, 0.1, { Color = C.line }) end
	end)
	btn.Activated:Connect(function() api.Set(onToggle()) end)
	return api
end

toggleButton(12, "shuffle", function()
	shuffleOn = not shuffleOn
	notify("music", "shuffle " .. (shuffleOn and "on" or "off"))
	return shuffleOn
end)
toggleButton(181, "repeat", function()
	ympSound.Looped = not ympSound.Looped
	notify("music", "loop " .. (ympSound.Looped and "on" or "off"))
	return ympSound.Looped
end)

do
	local V = { open = false, idle = 0, hover = false, hoverBtn = false, drag = false, tx = 30, tw = 88, ty = 13 }
	V.vol = math.clamp(ympSound.Volume, 0, 1)
	V.last = V.vol > 0.001 and V.vol or 0.5
	V.btn = createButton(widget, { Sound = true, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(125, 91), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(22, 22), BackgroundColor3 = C.field, BackgroundTransparency = 0 })
	corner(V.btn, 6)
	V.btnStroke = stroke(V.btn, C.line, 1)
	V.btnIcon = newIcon(V.btn, 16, "volume-2")
	V.btnIcon.Holder.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
	V.btnIcon.tint(C.sub)

	V.pop = create("CanvasGroup", {
		Name = "VolumePopup", Position = UDim2.fromOffset(51, 57), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(152, 26), BackgroundColor3 = C.panel,
		BorderSizePixel = 0, GroupTransparency = 1, Visible = false, ZIndex = 20, Active = true,
	}, widget)
	corner(V.pop, 13)
	V.popStroke = stroke(V.pop, C.accent, 1, 1)
	V.mute = createButton(V.pop, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(5, 4), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 18) })
	V.mIcon = newIcon(V.mute, 15, "volume-2")
	V.mIcon.Holder.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
	V.mIcon.tint(C.sub)
	V.track = createFrame(V.pop, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(V.tx, V.ty), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(V.tw, 4), BackgroundColor3 = C.track })
	corner(V.track, 999)
	V.fill = createFrame(V.track, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(V.vol, 1), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent })
	corner(V.fill, 999)
	create("UIGradient", { Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.55), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0) }) }, V.fill)
	for i = 1, 3 do
		local tk = createFrame(V.track, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(i / 4, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(2, 2), BackgroundColor3 = C.sub, BackgroundTransparency = 0.6, ZIndex = 2 })
		corner(tk, 999)
	end
	V.glow = createFrame(V.track, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(V.vol, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent, BackgroundTransparency = 0.85, ZIndex = 3 })
	corner(V.glow, 999)
	V.knob = createFrame(V.track, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(V.vol, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 10), BackgroundColor3 = C.white, ZIndex = 4 })
	corner(V.knob, 999)
	stroke(V.knob, C.accent, 1.5)
	V.knobScale = create("UIScale", { Scale = 0.9 }, V.knob)
	V.pct = createLabel(V.pop, "", 9, C.sub, FONT_BOLD, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(122, 7), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(26, 12), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right })
	V.pct:SetAttribute("NoTranslate", true)
	V.hit = createButton(V.pop, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(V.tx - 4, 4), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(V.tw + 8, 18) })

	function V.arm()
		V.idle += 1
		local t = V.idle
		task.delay(2.2, function()
			if t ~= V.idle or not V.open then return end
			if V.hover or V.drag then V.arm() else V.setOpen(false) end
		end)
	end
	function V.tint()
		local muted = V.vol <= 0.001
		V.btnIcon.tint(muted and C.red or (V.open and C.accent or C.sub), 0.12)
		V.mIcon.tint(muted and C.red or C.sub, 0.12)
	end
	function V.setOpen(on)
		if V.open == on then return end
		V.open = on
		if on then
			V.pop.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(51, 79)
			V.pop.Visible = true
			tween(V.pop, 0.2, { GroupTransparency = 0 })
			tween(V.pop, 0.4, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(51, 57) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
			tween(V.popStroke, 0.18, { Transparency = 0.55 })
			tween(V.btnStroke, 0.15, { Color = C.accent })
			V.arm()
		else
			V.drag, V.hover = false, false
			V.idle += 1
			tween(V.pop, 0.22, { GroupTransparency = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
			tween(V.pop, 0.26, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(51, 79) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Cubic, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
			tween(V.popStroke, 0.18, { Transparency = 1 })
			tween(V.btnStroke, 0.15, { Color = C.line })
			task.delay(0.28, function() if not V.open then V.pop.Visible = false end end)
		end
		V.tint()
	end
	function V.paint(v)
		v = math.clamp(v, 0, 1)
		V.vol = v
		ympSound.Volume = v
		V.fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(v, 1)
		V.knob.Position = UDim2.fromScale(v, 0.5)
		V.glow.Position = UDim2.fromScale(v, 0.5)
		V.pct.Text = math.floor(v * 100 + 0.5) .. "%"
		local muted = v <= 0.001
		local g = muted and "volume-x" or (v > 0.5 and "volume-2" or "volume-1")
		for _, ic in ipairs({ V.btnIcon, V.mIcon }) do
			if ic.setIcon and ic.name ~= g then ic.setIcon(g) end
		end
		if Icons.volumeParts and not V.btnIcon.setIcon then
			local ps = Icons.volumeParts
			for _, o in ipairs(ps.w1) do o.BackgroundTransparency = muted and 1 or 0 end
			for _, o in ipairs(ps.w2) do o.BackgroundTransparency = v > 0.5 and 0 or 1 end
			ps.slash.BackgroundTransparency = muted and 0 or 1
		end
		V.tint()
		if V.open then V.arm() end
	end
	function V.look()
		local on = V.hover or V.drag
		tween(V.knobScale, 0.15, { Scale = on and 1.2 or 0.9 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
		tween(V.glow, 0.2, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(on and 26 or 16, on and 26 or 16), BackgroundTransparency = on and 0.72 or 0.85 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
		V.pct.TextColor3 = on and C.accent or C.sub
		tween(V.track, 0.15, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(V.tw, on and 6 or 4) })
	end
	function V.fromInput(input) return (input.Position.X - V.track.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(V.track.AbsoluteSize.X, 1) end
	function V.toggleMute() if V.vol > 0.001 then V.last = V.vol V.paint(0) else V.paint(V.last) end end

	V.btn.Activated:Connect(function() V.setOpen(not V.open) end)
	V.btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() V.hoverBtn = true if not V.open then tween(V.btnStroke, 0.1, { Color = C.hoverLine }) end end)
	V.btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function() V.hoverBtn = false if not V.open then tween(V.btnStroke, 0.1, { Color = C.line }) end end)
	V.mute.Activated:Connect(V.toggleMute)
	V.pop.MouseEnter:Connect(function() V.hover = true end)
	V.pop.MouseLeave:Connect(function() V.hover = false V.look() end)
	V.hit.MouseEnter:Connect(function() V.hover = true V.look() end)
	V.hit.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if not isPress(input) then return end
		V.drag = true
		V.paint(V.fromInput(input))
		V.look()
	end)
	UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if V.drag and isMove(input) then
			V.paint(V.fromInput(input))
		elseif (V.hover or V.hoverBtn) and not V.drag and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseWheel then
			if not V.open then V.setOpen(true) end
			V.paint(V.vol + input.Position.Z * 0.05)
		end
	end)
	UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if not V.drag or not isPress(input) then return end
		V.drag = false
		if V.vol > 0.001 then V.last = V.vol end
		V.look()
	end)
	UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if V.open and isPress(input) and not isInside(V.pop, input.Position) and not isInside(V.btn, input.Position) then V.setOpen(false) end
	end)
	V.paint(V.vol)
end

do
	local LIB = { open = false, tok = 0, dir = 1, list = {}, meta = {} }
	State.lib = LIB
	local ROOT, MUS = "YouTube Music Player", "YouTube Music Player/Musics"
	local INTERNAL = ROOT .. "/Internal"
	local FILE = INTERNAL .. "/library.json"
	pcall(function()
		if isfile and isfile(FILE) then
			local d = HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(FILE))
			if type(d) == "table" then LIB.meta = d end
		end
	end)
	function LIB.remember(id, title, author)
		LIB.cur = id
		LIB.meta[id] = { title = title, author = author or "" }
		pcall(function()
			if writefile and isfolder then
				if not isfolder(ROOT) then makefolder(ROOT) end
				if not isfolder(INTERNAL) then makefolder(INTERNAL) end
				writefile(FILE, HttpService:JSONEncode(LIB.meta))
			end
		end)
	end

	local panel = create("CanvasGroup", {
		Name = "LibraryPanel", Size = UDim2.fromOffset(215, 150), BackgroundColor3 = C.panel, BorderSizePixel = 0,
		GroupTransparency = 1, Visible = false, ZIndex = 40,
	}, layoutFrame)
	corner(panel, 8)
	local libSlide = State.slide(panel, layoutFrame, widgetContainer)
	State.pinPop.music[#State.pinPop.music + 1] = libSlide.slot
	local pStroke = stroke(panel, C.line, 1, 1)
	local pScale = create("UIScale", { Scale = 1 }, panel)
	State.headGlow(panel)
	local dlIcon = newIcon(panel, 12, "download")
	dlIcon.Holder.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 11)
	dlIcon.tint(C.sub)
	createLabel(panel, "downloads", 10, C.sub, FONT_BOLD, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -108, 0, 22) })
	local count = createLabel(panel, "", 10, C.dim, FONT_BOLD, { NoTitle = true })
	State.pill(count)
	count:SetAttribute("NoTranslate", true)
	createFrame(panel, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 22), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1), BackgroundColor3 = C.divider })
	local scroll = create("ScrollingFrame", {
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 23), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -23), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ScrollBarThickness = 2,
		ScrollBarImageColor3 = C.accent, CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y,
		ElasticBehavior = Enum.ElasticBehavior.Never,
	}, panel)
	create("UIPadding", { PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 4), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 4), PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 4), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 6) }, scroll)
	create("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 2), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, scroll)
	local empty = createLabel(scroll, "no downloaded songs yet", 11, C.sub, FONT, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, LayoutOrder = -1, Visible = false })
	State.popFx(libSlide, panel, pStroke, function() return scroll:GetChildren() end)
	LIB.eq = {}

	function LIB.build()
		for _, ch in ipairs(scroll:GetChildren()) do if ch:IsA("TextButton") then ch:Destroy() end end
		LIB.eq = {}
		local entries, canList = {}, false
		pcall(function()
			if listfiles and isfolder and isfolder(MUS) then
				canList = true
				for _, f in ipairs(listfiles(MUS)) do
					local id = tostring(f):match("([^/\\]+)%.mp3$")
					if id then
						local m = LIB.meta[id]
						table.insert(entries, { id = id, title = m and m.title or id, author = m and m.author or "" })
					end
				end
			end
		end)
		table.sort(entries, function(a, b) return string.lower(a.title) < string.lower(b.title) end)
		LIB.list = entries
		count.Text = #entries .. (#entries == 1 and " song" or " songs")
		empty.Visible = #entries == 0
		if #entries == 0 then empty.Text = canList and "No Downloaded Songs Yet" or "Needs Executor File Access" end
		LIB.h = math.clamp(31 + math.max(#entries, 1) * 38, 84, 178)
		for i, e in ipairs(entries) do
			local btn = createButton(scroll, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 36), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent, BackgroundTransparency = 1, LayoutOrder = i })
			corner(btn, 5)
			local thumb = create("ImageLabel", { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 4), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 28), BackgroundColor3 = C.field, BorderSizePixel = 0, ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop }, btn)
			corner(thumb, 4)
			pcall(function()
				local cpath = INTERNAL .. "/Cache/Search_" .. e.id .. ".jpg"
				if isfile and isfile(cpath) then thumb.Image = getcustomasset(cpath) end
			end)
			local isCur = e.id == LIB.cur
			local restA = isCur and 0.84 or 1
			btn.BackgroundTransparency = restA
			create("UIGradient", { Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0, 0.7) }, btn)
			local bar = createFrame(btn, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, isCur and 18 or 0), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent })
			corner(bar, 2)
			local thumbStroke = stroke(thumb, isCur and C.accent or C.line, 1, isCur and 0.2 or 0.5)
			local t1 = createLabel(btn, e.title, 11, isCur and C.accent or C.text, FONT_BOLD, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(38, 3), Size = UDim2.new(1, -72, 0, 16), NoTitle = true })
			local t2 = createLabel(btn, e.author ~= "" and e.author or "Saved Track", 9, C.sub, FONT, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(38, 19), Size = UDim2.new(1, -72, 0, 12), NoTitle = true })
			t1:SetAttribute("NoTranslate", true)
			t2:SetAttribute("NoTranslate", true)
			local ppScale
			if isCur then
				local eqHold = createFrame(btn, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 14) })
				for b = 1, 3 do
					local eb = createFrame(eqHold, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1), Position = UDim2.fromOffset((b - 1) * 6, 14), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 4), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent })
					corner(eb, 1)
					table.insert(LIB.eq, eb)
				end
			else
				local pill = createFrame(btn, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -18, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 20), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent })
				corner(pill, 999)
				ppScale = create("UIScale", { Scale = 0 }, pill)
				local pi = newIcon(pill, 10, "play")
				pi.Holder.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
				pi.Holder.ZIndex = 3
				pi.tint(C.white)
			end
			btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
				tween(btn, 0.08, { BackgroundTransparency = isCur and 0.7 or 0.6 })
				tween(bar, 0.18, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 18) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
				tween(thumbStroke, 0.12, { Color = C.accent, Transparency = 0.2 })
				if not isCur then tween(t1, 0.1, { TextColor3 = C.accent }) end
				if ppScale then tween(ppScale, 0.22, { Scale = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back) end
			end)
			btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
				tween(btn, 0.1, { BackgroundTransparency = restA })
				tween(bar, 0.15, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, isCur and 18 or 0) })
				tween(thumbStroke, 0.15, { Color = isCur and C.accent or C.line, Transparency = isCur and 0.2 or 0.5 })
				if not isCur then tween(t1, 0.1, { TextColor3 = C.text }) end
				if ppScale then tween(ppScale, 0.15, { Scale = 0 }) end
			end)
			btn.Activated:Connect(function() LIB.pick(i) end)
		end
	end

	task.spawn(function()
		while gui.Parent do
			task.wait(0.14)
			if LIB.open and LIB.eq then
				for _, eb in ipairs(LIB.eq) do
					if eb.Parent then tween(eb, 0.14, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(4, playing and math.random(3, 14) or 3) }) end
				end
			end
		end
	end)

	function LIB.pick(i)
		local e = LIB.list[i]
		if not e then return end
		queue = {}
		for _, x in ipairs(LIB.list) do table.insert(queue, { id = x.id, title = x.title, author = x.author }) end
		qIndex = i
		LIB.cur = e.id
		LIB.dismiss()
		ympPlayTrack(e.id, e.title, e.author)
	end

	State.music = {}
	do
		local M = State.music
		function M.downloads()
			local out = {}
			pcall(function()
				if listfiles and isfolder and isfolder(MUS) then
					for _, p in ipairs(listfiles(MUS)) do
						local id = tostring(p):match("([^/\\]+)%.mp3$")
						if id then
							local m = LIB.meta[id]
							table.insert(out, { id = id, title = tostring(m and m.title or id), author = tostring(m and m.author or "") })
						end
					end
				end
			end)
			table.sort(out, function(a, b) return a.title:lower() < b.title:lower() end)
			return out
		end
		function M.has(id)
			local r = false
			pcall(function() r = (isfile and isfile(MUS .. "/" .. id .. ".mp3")) and true or false end)
			return r
		end
		function M.search(query, cb)
			task.spawn(function()
				local ok, res = pcall(function() return game:HttpGet(YMP_API .. "/search/youtube/free/" .. HttpService:UrlEncode(query)) end)
				if not ok or not res then pcall(cb, nil, "search failed - check http settings") return end
				if res:lower():find("too many requests") then pcall(cb, nil, "rate limited - try again in a moment") return end
				local jok, data = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(res) end)
				local out = {}
				if jok and type(data) == "table" and type(data.results) == "table" then
					for _, it in ipairs(data.results) do
						if type(it) == "table" and type(it.id) == "string" and it.id ~= "" then
							table.insert(out, { id = it.id, title = tostring(it.name or "Unknown"), author = tostring(it.author or ""), thumb = it.thumbnail })
						end
					end
				end
				pcall(cb, out)
			end)
		end
		function M.cover(id, url, cb)
			task.spawn(function()
				local img = M.thumb(id)
				if not img and type(url) == "string" and #url > 4 then
					pcall(function()
						local dir = INTERNAL .. "/Cache"
						if not isfolder(dir) then makefolder(dir) end
						local cpath = dir .. "/Search_" .. id .. ".jpg"
						if not isfile(cpath) then writefile(cpath, game:HttpGet(url)) end
						img = getcustomasset(cpath)
					end)
				end
				if img then pcall(cb, img) end
			end)
		end
		function M.download(id, title, author, cb)
			if M.has(id) then if cb then pcall(cb, true) end return true end
			if ympBusy then return false end
			ympPlayTrack(id, title, author, true, cb)
			return true
		end
		function M.thumb(id)
			local img
			pcall(function()
				local p = INTERNAL .. "/Cache/Search_" .. id .. ".jpg"
				if isfile and isfile(p) then img = getcustomasset(p) end
			end)
			return img
		end
		function M.play(list, i)
			local e = list[i]
			if not e then return end
			queue = {}
			for _, x in ipairs(list) do table.insert(queue, { id = x.id, title = x.title, author = x.author }) end
			qIndex = i
			M.queueRef = queue
			LIB.cur = e.id
			ympPlayTrack(e.id, e.title, e.author)
		end
		function M.toggle()
			if ended then
				ended, endBadge.Visible = false, false
				ympSound.TimePosition = 0
				ympSound:Play()
			elseif ympSound.IsPlaying then ympSound:Pause()
			elseif ympSound.IsPaused then ympSound:Resume()
			elseif #queue > 0 and not ympBusy then ympGo(qIndex > 0 and qIndex or 1) end
		end
		function M.next() RND.queueNext(false) end
		function M.prev()
			if ympBusy or #queue == 0 then return end
			if (ympSound.IsPlaying or ympSound.IsPaused) and ympSound.TimePosition > 3 then ympSound.TimePosition = 0 return end
			local p = qIndex - 1
			if p < 1 then p = #queue end
			ympGo(p)
		end
		function M.seek(frac)
			if ympSound.TimeLength > 0 then ympSound.TimePosition = math.clamp(frac, 0, 1) * ympSound.TimeLength end
		end
		function M.now()
			local t = queue[qIndex]
			return { id = t and t.id, title = t and t.title or "", author = t and t.author or "", playing = ympSound.IsPlaying, paused = ympSound.IsPaused,
				pos = ympSound.TimePosition, len = ympSound.TimeLength, busy = ympBusy }
		end
	end

	function LIB.place()
		local wp, h = widgetContainer.Position, LIB.h or 84
		local pref = State.popBelow(widgetContainer, 178, layoutMode == "side" and not (State.pin and State.pin.cur == "music"))
		if not (State.pin and State.pin.cur == "music") then pref = State.popSide(widgetContainer, 178, pref) end
		if pref then
			libSlide.set(wp.X.Offset, wp.Y.Offset + 143 + 6, 215, h, true)
		else
			libSlide.set(wp.X.Offset, wp.Y.Offset - 6 - h, 215, h, false)
		end
	end

	function LIB.show()
		if LIB.open then return end
		LIB.open = true
		LIB.tok += 1
		ympCloseOverlay()
		LIB.build()
		LIB.place()
		pStroke.Transparency = 0.55
		libSlide.show()
	end

	function LIB.close()
		if not LIB.open then return end
		LIB.open = false
		LIB.tok += 1
		libSlide.close(function() LIB.place() end)
	end

	function LIB.dismiss()
		if not LIB.open then return end
		LIB.close()
		if LIB.btn then LIB.btn.Set(false) end
	end

	LIB.btn = toggleButton(153, Icons.library, function()
		if LIB.open then LIB.close() else LIB.show() end
		return LIB.open
	end)

	function LIB.outside(input)
		if not LIB.open or not isPress(input) then return end
		if isInside(panel, input.Position) or isInside(musicPanel, input.Position) then return end
		LIB.dismiss()
	end
	layoutFrame.InputBegan:Connect(LIB.outside)
	UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input) if State.pin and State.pin.cur == "music" then LIB.outside(input) end end)

	local showSearch = ympShowOverlay
	ympShowOverlay = function(...)
		LIB.dismiss()
		return showSearch(...)
	end

	local basePlace = placeOverlay
	placeOverlay = function(...)
		basePlace(...)
		LIB.place()
	end
	LIB.place()
end

ympSound.Played:Connect(function() playing = true ended, endBadge.Visible = false, false pauseBtn.Text = "||" State.mw.started() end)
ympSound.Resumed:Connect(function() playing = true pauseBtn.Text = "||" State.mw.resumed() end)
ympSound.Paused:Connect(function()
	playing = false
	State.mw.stopped()
	if ympSound.IsPaused then pauseBtn.Text = "▶" end
end)
State.mpos, State.mlen = 0, 0
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
	if ympSound.IsPlaying and ympSound.TimeLength > 0 then
		State.mpos, State.mlen = ympSound.TimePosition, ympSound.TimeLength
	end
end)
ympSound.Ended:Connect(function()
	if ympBusy or tick() - lastManual < 1.5 then return end

	if ympSound.IsPlaying or State.mlen <= 0 or State.mpos < State.mlen - 2 then return end
	State.mpos, State.mlen = 0, 0
	playing = false
	if State.music and State.music.queueRef == queue and #queue > 1 then
		RND.queueNext(true)
	elseif shuffleOn then
		ympAdvance(true)
	else
		showEnded()
	end
end)

State.ympSearch = ympSearch

searchInput.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed)
	if enterPressed then ympSearch(searchInput.Text) end
end)

State.ovOutside = function(input)
	if not isPress(input) or not searchOverlay.Visible or not overlayShown then return end
	local pos = input.Position
	local function inside(frame)
		local a, s = frame.AbsolutePosition, frame.AbsoluteSize
		return pos.X >= a.X and pos.X <= a.X + s.X and pos.Y >= a.Y and pos.Y <= a.Y + s.Y
	end
	if not inside(searchOverlay) and not inside(searchBg) then
		ympCloseOverlay()
	end
end
layoutFrame.InputBegan:Connect(State.ovOutside)
UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input) if State.pin and State.pin.cur == "music" then State.ovOutside(input) end end)

function loadThumbs()
	local ok, img = pcall(function()
		return Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(player.UserId, Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot, Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size100x100)
	end)
	if ok then avatarImg.Image = img coverImg.Image = img end
	return ok
end
task.spawn(loadThumbs)

function State.musicStop()
	if State.lib then State.lib.dismiss() end
	lastManual = tick()
	State.musicOff = true
	ympSound:Stop()
	ympCloseOverlay()
	playing, ended, endBadge.Visible = false, false, false
	State.mw.stopped()
	pauseBtn.Text = "▶"
end
ympSound.Played:Connect(function()
	if State.musicOff then
		lastManual = tick()
		ympSound:Stop()
		playing = false
		State.mw.stopped()
		pauseBtn.Text = "▶"
	end
end)
State.dismissBtn(musicPanel, "music").Position = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 3)
State.pinBtn(musicPanel, "music").Position = UDim2.new(1, -30, 0, 3)
titleLabel:SetAttribute("NoTranslate", true)
trackLabel:SetAttribute("NoTranslate", true)
titleLabel:SetAttribute("NoNumbers", true)
trackLabel:SetAttribute("NoNumbers", true)
end

local sessionPanel, sessionContainer = createPanel(layoutFrame, "SessionWidget", 245, 449, 215, 130, 10)
State.cardHeader(sessionPanel)
do
	local sIcon = newIcon(sessionPanel, 12, Icons.session)
	sIcon.Holder.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 11)
	sIcon.tint(C.accent)
end
local sessionRow = createFrame(sessionPanel, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 11), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 16), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
create("UIListLayout", { FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal, Padding = UDim.new(0, 7), VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center, SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, sessionRow)
State.sessionTitle = createLabel(sessionRow, "session", 10, C.sub, FONT_BOLD, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 16), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, TextWrapped = false, LayoutOrder = 1 })

local liveDot = createFrame(sessionRow, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 6), BackgroundColor3 = C.green, LayoutOrder = 2 })
corner(liveDot, 999)

State.sig = { good = C.green, ok = Color3.fromRGB(255, 170, 50), bad = Color3.fromRGB(255, 62, 62) }
local GRAPH_N, GRAPH_H = 31, 11
local function statGraph(y)
	local g = createFrame(sessionPanel, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, y), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(187, GRAPH_H) })
	create("UIListLayout", { FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal, Padding = UDim.new(0, 2), VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Bottom, SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, g)
	local bars, hist = {}, {}
	for i = 1, GRAPH_N do
		local b = createFrame(g, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 2), BackgroundColor3 = C.track, LayoutOrder = i })
		corner(b, 1)
		b.BackgroundTransparency = State.barFade(i)
		bars[i], hist[i] = b, false
	end
	return { bars = bars, hist = hist }
end
function State.barFade(i)
	local t = (GRAPH_N - i) / math.max(GRAPH_N - 1, 1)
	return 0.88 * t ^ 1.25
end
local function pushGraph(graph, ratio, color)
	table.remove(graph.hist, 1)
	table.insert(graph.hist, { r = ratio, c = color })
	for i, b in ipairs(graph.bars) do
		local h = graph.hist[i]
		if h then
			b:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true)
			tween(b, 0.35, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(4, math.max(2, math.floor(h.r * GRAPH_H + 0.5))), BackgroundColor3 = h.c, BackgroundTransparency = State.barFade(i) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
			if i == #graph.bars then
				b.BackgroundTransparency = 1
				tween(b, 0.4, { BackgroundTransparency = 0 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
			end
		end
	end
end
local fpsValue = createInfoRow(sessionPanel, 14, 34, 187, "fps", "--", "chart-no-axes-column-increasing")
local fpsGraph = statGraph(43)
local pingValue = createInfoRow(sessionPanel, 14, 63, 187, "ping", "-- ms", "wifi")
local pingGraph = statGraph(72)
local playersValue = createInfoRow(sessionPanel, 14, 95, 187, "players", #Players:GetPlayers() .. "/" .. Players.MaxPlayers, Icons.players)
local uptimeValue = createInfoRow(sessionPanel, 14, 114, 187, "uptime", "00:00", "timer")

liveDot.BackgroundTransparency = 0
State.dismissBtn(sessionPanel, "session").Position = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 3)
State.pinBtn(sessionPanel, "session").Position = UDim2.new(1, -30, 0, 3)

local sessionStart = os.clock()
local frames, accum = 0, 0
local statConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
	frames += 1
	accum += dt
	if accum < 0.5 then return end
	local fps = math.floor(frames / accum + 0.5)
	frames, accum = 0, 0
	fpsValue.Text = tostring(fps)
	local okPing, ping = pcall(function() return player:GetNetworkPing() end)
	local ms = okPing and math.floor(ping * 2000) or 0
	pingValue.Text = ms .. " ms"
	pushGraph(fpsGraph, math.clamp(fps / 120, 0.1, 1), fps >= 50 and State.sig.good or (fps >= 30 and State.sig.ok or State.sig.bad))
	pushGraph(pingGraph, math.clamp(ms / 300, 0.1, 1), ms <= 90 and State.sig.good or (ms <= 170 and State.sig.ok or State.sig.bad))
	playersValue.Text = #Players:GetPlayers() .. "/" .. Players.MaxPlayers
	local t = math.floor(os.clock() - sessionStart)
	uptimeValue.Text = string.format("%02d:%02d", t // 60, t % 60)
end)

do
	local LH = 108
	local langPanel, langContainer = createPanel(layoutFrame, "LangWidget", 0, 602, 460, LH, 10)
	State.langContainer = langContainer

	local LANGS = {
		{ "en", "English", "en", "English" }, { "vi", "Tiếng Việt", "vi", "Vietnamese" }, { "zh", "中文", "zh-CN", "Chinese" }, { "ja", "日本語", "ja", "Japanese" },
		{ "ko", "한국어", "ko", "Korean" }, { "th", "ไทย", "th", "Thai" }, { "id", "Indonesia", "id", "Indonesian" }, { "fr", "Français", "fr", "French" },
		{ "de", "Deutsch", "de", "German" }, { "es", "Español", "es", "Spanish" }, { "pt", "Português", "pt", "Portuguese" }, { "ru", "Русский", "ru", "Russian" },
		{ "it", "Italiano", "it", "Italian" }, { "tr", "Türkçe", "tr", "Turkish" }, { "pl", "Polski", "pl", "Polish" }, { "nl", "Nederlands", "nl", "Dutch" },
		{ "ar", "العربية", "ar", "Arabic" }, { "hi", "हिन्दी", "hi", "Hindi" }, { "uk", "Українська", "uk", "Ukrainian" }, { "cs", "Čeština", "cs", "Czech" },
		{ "sv", "Svenska", "sv", "Swedish" }, { "da", "Dansk", "da", "Danish" }, { "fi", "Suomi", "fi", "Finnish" }, { "el", "Ελληνικά", "el", "Greek" },
		{ "he", "עברית", "iw", "Hebrew" }, { "hu", "Magyar", "hu", "Hungarian" }, { "ro", "Română", "ro", "Romanian" }, { "bg", "Български", "bg", "Bulgarian" },
		{ "ms", "Melayu", "ms", "Malay" }, { "fil", "Filipino", "tl", "Filipino" },
	}
	local L = {
		code = "en", cache = {}, tracked = {}, token = 0, pending = {}, LANGS = LANGS, GCODE = {}, BYCODE = {}, DIR = "NauruHub",
		recent = { "en", "fil", "es", "ja", "zh", "ko", "fr", "de" },
	}
	for _, l in ipairs(LANGS) do L.GCODE[l[1]] = l[3] L.BYCODE[l[1]] = l end
	State.lang = L

	do
		local DIR = L.DIR

		local function ensureDir() pcall(function() if makefolder and isfolder and not isfolder(DIR) then makefolder(DIR) end end) end
		local function loadCache(code)
			if L.cache[code] then return L.cache[code] end
			local t = {}
			pcall(function()
				local path = DIR .. "/lang_" .. code .. ".json"
				if isfile and isfile(path) then t = HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(path)) end
			end)
			L.cache[code] = type(t) == "table" and t or {}
			return L.cache[code]
		end
		local function saveCache(code)
			pcall(function()
				if writefile then
					ensureDir()
					writefile(DIR .. "/lang_" .. code .. ".json", HttpService:JSONEncode(L.cache[code]))
				end
			end)
		end
		L.loadCache = loadCache
		function L.savePick(code) pcall(function() if writefile then ensureDir() writefile(DIR .. "/lang.txt", code) end end) end

		local function httpGet(url)
			local req = request or http_request or (syn and syn.request)
			if req then
				local ok, res = pcall(req, { Url = url, Method = "GET" })
				if ok and res and type(res.Body) == "string" and (res.StatusCode == nil or res.StatusCode == 200) then return res.Body end
				return nil
			end
			local ok, body = pcall(function() return game:HttpGet(url) end)
			return ok and body or nil
		end

		local function translateLines(gcode, list)
			local url = "https://translate.googleapis.com/translate_a/single?client=gtx&sl=en&tl=" .. gcode .. "&dt=t&q=" .. HttpService:UrlEncode(table.concat(list, "\n"))
			local body = httpGet(url)
			if not body then return nil end
			local ok, data = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONDecode(body) end)
			if not ok or type(data) ~= "table" or type(data[1]) ~= "table" then return nil end
			local parts = {}
			for _, seg in ipairs(data[1]) do
				if type(seg) == "table" and type(seg[1]) == "string" then table.insert(parts, seg[1]) end
			end
			local out = string.split(table.concat(parts), "\n")
			if #out == #list + 1 and out[#out] == "" then table.remove(out) end
			if #out ~= #list then return nil, true end
			for i, line in ipairs(out) do out[i] = line:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") end
			return out
		end

		function L.fetchMissing(code, strings, progress)
			local cache = loadCache(code)
			local missing = {}
			for _, str in ipairs(strings) do if cache[str] == nil then table.insert(missing, str) end end
			local total, done, failed, i = #missing, 0, 0, 1
			while i <= total do
				local batch, len = {}, 0
				while i <= total and #batch < 25 and len < 1200 do
					table.insert(batch, missing[i])
					len += #missing[i] + 3
					i += 1
				end
				local out, mismatch = translateLines(L.GCODE[code], batch)
				if not out and mismatch then
					out = {}
					for k, str in ipairs(batch) do
						local o = translateLines(L.GCODE[code], { str })
						out[k] = o and o[1] or nil
						task.wait(0.15)
					end
				end
				for k, str in ipairs(batch) do
					if out and out[k] and out[k] ~= "" then cache[str] = out[k] else failed += 1 end
				end
				done += #batch
				if progress then progress(done, total) end
				task.wait(0.25)
			end
			if total > 0 then saveCache(code) end
			return total == 0 or failed < total
		end

		function L.translatable(t)
			return #t >= 2 and #t <= 80 and t:find("%a") ~= nil and t:find("%d") == nil and t:find("^@") == nil and t:find("%l") ~= nil
		end
		function L.candidate(inst)
			if not (inst:IsA("TextLabel") or inst:IsA("TextButton")) then return false end
			if inst.RichText or inst:GetAttribute("NoTranslate") then return false end
			return L.translatable(inst.Text)
		end

		function L.track(inst, orig)
			local e = L.tracked[inst]
			if e then return e end
			e = { orig = orig, tr = orig }
			L.tracked[inst] = e
			inst:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
				if inst.Text == e.tr then return end

				local new = inst.Text
				e.orig, e.tr = new, new
				L.fit(inst, e)
				if L.code == "en" or not L.translatable(new) then return end
				local cached = loadCache(L.code)[new]
				if cached then
					e.tr = cached
					inst.Text = cached
					L.fit(inst, e)
				else
					L.queue(inst, new)
				end
			end)
			return e
		end

		function L.queue(inst, str)
			L.pending[inst] = str
			if L.flushing then return end
			L.flushing = true
			task.delay(0.3, function()
				local batch, seen = {}, {}
				for _, st in pairs(L.pending) do if not seen[st] then seen[st] = true table.insert(batch, st) end end
				local code = L.code
				L.fetchMissing(code, batch)
				local cache = loadCache(code)
				for obj, st in pairs(L.pending) do
					local te = L.tracked[obj]
					if te and code == L.code and cache[st] and obj.Text == st then te.tr = cache[st] obj.Text = cache[st] L.fit(obj, te) end
				end
				L.pending, L.flushing = {}, false
			end)
		end

		L.watched = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
		function L.adopt(inst)
			if L.watched[inst] or not (inst:IsA("TextLabel") or inst:IsA("TextButton")) then return end
			L.watched[inst] = true
			local function try()
				if L.code == "en" or L.tracked[inst] or not L.candidate(inst) then return end
				local orig = inst.Text
				local e = L.track(inst, orig)
				local cached = loadCache(L.code)[orig]
				if cached then e.tr = cached inst.Text = cached L.fit(inst, e) else L.queue(inst, orig) end
			end
			try()
			inst:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(try)
		end

		function L.fit(inst, e)
			if not (inst:IsA("TextLabel") or inst:IsA("TextButton")) then return end
			e.ts = e.ts or inst.TextSize
			if e.wrap == nil then e.wrap = inst.TextWrapped end
			inst.TextSize, inst.TextWrapped = e.ts, e.wrap
			if inst.Text == e.orig or inst.RichText or inst.TextTruncate ~= Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd then return end
			if inst.AutomaticSize ~= Enum.AutomaticSize.None then return end
			local sc, p = 1, inst
			while p and p ~= gui do
				for _, ch in ipairs(p:GetChildren()) do if ch:IsA("UIScale") then sc *= ch.Scale end end
				p = p.Parent
			end
			local avail = inst.AbsoluteSize.X / math.max(sc, 0.01)
			if avail < 8 then return end
			local TS = game:GetService("TextService")
			local function width(sz)
				local ok, v = pcall(function() return TS:GetTextSize(inst.Text, sz, inst.Font, Vector2.new(10000, 1000)).X end)
				return ok and v or 0
			end
			if width(e.ts) <= avail - 1 then return end
			local minS, sz = math.max(7, math.floor(e.ts * 0.62)), e.ts
			while sz > minS and width(sz) > avail - 1 do sz -= 1 end
			inst.TextSize = sz
			if width(sz) > avail - 1 and (inst.AbsoluteSize.Y / math.max(sc, 0.01)) >= sz * 2 + 2 then inst.TextWrapped = true end
		end

		function L.setText(inst, text)
			local e = L.track(inst, inst.Text)
			e.tr = text
			inst.Text = text
			L.fit(inst, e)
		end
	end

		local function rowAlpha(code)
			if code == U.active then return 0.8 end
			if U.q ~= "" and code == U.top then return 0.94 end
			return 1
		end
		local function hl(text, q, col)
			if q == "" then return text end
			local s, e = string.lower(text):find(q, 1, true)
			if not s then return text end
			return text:sub(1, s - 1) .. '<font color="' .. col .. '">' .. text:sub(s, e) .. "</font>" .. text:sub(e + 1)
		end
		local function score(l, q)
			if q == "" then return 0 end
			local n, e = string.lower(l[2]), string.lower(l[4])
			if l[1] == q then return 0 end
			if n:sub(1, #q) == q or e:sub(1, #q) == q then return 1 end
			if n:find(q, 1, true) or e:find(q, 1, true) then return 2 end
			if l[1]:find(q, 1, true) then return 3 end
			return nil
		end

		for i, l in ipairs(LANGS) do
			local btn = createButton(scroll, { Sound = true, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent, BackgroundTransparency = 1, LayoutOrder = i })
			corner(btn, 6)

			create("UIGradient", { Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0, 0.7) }, btn)
			r.bar = createFrame(btn, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 0), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent })
			corner(r.bar, 2)

			r.sub = createLabel(btn, l[4], 9, C.sub, FONT, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(38, 17), Size = UDim2.new(1, -100, 0, 12), RichText = true })
			local tag = createLabel(btn, string.upper(l[1]), 9, C.dim, FONT_BOLD, {
				AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(26, 12), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right,

			for _, b in ipairs({ { 5.2, 8.2, 3.6, 45 }, { 8.6, 6.6, 7.5, -52 } }) do
