				tween(r.bar, 0.18, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 18) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
				tween(tag, 0.1, { TextColor3 = C.accent })
			end)
			btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
				tween(btn, 0.1, { BackgroundTransparency = rowAlpha(l[1]) })
				tween(r.bar, 0.15, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, l[1] == U.active and 18 or 0) })
				tween(tag, 0.1, { TextColor3 = C.dim })
			end)
			btn.Activated:Connect(function() L.pick(l[1]) end)
		end

		for _, l in ipairs(LANGS) do
			pcall(function() if isfile and isfile(L.DIR .. "/lang_" .. l[1] .. ".json") then U.cached[l[1]] = true end end)
		end

		function U.refresh()
			local q = U.q
			local acc = string.format("rgb(%d,%d,%d)", math.floor(C.accent.R * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(C.accent.G * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(C.accent.B * 255 + 0.5))
			local keys, best, bestKey, n = {}, nil, math.huge, 0
			for i, l in ipairs(LANGS) do
				local s = score(l, q)
				if s then
					local k = s * 100 + i
					keys[l[1]] = k
					n += 1
					if k < bestKey then bestKey, best = k, l[1] end
				end
			end
			U.top = best
			for _, l in ipairs(LANGS) do
				local r, k = U.rows[l[1]], keys[l[1]]
				r.btn.Visible = k ~= nil
				if k then
					local on = l[1] == U.active
					r.btn.LayoutOrder = k
					r.name.Text = hl(l[2], q, acc)
					r.name.TextColor3 = on and C.accent or C.text
					r.sub.Text = hl(l[4], q, acc) .. (U.cached[l[1]] and "  -  cached" or "")
					r.check.Visible = on
					r.bar.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, on and 18 or 0)
					r.btn.BackgroundTransparency = rowAlpha(l[1])
				end
			end
			U.count.Text = n .. " / " .. #LANGS
			U.empty.Visible = n == 0
			return n
		end

		function U.fit(n, instant)
			local cp, w = langContainer.Position, L.w or 460
			local pw = w >= 400 and 270 or w
			local px = w >= 400 and (cp.X.Offset + w - 14 - pw) or cp.X.Offset
			local py = cp.Y.Offset - 6
			local room = math.max(py - 4, 60)
			U.down = false
			local belowY = cp.Y.Offset + langContainer.Size.Y.Offset + 6
			if State.pin and State.pin.cur == "lang" then
				local up, dn = State.pin.roomAbove(), State.pin.roomBelow()
				if up < 150 and dn > up then U.down, room = true, dn else room = math.max(up, 60) end
				belowY += 8 + 26
			else
				local up, dn = py - 4, layoutFrame.Size.Y.Offset - belowY - 4
				if up < 150 and dn > up then U.down, room = true, math.min(dn, 238) else room = math.max(up, 60) end
			end
			local h = math.min(23 + 8 + math.min(math.max(n, 1), 6) * 34, room)
			local dur = (not instant) and 0.18 or nil
			if U.down then
				U.slide.set(px, belowY, pw, h, true, dur)
			else
				U.slide.set(px, py - h, pw, h, false, dur)
			end
		end

		function U.setPop(v, instant)
			if U.open == v then return end
			U.open = v
			if v then
				local n = U.refresh()
				U.fit(n, true)
				U.popStroke.Transparency = 0.55
				U.slide.show()
				tween(U.chev.Holder, 0.18, { Rotation = 180 })
				tween(U.ddStroke, 0.15, { Color = C.accent })
				task.defer(function()
					local idx = 1
					for i, l in ipairs(LANGS) do if l[1] == U.active then idx = i end end
					scroll.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, U.q == "" and math.max(0, (idx - 2) * 34) or 0)
				end)
			else
				tween(U.chev.Holder, 0.18, { Rotation = 0 })
				tween(U.ddStroke, 0.15, { Color = C.line })
				if instant then
					U.slide.snap()
					U.pop.GroupTransparency = 1
					U.popStroke.Transparency = 1
					return
				end
				U.slide.close()
			end
		end

		function U.statusHover(text)
			U.status.Text = text or U.statusBase
		end

		function U.buildChips()
			for i, c in ipairs(L.recent) do if c == "en" then table.remove(L.recent, i) break end end
			table.insert(L.recent, 1, "en")
			for _, ch in ipairs(U.chips:GetChildren()) do if ch:IsA("TextButton") then ch:Destroy() end end
			local w = L.w or 460
			local wide = w >= 400
			local cw = wide and 56 or 32
			local fit = math.max(1, math.floor((w - 28 + 6) / (cw + 6)))
			for i, code in ipairs(L.recent) do
				if i > fit then break end
				local l, on = L.BYCODE[code], code == U.active
				local b = createButton(U.chips, { Sound = true, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(cw, 24), LayoutOrder = i, BackgroundColor3 = C.field, BackgroundTransparency = 0 })
				corner(b, 5)
				local st = stroke(b, on and C.accent or C.line, 1)
				local fl = U.flag(b, code, 20, 14)
				fl.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
				fl.Position = wide and UDim2.fromOffset(17, 12) or UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
				if wide then
					local t = createLabel(b, string.upper(code), 9, on and C.accent or C.sub, FONT_BOLD, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(31, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(22, 24) })
					t:SetAttribute("NoTranslate", true)
				end
				b.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
					U.statusHover(l[4])
					if code ~= U.active then tween(st, 0.1, { Color = C.hoverLine }) end
				end)
				b.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
					U.statusHover(nil)
					if code ~= U.active then tween(st, 0.1, { Color = C.line }) end
				end)
				b.Activated:Connect(function() L.apply(code) end)
			end
		end

		function U.markActive(code)
			U.active = code
			local l = L.BYCODE[code]
			U.ddName.Text = l[2]
			U.ddTag.Text = string.upper(code)
			if U.ddFlag then U.ddFlag:Destroy() end
			U.ddFlag = U.flag(U.dd, code, 20, 14)
			U.ddFlag.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 5)
			U.buildChips()
			if U.open then U.refresh() end
		end

		function L.pushRecent(code)
			for i, c in ipairs(L.recent) do if c == code then table.remove(L.recent, i) break end end
			table.insert(L.recent, 1, code)

			for i, c in ipairs(L.recent) do if c == "en" then table.remove(L.recent, i) break end end
			table.insert(L.recent, 1, "en")
			while #L.recent > 8 do table.remove(L.recent) end
			U.buildChips()
		end

		function L.pick(code)
			U.box.Text = ""
			U.setPop(false)
			if code ~= L.code or code ~= U.active then L.apply(code) end
		end

		function L.setStatus(t) t = State.titleCase(t) U.statusBase = t U.status.Text = t end
		function L.setProgress(f) tween(U.barFill, 0.2, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(math.clamp(f, 0, 1), 1) }) end
		function L.closePop() U.setPop(false) end
		function L.reveal() end

		U.box:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
			U.q = string.lower(U.box.Text):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
			U.clear.Visible = U.box.Text ~= ""
			local n = U.refresh()
			if U.box.Text ~= "" and not U.open then U.setPop(true) elseif U.open then U.fit(n) end
		end)
		U.box.Focused:Connect(function()
			tween(U.searchStroke, 0.12, { Color = C.accent })
			U.setPop(true)
		end)
		U.box.FocusLost:Connect(function(enter)
			tween(U.searchStroke, 0.12, { Color = C.line })
			if enter and U.top and U.q ~= "" then L.pick(U.top) end
		end)
		U.dd.MouseEnter:Connect(function() if not U.open then tween(U.ddStroke, 0.1, { Color = C.hoverLine2 }) end end)
		U.dd.MouseLeave:Connect(function() if not U.open then tween(U.ddStroke, 0.1, { Color = C.line }) end end)
		U.dd.Activated:Connect(function() U.setPop(not U.open) end)
		UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
			if U.open and isPress(input) and not isInside(U.pop, input.Position) and not isInside(U.dd, input.Position) and not isInside(U.search, input.Position) then
				U.setPop(false)
			end
		end)

		function L.layout(w)
			L.w = w
			local iw, wide = w - 28, w >= 400
			local ddW = wide and 190 or 92
			local sw = iw - ddW - 8
			U.search.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(sw, 24)
			U.dd.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14 + sw + 8, 42)
			U.dd.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(ddW, 24)
			U.chips.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(iw, 30)
			U.ddName.Visible = wide
			U.ddTag.AnchorPoint = wide and Vector2.new(1, 0.5) or Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
			U.ddTag.Position = wide and UDim2.new(1, -24, 0.5, 0) or UDim2.fromOffset(34, 12)
			U.ddTag.TextXAlignment = wide and Enum.TextXAlignment.Right or Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
			U.buildChips()
			U.setPop(false, true)
		end

		do
			local SETS = { ar = "٠١٢٣٤٥٦٧٨٩", hi = "०१२३४५६७८९", th = "๐๑๒๓๔๕๖๗๘๙" }
			local FWD, REV = {}, {}
			for code, str in pairs(SETS) do
				local m, i = {}, 0
				for _, cp in utf8.codes(str) do
					local ch = utf8.char(cp)
					m[tostring(i)] = ch
					REV[ch] = tostring(i)
					i += 1
				end
				FWD[code] = m
			end
			local function conv(text, m)
				if not text:find("[%d\128-\255]") then return text end
				local ok, out = pcall(function()
					local t = {}
					for _, cp in utf8.codes(text) do
						local ch = utf8.char(cp)
						local a = REV[ch] or ch
						t[#t + 1] = (m and m[a]) or a
					end
					return table.concat(t)
				end)
				return ok and out or text
			end
			function L.ascii(text) return conv(text, nil) end
			function L.num(text) return conv(text, FWD[L.code]) end
			local hooked = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
			function L.applyNumbers()
				local scan = layoutFrame:GetDescendants()
				for _, h in pairs(State.pinHosts or {}) do
					for _, x in ipairs(h.inner:GetDescendants()) do table.insert(scan, x) end
				end
				for _, inst in ipairs(scan) do
					if (inst:IsA("TextLabel") or inst:IsA("TextButton")) and not inst.RichText and not inst:GetAttribute("NoNumbers") and not L.tracked[inst] then
						if not hooked[inst] then
							hooked[inst] = true
							inst:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
								if FWD[L.code] and not L.tracked[inst] then
									local n = L.num(inst.Text)
									if n ~= inst.Text then inst.Text = n end
								end
							end)
						end
						local n = L.num(inst.Text)
						if n ~= inst.Text then inst.Text = n end
					end
				end
			end
		end

		function L.apply(code, silent)
			local def = L.BYCODE[code]
			if not def then return end
			L.token += 1
			local token, prev = L.token, L.code
			U.markActive(code)

			local items = {}
			local scan = layoutFrame:GetDescendants()
			for _, h in pairs(State.pinHosts or {}) do
				for _, x in ipairs(h.inner:GetDescendants()) do table.insert(scan, x) end
			end
			for _, r in ipairs(State.langRoots or {}) do
				for _, x in ipairs(r:GetDescendants()) do table.insert(scan, x) end
			end
			for _, inst in ipairs(scan) do
				local e = L.tracked[inst]
				if e and inst.Text == e.tr then
					table.insert(items, { inst, e.orig })
				elseif L.candidate(inst) then
					table.insert(items, { inst, inst.Text })
				end
			end

			if code == "en" then
				for _, it in ipairs(items) do
					local e = L.track(it[1], it[2])
					e.orig = it[2]
					L.setText(it[1], it[2])
				end
				L.code = "en"
				L.applyNumbers()
				L.setProgress(0)
				L.setStatus("ready")
				L.savePick("en")
				L.pushRecent("en")
				if not silent then notify("language", def[2]) end
				return
			end

			local strings, seen = {}, {}
			for _, it in ipairs(items) do
				if not seen[it[2]] then seen[it[2]] = true table.insert(strings, it[2]) end
			end
			L.setStatus("translating")
			L.setProgress(0)
			task.spawn(function()
				local ok = L.fetchMissing(code, strings, function(d, t)
					if token == L.token then
						L.setStatus(d .. "/" .. t)
						L.setProgress(d / t)
					end
				end)
				if token ~= L.token then return end
				if not ok then
					U.markActive(prev)
					L.setProgress(0)
					L.setStatus("failed")
					notify("language", "translation failed (no http access?)")
					return
				end
				U.cached[code] = true
				local cache = L.loadCache(code)
				for _, it in ipairs(items) do
					local e = L.track(it[1], it[2])
					e.orig = it[2]
					L.setText(it[1], cache[it[2]] or it[2])
				end
				L.code = code
				L.applyNumbers()
				L.setProgress(1)
				task.delay(1.2, function() if token == L.token then L.setProgress(0) end end)
				L.setStatus("done")
				L.savePick(code)
				L.pushRecent(code)
				if not silent then notify("language", def[2]) end
			end)
		end

		U.markActive("en")
	end

	function State.langResize(w)
		if L.w == w then return end
		langContainer.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, LH)
		local i = 0
		for _, ch in ipairs(langContainer:GetChildren()) do
			if ch ~= langPanel and ch:IsA("Frame") then
				i += 1
				ch.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w + i * 6, LH + i * 6)
			end
		end
		L.layout(w)
		if State.pinFit then State.pinFit() end
	end
	State.langResize(460)
end

task.spawn(function()
	while gui.Parent do
		clockLabel.Text = State.clockText()
		task.wait(0.5)
	end
end)

State.EXT = { CW = 215, CH = 156, GAP = 10 }
State.apPanel, State.apC = createPanel(layoutFrame, "AppearanceWidget", 0, 720, State.EXT.CW, State.EXT.CH, 10)
State.plPanel, State.plC = createPanel(layoutFrame, "PlayersWidget", 245, 720, State.EXT.CW, State.EXT.CH, 10)

do
	local dock = {
		music = { c = widgetContainer, off = false },
		session = { c = sessionContainer, off = false },
		lang = { c = State.langContainer, off = false },
		appearance = { c = State.apC, off = true },
		players = { c = State.plC, off = true },
	}
	State.dock = dock
	local CARD_W, CARD_H, TUCK = 215, 134, 0.5
	local ORDER = { "music", "session", "lang", "appearance", "players" }
	local function baseZ(key) return 0 end
	local CARD_HEIGHT = { music = 143, session = 130, lang = 108, appearance = State.EXT.CH, players = State.EXT.CH }
	local function gone(k) return dock[k].off or dock[k].pinned end
	State.slots = { "music", "session", "lang" }
	local function home(key)
		if State.extHome and (key == "appearance" or key == "players") then return State.extHome(key) end
		local so = State.slots
		local function vis(k) return k == key or not gone(k) end
		if layoutMode == "side" then
			local y = 38
			for _, k in ipairs(so) do
				if k == key then return UDim2.fromOffset(472, y) end
				if vis(k) then y += CARD_HEIGHT[k] + 10 end
			end
		end

		if so[1] == key then return UDim2.fromOffset(0, 449) end
		if so[2] == key then return UDim2.fromOffset(gone(so[1]) and 0 or 245, 449) end
		local rowH = 0
		for i = 1, 2 do if not gone(so[i]) then rowH = math.max(rowH, CARD_HEIGHT[so[i]]) end end
		return UDim2.fromOffset(0, rowH > 0 and (449 + rowH + 10) or 449)
	end
	function State.dockBottom()
		local k = State.slots[3]
		return home(k).Y.Offset + CARD_HEIGHT[k]
	end
	local SLIDE = 0.6
	State.fxTok = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
	function State.dockFx(c, hide, delay, dur)
		State.fxTok[c] = (State.fxTok[c] or 0) + 1
		local tok = State.fxTok[c]
		local function go()
			if State.fxTok[c] ~= tok then return end
			local t = dur or (hide and SLIDE * 0.85 or SLIDE * 0.7)
			local base = 0.93 + 0.07 * State.uiTransparency / 100
			for _, ch in ipairs(c:GetChildren()) do
				if ch:IsA("Frame") then
					local st = ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
					if hide then
						if st then tween(st, t, { Transparency = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In) else tween(ch, t, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In) end
					elseif st then
						st.Transparency = 1
						tween(st, t, { Transparency = 0.8 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine)
					else
						ch.BackgroundTransparency = 1
						tween(ch, t, { BackgroundTransparency = base }, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine)
					end
				end
			end
		end
		if delay and delay > 0 then task.delay(delay, go) else go() end
	end
	local function tuckPos(key)
		local ch = CARD_HEIGHT[key]
		local hp = home(key)
		if key == "appearance" or key == "players" then return UDim2.fromOffset(hp.X.Offset, 439 - ch - 20) end
		if layoutMode == "side" then
			return UDim2.fromOffset(460 - CARD_W - 20, hp.Y.Offset)
		end
		return UDim2.fromOffset(hp.X.Offset, 439 - ch - 20)
	end
	local function allOff() return gone("music") and gone("session") and gone("lang") end
	local glideTok = 0
	local function glideCenter()
		glideTok += 1
		local tok = glideTok
		local vp = gui.AbsoluteSize
		local sx, sy = view.x, view.y
		local tx, ty = (vp.X - view.w * view.scale) / 2, (vp.Y - view.h * view.scale) / 2
		local nv = Instance.new("NumberValue")
		nv.Changed:Connect(function(f)
			if tok ~= glideTok then return end
			view.x, view.y = sx + (tx - sx) * f, sy + (ty - sy) * f
			applyView()
		end)
		local tw = TweenService:Create(nv, TweenInfo.new(0.6, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut), { Value = 1 })
		tw.Completed:Connect(function() nv:Destroy() end)
		tw:Play()
	end
	local function rl()
		local prev = State.skipPlace
		State.skipPlace = true
		local ok, err = pcall(State.relayout)
		State.skipPlace = prev
		if not ok then warn(err) end
	end
	local function relayout()
		if not State.relayout then return end
		local sig = tostring(gone("appearance") and not dock.appearance.hold) .. tostring(gone("players") and not dock.players.hold)
		if State.extSig ~= sig then State.extSig = sig rl() end
		if allOff() then
			task.delay(SLIDE + 0.05, function()
				if allOff() and not State.allDocked then
					State.allDocked = true
					rl()
					glideCenter()
				end
			end)
		elseif State.allDocked then
			State.allDocked = false
			rl()
		end
	end
	for _, key in ipairs(ORDER) do
		local d = dock[key]
		d.c.ZIndex = baseZ(key)
		d.token = 0
		if d.off then d.c.Visible = false end
	end
	function State.dockPlace()
		if State.skipPlace then return end
		for _, key in ipairs({ "music", "session", "lang" }) do
			if not gone(key) and dock[key].c.Parent == layoutFrame and not dock[key].c:FindFirstChild("PopScale") then dock[key].c.Position = home(key) end
		end
	end
	function State.dockReflow(except)
		for _, k in ipairs(ORDER) do
			local o = dock[k]
			if k ~= except and not gone(k) and o.c.Parent == layoutFrame and not o.c:FindFirstChild("PopScale") then
				if k == "lang" and State.lang and State.lang.closePop then State.lang.closePop() end
				tween(o.c, SLIDE, { Position = home(k) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut)
			end
		end
	end

	local pin = { cur = nil, wx = nil, wy = nil }
	State.pin = pin
	local FM, FTOP, PILL_H = 16, 250, 26
	local PILLS = { { "music", "music", 0, "audio-lines" }, { "session", "session", 0, Icons.session }, { "lang", "translator", 0, "languages" },
		{ "appearance", "appearance", 0, Icons.palette }, { "players", "players", 0, Icons.players } }
	local PILL_W = 6
	for i, pd in ipairs(PILLS) do
		local ok, sz = pcall(function() return game:GetService("TextService"):GetTextSize(pd[2], 11, Enum.Font.GothamBold, Vector2.new(500, 30)).X end)
		pd.tw = ok and sz or 40
		pd[3] = math.ceil(pd.tw + 17 + 18)
		PILL_W += pd[3] + (i > 1 and 2 or 0)
	end
	local hosts = {}
	State.pinHosts = hosts
	toastHolder.ZIndex = 6
	for _, key in ipairs(ORDER) do
		local fx = create("CanvasGroup", {
			Name = "Float_" .. key, BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0,
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(460 + FM * 2, FTOP + 160 + 8 + PILL_H + 6 + 238 + FM), GroupTransparency = 1, Visible = false, ZIndex = 3,
		}, gui)
		local sc = create("UIScale", { Scale = 1 }, fx)
		local inner = createFrame(fx, { Name = "Inner", Position = UDim2.fromOffset(FM, FTOP), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(215, 143) })
		hosts[key] = { fx = fx, scale = sc, inner = inner }
	end

	function pin.fs() return math.max(view.scale, 0.6) end
	local function cardSize(key) local c = dock[key].c return c.Size.X.Offset, c.Size.Y.Offset end
	local function spotPos(key, wx, wy, s)
		s = s or pin.fs()
		return UDim2.fromOffset(wx - FM * s, wy - FTOP * s)
	end
	local function clampSpot(key, wx, wy)
		local w, h = cardSize(key)
		local s = pin.fs()
		local vp = gui.AbsoluteSize
		return math.clamp(wx, 0, math.max(0, vp.X - w * s)), math.clamp(wy, 0, math.max(0, vp.Y - (h + 8 + PILL_H) * s))
	end

	local function hgo(h, obj, prop, value, t, style, dir)
		h.tws = h.tws or {}
		local slot = h.tws[obj]
		if not slot then slot = {} h.tws[obj] = slot end
		if slot[prop] then slot[prop]:Cancel() end
		local tw = TweenService:Create(obj, TweenInfo.new(t, style or Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, dir or Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { [prop] = value })
		slot[prop] = tw
		tw:Play()
		return tw
	end
	local function hset(h, obj, prop, value)
		local slot = h.tws and h.tws[obj]
		if slot and slot[prop] then slot[prop]:Cancel() slot[prop] = nil end
		obj[prop] = value
	end
	local function busy(h, t) h.busyUntil = os.clock() + t end
	local function isBusy(h) return (h.busyUntil or 0) > os.clock() end

	local P = { tws = nil, tok = 0, segs = {}, pos = {}, icons = {} }
	do
		local pillHost = create("CanvasGroup", {
			Name = "Float_Switcher", BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0,
			Size = UDim2.fromOffset(PILL_W + FM * 2, PILL_H + FM * 2), GroupTransparency = 1, Visible = false, ZIndex = 4,
		}, gui)
		P.host = pillHost
		P.scale = create("UIScale", { Scale = 1 }, pillHost)
		local pill = createFrame(pillHost, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(FM, FM), BackgroundColor3 = C.bg, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(PILL_W, PILL_H), ZIndex = 5 })
		corner(pill, PILL_H / 2)
		stroke(pill, C.accent, 1, 0.8)
		P.hl = createFrame(pill, {
			Position = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 2), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(PILLS[1][3], PILL_H - 4),
			BackgroundColor3 = C.accent, BackgroundTransparency = 0.85, ZIndex = 5,
		})
		corner(P.hl, (PILL_H - 4) / 2)
		local sx = 3
		for _, pd in ipairs(PILLS) do
			local seg = createButton(pill, {
				Sound = true, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(sx, 2), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(pd[3], PILL_H - 4), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent,
				BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = pd[2], TextSize = 11, Font = FONT_BOLD, TextColor3 = C.sub, ZIndex = 6,
				TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
			})
			seg:SetAttribute("NoTranslate", true)
			local lead = math.floor((pd[3] - (17 + pd.tw)) / 2)
			create("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, lead + 17) }, seg)
			local ic = newIcon(seg, 12, pd[4])
			ic.Holder.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-11, (PILL_H - 4) / 2)
			P.icons[pd[1]] = ic
			seg.Activated:Connect(function() pin.set(pd[1]) end)
			P.segs[pd[1]] = seg
			P.pos[pd[1]] = { x = sx, w = pd[3] }
			sx += pd[3] + 2
		end
	end

	local function pillPlace(anim)
		local key = pin.cur
		if not key or not pin.wx then return end
		local w, ch = cardSize(key)
		local s = pin.fs()
		local px = pin.wx + math.floor((w - PILL_W) / 2) * s
		px = math.clamp(px, 0, math.max(0, gui.AbsoluteSize.X - PILL_W * s))
		local pos = UDim2.fromOffset(px - FM * s, pin.wy + (ch + 8) * s - FM * s)
		if P.scale.Scale ~= s then hset(P, P.scale, "Scale", s) end
		if anim then hgo(P, P.host, "Position", pos, 0.32, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
		else hset(P, P.host, "Position", pos) end
	end

	local function placeFloat(wx, wy)
		local key = pin.cur
		if not key then return end
		pin.wx, pin.wy = clampSpot(key, wx, wy)
		local h = hosts[key]
		if not isBusy(h) then hset(h, h.fx, "Position", spotPos(key, pin.wx, pin.wy)) end
		pillPlace(false)
	end
	function pin.roomBelow()
		local s = pin.fs()
		local _, ch = cardSize(pin.cur or "lang")
		return math.clamp((gui.AbsoluteSize.Y - ((pin.wy or 0) + (ch + 8 + PILL_H + 6) * s)) / s - 8, 0, 238)
	end
	function pin.roomAbove() return math.clamp(((pin.wy or 0) / math.max(pin.fs(), 0.01)) - 10, 0, 238) end
	function pin.room()
		local s = pin.fs()
		return math.clamp(((pin.wy or 0) / math.max(s, 0.01)) - 10, 60, 238)
	end
	function State.pinFit()
		local key = pin.cur
		if not key then return end
		local h = hosts[key]
		local w, ch = cardSize(key)
		h.inner.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, ch)
		if pin.wx then placeFloat(pin.wx, pin.wy) end
	end
	function State.pinSync()
		local s = pin.fs()
		if pin.lastS == s then return end
		pin.lastS = s
		for _, h in pairs(hosts) do if not isBusy(h) then h.scale.Scale = s end end
		if pin.cur and pin.wx then placeFloat(pin.wx, pin.wy) end
	end

	local function refreshPills()
		P.tok += 1
		local tok = P.tok
		local key = pin.cur
		for k, seg in pairs(P.segs) do
			tween(seg, 0.15, { TextColor3 = k == key and C.accent or C.sub })
			if P.icons[k] then P.icons[k].tint(k == key and C.accent or C.sub, 0.15) end
		end
		for k, a in pairs(State.pinArrows) do tween(a, 0.25, { Rotation = k == key and 180 or 0 }) end
		if key then
			local sp = P.pos[key]
			local pos, size = UDim2.fromOffset(sp.x, 2), UDim2.fromOffset(sp.w, PILL_H - 4)
			local fresh = (not P.host.Visible) or P.host.GroupTransparency > 0.6
			P.host.Visible = true
			if fresh then
				hset(P, P.hl, "Position", pos)
				hset(P, P.hl, "Size", size)
				hset(P, P.host, "GroupTransparency", 1)
				task.delay(0.28, function()
					if tok == P.tok then hgo(P, P.host, "GroupTransparency", 0, 0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out) end
				end)
			else
				hgo(P, P.hl, "Position", pos, 0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
				hgo(P, P.hl, "Size", size, 0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
				hgo(P, P.host, "GroupTransparency", 0, 0.15, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
			end
		else
			hgo(P, P.host, "GroupTransparency", 1, 0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
			task.delay(0.22, function() if tok == P.tok then P.host.Visible = false end end)
		end
	end

	local function closePopups(key)
		pcall(function()
			if key == "lang" and State.lang and State.lang.closePop then State.lang.closePop() end
			if key == "music" and State.lib then State.lib.dismiss() end
			if key == "players" and State.plClose then State.plClose() end
		end)
	end
	local function movePopups(key, to)
		pcall(function()
			for _, o in ipairs(State.pinPop[key] or {}) do o.Parent = to end
			if key == "music" then placeOverlay() end
		end)
	end

	local function playOut(key, quick, dx)
		local h = hosts[key]
		local p = h.fx.Position
		busy(h, 0.3)
		if dx then
			hgo(h, h.fx, "GroupTransparency", 1, 0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
			hgo(h, h.fx, "Position", UDim2.new(p.X.Scale, p.X.Offset - dx * 46 * h.scale.Scale, p.Y.Scale, p.Y.Offset), 0.26, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
			return
		end
		hgo(h, h.fx, "GroupTransparency", 1, quick and 0.16 or 0.22, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
		hgo(h, h.fx, "Position", UDim2.new(p.X.Scale, p.X.Offset, p.Y.Scale, p.Y.Offset + (quick and 8 or 14) * h.scale.Scale), 0.26, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
	end

	local function attach(key, fromAbs, switching, dx)
		local d, h = dock[key], hosts[key]
		local c = d.c
		d.token += 1
		local wasOff = d.off
		d.pinned, pin.cur = true, key
		if key == "music" then State.musicOff = false end
		local s = pin.fs()
		hset(h, h.scale, "Scale", s)
		closePopups(key)

		State.stopTweens(c)
		if wasOff then State.dockFx(c, false) end
		c.Parent = h.inner
		c.ZIndex = 1
		c.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
		c.Visible = true
		movePopups(key, h.inner)
		h.busyUntil = 0
		local fromPill = P.host.Position
		State.pinFit()
		local _, ch = cardSize(key)
		local vp = gui.AbsoluteSize
		local dfx, dfy = 16, vp.Y - (ch + 8 + PILL_H) * s - 16
		if not pin.wx and holder.Visible then
			local hp, hs, cw = holder.AbsolutePosition, holder.AbsoluteSize, cardSize(key)
			local padS = PAD * view.scale
			local y = fromAbs and fromAbs.Y or (hp.Y + padS)
			if hp.X + hs.X - padS + 12 + cw * s <= vp.X then dfx, dfy = hp.X + hs.X - padS + 12, y
			elseif hp.X + padS - 12 - cw * s >= 0 then dfx, dfy = hp.X + padS - 12 - cw * s, y end
		end
		local wx, wy = clampSpot(key, pin.wx or dfx, pin.wy or dfy)
		pin.wx, pin.wy = wx, wy
		local target = spotPos(key, wx, wy, s)
		local r = layoutFrame.AbsoluteSize.X / math.max(layoutFrame.Size.X.Offset, 1)
		h.fx.Visible = true
		busy(h, 0.7)
		if fromAbs and r > 0.05 then
			hset(h, h.fx, "GroupTransparency", 0)
			hset(h, h.scale, "Scale", r)
			hset(h, h.fx, "Position", spotPos(key, fromAbs.X, fromAbs.Y, r))
			hgo(h, h.scale, "Scale", s, 0.55)
			hgo(h, h.fx, "Position", target, 0.55)
		else
			hset(h, h.fx, "GroupTransparency", 1)
			if switching and dx then
				hset(h, h.fx, "Position", UDim2.fromOffset(target.X.Offset + dx * 46 * s, target.Y.Offset))
				hgo(h, h.fx, "GroupTransparency", 0, 0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
				hgo(h, h.fx, "Position", target, 0.45, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
			else
				hset(h, h.fx, "Position", UDim2.fromOffset(target.X.Offset, target.Y.Offset + (switching and 8 or 14) * s))
				hgo(h, h.fx, "GroupTransparency", 0, 0.25, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
				hgo(h, h.fx, "Position", target, 0.4)
			end
		end

		if switching and P.host.Visible then
			hset(P, P.host, "Position", fromPill)
			pillPlace(true)
		else
			pillPlace(false)
		end
	end

	local function land(key, animate)
		local d, h = dock[key], hosts[key]
		local c = d.c
		State.stopTweens(c)
		c.ZIndex = baseZ(key)
		c.Parent = layoutFrame
		movePopups(key, layoutFrame)
		h.fx.Visible = false
		hset(h, h.fx, "GroupTransparency", 1)
		hset(h, h.scale, "Scale", pin.fs())
		if animate then
			c.Position = tuckPos(key)
			c.Visible = true
			State.dockFx(c, false)
			tween(c, SLIDE, { Position = home(key) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
		else
			c.Position = home(key)
			c.Visible = true
		end
	end

	local function release(key, quick, dx)
		local d = dock[key]
		d.token += 1
		local tok = d.token
		d.pinned = false
		if pin.cur == key then pin.cur = nil end
		closePopups(key)
		playOut(key, quick, dx)
		task.delay(0.27, function()
			if d.token ~= tok or d.pinned or d.off then return end
			land(key, holder.Visible)
		end)
	end

	function State.pinReclaim(key)
		local d, h = dock[key], hosts[key]
		if d.c.Parent == layoutFrame then return end
		State.stopTweens(d.c)
		d.c.ZIndex = baseZ(key)
		d.c.Parent = layoutFrame
		d.c.Position = tuckPos(key)
		movePopups(key, layoutFrame)
		h.fx.Visible = false
		hset(h, h.fx, "GroupTransparency", 1)
	end

	function pin.drop(key)
		local d = dock[key]
		local tok = d.token
		d.pinned = false
		if pin.cur == key then pin.cur = nil end
		closePopups(key)
		playOut(key)
		refreshPills()
		if pin.onChange then pin.onChange(pin.cur) end
		task.delay(0.3, function()
			if d.off and d.token == tok then
				State.pinReclaim(key)
				d.c.Visible = false
			end
		end)
	end

	local function setImpl(key)
		if key == pin.cur then return end
		local prev = pin.cur
		local fromAbs
		if key then
			local d = dock[key]
			if holder.Visible and not d.off and d.c.Visible and d.c.Parent == layoutFrame then fromAbs = d.c.AbsolutePosition end
			if d.off then
				d.off = false
				if d.cb then d.cb.Set(true, true) end
			end
		end
		if prev and key then
		local so = State.slots
		local i, j
		for n, k in ipairs(so) do if k == prev then i = n elseif k == key then j = n end end
		if i and j then so[i], so[j] = so[j], so[i] end
		State.skipPlace = true
		if State.relayout then State.relayout() end
		State.skipPlace = false
	end
	local dx
	if prev and key then dx = P.pos[key].x > P.pos[prev].x and 1 or -1 end
	if prev then release(prev, key ~= nil, dx) end
		if key then attach(key, fromAbs, prev ~= nil, dx) end
		State.dockReflow()
		refreshPills()
		relayout()
		if pin.onChange then pin.onChange(pin.cur) end
	end
	function pin.set(key)
		local ok, err = pcall(setImpl, key)
		if not ok then notify("pop-out", tostring(err):sub(1, 70)) end
	end
	function pin.toggle(key) if pin.cur == key then pin.set(nil) else pin.set(key) end end
	function pin.cycle()
		local nxt = ORDER[1]
		if pin.cur then
			nxt = nil
			for i, k in ipairs(ORDER) do if k == pin.cur then nxt = ORDER[i + 1] end end
		end
		pin.set(nxt)
	end

	local drag
	for _, key in ipairs(ORDER) do
		dock[key].c.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
			if pin.cur ~= key or not isPress(input) then return end
			drag = { p = input.Position, wx = pin.wx, wy = pin.wy, moved = false }
		end)
	end
	UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if not drag or not isMove(input) then return end
		local dl = input.Position - drag.p
		if not drag.moved and dl.Magnitude < 4 then return end
		drag.moved = true
		placeFloat(drag.wx + dl.X, drag.wy + dl.Y)
	end)
	UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input) if isPress(input) then drag = nil end end)
	gui:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(function() if pin.cur and pin.wx then placeFloat(pin.wx, pin.wy) end end)

	State.popTok = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
	local POP_FROM, POP_T = 0.72, 0.5
	State.popBase = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
	local function popRestore(c)
		c.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
		local base = State.popBase[c]
		if base then c.Position = base() end
	end
	function State.popCancel(c)
		State.popTok[c] = (State.popTok[c] or 0) + 1
		local sc = c:FindFirstChild("PopScale")
		if sc then
			sc:Destroy()
			if sc:IsA("UIScale") then popRestore(c) end
		end
	end

	function State.popCard(c, delay, base, onStart)
		State.popCancel(c)
		local tok = State.popTok[c]
		State.popBase[c] = base
		c.Visible = false
		task.delay(delay or 0, function()
			if State.popTok[c] ~= tok or not c.Parent then return end
			local sc = c:FindFirstChild("PopScale") or create("UIScale", { Name = "PopScale", Scale = POP_FROM }, c)
			local nv = Instance.new("NumberValue")
			local function place()
				local b = base()
				local lw, lh = math.max(layoutFrame.Size.X.Offset, 1), math.max(layoutFrame.Size.Y.Offset, 1)
				c.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
				c.Position = UDim2.fromScale((b.X.Offset + c.Size.X.Offset / 2) / lw, (b.Y.Offset + c.Size.Y.Offset / 2) / lh)
			end
			local function step(f)
				if State.popTok[c] ~= tok then return end
				sc.Scale = POP_FROM + (1 - POP_FROM) * f
				place()
			end
			nv.Changed:Connect(step)
			step(0)
			c.Visible = true
			if onStart then onStart() end
			local tw = TweenService:Create(nv, TweenInfo.new(POP_T, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), { Value = 1 })
			tw.Completed:Connect(function()
				nv:Destroy()
				if State.popTok[c] == tok then
					sc:Destroy()
					popRestore(c)
				end
			end)
			tw:Play()
		end)
	end
	function State.popCards()
		local i = 0
		for _, key in ipairs(ORDER) do
			local d = dock[key]
			local c = d.c
			if not gone(key) and c.Parent == layoutFrame then
				State.popCancel(c)
				State.stopTweens(c)
				local guard = c:FindFirstChild("PopScale") or Instance.new("BoolValue")
				guard.Name = "PopScale"
				guard.Parent = c
				c.Visible = false
				c.Position = tuckPos(key)
				local tok = (d.slideTok or 0) + 1
				d.slideTok = tok
				task.delay(0.1 + i * 0.07, function()
					if d.slideTok ~= tok then return end
					if guard.Parent then guard:Destroy() end
					if gone(key) or c.Parent ~= layoutFrame then return end
					c.Visible = true
					State.dockFx(c, false)
					local g2 = Instance.new("BoolValue")
					g2.Name = "PopScale"
					g2.Parent = c
					tween(c, SLIDE, { Position = home(key) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
					task.delay(SLIDE + 0.05, function()
						if d.slideTok == tok and g2.Parent then g2:Destroy() end
					end)
				end)
				i += 1
			end
		end
	end

	function State.tuckCards(dur)
		local i = 0
		for _, key in ipairs(ORDER) do
			local d = dock[key]
			local c = d.c
			d.slideTok = (d.slideTok or 0) + 1
			if not gone(key) and c.Parent == layoutFrame and c.Visible then
				State.popCancel(c)
				State.stopTweens(c)
				local guard = Instance.new("BoolValue")
				guard.Name = "PopScale"
				guard.Parent = c
				local tok = d.slideTok
				task.delay(i * 0.02, function()
					if d.slideTok ~= tok then return end
					tween(c, dur, { Position = tuckPos(key) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut)
					State.dockFx(c, true, dur * 0.6, dur * 0.35)
				end)
				task.delay(dur + 0.2 + i * 0.02, function() if guard.Parent then guard:Destroy() end end)
				i += 1
			end
		end
		return i
	end

	function dock.set(key, show)
		local d = dock[key]
		if not d or d.off == not show then return end
		d.off = not show
		d.token += 1
		local token = d.token
		d.hold = false
		if show then
			if key == "music" then State.musicOff = false end
			relayout()
			if d.c.Parent ~= layoutFrame then State.pinReclaim(key) d.c.Visible = false end
			if key == "appearance" or key == "players" then
				State.popCancel(d.c)
				State.stopTweens(d.c)
				d.c.Position = tuckPos(key)
				d.c.Visible = true
				State.dockFx(d.c, false)
				tween(d.c, SLIDE, { Position = home(key) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
			else
				d.c.Position = home(key)
				State.popCard(d.c, 0, function() return home(key) end, function() State.dockFx(d.c, false) end)
			end
			State.dockReflow(key)
		else
			if key == "music" then
				State.musicStop()
				notify("music", "player dismissed, music stopped")
			end
			if key == "lang" and State.lang and State.lang.closePop then State.lang.closePop() end
			if key == "music" and State.lib then State.lib.dismiss() end
			if d.cb then d.cb.Set(false, true) end
			if d.pinned then
				pin.drop(key)
			else
				State.popCancel(d.c)
				d.c.Visible = true
				State.dockFx(d.c, true, SLIDE * 0.55, SLIDE * 0.4)
				tween(d.c, SLIDE, { Position = tuckPos(key) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut)
				if key == "appearance" or key == "players" then d.hold = true end
				task.delay(SLIDE + 0.06, function()
					if d.off and d.token == token then d.c.Visible = false end
					if d.hold and d.token == token then d.hold = false relayout() end
				end)
			end
			State.dockReflow(key)
			relayout()
		end
	end
end

local function updateLayout()
	local vp = gui.AbsoluteSize
	if vp.X < 1 or vp.Y < 1 then return end

	local fitWide = math.min(vp.X / (460 + 0), vp.Y / (math.max(710, State.dockBottom and State.dockBottom() or 0) + (State.extH or 0)))
	local fitSide = math.min(vp.X / 687, vp.Y / (439 + (State.extH or 0)))
	local compact = fitSide > fitWide
	layoutMode = compact and "side" or "wide"
	if State.langResize then State.langResize((compact or (State.slots and State.slots[3] ~= "lang")) and 215 or 460) end
	local w, h
	if State.allDocked then
		w, h = 460, 439
	elseif compact then
		w, h = 687, 439
	else
		w, h = 460, math.max(710, State.dockBottom and State.dockBottom() or 0)
	end
	if State.extPlace then h = State.extPlace(h) end
	State.dockPlace()
	layoutFrame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, h)
	placeOverlay()
	view.w, view.h = w + PAD * 2, h + PAD * 2
	applyView()
	if viewportValue then viewportValue.Text = string.format("%dx%d %s", vp.X, vp.Y, layoutMode) end
end
State.relayout = updateLayout
gui:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(updateLayout)
do
	local function hookCamera()
		local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
		if cam then cam:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(updateLayout) end
	end
	hookCamera()
	workspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CurrentCamera"):Connect(function() hookCamera() updateLayout() end)
end

local Isl = { touch = UserInputService.TouchEnabled and not UserInputService.KeyboardEnabled, top = 10, ew = 340, eh = 112, re = 30 }
Isl.w, Isl.h = 210, Isl.touch and 36 or 32
Isl.ax, Isl.y = 0.5, Isl.top
Isl.hideY = -(Isl.h + 16)

local reopen = createButton(gui, {
	Name = "ReopenButton", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, Isl.hideY),
	Size = UDim2.fromOffset(Isl.w, Isl.h), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 10), BackgroundTransparency = 0,
	ClipsDescendants = true, Visible = false, ZIndex = 30,
})
reopen:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true)
Isl.corner = corner(reopen, Isl.h / 2)
Isl.stroke = stroke(reopen, C.accent, 1, 0.65)
Isl.scale = create("UIScale", {}, reopen)
Isl.big = create("UIScale", {}, reopen)
Isl.sf = 1
reopen.BackgroundColor3 = C.bg
table.insert(C.hooks, function() tween(reopen, 0.25, { BackgroundColor3 = C.bg }) end)

do
	local I = { open = false, hovered = false, token = 0, vis = 0 }

	local function nt(x) x:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true) return x end
	local DIM = Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 76)
	local row = create("CanvasGroup", { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, Isl.h), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, reopen)
	local rowList = create("UIListLayout", {
		FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal, HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center,
		VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center, SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Padding = UDim.new(0, 7),
	}, row)
	local function hex(c) return string.format("#%02X%02X%02X", math.floor(c.R * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(c.G * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(c.B * 255 + 0.5)) end
	function Isl.fmt() return string.format('<font color="#F4F4F6">%s</font> <font color="%s">%s</font>', State.brand.a, hex(C.accent), State.brand.b) end
	Isl.title = createLabel(row, Isl.fmt(), 12, Color3.fromRGB(244, 244, 246), nil, {
		Font = Enum.Font.Michroma, RichText = true, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, Isl.h), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X,
		TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None, LayoutOrder = 2,
	})
	local function statGroup(order, wifi)
		local g = create("CanvasGroup", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 16), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, LayoutOrder = order }, row)
		I.groups = I.groups or {}
		I.groups[#I.groups + 1] = g
		create("UIListLayout", { FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal, VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center, SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Padding = UDim.new(0, 4) }, g)
		local lbl = nt(createLabel(g, "--", 9, Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 206), FONT_BOLD, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 16), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, LayoutOrder = 1, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None }))
		if wifi then
			local ic = newIcon(g, 16, "wifi")
			table.remove(State.icons)
			ic.Holder.LayoutOrder = 2
			if ic.img then nt(ic.img) end
			for _, f in ic.inks do nt(f) end
			ic.paint(DIM)
			local cv = Instance.new("Color3Value")
			cv.Value = DIM
			cv.Changed:Connect(function(v) ic.paint(v) end)
			local parts = {}
			for lv = 1, 3 do
				parts[lv] = setmetatable({}, { __newindex = function(_, k, v)
					if lv == 1 and k == "BackgroundColor3" then cv.Value = v end
				end })
			end
			return lbl, parts
		end
		local box = createFrame(g, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(13, 12), LayoutOrder = 2 })
		local bars = {}
		for i = 1, 3 do
			local bar = nt(createFrame(box, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1), Position = UDim2.new(0, (i - 1) * 5, 1, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 4 + (i - 1) * 3), BackgroundColor3 = DIM }))
			corner(bar, 1)
			bars[i] = bar
		end
		return lbl, bars
	end
	I.cFps, I.fpsBars = statGroup(3)
	I.cMs, I.msBars = statGroup(4, true)
	local function sig(level) return level == 3 and State.sig.good or (level == 2 and State.sig.ok or State.sig.bad) end
	local function updateCompact()
		local asc = State.lang and State.lang.ascii or function(x) return x end
		local fps, ms = tonumber(asc(fpsValue.Text):match("%d+")), tonumber(asc(pingValue.Text):match("%d+"))
		if not fps or not ms then return end
		local lf = fps >= 50 and 3 or (fps >= 30 and 2 or 1)
		local lp = ms <= 90 and 3 or (ms <= 170 and 2 or 1)
		I.cFps.Text, I.cFps.TextColor3 = fps .. " fps", sig(lf)
		I.cMs.Text, I.cMs.TextColor3 = ms .. " ms", sig(lp)
		for i = 1, 3 do
			I.fpsBars[i].BackgroundColor3 = i <= lf and sig(lf) or DIM
			I.msBars[i].BackgroundColor3 = i <= lp and sig(lp) or DIM
		end
	end

	local details = create("CanvasGroup", {
		AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 6),
		Size = UDim2.fromOffset(Isl.ew, Isl.eh), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, GroupTransparency = 1, Visible = false,
	}, reopen)
	Isl.title2 = createLabel(details, Isl.fmt(), 12, Color3.fromRGB(244, 244, 246), nil, {
		Font = Enum.Font.Michroma, RichText = true, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 9), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(190, 18),
		TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None,
	})
	nt(Isl.title2)
	I.live = nt(createLabel(details, "", 10, C.sub, FONT_BOLD, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(Isl.ew - 18, 9), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(110, 18), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, RichText = true }))
	createFrame(details, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 34), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(Isl.ew - 36, 1), BackgroundColor3 = C.line, BackgroundTransparency = 0.35 })
	I.vals, I.meters, I.cols = {}, {}, {}
	local colW = (Isl.ew - 36) / 4
	for i, k in ipairs({ "fps", "ping", "players", "session" }) do
		local x = 18 + (i - 1) * colW
		local col = createFrame(details, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(colW - 8, Isl.eh) })
		createLabel(col, k, 10, C.sub, FONT, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 42), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 14) })
		I.vals[k] = nt(createLabel(col, "--", 15, C.text, FONT_BOLD, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 56), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22), TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None }))
		local track = createFrame(col, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 82), Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 3), BackgroundColor3 = C.line, BackgroundTransparency = 0 })
		corner(track, 2)
		local fill = createFrame(track, { Size = UDim2.new(0.05, 0, 1, 0), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent, BackgroundTransparency = 0 })
		corner(fill, 2)
		I.meters[k] = fill
		I.cols[i] = { f = col, x = x }
	end
	I.hint = createLabel(details, "", 10, C.dim, FONT, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 92), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(Isl.ew, 14), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center })

	local function refresh()
		local asc = State.lang and State.lang.ascii or function(x) return x end
		local fps = tonumber(asc(fpsValue.Text):match("%d+")) or 0
		local ms = tonumber(asc(pingValue.Text):match("%d+")) or 0
		local mm, ss = asc(uptimeValue.Text):match("(%d+):(%d+)")
		mm, ss = tonumber(mm) or 0, tonumber(ss) or 0
		local lf = fps >= 50 and 3 or (fps >= 30 and 2 or 1)
		local lp = ms <= 90 and 3 or (ms <= 170 and 2 or 1)
		local n, mx = #Players:GetPlayers(), math.max(Players.MaxPlayers, 1)
		I.vals.fps.Text, I.vals.fps.TextColor3 = tostring(fps), sig(lf)
		I.vals.ping.Text, I.vals.ping.TextColor3 = ms .. " ms", sig(lp)
		I.vals.players.Text = playersValue.Text
		I.vals.session.Text = uptimeValue.Text
		I.live.Text = string.format('<font color="#1ED760">\u{25CF}</font>  live  %s', uptimeValue.Text)
		tween(I.meters.fps, 0.3, { Size = UDim2.new(math.clamp(fps / 120, 0.04, 1), 0, 1, 0), BackgroundColor3 = sig(lf) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
		tween(I.meters.ping, 0.3, { Size = UDim2.new(math.clamp(1 - ms / 300, 0.04, 1), 0, 1, 0), BackgroundColor3 = sig(lp) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
		tween(I.meters.players, 0.3, { Size = UDim2.new(math.clamp(n / mx, 0.04, 1), 0, 1, 0) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
		tween(I.meters.session, 0.3, { Size = UDim2.new(math.clamp(ss / 60, 0.04, 1), 0, 1, 0) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
		local touchNow = Isl.touch or UserInputService:GetLastInputType() == Enum.UserInputType.Touch
		I.hint.Text = touchNow and "tap to open  -  drag to move" or ((CONFIG.toggleKey and ("click or press " .. CONFIG.toggleKey.Name .. " to open") or "click to open") .. "  -  drag to move")
	end
	for _, k in ipairs({ "fps", "ping" }) do nt(I.meters[k]) end

	local function setOpen(v)
		if I.open == v then return end
		I.open = v
		I.openTok = (I.openTok or 0) + 1
		local tok = I.openTok
		for _, k in ipairs({ "sizeTween", "cornerTween", "clampTween" }) do
			if I[k] then I[k]:Cancel() I[k] = nil end
		end
		if v then refresh() end
		local dur = v and 0.5 or 0.38
		I.sizeTween = tween(reopen, dur, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(v and Isl.ew or Isl.w, v and Isl.eh or Isl.h) }, v and Enum.EasingStyle.Back or Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
		I.cornerTween = tween(Isl.corner, dur, { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, v and Isl.re or Isl.h / 2) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)

		local off = 0
		if v then
			local vp = gui.AbsoluteSize
			local half = Isl.ew * Isl.sf / 2 + 6
			local px = Isl.ax * vp.X
			off = math.clamp(px, half, math.max(vp.X - half, half)) - px
		end
		if v or reopen.Visible then I.clampTween = tween(reopen, dur, { Position = UDim2.new(Isl.ax, off, 0, Isl.y) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint) end

		if not I.pressed and not I.dragging then
			Isl.scale.Scale = v and 0.96 or 1.03
			tween(Isl.scale, v and 0.55 or 0.4, { Scale = (not v and I.hovered) and 1.04 or 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
		end
		if v then
			details.Visible = true
			details.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 6)
			tween(row, 0.14, { GroupTransparency = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
			for i, c in ipairs(I.cols) do c.f.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(c.x, 8) end
			task.delay(0.1, function()
				if I.openTok ~= tok then return end
				tween(details, 0.22, { GroupTransparency = 0 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
				tween(details, 0.32, { Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 0) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
				for i, c in ipairs(I.cols) do
					task.delay(0.03 + i * 0.045, function()
						if I.openTok == tok then tween(c.f, 0.4, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(c.x, 0) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back) end
					end)
				end
			end)
		else
			tween(details, 0.14, { GroupTransparency = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad)
			task.delay(0.1, function()
				if I.openTok == tok then tween(row, 0.22, { GroupTransparency = 0 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad) end
			end)
			task.delay(0.18, function()
				if I.openTok == tok then details.Visible = false end
			end)
		end
	end

	function I.snapClosed()
		I.open = false
		I.openTok = (I.openTok or 0) + 1
		for _, k in ipairs({ "sizeTween", "cornerTween", "clampTween" }) do
			if I[k] then I[k]:Cancel() I[k] = nil end
		end
		reopen.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(Isl.w, Isl.h)
		Isl.corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, Isl.h / 2)
		details.GroupTransparency = 1
		details.Visible = false
		row.GroupTransparency = 0
	end

	function I.fadeStats()
		for _, g in ipairs(I.groups or {}) do g.GroupTransparency = 0 end
	end
	function I.show()
		I.vis += 1
		reopen.Visible = true
		if Isl.fit then Isl.fit() end
		updateCompact()
		I.fadeStats()
		tween(Isl.scale, 0.3, { Scale = 1 })
		I.posTween = tween(reopen, 0.55, { Position = UDim2.new(Isl.ax, 0, 0, Isl.y) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
	end
	function I.hide()
		I.vis += 1
		local mine = I.vis
		I.hovered = false
		if I.finish then I.finish(true) end
		setOpen(false)
		I.posTween = tween(reopen, 0.22, { Position = UDim2.new(Isl.ax, 0, 0, Isl.hideY) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
		task.delay(0.24, function()
			if I.vis == mine then reopen.Visible = false end
		end)
	end
	Isl.ctl = I

	reopen.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		if Isl.touch or I.dragging or I.pressed or UserInputService:GetLastInputType() == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
		I.hovered = true
		I.token += 1
		local t = I.token
		if I.open then return end
		tween(Isl.scale, 0.18, { Scale = 1.04 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
		task.delay(0.12, function()
			if I.token == t and I.hovered and not I.pressed then setOpen(true) end
		end)
	end)
	reopen.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		I.hovered = false
		if I.dragging then return end
		I.token += 1
		local t = I.token
		if not I.pressed then tween(Isl.scale, 0.25, { Scale = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint) end
		task.delay(I.open and 0.28 or 0.2, function()
			if I.token == t and not I.hovered then setOpen(false) end
		end)
	end)

	I.pressed, I.dragging, I.dragged = false, false, false
	local home = create("Frame", {
		Name = "IslandHome", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, Isl.top), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(Isl.w, Isl.h),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 29, Visible = false,
	}, gui)
	corner(home, Isl.h / 2)
	local homeBig = create("UIScale", {}, home)
	local homeStroke = stroke(home, C.accent, 1.5, 1)

	function Isl.rescale()
		local vp = gui.AbsoluteSize
		if vp.Y <= 0 then vp = workspace.CurrentCamera and workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize or Vector2.new(1280, 720) end
		Isl.sf = math.clamp(math.min(vp.Y / 520, vp.X / 820), 1.35, 2)
		Isl.big.Scale, homeBig.Scale = Isl.sf, Isl.sf
		Isl.hideY = -(Isl.h * Isl.sf + 16)
		if not reopen.Visible then reopen.Position = UDim2.new(Isl.ax, 0, 0, Isl.hideY) end
	end
	Isl.rescale()
	gui:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(Isl.rescale)

	local function fit()
		Isl.w = math.max(120, math.ceil(rowList.AbsoluteContentSize.X) + 24)
		home.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(Isl.w, Isl.h)
		if not I.open and not I.dragging then reopen.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(Isl.w, Isl.h) end
	end
	rowList:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(fit)
	Isl.fit = fit
	fit()

	local function cancelMove()
		if I.posTween then I.posTween:Cancel() I.posTween = nil end
	end

	function I.finish(drop)
		if not I.pressed and not I.dragging then return end
		local wasDragging = I.dragging
		I.pressed, I.dragging = false, false
		I.homeTween = tween(homeStroke, 0.15, { Transparency = 1 })
		home.Visible = false
		homeStroke.Transparency = 1
		tween(Isl.stroke, 0.25, { Transparency = 0.65, Thickness = 1 })
		tween(Isl.scale, 0.25, { Scale = (I.hovered and not wasDragging) and 1.04 or 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
		if wasDragging and drop then
			if I.near then
				I.snapping = true
				Isl.ax, Isl.y = 0.5, Isl.top
			else

				local vp, pos = gui.AbsoluteSize, reopen.Position
				local px = pos.X.Scale * vp.X + pos.X.Offset
				local py = pos.Y.Scale * vp.Y + pos.Y.Offset
				Isl.ax = math.clamp(px / math.max(vp.X, 1), 0, 1)
				Isl.y = math.clamp(py, 0, math.max(vp.Y - Isl.h * Isl.sf, 0))
			end
			I.near = false
		end
		if I.snapping then
			I.posTween = tween(reopen, 0.3, { Position = UDim2.new(Isl.ax, 0, 0, Isl.y) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
		else
			reopen.Position = UDim2.new(Isl.ax, 0, 0, Isl.y)
		end
		I.snapping = false
	end

	reopen.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if not isPress(input) then return end
		if I.pressed then

			if I.input and I.input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then I.finish(true) else return end
		end
		cancelMove()
		tween(Isl.scale, 0.1, { Scale = 0.95 })
		I.pressed, I.dragging, I.dragged, I.near = true, false, false, false
		I.input = input
		I.p0 = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y)

		local vp0, pos = gui.AbsoluteSize, reopen.Position
		I.x0 = pos.X.Scale * vp0.X + pos.X.Offset
		I.y0 = pos.Y.Scale * vp0.Y + pos.Y.Offset

		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and not I.open then
			task.delay(0.32, function()
				if I.pressed and I.input == input and not I.dragging and not I.open then
					I.dragged = true
					setOpen(true)
					task.spawn(function()
						while I.pressed and I.open do task.wait(0.1) end
						task.wait(3)
						if I.open and not I.pressed and not I.hovered then setOpen(false) end
					end)
				end
			end)
		end
	end)
	UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if not I.pressed or not isMove(input) then return end

		if I.input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			if input ~= I.input then return end
		elseif input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
			return
		end
		local d = Vector2.new(input.Position.X, input.Position.Y) - I.p0
		if not I.dragging then
			if d.Magnitude < 6 then return end
			I.dragging, I.dragged = true, true
			I.hovered = false
			I.token += 1
			I.snapClosed()
			home.Visible = true
			homeStroke.Transparency = 1
			tween(Isl.stroke, 0.15, { Transparency = 0, Thickness = 2.2 })
			tween(Isl.scale, 0.2, { Scale = 1.06 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
		end
		local vp = gui.AbsoluteSize
		local hw = Isl.w * Isl.sf / 2
		local nx = math.clamp(I.x0 + d.X, hw, math.max(vp.X - hw, hw))
		local ny = math.clamp(I.y0 + d.Y, 0, math.max(vp.Y - Isl.h * Isl.sf, 0))
		reopen.Position = UDim2.new(nx / math.max(vp.X, 1), 0, 0, ny)
		local near = math.abs(nx - vp.X / 2) < 44 and ny < Isl.top + 40
		if near ~= I.near then
			I.near = near
			tween(homeStroke, 0.12, { Transparency = near and 0.1 or 1, Thickness = near and 2.5 or 1.5 })
		end
		I.nx, I.ny = nx, ny
	end)
	local islandConns = {}
	islandConns[1] = UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if not I.pressed or not isPress(input) then return end

		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and I.input and I.input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and input ~= I.input then return end
		I.finish(true)
	end)

	islandConns[2] = reopen.InputBegan:Connect(function() end)
	gui.Destroying:Connect(function() for _, c in ipairs(islandConns) do c:Disconnect() end end)

	task.spawn(function()
		while gui.Parent do
			task.wait(0.3)
			if reopen.Visible then updateCompact() end
			if I.open then refresh() end
		end
	end)
end

local ANIM_NAMES = { "pop", "fade", "grow", "zoom", "slide up", "slide down", "slide left", "slide right", "drop", "swing", "glide", "bloom" }
local ANIMS = {
	pop = { s = 0.93, t = 0.32, style = Enum.EasingStyle.Back },
	fade = { s = 1, t = 0.3, style = Enum.EasingStyle.Quad },
	grow = { s = 0.78, t = 0.34, style = Enum.EasingStyle.Quint },
	zoom = { s = 0.5, t = 0.45, style = Enum.EasingStyle.Back },
	["slide up"] = { s = 0.98, dy = 60, t = 0.38, style = Enum.EasingStyle.Quint },
	["slide down"] = { s = 0.98, dy = -60, t = 0.38, style = Enum.EasingStyle.Quint },
	["slide left"] = { s = 0.98, dx = 80, t = 0.38, style = Enum.EasingStyle.Quint },
	["slide right"] = { s = 0.98, dx = -80, t = 0.38, style = Enum.EasingStyle.Quint },
	drop = { s = 1, dy = -140, t = 0.6, style = Enum.EasingStyle.Bounce },
	swing = { s = 0.92, rot = -8, t = 0.5, style = Enum.EasingStyle.Back },

	glide = { s = 0.95, dy = 34, rot = 1.6, t = 0.7, style = Enum.EasingStyle.Exponential, fade = 0.42, close = 0.26 },

	bloom = { s = 0.88, t = 0.58, style = Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, fade = 0.4, settle = 0.014, sheen = true, close = 0.26 },
}
local shown = false
State.animTok = 0
State.openSheen = State.makeSheen(mainWindow, 10, 300, 0.9)
local function applyDim()
	tween(dimFrame, 0.25, { BackgroundTransparency = shown and (1 - State.dim / 100) or 1 })
end
local function setShown(v)
	if v == shown then return end
	shown = v
	applyDim()
	local a = ANIMS[State.openAnim] or ANIMS.pop
	local dx, dy = a.dx or 0, a.dy or 0
	if v then
		holder.Visible = true
		Isl.ctl.hide()
		root.GroupTransparency = 1
		popScale.Scale = a.s
		root.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(dx, dy)
		root.Rotation = a.rot or 0

		for i, n in ipairs(navs) do
			n.icon.tint(n.selected and C.accent or C.sub, 0)
			n.btn.BackgroundTransparency = n.selected and 0.9 or 1
			n.sc.Scale = 0.01
			task.delay(0.08 + i * 0.035, function()
				if shown then tween(n.sc, 0.32, { Scale = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back) end
			end)
		end
		State.animTok += 1
		local my = State.animTok
		tween(root, a.fade or 0.25, { GroupTransparency = 0 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine)
		if a.settle then
			tween(popScale, a.t * 0.72, { Scale = 1 + a.settle }, a.style)
			task.delay(a.t * 0.72, function()
				if my == State.animTok and shown then tween(popScale, a.t * 0.5, { Scale = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut) end
			end)
		else
			tween(popScale, a.t, { Scale = 1 }, a.style)
		end
		tween(root, a.t, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), Rotation = 0 }, a.style)
		if a.sheen and State.openSheen then State.openSheen.play(1.1, a.t * 0.35) end
		if State.popCards then State.popCards() end
	else
		State.animTok += 1
		if State.lang and State.lang.closePop then State.lang.closePop() end
		if State.lib then State.lib.dismiss() end
		local ct = math.min(a.close or 0.18, 0.16)
		local my = State.animTok
		tween(root, ct, { GroupTransparency = 1, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(dx * 0.4, dy * 0.4) }, a.close and Enum.EasingStyle.Sine or nil)
		tween(popScale, ct, { Scale = a.s }, a.close and Enum.EasingStyle.Sine or nil)
		task.delay(ct + 0.01, function()
			if my == State.animTok and not shown then
				for _, nv in ipairs(navs) do nv.sc.Scale = 1 end
				holder.Visible = false Isl.ctl.show()
			end
		end)
	end
end

closeBtn.Activated:Connect(function() setShown(false) end)
reopen.Activated:Connect(function() if Isl.ctl.dragged then return end setShown(true) end)
UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
	if processed or capturing then return end
	if CONFIG.toggleKey and input.KeyCode == CONFIG.toggleKey then setShown(not shown) end
end)

gui.Destroying:Connect(function()
	if State.LA and State.LA.destroy then pcall(State.LA.destroy) end
	waveConn:Disconnect()
	statConn:Disconnect()
	if gripConn then gripConn:Disconnect() end
end)
