local DERIVE = {
	secTitle = function(t) return t.text:Lerp(t.sub, 0.3) end,
	track = function(t) return t.line:Lerp(t.sub, 0.06) end,
	divider = function(t) return t.line:Lerp(t.sub, 0.35) end,
	topText = function(t) return t.text:Lerp(t.sub, 0.35) end,
	hoverLine = function(t) return t.line:Lerp(t.accent, 0.4) end,
	hoverLine2 = function(t) return t.line:Lerp(t.accent, 0.25) end,
	hoverField = function(t) return t.field:Lerp(t.text, 0.06) end,
	accentHover = function(t) return t.accent:Lerp(C.white, 0.15) end,
	waveA = function(t) return t.accent:Lerp(C.white, 0.25) end,
	waveB = function(t) return t.accent:Lerp(C.black, 0.15) end,
}
for _, t in ipairs(THEMES) do
	if t.name == "ember" then
		for _, k in ipairs(THEME_KEYS) do t[k] = C[k] end
	else
		for k, f in pairs(DERIVE) do t[k] = f(t) end
	end
end

local function colorKey(c) return math.floor(c.R * 255 + 0.5) * 65536 + math.floor(c.G * 255 + 0.5) * 256 + math.floor(c.B * 255 + 0.5) end
local function rgbStr(c) return string.format("rgb(%d,%d,%d)", math.floor(c.R * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(c.G * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(c.B * 255 + 0.5)) end
local function noTheme(inst) inst:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true) return inst end

local function refreshBrand()
	local br = State.brand
	if br.b ~= "" then
		logo.Text = string.format('<font color="%s">%s</font><font color="%s"> %s</font>', rgbStr(C.accent), br.a, rgbStr(C.red), br.b)
	else
		logo.Text = string.format('<font color="%s">%s</font>', rgbStr(C.accent), br.a)
	end
	brandLabel.Text = br.name .. (br.tag ~= "" and string.format(' <font color="%s" face="GothamMedium">[%s]</font>', rgbStr(C.sub), br.tag) or "")
	if Isl.title then Isl.title.Text = Isl.fmt() end
	if Isl.title2 then Isl.title2.Text = Isl.fmt() end
end
refreshBrand()

do
	local function seq(g)
		local n, kps = #g, {}
		for i, c in ipairs(g) do table.insert(kps, ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(n == 1 and 0 or (i - 1) / (n - 1), c)) end
		if n == 1 then table.insert(kps, ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, g[1])) end
		return ColorSequence.new(kps)
	end
	table.insert(C.hooks, function()
		local g = C.grad
		for _, e in ipairs(State.panelGrads or {}) do
			e[1].Visible = g ~= nil
			if g then e[2].Color = seq(g) e[2].Rotation = g.rot or 35 end
		end
	end)
	local t0, conn = 0, nil
	conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
		if not gui.Parent then conn:Disconnect() return end
		local g = C.grad
		if not g or not holder.Visible then return end
		t0 += dt
		local r = (g.rot or 35) + math.sin(t0 * 0.35) * 14
		for _, e in ipairs(State.panelGrads or {}) do if e[1].Visible then e[2].Rotation = r end end
	end)
end

local currentTheme = "ember"
local onThemeChanged

local function setTheme(name, silent)
	local theme
	for _, t in ipairs(THEMES) do if t.name == name then theme = t end end
	if not theme then return end

	local map = {}
	for _, k in ipairs(THEME_KEYS) do
		local key = colorKey(C[k])
		if map[key] == nil then map[key] = theme[k] end
	end
	local function m(c) return map[colorKey(c)] end

	for _, inst in ipairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do
		if not inst:GetAttribute("NoTheme") then
			if inst:IsA("GuiObject") then
				local n = m(inst.BackgroundColor3)
				if n then inst.BackgroundColor3 = n end
				if inst:IsA("TextLabel") or inst:IsA("TextButton") or inst:IsA("TextBox") then
					n = m(inst.TextColor3)
					if n then inst.TextColor3 = n end
				end
				if inst:IsA("TextBox") then
					n = m(inst.PlaceholderColor3)
					if n then inst.PlaceholderColor3 = n end
				end
				if inst:IsA("ImageLabel") or inst:IsA("ImageButton") then
					n = m(inst.ImageColor3)
					if n then inst.ImageColor3 = n end
				end
				if inst:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
					n = m(inst.ScrollBarImageColor3)
					if n then inst.ScrollBarImageColor3 = n end
				end
			elseif inst:IsA("UIStroke") then
				local n = m(inst.Color)
				if n then inst.Color = n end
			elseif inst:IsA("UIGradient") then
				local kps, changed = {}, false
				for _, kp in ipairs(inst.Color.Keypoints) do
					local n = m(kp.Value)
					if n then changed = true end
					table.insert(kps, ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(kp.Time, n or kp.Value))
				end
				if changed then inst.Color = ColorSequence.new(kps) end
			end
		end
	end

	local pending = {}
	for tw, rec in pairs(liveTw) do pending[#pending + 1] = { tw, rec } end
	for _, e in ipairs(pending) do
		local tw, rec = e[1], e[2]
		local np, changed = {}, false
		for k, v in pairs(rec.props) do
			local n = (typeof(v) == "Color3") and m(v) or nil
			if n then changed = true end
			np[k] = n or v
		end
		if changed then
			pcall(function() tw:Cancel() end)
			liveTw[tw] = nil
			if rec.obj.Parent then tween(rec.obj, rec.t, np, rec.style, rec.dir) end
		end
	end

	for _, ic in ipairs(State.icons) do
		local n = m(ic.cur)
		if n then ic.tint(n, 0) end
	end

	for _, k in ipairs(THEME_KEYS) do C[k] = theme[k] end
	C.grad = theme.grad
	for _, h in ipairs(C.hooks) do pcall(h) end
	currentTheme = name
	refreshBrand()
	if onThemeChanged then onThemeChanged(name) end
	if not silent then
		notify("Theme", name)
		pcall(function()
			if writefile then
				if not isfolder("NauruHub") then makefolder("NauruHub") end
				writefile("NauruHub/theme_v2.txt", name)
			end
		end)
	end
end

local L, R, W = 16, 202, 162

local function colLabel(p, x, y, text)
	createLabel(p, text, 12, C.text, FONT_BOLD, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(90, 16) })
end

State.features = {}
function State.onFeature(name, value)

end
local function hook(name)
	return function(v) State.features[name] = v State.onFeature(name, v) end
end

function State.fitCanvas(sf, pad)
	local bottom = 0
	for _, c in ipairs(sf:GetChildren()) do
		if c:IsA("GuiObject") and c.Visible then
			bottom = math.max(bottom, c.Position.Y.Offset + c.Size.Y.Offset * (1 - c.AnchorPoint.Y))
		end
	end
	sf.CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0, bottom + (pad or 10))
end
function State.makeScroll(sf)
	State.fitCanvas(sf, 10)

	sf.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(sf.Size.X.Offset - 7, sf.Size.Y.Offset - 12)
	sf.ScrollBarThickness = 2
	sf.ScrollBarImageColor3 = C.accent
	State.fadeBar(sf)
end

State.LAok = true
State.LA = setmetatable({ destroy = function() end }, { __index = function() return function() end end })

local function buildMain(p)
	local LA = State.LA
	local sf = create("ScrollingFrame", {
		Name = "main", Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 96), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(383, 305),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ScrollBarThickness = 0, CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 302),
		ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y, ElasticBehavior = Enum.ElasticBehavior.Never,
	}, p)
	local function cb(x, y, text, key) createCheckbox(sf, x, y, W, text, false, function(v) LA[key](v) end) end

	createSection(sf, L, 8, W, "movement")
	cb(L, 32, "speed multiplier", "speed")
	createSlider(sf, L, 54, W, { label = "multiplier", min = 1, max = 5, value = 2, decimals = 1, onChange = function(v) LA.speedVal(v) end })
	cb(L, 78, "jump multiplier", "jump")
	createSlider(sf, L, 100, W, { label = "multiplier", min = 1, max = 5, value = 2, decimals = 1, onChange = function(v) LA.jumpVal(v) end })

	createSection(sf, L, 124, W, "automation")
	cb(L, 148, "instant prompt", "instantPrompt")
	cb(L, 174, "auto collect scraps", "autoCollect")
	cb(L, 200, "auto pickup pumpkins", "autoPumpkins")
	cb(L, 226, "auto sell (machine)", "autoSell")

	createSection(sf, R, 8, W, "protection")
	cb(R, 32, "anti jumpscare", "antiJump")
	cb(R, 58, "anti static image", "antiUi")

	createSection(sf, R, 84, W, "monster proximity alert")
	cb(R, 108, "alert when monster near", "monsterAlert")
	createSlider(sf, R, 130, W, { label = "distance threshold", min = 10, max = 300, value = 60, decimals = 0, suffix = " studs", onChange = function(v) LA.alertDist(v) end })

	createSection(sf, R, 154, W, "view")
	cb(R, 178, "third-person view", "third")
	local fovInit = false
	createSlider(sf, R, 200, W, { label = "field of view", min = 40, max = 120, value = 70, decimals = 0, onChange = function(v)
		if not fovInit then fovInit = true return end
		LA.fov(v)
	end })

	createSection(sf, R, 224, W, "teleport")
	createActionButton(sf, R, 248, W, "teleport to monster room", function() LA.tpMonsterRoom() end)
	createActionButton(sf, R, 280, W, "quick play", function() LA.tpQuickPlay() end)
	State.makeScroll(sf)
end

local function buildVisuals(p)
	local LA = State.LA
	local sf = create("ScrollingFrame", {
		Name = "visuals", Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 96), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(383, 305),
		BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ScrollBarThickness = 0, CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 305),
		ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y, ElasticBehavior = Enum.ElasticBehavior.Never,
	}, p)
	local function cb(x, y, text, key) createCheckbox(sf, x, y, W, text, false, function(v) LA[key](v) end) end

	createSection(sf, L, 8, W, "monsters")
	cb(L, 32, "monster esp", "monsterEsp")
	cb(L, 58, "monster billboards", "monsterTags")
	createSection(sf, L, 84, W, "players")
	cb(L, 108, "player billboards", "playerTags")
	createSection(sf, L, 134, W, "items")
	cb(L, 158, "scraps esp", "scrapsEsp")
	cb(L, 184, "scraps billboards", "scrapsTags")
	cb(L, 210, "scrap machine esp", "machinesEsp")
	cb(L, 236, "pumpkins esp", "pumpkinsEsp")
	cb(L, 262, "pumpkins billboards", "pumpkinsTags")

	createSection(sf, R, 8, W, "colors")
	local function color(y, label, key, c)
		colLabel(sf, R, y, label)
		createColorPicker(sf, R + W - 24, y, c, function(v) LA.color(key, v) end)
	end
	color(32, "monsters", "Monsters", Color3.fromRGB(255, 30, 30))
	color(58, "monster tags", "monsterTag", Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 80))
	color(84, "player tags", "playerTag", Color3.new(1, 1, 1))
	color(110, "scraps", "Scraps", Color3.fromRGB(255, 165, 0))
	color(136, "machines", "Machines", Color3.fromRGB(80, 200, 255))
	color(162, "pumpkins", "Pumpkins", Color3.fromRGB(255, 100, 0))
	color(188, "outline", "outline", Color3.new(1, 1, 1))
	State.makeScroll(sf)
end

local function buildEffects(p)
	createSection(p, L, 104, W, "motion")
	createCheckbox(p, L, 128, W, "animations", true)
	createCheckbox(p, L, 154, W, "hover effects", true)
	createSlider(p, L, 188, W, { label = "dim background", min = 0, max = 80, value = State.dim, suffix = "%", onChange = function(v) State.dim = v applyDim() end })
	createSlider(p, L, 228, W, { label = "ui transparency", min = 0, max = 70, value = State.uiTransparency, suffix = "%", onChange = function(v) State.uiTransparency = v applyTransparency() end })
	createActionButton(p, L, 270, W, "replay open animation", function()
		setShown(false)
		task.delay(0.45, function() setShown(true) end)
	end, true)

	createSection(p, R, 104, W, "appearance")
	createCheckbox(p, R, 128, W, "dark mode", true)
	createCheckbox(p, R, 154, W, "accent glow", true)
	colLabel(p, R, 184, "open animation")
	local chips = {}
	local function refreshChips()
		for n, c in pairs(chips) do
			local on = n == State.openAnim
			tween(c.btn, 0.12, { TextColor3 = on and C.accent or C.sub })
			tween(c.stroke, 0.12, { Color = on and C.accent or C.line })
		end
	end
	for i, name in ipairs(ANIM_NAMES) do
		local col, row = (i - 1) % 2, (i - 1) // 2
		local b = createButton(p, { Sound = true, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(R + col * 84, 196 + row * 25), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(78, 21), BackgroundColor3 = C.field, BackgroundTransparency = 0, Text = name, TextSize = 10, Font = FONT_BOLD, TextColor3 = C.sub })
		corner(b, 5)
		local st = stroke(b, C.line, 1)
		State.fitText(b, 78, 10, 7, FONT_BOLD)
		chips[name] = { btn = b, stroke = st }
		b.MouseEnter:Connect(function() if State.openAnim ~= name then tween(st, 0.1, { Color = C.hoverLine }) end end)
		b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() if State.openAnim ~= name then tween(st, 0.1, { Color = C.line }) end end)
		b.Activated:Connect(function()
			State.openAnim = name
			refreshChips()
			setShown(false)
			task.delay(0.4, function() setShown(true) end)
		end)
	end
	refreshChips()
	table.insert(C.hooks, function()
		for n, c in pairs(chips) do
			local on = n == State.openAnim
			c.btn.TextColor3 = on and C.accent or C.sub
			c.stroke.Color = on and C.accent or C.line
		end
	end)
end

local function buildThemes(p)
	createSection(p, 16, 88, 351, "themes")
	local previews = {}
	local rowsN = math.ceil(#THEMES / 5)
	local sfT = create("ScrollingFrame", { Name = "themeList", Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 96), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(383, 305), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0,
		ScrollBarThickness = 0, CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0, (rowsN - 1) * 92 + 4 + 74 + 12), ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y, ElasticBehavior = Enum.ElasticBehavior.Never }, p)
	local pFull = p
	p = sfT

	local ring = createFrame(p, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(74, 82), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ZIndex = 5 })
	corner(ring, 10)
	local ringStroke = stroke(ring, C.accent, 2)
	for i, t in ipairs(THEMES) do
		local col, row = (i - 1) % 5, (i - 1) // 5
		local x, y = 16 + col * 71, 4 + row * 92
		local btn = noTheme(createButton(p, { Sound = true, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(66, 74), BackgroundColor3 = t.grad and Color3.new(1, 1, 1) or t.bg, BackgroundTransparency = 0, ClipsDescendants = true }))
		noTheme(corner(btn, 7))
		if t.grad then
			local kps = {}
			for gi, gc in ipairs(t.grad) do table.insert(kps, ColorSequenceKeypoint.new((gi - 1) / math.max(#t.grad - 1, 1), gc)) end
			noTheme(create("UIGradient", { Color = ColorSequence.new(kps), Rotation = t.grad.rot or 35 }, btn))
		end
		local btnStroke = noTheme(stroke(btn, t.line, 1.5))
		local function box(px, py, w, h, color, r, transp)
			local f = noTheme(createFrame(btn, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(px, py), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, h), BackgroundColor3 = color, BackgroundTransparency = transp or 0 }))
			if r then noTheme(corner(f, r)) end
			return f
		end
		box(0, 0, 24, 74, t.panel, 7, t.grad and 0.45 or 0)
		if not t.grad then box(15, 0, 12, 74, t.bg) end
		box(5, 9, 5, 5, t.accent, 2) box(5, 21, 5, 5, t.sub, 2) box(5, 33, 5, 5, t.sub, 2) box(5, 45, 5, 5, t.sub, 2)
		box(15, 0, 1, 74, t.accent, nil, 0.4)
		box(20, 5, 20, 4, t.text, 2) box(41, 5, 10, 4, t.red, 2)
		box(16, 13, 50, 1, t.accent, nil, 0.5)
		box(20, 18, 10, 3, t.accent, 1) box(33, 18, 10, 3, t.sub, 1) box(46, 18, 10, 3, t.sub, 1)
		box(20, 28, 7, 7, t.accent, 2) box(30, 30, 26, 3, t.text, 1)
		local off = box(20, 39, 7, 7, t.field, 2) noTheme(stroke(off, t.line, 1))
		box(30, 41, 20, 3, t.dim, 1)
		box(20, 53, 38, 3, t.line, 2) box(20, 53, 22, 3, t.accent, 2) box(39, 51, 7, 7, C.white, 4)
		box(20, 62, 26, 8, t.accent, 3)
		local b2 = box(49, 62, 12, 8, t.field, 3) noTheme(stroke(b2, t.line, 1))
		local sc = noTheme(create("UIScale", {}, btn))
		btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() tween(sc, 0.14, { Scale = 1.06 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back) end)
		btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tween(sc, 0.14, { Scale = 1 }) end)
		btn.Activated:Connect(function() setTheme(t.name) end)
		local label = createLabel(p, t.name, 10, C.sub, FONT_BOLD, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y + 78), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(66, 14), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center })
		previews[t.name] = { stroke = btnStroke, line = t.line, label = label, cx = x + 33, cy = y + 37 }
	end
	local selected = {}
	onThemeChanged = function(name)
		selected.Text = name
		for n, d in pairs(previews) do
			d.label.TextColor3 = n == name and C.accent or C.sub
		end
		local d = previews[name]
		if d then
			tween(ring, 0.35, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(d.cx, d.cy) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
			tween(ringStroke, 0.25, { Color = C.accent })
		end
	end
	do local d = previews[currentTheme] if d then ring.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(d.cx, d.cy) end end
	onThemeChanged(currentTheme)
end

local function buildPlayer(p)
	createSection(p, L, 104, W, "profile")
	createInfoRow(p, L, 130, W, "name", player.Name)
	createInfoRow(p, L, 152, W, "display", player.DisplayName)
	createInfoRow(p, L, 174, W, "user id", tostring(player.UserId))
	createInfoRow(p, L, 196, W, "account age", player.AccountAge .. " days")
	createSection(p, L, 228, W, "server")
	createInfoRow(p, L, 254, W, "players", #Players:GetPlayers() .. "/" .. Players.MaxPlayers)
	createInfoRow(p, L, 276, W, "place id", tostring(game.PlaceId))

	createSection(p, R, 104, W, "display")
	createCheckbox(p, R, 128, W, "show avatar", true, function(v) avatar.Visible = v end)
	createCheckbox(p, R, 154, W, "show clock", true, function(v) clockLabel.Visible = v end)
	State.dock.music.cb = createCheckbox(p, R, 180, W, "show music", true, function(v) State.dock.set("music", v) end)
	State.dock.session.cb = createCheckbox(p, R, 206, W, "show session", true, function(v) State.dock.set("session", v) end)
	createCheckbox(p, R, 232, W, "show game title", true, function(v) State.gameTitle.set(v) end)
	State.dock.lang.cb = createCheckbox(p, R, 258, W, "show translator", true, function(v) State.dock.set("lang", v) end)

	createSection(p, L, 310, W, "widgets")
	State.dock.appearance.cb = createCheckbox(p, L, 334, W, "show appearance", false, function(v) State.dock.set("appearance", v) end)
	State.dock.players.cb = createCheckbox(p, L, 360, W, "show players", false, function(v) State.dock.set("players", v) end)

	createSection(p, R, 290, W, "floating widget")
	local floatNames = { music = "music", session = "session", lang = "translator", appearance = "appearance", players = "players" }
	local floatBtn = createActionButton(p, R, 318, W, "off", function() State.pin.cycle() end)
	State.pin.onChange = function(k) floatBtn.Text = State.titleCase(k and floatNames[k] or "off") end
end

local function buildConfig(p)
	local Cfg = State.cfg
	local pendingDel
	local confirmBox

	createSection(p, L, 104, W, "configs")
	colLabel(p, L, 128, "selected")
	local dd = createDropdown(p, L, 139, W, Cfg.list(), Cfg.selected, function(v) Cfg.selected = v Cfg.savePrefs() end, true)
	local function refresh(sel) dd.SetOptions(Cfg.list(), sel or Cfg.selected) end

	createActionButton(p, L, 192, 76, "save", function()
		local where = Cfg.write(Cfg.selected)
		notify("config saved", '"' .. Cfg.selected .. '"' .. (where == "memory" and " (this session only)" or ""))
		refresh()
	end, true)
	createActionButton(p, L + 86, 192, 76, "load", function()
		local data = Cfg.read(Cfg.selected)
		if data and Cfg.apply(data) then
			notify("config loaded", '"' .. Cfg.selected .. '" loaded')
		else
			notify("config", '"' .. Cfg.selected .. '" has nothing saved yet')
		end
	end)
	createActionButton(p, L, 224, W, "delete selected", function()
		local name = Cfg.selected
		if name == "default" and not Cfg.exists("default") then notify("config", "the default config is built in") return end
		if confirmBox.Get() and pendingDel ~= name then
			pendingDel = name
			notify("config", 'press delete again to remove "' .. name .. '"')
			task.delay(3, function() if pendingDel == name then pendingDel = nil end end)
			return
		end
		pendingDel = nil
		if Cfg.remove(name) then
			notify("config deleted", '"' .. name .. '" removed')
			Cfg.selected = "default"
			Cfg.savePrefs()
			refresh("default")
		else
			notify("config", "couldn't delete that config")
		end
	end)

	createSection(p, L, 256, W, "new config")
	local nameBox = createTextInput(p, L, 282, W, "config name...")
	createActionButton(p, L, 314, W, "create config", function()
		local n = (nameBox.Text:gsub("[^%w _%-]", ""))
		n = n:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$"):sub(1, 24)
		if n == "" then
			notify("config", "enter a name first")
		elseif string.lower(n) == "default" or Cfg.exists(n) then
			notify("config", '"' .. n .. '" already exists')
		else
			local where = Cfg.write(n)
			Cfg.selected = n
			Cfg.savePrefs()
			refresh(n)
			nameBox.Text = ""
			notify("config created", n .. (where == "memory" and " (this session only)" or ""))
		end
	end, true)

	createSection(p, R, 104, W, "options")
	createCheckbox(p, R, 128, W, "autosave", true, function(v) Cfg.autosave = v end)
	local joinBox = createCheckbox(p, R, 154, W, "load on join", false, function(v) Cfg.loadOnJoin = v Cfg.savePrefs() end)
	confirmBox = createCheckbox(p, R, 180, W, "confirm delete", true)
	createSection(p, R, 206, W, "danger zone")
	createActionButton(p, R, 232, W, "reset all settings", function()
		if Cfg.factory then Cfg.apply(Cfg.factory) end
		notify("reset", "all settings restored")
	end)
	createActionButton(p, R, 264, W, "close ui", function() setShown(false) end)

	function Cfg.sync()
		refresh(Cfg.selected)
		joinBox.Set(Cfg.loadOnJoin and true or false, true)
	end
end

do
	local ov = create("CanvasGroup", { Name = "UnloadDialog", Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, GroupTransparency = 1, Visible = false, ZIndex = 200 }, mainWindow)
	corner(ov, 10)
	local card = createFrame(ov, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(280, 130), BackgroundColor3 = C.panel, ZIndex = 2 })
	corner(card, 8)
	stroke(card, C.accent, 1, 0.5)
	local cardScale = create("UIScale", { Scale = 0.92 }, card)
	local function closeDialog()
		tween(ov, 0.15, { GroupTransparency = 1 })
		task.delay(0.16, function() if not State.unloading then ov.Visible = false end end)
	end

	local backdrop = createButton(ov, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = C.black, BackgroundTransparency = 0.4 })
	backdrop.Activated:Connect(closeDialog)
	createLabel(card, "unload ui?", 14, C.text, FONT_BOLD, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 22), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(248, 18) })
	createLabel(card, "This closes the menu and turns off every feature until you run the script again.", 11, C.sub, FONT, {
		Position = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 38), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(248, 36), TextWrapped = true, TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top,
		TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None, NoTitle = true,
	})
	createActionButton(card, 16, 100, 118, "cancel", closeDialog)
	createActionButton(card, 146, 100, 118, "unload", function()
		if State.unloading then return end
		State.unloading = true

		local FADE = 0.4
		tween(root, FADE, { GroupTransparency = 1 })
		tween(dimFrame, FADE, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
		local function fadeTree(top)
			local list = top:GetDescendants()
			table.insert(list, top)
			for _, o in ipairs(list) do
				local props
				if o:IsA("CanvasGroup") then props = { GroupTransparency = 1 }
				elseif o:IsA("TextLabel") or o:IsA("TextButton") or o:IsA("TextBox") then props = { TextTransparency = 1, BackgroundTransparency = 1 }
				elseif o:IsA("ImageLabel") or o:IsA("ImageButton") then props = { ImageTransparency = 1, BackgroundTransparency = 1 }
				elseif o:IsA("GuiObject") then props = { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }
				elseif o:IsA("UIStroke") then props = { Transparency = 1 } end
				if props then pcall(function() TweenService:Create(o, TweenInfo.new(FADE, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad), props):Play() end) end
			end
		end
		for _, ch in ipairs(gui:GetChildren()) do
			if ch ~= holder and ch ~= dimFrame and ch:IsA("GuiObject") then fadeTree(ch) end
		end
		task.delay(FADE + 0.05, function() gui:Destroy() end)
	end, true)
	function State.askUnload()
		if State.unloading then return end
		ov.Visible = true
		cardScale.Scale = 0.92
		tween(ov, 0.18, { GroupTransparency = 0 })
		tween(cardScale, 0.25, { Scale = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
	end
end

local function buildSettings(p)
	createSection(p, L, 104, W, "interface")
	createCheckbox(p, L, 128, W, "animations", true)
	createCheckbox(p, L, 154, W, "tooltips", true)
	createCheckbox(p, L, 180, W, "watermark", true, function(v) topBarContainer.Visible = v end)
	createCheckbox(p, L, 206, W, "sounds", false, function(v) State.sounds = v if v then State.sfx("on") end end)

	createSection(p, L, 232, W, "window size")
	scaleSlider = createSlider(p, L, 256, W, {
		label = "ui scale", min = 0.5, max = 3, value = view.scale, decimals = 2, suffix = "x",
		onChange = function(v) view.scale = v applyView() end,
	})
	for i, pr in ipairs({ { "S", 1 }, { "M", 1.35 }, { "L", 1.8 }, { "max", "fit" } }) do
		createActionButton(p, L + (i - 1) * 42, 298, 36, pr[1], function()
			view.scale = pr[2] == "fit" and maxScale() or pr[2]
			applyView()
		end)
	end
	createActionButton(p, L, 330, W, "reset position", function() centerView() end)

	createSection(p, R, 104, W, "keybinds")
	colLabel(p, R, 128, "menu key")
	createKeybind(p, R + 124, 128, "RSHFT", function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
			CONFIG.toggleKey = input.KeyCode ~= Enum.KeyCode.Escape and input.KeyCode or nil
		end
	end)
	createCheckbox(p, R, 154, W, "hold to open", false)

	createSection(p, R, 186, W, "about")
	createInfoRow(p, R, 210, W, "version", "2.4.0")
	createInfoRow(p, R, 230, W, "build", "beta")
	viewportValue = createInfoRow(p, R, 250, W, "viewport", "--")
	updateLayout()
	createActionButton(p, R, 286, W, "open phone", function() if State.phone then State.phone.toggle() end end)
	createActionButton(p, R, 318, W, "unload ui", function() State.askUnload() end)
end

do
	local Cfg = { mem = {}, selected = "default", autosave = true, loadOnJoin = false, loading = false }
	State.cfg = Cfg
	local DIR = "NauruHub/configs"
	local SKIP = { config = true, settings = true }

	local function fsReady()
		return writefile ~= nil and readfile ~= nil and isfile ~= nil and makefolder ~= nil and isfolder ~= nil and listfiles ~= nil
	end
	local function ensure()
		pcall(function()
			if not isfolder("NauruHub") then makefolder("NauruHub") end
			if not isfolder(DIR) then makefolder(DIR) end
		end)
	end
	local function path(name) return DIR .. "/" .. name .. ".json" end

	function Cfg.snapshot()
		local values = {}
		for key, r in pairs(State.reg) do
			if not SKIP[r.page] then
				local ok, v = pcall(r.get)
				if ok and v ~= nil then values[key] = v end
			end
		end
		return { theme = currentTheme, values = values }
	end

	function Cfg.apply(data)
		if type(data) ~= "table" then return false end
		Cfg.loading = true
		if data.theme then pcall(setTheme, data.theme, true) end
		for key, v in pairs(data.values or {}) do
			local r = State.reg[key]
			if r and not SKIP[r.page] then pcall(r.set, v) end
		end
		Cfg.loading = false
		return true
	end

	function Cfg.exists(name)
		if Cfg.mem[name] then return true end
		if fsReady() then
			local ok, r = pcall(isfile, path(name))
			return ok and r == true
		end
		return false
	end

	function Cfg.list()
		local names, seen = { "default" }, { default = true }
		if fsReady() then
			pcall(function()
				if isfolder(DIR) then
					for _, f in ipairs(listfiles(DIR)) do
						local n = tostring(f):match("([^/\\]+)%.json$")
						if n and not seen[n] then seen[n] = true table.insert(names, n) end
					end
				end
			end)
		end
		for n in pairs(Cfg.mem) do
			if not seen[n] then seen[n] = true table.insert(names, n) end
		end
		table.sort(names, function(a, b)
			if a == "default" then return b ~= "default" end
			if b == "default" then return false end
			return a:lower() < b:lower()
		end)
		return names
	end

	function Cfg.write(name)
		local data = Cfg.snapshot()
		if fsReady() then
			ensure()
			local ok = pcall(function() writefile(path(name), HttpService:JSONEncode(data)) end)
			if ok then Cfg.mem[name] = nil return "file" end
		end
		Cfg.mem[name] = data
		return "memory"
	end

	function Cfg.read(name)
		if fsReady() then
			local ok, res = pcall(function()
				if isfile(path(name)) then return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(path(name))) end
			end)
			if ok and res then return res end
		end
		if Cfg.mem[name] then return Cfg.mem[name] end
		if name == "default" then return Cfg.factory end
	end

	function Cfg.remove(name)
		Cfg.mem[name] = nil
		if fsReady() then
			pcall(function() if isfile(path(name)) and delfile then delfile(path(name)) end end)
		end
		return not Cfg.exists(name)
	end

	function Cfg.savePrefs()
		if not fsReady() then return end
		ensure()
		pcall(function() writefile("NauruHub/cfgprefs.json", HttpService:JSONEncode({ last = Cfg.selected, loadOnJoin = Cfg.loadOnJoin })) end)
	end

	function Cfg.boot()
		Cfg.factory = Cfg.snapshot()
		pcall(function()
			if fsReady() and isfile("NauruHub/cfgprefs.json") then
				local prefs = HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile("NauruHub/cfgprefs.json"))
				if type(prefs.last) == "string" then Cfg.selected = prefs.last end
				Cfg.loadOnJoin = prefs.loadOnJoin == true
			end
		end)
		if not Cfg.exists(Cfg.selected) and Cfg.selected ~= "default" then Cfg.selected = "default" end
		if Cfg.loadOnJoin then
			local data = Cfg.read(Cfg.selected)
			if data then Cfg.apply(data) end
		end
		if Cfg.sync then Cfg.sync() end
	end

	function State.touch()
		if Cfg.loading or not Cfg.autosave then return end
		Cfg.stamp = (Cfg.stamp or 0) + 1
		local stamp = Cfg.stamp
		task.delay(1.5, function()
			if Cfg.stamp == stamp and Cfg.autosave and not Cfg.loading then Cfg.write(Cfg.selected) end
		end)
	end
end

buildMain(createPage("main"))
buildVisuals(createPage("visuals"))
buildEffects(createPage("effects"))
buildPlayer(createPage("player"))
buildConfig(createPage("config"))
buildThemes(createPage("themes"))
buildSettings(createPage("settings"))

State.extOk, State.extErr = pcall(function()
	local CW, CH, GAP = State.EXT.CW, State.EXT.CH, State.EXT.GAP
	State.extH = CH + GAP
	local function nt(o) o:SetAttribute("NoTranslate", true) return o end
	local function noth(o) o:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true) return o end

	local apPanel, apC = State.apPanel, State.apC
	local plPanel, plC = State.plPanel, State.plC

	local function header(panel, icon, title, key)

		local clip = createFrame(panel, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22), ClipsDescendants = true, ZIndex = 1 })
		local g = State.headGlow(clip, 1)
		g.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40)
		corner(g, 10)
		local ic = newIcon(panel, 12, icon)
		ic.Holder.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 11)
		ic.tint(C.accent)
		createLabel(panel, title, 10, C.sub, FONT_BOLD, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -110, 0, 22) })
		createFrame(panel, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 22), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1), BackgroundColor3 = C.divider })
		if key then
			State.dismissBtn(panel, key).Position = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 3)
			State.pinBtn(panel, key).Position = UDim2.new(1, -30, 0, 3)
		end
	end

	header(apPanel, Icons.palette, "appearance", "appearance")
	local AP = { tab = nil, pages = {}, segs = {}, swatches = {}, sst = {} }
	local TABS = { "theme", "accent", "font" }
	local PY = 50

	local function mini(parent, x, y, w, text, onClick, filled)
		local b = createButton(parent, {
			Sound = true, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, 20),
			BackgroundColor3 = filled and C.accent or C.field, BackgroundTransparency = 0, Text = text, TextSize = 10, Font = FONT_BOLD,
			TextColor3 = filled and C.bg or C.text,
		})
		corner(b, 5)
		local s = stroke(b, filled and C.accent or C.line, 1)
		State.fitText(b, w, 10, 7, FONT_BOLD)
		local sc = create("UIScale", {}, b)
		b.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
			tween(b, 0.1, { BackgroundColor3 = filled and C.accentHover or C.hoverField })
			if not filled then tween(s, 0.1, { Color = C.accent }) end
		end)
		b.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
			tween(b, 0.1, { BackgroundColor3 = filled and C.accent or C.field })
			tween(s, 0.1, { Color = filled and C.accent or C.line })
			tween(sc, 0.1, { Scale = 1 })
		end)
		b.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function() tween(sc, 0.07, { Scale = 0.95 }) end)
		b.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function() tween(sc, 0.14, { Scale = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back) end)
		b.Activated:Connect(function() if onClick then onClick() end end)
		return b
	end

	for i, name in ipairs(TABS) do
		local b = createButton(apPanel, {
			Sound = true, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14 + (i - 1) * ((CW - 28 - 8) / 3 + 4), 28), Size = UDim2.fromOffset((CW - 28 - 8) / 3, 20),
			BackgroundColor3 = C.accent, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = name, TextSize = 10, Font = FONT_BOLD, TextColor3 = C.sub,
		})
		corner(b, 6)
		AP.sst[name] = stroke(b, C.accent, 1, 1)
		AP.segs[name] = b
		b.MouseEnter:Connect(function() if AP.tab ~= name then tween(b, 0.1, { TextColor3 = C.text }) end end)
		b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() if AP.tab ~= name then tween(b, 0.1, { TextColor3 = C.sub }) end end)
		b.Activated:Connect(function() AP.show(name) end)
	end
	local function mkPage(name)
		local p = create("CanvasGroup", {
			Name = "ap_" .. name, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, PY), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(CW, CH - PY),
			BackgroundTransparency = 1, GroupTransparency = 1, Visible = false,
		}, apPanel)
		AP.pages[name] = p
		return p
	end
	local pgTheme, pgAccent, pgFont = mkPage("theme"), mkPage("accent"), mkPage("font")
	function AP.popTheme()
		for i, sc in ipairs(AP.swatches) do
			sc.Scale = 0.55
			task.delay(0.022 * i, function() tween(sc, 0.38, { Scale = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back) end)
		end
	end
	function AP.show(name, instant)
		if AP.tab == name then return end
		local oldName, new = AP.tab, AP.pages[name]
		AP.tab = name
		for _, n in ipairs(TABS) do
			local on, t = n == name, instant and 0 or 0.15
			tween(AP.segs[n], t, { TextColor3 = on and C.accent or C.sub, BackgroundTransparency = on and 0.84 or 1 })
			tween(AP.sst[n], t, { Transparency = on and 0.55 or 1 })
		end
		local old = oldName and AP.pages[oldName]
		if old then
			tween(old, 0.12, { GroupTransparency = 1 })
			task.delay(0.13, function() if AP.tab ~= oldName then old.Visible = false end end)
		end
		new.Visible = true
		new.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(instant and 0 or 10, PY)
		new.GroupTransparency = instant and 0 or 1
		tween(new, instant and 0 or 0.24, { GroupTransparency = 0, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, PY) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
		if name == "theme" and not instant then AP.popTheme() end
	end

	local base, baseName, shownTheme, moveRing, pickFrom = nil, "royal", currentTheme, nil, nil
	local function findTheme(name)
		for _, t in ipairs(THEMES) do if t.name == name then return t end end
	end

	local function customTheme(acc)
		if not base then
			if currentTheme ~= "custom" then baseName = currentTheme end
			local src = findTheme(currentTheme) or findTheme("ember")
			base = {}
			for _, k in ipairs({ "bg", "panel", "field", "line", "text", "sub", "dim", "red" }) do base[k] = src[k] end
		end
		local t = findTheme("custom")
		if not t then t = { name = "custom" } table.insert(THEMES, t) end
		for k, v in pairs(base) do t[k] = v end
		t.accent = acc
		for k, f in pairs(DERIVE) do t[k] = f(t) end
		return t
	end
	local function saveAccent()
		pcall(function()
			if not writefile then return end
			local t = findTheme("custom")
			if not t then return end
			if not isfolder("NauruHub") then makefolder("NauruHub") end
			local parts = {}
			for _, k in ipairs({ "bg", "panel", "field", "line", "text", "sub", "dim", "red", "accent" }) do
				local c = t[k]
				parts[#parts + 1] = string.format("%s=%d,%d,%d", k, math.floor(c.R * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(c.G * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(c.B * 255 + 0.5))
			end
			writefile("NauruHub/accent.txt", table.concat(parts, ";"))
			writefile("NauruHub/theme_v2.txt", "custom")
		end)
	end
	local function adopt(name)
		base = nil
		shownTheme = name
		moveRing(name)
		setTheme(name)
		if pickFrom then pickFrom(C.accent) end
	end

	local DOT, DG, COLS, GY = 20, 4, 8, 55
	local function slot(i)
		local col, row = (i - 1) % COLS, (i - 1) // COLS
		return UDim2.fromOffset(14 + col * (DOT + DG) + DOT / 2, GY + row * (DOT + DG) + DOT / 2)
	end
	local centers, n = {}, 0
	for _, t in ipairs(THEMES) do
		if t.name ~= "custom" then n += 1 centers[t.name] = slot(n) end
	end
	centers.custom = slot(n + 1)
	local dotRows = math.ceil((n + 1) / COLS)

	local dotSf = create("ScrollingFrame", { Name = "dots", Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, GY - 4), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(CW, CH - PY - GY + 4), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0,
		ScrollBarThickness = 2, ScrollBarImageColor3 = C.accent, CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0, dotRows * (DOT + DG) + 8), ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y, ElasticBehavior = Enum.ElasticBehavior.Never }, pgTheme)
	for k, v in pairs(centers) do centers[k] = v - UDim2.fromOffset(0, GY - 4) end
	local pgThemeDots = pgTheme
	local function hexOf(c) return string.format("#%02X%02X%02X", math.floor(c.R * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(c.G * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(c.B * 255 + 0.5)) end

	local NAMEF = FONT_BOLD
	pcall(function() NAMEF = Enum.Font.FredokaOne end)
	local thName = noth(nt(createLabel(pgTheme, "", 22, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), NAMEF, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 13), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(128, 26), NoTitle = true })))
	thName.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None
	thName:SetAttribute("NoFont", true)
	thName:SetAttribute("DynFont", true)
	local nameGrad = noth(create("UIGradient", { Rotation = 0 }, thName))
	local thSub = nt(createLabel(pgTheme, "", 10, C.sub, FONT, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(15, 27), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(128, 11), NoTitle = true }))
	local chipKeys = { "bg", "field", "line", "text", "accent", "red" }
	local chips = {}
	for i = 1, #chipKeys do
		local f = noth(createFrame(pgTheme, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(15 + (i - 1) * 17, 40), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 7), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent }))
		corner(f, 3)
		stroke(f, C.line, 1)
		chips[i] = f
	end

	local THEME_FONTS = {
		ember = "Bangers", ocean = "JosefinSans", forest = "Kalam", amethyst = "Antique", rose = "Fondamento",
		mono = "RobotoMono", crimson = "LuckiestGuy", sunset = "PermanentMarker", mint = "Nunito", midnight = "Michroma",
		halloween = "Creepster", christmas = "Merriweather", haunted = "GrenzeGotisch", ["northern lights"] = "Jura",
		desert = "SpecialElite", custom = "FredokaOne",
		sakura = "Fondamento", cyberpunk = "SciFi", lava = "LuckiestGuy", ice = "JosefinSans", slate = "RobotoCondensed", gold = "Merriweather",
		toxic = "SpecialElite", coffee = "Kalam", lavender = "Nunito", neon = "Michroma", aurora = "Jura", vaporwave = "Michroma", sunrise = "PermanentMarker",
		["deep sea"] = "JosefinSans", nebula = "Antique", inferno = "Bangers", candy = "FredokaOne", matrix = "Code", twilight = "Fondamento",
		peach = "Nunito", galaxy = "SciFi", royal = "Merriweather",
	}
	local function themeFont(name)
		local f
		pcall(function() f = Enum.Font[THEME_FONTS[name] or "FredokaOne"] end)
		return f or NAMEF
	end
	local function preview(name)
		local t = findTheme(name)
		if not t then return end
		thName.Font = themeFont(name)
		thName.Text = State.titleCase(name)

		local fit = 13
		pcall(function()
			local TS = game:GetService("TextService")
			for sz = 26, 12, -1 do
				local b = TS:GetTextSize(thName.Text, sz, thName.Font, Vector2.new(10000, 1000))
				if b.X <= 126 and b.Y <= 26 then fit = sz break end
				fit = 12
			end
		end)
		thName.TextSize = fit

		nameGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, t.accent),
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.4, t.red),
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.75, t.accentHover or t.accent),
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, t.text),
		})
		thSub.Text = "Accent  " .. hexOf(t.accent)
		for i, k in ipairs(chipKeys) do chips[i].BackgroundColor3 = t[k] end
	end
	table.insert(C.hooks, function() preview(shownTheme) end)

	local ring = noth(createFrame(dotSf, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(DOT + 6, DOT + 6), Position = centers[currentTheme] or centers.ember, ZIndex = 8 }))
	corner(ring, 999)
	stroke(ring, C.accent, 1.5, 0)
	function moveRing(name, instant)
		preview(name)
		local pos = centers[name]
		ring.Visible = pos ~= nil
		if not pos then return end
		pcall(function()
			local y = pos.Y.Offset - 22
			local maxY = math.max(0, dotSf.CanvasSize.Y.Offset - dotSf.AbsoluteWindowSize.Y / math.max(responsiveScale.Scale, 0.01))
			tween(dotSf, 0.3, { CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, math.clamp(y, 0, maxY)) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
		end)
		if instant then ring.Position = pos else tween(ring, 0.3, { Position = pos }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint) end
	end
	local si = 0
	for _, t in ipairs(THEMES) do
		if t.name ~= "custom" then
			si += 1
			local b = noth(createButton(dotSf, {
				AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = centers[t.name], Size = UDim2.fromOffset(DOT, DOT),
				BackgroundColor3 = t.grad and Color3.new(1, 1, 1) or t.panel, BackgroundTransparency = 0, Sound = true, ZIndex = 3,
			}))
			corner(b, 999)
			if t.grad then
				local kps = {}
				for gi, gc in ipairs(t.grad) do table.insert(kps, ColorSequenceKeypoint.new((gi - 1) / math.max(#t.grad - 1, 1), gc)) end
				noth(create("UIGradient", { Color = ColorSequence.new(kps), Rotation = t.grad.rot or 35 }, b))
			end
			noth(stroke(b, t.line, 1))
			local core = noth(createFrame(b, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 10), BackgroundColor3 = t.accent, ZIndex = 4 }))
			corner(core, 999)
			local sc = create("UIScale", {}, b)
			AP.swatches[si] = sc
			b.MouseEnter:Connect(function() tween(sc, 0.15, { Scale = 1.15 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back) preview(t.name) end)
			b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tween(sc, 0.15, { Scale = 1 }) preview(shownTheme) end)
			b.Activated:Connect(function() adopt(t.name) end)
		end
	end

	do
		local b = noth(createButton(dotSf, {
			AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = centers.custom, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(DOT, DOT),
			BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 0, Sound = true, ZIndex = 3,
		}))
		corner(b, 999)
		local rk = {}
		for i = 0, 6 do table.insert(rk, ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(i / 6, Color3.fromHSV((i / 6) % 1, 0.85, 1))) end
		noth(create("UIGradient", { Color = ColorSequence.new(rk), Rotation = 45 }, b))
		local sc = create("UIScale", {}, b)
		AP.swatches[si + 1] = sc
		b.MouseEnter:Connect(function() tween(sc, 0.15, { Scale = 1.15 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back) end)
		b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tween(sc, 0.15, { Scale = 1 }) end)
		b.Activated:Connect(function() AP.show("accent") end)
	end
	mini(pgTheme, CW - 14 - 56, 18, 56, "random", function()
		local pick
		for _ = 1, 8 do
			pick = THEMES[math.random(#THEMES)]
			if pick.name ~= currentTheme and pick.name ~= "custom" then break end
		end
		adopt(pick.name)
	end)
	preview(currentTheme)

	local PK = { h = 0, s = 1, v = 1 }
	PK.h, PK.s, PK.v = C.accent:ToHSV()
	local sv = noth(createFrame(pgAccent, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 2), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(187, 34), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromHSV(PK.h, 1, 1) }))
	corner(sv, 6)
	local wh = noth(createFrame(sv, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = C.white }))
	corner(wh, 6)
	noth(create("UIGradient", { Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0, 1) }, wh))
	local dk = noth(createFrame(sv, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = C.black }))
	corner(dk, 6)
	noth(create("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Transparency = NumberSequence.new(1, 0) }, dk))
	local marker = noth(createFrame(sv, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 10), ZIndex = 3 }))
	corner(marker, 999)
	noth(stroke(marker, C.white, 1.5))
	local hue = noth(createFrame(pgAccent, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 42), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(187, 10), BackgroundColor3 = C.white }))
	corner(hue, 5)
	local hkeys = {}
	for i = 0, 6 do table.insert(hkeys, ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(i / 6, Color3.fromHSV((i / 6) % 1, 1, 1))) end
	noth(create("UIGradient", { Color = ColorSequence.new(hkeys) }, hue))
	local hueMark = noth(createFrame(hue, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 13), BackgroundColor3 = C.white, ZIndex = 3 }))
	corner(hueMark, 2)
	local pickDot = noth(createFrame(pgAccent, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 60), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 12), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent }))
	corner(pickDot, 999)
	noth(stroke(pickDot, C.white, 1, 0.7))
	local hexL = nt(createLabel(pgAccent, "", 10, C.text, FONT_BOLD, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(32, 59), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(90, 14), NoTitle = true }))

	local function pickerVisual()
		local c = Color3.fromHSV(PK.h, PK.s, PK.v)
		sv.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromHSV(PK.h, 1, 1)
		marker.Position = UDim2.fromScale(PK.s, 1 - PK.v)
		hueMark.Position = UDim2.fromScale(PK.h, 0.5)
		pickDot.BackgroundColor3 = c
		hexL.Text = string.format("#%02X%02X%02X", math.floor(c.R * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(c.G * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(c.B * 255 + 0.5))
	end
	function pickFrom(color)
		PK.h, PK.s, PK.v = color:ToHSV()
		pickerVisual()
	end
	local pend, saveTok = false, 0
	local function applyCustom()
		customTheme(Color3.fromHSV(PK.h, PK.s, PK.v))
		shownTheme = "custom"
		setTheme("custom", true)
		moveRing("custom")
	end
	local function changed()
		pickerVisual()
		if not pend then
			pend = true
			task.delay(0.08, function() pend = false applyCustom() end)
		end
		saveTok += 1
		local my = saveTok
		task.delay(0.7, function() if my == saveTok then saveAccent() end end)
	end
	bindDrag(createButton(sv, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 6 }), function(rx, ry) PK.s, PK.v = rx, 1 - ry changed() end)
	bindDrag(createButton(hue, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 6 }), function(rx) PK.h = math.min(rx, 0.999) changed() end)
	local PRE = { { 255, 117, 24 }, { 56, 160, 255 }, { 64, 216, 120 }, { 168, 100, 255 }, { 255, 92, 140 }, { 230, 40, 50 }, { 236, 190, 92 }, { 60, 230, 200 } }
	for i, c in ipairs(PRE) do
		local col = Color3.fromRGB(c[1], c[2], c[3])
		local b = noth(createButton(pgAccent, {
			Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14 + math.floor((i - 1) * 167 / 7), 84), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 16),
			BackgroundColor3 = col, BackgroundTransparency = 0, Sound = true, ZIndex = 3,
		}))
		corner(b, 4)
		noth(stroke(b, C.white, 1, 0.8))
		local sc = create("UIScale", {}, b)
		b.MouseEnter:Connect(function() tween(sc, 0.12, { Scale = 1.15 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back) end)
		b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tween(sc, 0.12, { Scale = 1 }) end)
		b.Activated:Connect(function() PK.h, PK.s, PK.v = col:ToHSV() changed() end)
	end
	mini(pgAccent, CW - 14 - 60, 57, 60, "reset", function() adopt(baseName) end)
	pickerVisual()

	task.spawn(function()
		while gui.Parent do
			if currentTheme ~= shownTheme then
				shownTheme = currentTheme
				if shownTheme ~= "custom" then base = nil end
				moveRing(shownTheme)
				pickFrom(C.accent)
			end
			task.wait(0.25)
		end
	end)

	local function F(n) local ok, v = pcall(function() return Enum.Font[n] end) return ok and v or nil end
	local FAMS = {}
	for _, d in ipairs({
		{ "Gotham", "GothamMedium", "GothamBold" }, { "Source Sans", "SourceSans", "SourceSansBold" }, { "Arial", "Arial", "ArialBold" },
		{ "Nunito", "Nunito", "Nunito" }, { "Ubuntu", "Ubuntu", "Ubuntu" }, { "Roboto", "Roboto", "Roboto" },
		{ "Roboto Mono", "RobotoMono", "RobotoMono" }, { "Roboto Cond.", "RobotoCondensed", "RobotoCondensed" }, { "Oswald", "Oswald", "Oswald" },
		{ "Titillium", "TitilliumWeb", "TitilliumWeb" }, { "Jura", "Jura", "Jura" }, { "Josefin Sans", "JosefinSans", "JosefinSans" },
		{ "Merriweather", "Merriweather", "Merriweather" }, { "Sarpanch", "Sarpanch", "Sarpanch" }, { "Kalam", "Kalam", "Kalam" },
		{ "Highway", "Highway", "Highway" }, { "Code", "Code", "Code" }, { "Sci-Fi", "SciFi", "SciFi" },
	}) do
		local a, b = F(d[2]), F(d[3])
		if a and b then table.insert(FAMS, { d[1], a, b }) end
	end
	local fontCur, boldAll = 1, false
	local origFont = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })

	local function setFace(inst, enumF)
		if boldAll then
			local ok = pcall(function()
				local ff = Font.fromEnum(enumF)
				inst.FontFace = Font.new(ff.Family, Enum.FontWeight.Bold, ff.Style)
			end)
			if ok then return end
		end
		inst.Font = enumF
	end
	local function mapFont(inst)
		if not (inst:IsA("TextLabel") or inst:IsA("TextButton") or inst:IsA("TextBox")) or inst:GetAttribute("NoFont") then return end
		local o = origFont[inst]
		if not o then
			local f = inst.Font
			if f ~= Enum.Font.GothamMedium and f ~= Enum.Font.GothamBold then return end
			origFont[inst], o = f, f
		end
		local fam = FAMS[fontCur]
		setFace(inst, (o == Enum.Font.GothamBold or boldAll) and fam[3] or fam[2])
	end
	local function nf(o) o:SetAttribute("NoFont", true) o:SetAttribute("NoTranslate", true) return o end

	local prevBox = createFrame(pgFont, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 2), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(187, 32), BackgroundColor3 = C.field })
	corner(prevBox, 6)

	local prevSmall = nf(createLabel(prevBox, "Aa Bb Cc 0123456789", 10, C.sub, FONT, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 18), Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 13), TextXAlignment = CEN, NoTitle = true }))

	local function arrow(x, text)
		local b = createButton(pgFont, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, 36), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(24, 22), BackgroundColor3 = C.field, BackgroundTransparency = 0, Sound = true, Text = text, TextColor3 = C.sub, Font = FONT_BOLD, TextSize = 12 })
		b:SetAttribute("NoTranslate", true)
		corner(b, 5)
		local st = stroke(b, C.line, 1)
		local sc = create("UIScale", {}, b)
		b.MouseEnter:Connect(function() tween(st, 0.1, { Color = C.accent }) tween(b, 0.1, { TextColor3 = C.accent }) end)
		b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tween(st, 0.1, { Color = C.line }) tween(b, 0.1, { TextColor3 = C.sub }) tween(sc, 0.1, { Scale = 1 }) end)
		b.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function() tween(sc, 0.08, { Scale = 0.9 }) end)
		b.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function() tween(sc, 0.12, { Scale = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back) end)
		return b
	end
	local prevB, nextB = arrow(14, "<"), arrow(CW - 14 - 24, ">")
	local fieldB = createButton(pgFont, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(44, 36), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(CW - 88, 22), BackgroundColor3 = C.field, BackgroundTransparency = 0, Sound = true })

	local fontNum = nf(createLabel(fieldB, "", 9, C.dim, FONT_BOLD, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, -7, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(34, 22), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, NoTitle = true }))

	local function applyFont(i, silent)
		fontCur = ((i - 1) % #FAMS) + 1
		local fam = FAMS[fontCur]
		for _, d in ipairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do mapFont(d) end
		setFace(prevBig, fam[3])
		setFace(prevSmall, boldAll and fam[3] or fam[2])
		fontLbl.Font = fam[3]
		fontLbl.Text = fam[1]
		fontNum.Text = fontCur .. "/" .. #FAMS
		if not silent then
			pcall(function()
				if writefile then
					if not isfolder("NauruHub") then makefolder("NauruHub") end
					writefile("NauruHub/font.txt", fam[1] .. "|" .. (boldAll and "1" or "0"))
				end
			end)
		end
	end
	gui.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d) if fontCur ~= 1 or boldAll then mapFont(d) end end)
	prevB.Activated:Connect(function() applyFont(fontCur - 1) end)
	nextB.Activated:Connect(function() applyFont(fontCur + 1) end)
	fieldB.Activated:Connect(function() applyFont(fontCur + 1) end)
	local boldCb = createCheckbox(pgFont, 14, 93, 187, "bold everywhere", false, function(v)
		boldAll = v
		applyFont(fontCur)
		notify("font", v and "bold everywhere: on" or "bold everywhere: off")
	end)
	mini(pgFont, 14, 60, 90, "random", function() applyFont(math.random(#FAMS)) end)
	mini(pgFont, 111, 60, 90, "default", function()
		boldAll = false
		boldCb.Set(false, true)
		applyFont(1)
	end)
	applyFont(1, true)
	pcall(function()
		if isfile and isfile("NauruHub/font.txt") then
			local raw = readfile("NauruHub/font.txt")
			local name = raw:match("^[^|]*"):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
			boldAll = raw:match("|%s*(%d)") == "1"
			boldCb.Set(boldAll, true)
			local found = 1
			for i, fam in ipairs(FAMS) do if fam[1] == name then found = i end end
			if found ~= 1 or boldAll then applyFont(found, true) end
		end
	end)

	pcall(function()
		if isfile and isfile("NauruHub/accent.txt") then
			local t = { name = "custom" }
			for k, r, g, b in readfile("NauruHub/accent.txt"):gmatch("(%a+)=(%d+),(%d+),(%d+)") do
				t[k] = Color3.fromRGB(tonumber(r), tonumber(g), tonumber(b))
			end
			if t.accent and t.bg and t.panel and t.field and t.line and t.text and t.sub and t.dim and t.red and not findTheme("custom") then
				for k, f in pairs(DERIVE) do t[k] = f(t) end
				table.insert(THEMES, t)
			end
		end
	end)
	AP.show("theme", true)

		ScrollBarImageColor3 = C.accent, CanvasSize = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
		ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y, ElasticBehavior = Enum.ElasticBehavior.Never,
	}, plPanel)

	create("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 2), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, plScroll)

	local function thumb(p) return "rbxthumb://type=AvatarHeadShot&id=" .. p.UserId .. "&w=48&h=48" end
	local function char(p) return p and p.Character end
	local function root(p) local c = char(p) return c and (c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or c:FindFirstChild("Torso")) end
	local function hum(p) local c = char(p) return c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") end

	local rows, selected, popOpen = {}, nil, false

	local VP = { on = false, yaw = 0.4, drag = false, tok = 0 }
	local B = {}

	local PH_BASE = 216
	local pop = create("CanvasGroup", { Name = "PlayerPopup", Size = UDim2.fromOffset(PW, PH), BackgroundColor3 = C.panel, BorderSizePixel = 0, GroupTransparency = 1, Visible = false, ZIndex = 40 }, layoutFrame)
	corner(pop, 10)
	local popStroke = stroke(pop, C.line, 1, 0.35)

	State.popFx(pslide, pop, popStroke, function() return pop:GetChildren() end)
	State.headGlow(pop, 1).Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 44)
	local pAv = create("ImageLabel", { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 7), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(30, 30), BackgroundColor3 = C.field, BorderSizePixel = 0, ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop }, pop)
	corner(pAv, 999)
	stroke(pAv, C.accent, 1.5, 0.2)
	local pName = nt(createLabel(pop, "", 12, C.text, FONT_BOLD, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(48, 6), Size = UDim2.new(1, -84, 0, 16), NoTitle = true }))
	local pSub = nt(createLabel(pop, "", 9, C.sub, FONT, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(48, 22), Size = UDim2.new(1, -84, 0, 13), NoTitle = true }))
	local xb = createButton(pop, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 9), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(22, 18), BackgroundColor3 = C.field, BackgroundTransparency = 1, ZIndex = 3 })
	corner(xb, 4)

	local xBars = {}
	for _, rot in { 45, -45 } do
		local bar = createFrame(xb, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(9, 1.6), Rotation = rot, BackgroundColor3 = C.sub, ZIndex = 4 })
		corner(bar, 1)
		table.insert(xBars, bar)
	end
	xb.MouseEnter:Connect(function() for _, x in ipairs(xBars) do tween(x, 0.1, { BackgroundColor3 = C.red }) end tween(xSt, 0.1, { Color = C.red, Transparency = 0 }) end)
	xb.MouseLeave:Connect(function() for _, x in ipairs(xBars) do tween(x, 0.1, { BackgroundColor3 = C.sub }) end tween(xSt, 0.1, { Color = C.line, Transparency = 1 }) end)
	createFrame(pop, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 44), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1), BackgroundColor3 = C.divider })
	local pInfo = nt(createLabel(pop, "", 10, C.sub, FONT, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 51), Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 14), NoTitle = true }))

	HB.back = nt(createFrame(pop, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 55), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(46, 7), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 22), BorderSizePixel = 0, Visible = false, ZIndex = 3 }))
	corner(HB.back, 3)
	HB.stroke = stroke(HB.back, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), 1)
	HB.stroke.Transparency = 0.55
	HB.fill = nt(createFrame(HB.back, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(90, 220, 90), BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 4 }))
	corner(HB.fill, 3)
	HB.team = nt(createLabel(pop, "", 10, C.sub, FONT, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 51), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(100, 14), NoTitle = true, Visible = false }))

	local function act(i, text, cb, full)
		local col, row = (i - 1) % 2, (i - 1) // 2
		local w = (PW - 24 - 6) / 2
		if full then col, row, w = 0, (i - 1) // 2, PW - 24 end
		local b = createButton(pop, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12 + col * (w + 6), 74 + row * 34), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, 28), BackgroundColor3 = C.field, BackgroundTransparency = 0, Sound = true, ZIndex = 2 })
		corner(b, 6)
		local st = stroke(b, C.line, 1)
		local sc = create("UIScale", {}, b)
		local l = createLabel(b, text, 10, C.text, FONT_BOLD, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, ZIndex = 3 })
		local o = { on = false }
		function o.set(on, txt)
			o.on = on
			l.Text = State.titleCase(txt or text)
			tween(l, 0.12, { TextColor3 = on and C.accent or C.text })
			tween(st, 0.12, { Color = on and C.accent or C.line })
		end
		b.MouseEnter:Connect(function() tween(b, 0.08, { BackgroundColor3 = C.hoverField }) tween(st, 0.1, { Color = C.accent }) end)
		b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tween(b, 0.1, { BackgroundColor3 = C.field }) tween(st, 0.1, { Color = o.on and C.accent or C.line }) tween(sc, 0.1, { Scale = 1 }) end)
		b.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function() tween(sc, 0.08, { Scale = 0.95 }) end)
		b.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function() tween(sc, 0.14, { Scale = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back) end)
		b.Activated:Connect(function() if selected then cb(selected) end end)
		return o
	end

	local function sync()
		local t = selected
		B.view.set(t ~= nil and V.view == t, (t ~= nil and V.view == t) and "stop view" or "view")
		B.add.set(t ~= nil and V.friend[t] == true, (t ~= nil and V.friend[t]) and "friends" or "add player")
		B.hl.set(t ~= nil and V.hlOn[t] == true, (t ~= nil and V.hlOn[t]) and "unhighlight" or "highlight")
		if B.v3 then B.v3.set(VP.on, VP.on and "hide 3d" or "3d view") end
	end
	local function stopView()
		V.view = nil
		local c, h = workspace.CurrentCamera, hum(player)
		if c and h then c.CameraSubject = h end
	end
	local function setHL(p, on)
		local h = V.hl[p]
		if on then
			if h and h.Parent then return end
			local c = p.Character
			if not c then return end
			pcall(function()
				h = Instance.new("Highlight")
				h.Name = "NauruHighlight"
				h.FillColor, h.FillTransparency = C.accent, 0.65
				h.OutlineColor = C.white
				h.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
				h.Parent = c
				V.hl[p] = h
			end)
		elseif h then
			h:Destroy()
			V.hl[p] = nil
		end
	end
	local function copy(text, what)
		local f = setclipboard or toclipboard
		local ok = f and pcall(f, text)
		if ok then notify("copied", what .. ": " .. text) else notify("copy failed", "your executor has no clipboard function") end
	end

	B.tp = act(1, "teleport", function(t)
		local mine, theirs = root(player), root(t)
		if mine and theirs then
			mine.CFrame = theirs.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 3)
			notify("teleport", "to " .. t.DisplayName)
		else
			notify("teleport", t.DisplayName .. " has no character")
		end
	end)
	B.view = act(2, "view", function(t)
		if V.view == t then
			stopView()
			notify("view", "back to your character")
		else
			local h = hum(t)
			local c = workspace.CurrentCamera
			if h and c then V.view = t c.CameraSubject = h notify("view", "watching " .. t.DisplayName) else notify("view", t.DisplayName .. " has no character") end
		end
		sync()
	end)
	B.add = act(3, "add player", function(t)
		if V.friend[t] then notify("add player", "you're already friends with " .. t.DisplayName) return end
		local ok = pcall(function() game:GetService("StarterGui"):SetCore("PromptSendFriendRequest", t) end)
		if ok then notify("add player", "friend request for " .. t.DisplayName) else notify("add player", "couldn't open the friend request") end
	end)
	B.hl = act(4, "highlight", function(t)
		V.hlOn[t] = not V.hlOn[t] or nil
		setHL(t, V.hlOn[t] == true)
		sync()
	end)
	B.id = act(5, "copy id", function(t) copy(tostring(t.UserId), "user id") end)
	B.name = act(6, "copy name", function(t) copy(t.Name, "username") end)

	local function updateInfo()
		local t = selected
		if not t then return end
		local a, b, h = root(player), root(t), hum(t)
		local d = (a and b) and (math.floor((a.Position - b.Position).Magnitude) .. "m away") or "no character"
		local hp = h and ("  ·  " .. math.floor(h.Health) .. " hp") or ""
		pInfo.Text = d .. hp
		local x = 12
		pcall(function() x = 12 + game:GetService("TextService"):GetTextSize(pInfo.Text, pInfo.TextSize, pInfo.Font, Vector2.new(10000, 100)).X end)
		if h then
			local frac = h.MaxHealth > 0 and math.clamp(h.Health / h.MaxHealth, 0, 1) or 0
			local col = frac > 0.5 and Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 60):Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(90, 220, 90), (frac - 0.5) * 2) or Color3.fromRGB(235, 60, 60):Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 60), frac * 2)
			HB.back.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x + 6, 55)
			HB.back.Visible = true
			HB.fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(frac, 1)
			HB.fill.BackgroundColor3 = col
			x = x + 6 + 46
		else
			HB.back.Visible = false
		end
		if t.Team then
			HB.team.Text = "  ·  " .. t.Team.Name
			HB.team.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, 51)
			HB.team.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(20, PW - 12 - x), 14)
			HB.team.Visible = true
		else
			HB.team.Visible = false
		end
	end

	local VPY, VPH = 212, 150
	local vpf = create("ViewportFrame", {
		Name = "Preview3D", Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, VPY), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(PW - 24, VPH), BackgroundColor3 = C.field, BackgroundTransparency = 0,
		BorderSizePixel = 0, Visible = false, ZIndex = 2, Active = true, Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(190, 190, 195), LightColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 250, 245),
		LightDirection = Vector3.new(-0.5, -1, 0.6),
	}, pop)
	corner(vpf, 8)
	stroke(vpf, C.line, 1)
	local vcam = Instance.new("Camera")
	vcam.FieldOfView = 35
	vcam.Parent = vpf
	vpf.CurrentCamera = vcam
	local vpMsg = nt(createLabel(pop, "", 10, C.sub, FONT, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, VPY), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(PW - 24, VPH), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, ZIndex = 4, NoTitle = true }))
	vpMsg.Visible = false

	local function clearModel()
		VP.tok += 1
		if VP.conn then VP.conn:Disconnect() VP.conn = nil end
		if VP.model then VP.model:Destroy() end
		VP.model, VP.pairs, VP.root, VP.src = nil, nil, nil, nil
	end

	local DROP = { "BaseScript", "Sound", "Highlight", "BillboardGui", "ForceField", "Fire", "Smoke", "Sparkles", "ParticleEmitter", "Beam", "Trail" }
	local function buildClone(src)
		local rootO = src:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or src.PrimaryPart
		if not rootO then return end
		local ok, m = pcall(function()
			local was = src.Archivable
			src.Archivable = true
			local cl = src:Clone()
			src.Archivable = was
			return cl
		end)
		if not ok or not m then return end
		local a, b = src:GetDescendants(), m:GetDescendants()
		local list
		if #a == #b then
			list = {}
			for i = 1, #a do
				if a[i]:IsA("BasePart") and b[i]:IsA("BasePart") and a[i].Name == b[i].Name then list[#list + 1] = { a[i], b[i] } end
			end
		end
		for _, d in ipairs(b) do
			for _, cls in ipairs(DROP) do
				if d:IsA(cls) then d:Destroy() break end
			end
		end
		return m, list, rootO
	end

	local function frame(dt)
		local t = selected
		if not t or not popOpen or not VP.on or not VP.model then return end
		if VP.src then
			if t.Character ~= VP.src or not VP.root.Parent then VP.loadModel(t) return end
		elseif t.Character and t.Character ~= VP.failed then
			VP.loadModel(t) return
		end
		if VP.pairs then
			local inv = VP.root.CFrame:Inverse()
			for i = 1, #VP.pairs do
				local pr = VP.pairs[i]
				if pr[1].Parent and pr[2].Parent then pr[2].CFrame = inv * pr[1].CFrame end
			end
		end
		if not VP.drag then VP.yaw += dt * 0.7 end
		local dir = Vector3.new(math.sin(VP.yaw), 0.12, -math.cos(VP.yaw))
		vcam.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(VP.center + dir * VP.dist, VP.center)
	end

	local function attach(m, list, rootO, srcChar)
		for _, d in ipairs(m:GetDescendants()) do
			if d:IsA("BasePart") then d.Anchored = true end
		end
		local h = m:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if h then h.DisplayDistanceType = Enum.HumanoidDisplayDistanceType.None end
		if list and rootO then
			local inv = rootO.CFrame:Inverse()
			for _, pr in ipairs(list) do pr[2].CFrame = inv * pr[1].CFrame end
		else
			m:PivotTo(CFrame.new())
		end
		m.Parent = vpf
		local cf, size = m:GetBoundingBox()
		VP.center = cf.Position
		VP.dist = math.max(size.Y * 0.5 / math.tan(math.rad(vcam.FieldOfView / 2)) * 1.2, size.X * 1.6, 6)
		VP.model, VP.pairs, VP.root, VP.src = m, list, rootO, (list and srcChar or nil)
		vpMsg.Visible = false
		if VP.conn then VP.conn:Disconnect() end
		VP.conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(frame)
	end

	function VP.loadModel(p)
		clearModel()
		VP.failed = nil
		if not p or not VP.on then return end
		local tok = VP.tok
		vpMsg.Text = "loading..."
		vpMsg.Visible = true
		local c = p.Character
		if c then
			local m, list, rootO = buildClone(c)
			if m then attach(m, list, rootO, c) return end
			VP.failed = c
		else
			vpMsg.Text = "no character"
		end
		task.spawn(function()
			local ok, m = pcall(function() return Players:CreateHumanoidModelFromUserId(p.UserId) end)
			if VP.tok ~= tok or selected ~= p or not VP.on then
				if ok and m then m:Destroy() end
				return
			end
			if ok and m then attach(m, nil, nil, nil) else vpMsg.Text = "preview unavailable" end
		end)
	end

	local function applyVP()
		PH = VP.on and (VPY + VPH + 12) or PH_BASE
		if popOpen then State.plPlace(0.3) end
		if VP.on then
			vpf.Visible = true
			vpf.BackgroundTransparency, vpf.ImageTransparency = 1, 1
			tween(vpf, 0.3, { BackgroundTransparency = 0, ImageTransparency = 0 })
			VP.loadModel(selected)
		else
			vpf.Visible, vpMsg.Visible = false, false
			clearModel()
		end
		sync()
	end
	B.v3 = act(7, "3d view", function() VP.on = not VP.on applyVP() end, true)

	vpf.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if isPress(input) then VP.drag, VP.lx = true, input.Position.X end
	end)
	UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if VP.drag and isMove(input) then
			local x = input.Position.X
			VP.yaw -= (x - VP.lx) * 0.012
			VP.lx = x
		end
	end)
	UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input) if isPress(input) then VP.drag = false end end)

	State.pinPop.players[1] = pslide.slot
	function State.plPlace(dur)
		if State.dock and State.dock.players.pinned then pslide.set(CW - PW, -6 - PH, PW, PH, false, dur) return end
		local p = plC.Position
		pslide.set(p.X.Offset + CW - PW, p.Y.Offset - 6 - PH, PW, PH, false, dur)
	end

	local function restyle()
		for p, r in pairs(rows) do
			local on = selected == p
			tween(r.btn, 0.15, { BackgroundTransparency = on and 0.8 or 1 })
			tween(r.bar, 0.18, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, on and 18 or 0) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
			tween(r.name, 0.12, { TextColor3 = on and C.accent or C.text })
			tween(r.avStroke, 0.12, { Color = on and C.accent or C.line })
		end
	end

	local function closePop()
		if not popOpen then return end
		popOpen, selected = false, nil
		clearModel()
		restyle()
		pslide.close()
	end
	xb.Activated:Connect(closePop)
	State.plClose = closePop

	local function selectPlayer(p)
		if p == player then notify("players", "that's you") return end
		if selected == p and popOpen then closePop() return end
		selected = p
		pAv.Image = thumb(p)
		pName.Text = p.DisplayName
		pSub.Text = "@" .. p.Name .. "  ·  " .. p.UserId
		V.friend[p] = nil
		task.spawn(function()
			local ok, f = pcall(function() return player:IsFriendsWith(p.UserId) end)
			if selected == p then V.friend[p] = ok and f == true or false sync() end
		end)
		sync()
		restyle()
		updateInfo()
		if VP.on then VP.loadModel(p) end
		if popOpen then
			if pslide.fx then pslide.fx() end
		else
			popOpen = true
			State.plPlace()
			pslide.show()
		end
	end

	local function addRow(p)
		local isMe = p == player
		local btn = createButton(plScroll, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Sound = not isMe })
		corner(btn, 6)
		create("UIGradient", { Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0, 0.7) }, btn)
		local bar = createFrame(btn, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 0), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent })
		corner(bar, 2)
		local av = create("ImageLabel", { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(22, 22), BackgroundColor3 = C.field, BorderSizePixel = 0, Image = thumb(p), ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop }, btn)
		corner(av, 999)
		local avStroke = stroke(av, C.line, 1, 0.3)
		local nameL = nt(createLabel(btn, p.DisplayName, 10, C.text, FONT_BOLD, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(34, 3), Size = UDim2.new(1, -84, 0, 14), NoTitle = true }))
		nt(createLabel(btn, "@" .. p.Name, 9, C.sub, FONT, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(34, 17), Size = UDim2.new(1, -84, 0, 12), NoTitle = true }))
		local distL = nt(createLabel(btn, isMe and "you" or "", 9, isMe and C.accent or C.dim, FONT_BOLD, {
			AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(44, 14), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, NoTitle = true,
		}))
		btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
			tween(btn, 0.08, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.6 })
			tween(bar, 0.18, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 18) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
			tween(avStroke, 0.12, { Color = C.accent, Transparency = 0.1 })
		end)
		btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
			local on = selected == p
			tween(btn, 0.1, { BackgroundTransparency = on and 0.8 or 1 })
			tween(bar, 0.15, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, on and 18 or 0) })
			tween(avStroke, 0.15, { Color = on and C.accent or C.line, Transparency = 0.3 })
		end)
		btn.Activated:Connect(function() selectPlayer(p) end)
		rows[p] = { btn = btn, bar = bar, avStroke = avStroke, name = nameL, dist = distL }
	end

	local function refresh()
		local list = Players:GetPlayers()
		table.sort(list, function(a, b)
			if a == player then return b ~= player end
			if b == player then return false end
			return a.DisplayName:lower() < b.DisplayName:lower()
		end)
		for i, p in ipairs(list) do
			if not rows[p] then addRow(p) end
			rows[p].btn.LayoutOrder = i
		end
		plCount.Text = #list .. "/" .. Players.MaxPlayers
	end
	Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function() refresh() end)
	Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(p)
		local r = rows[p]
		if r then r.btn:Destroy() rows[p] = nil end
		if selected == p then closePop() end
		if V.view == p then stopView() end
		V.friend[p] = nil
		V.hlOn[p] = nil
		setHL(p, false)
		sync()
		plCount.Text = (#Players:GetPlayers() - 1) .. "/" .. Players.MaxPlayers
	end)
	refresh()

	layoutFrame.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if not popOpen or not isPress(input) then return end
		if isInside(pop, input.Position) or isInside(plPanel, input.Position) then return end
		closePop()
	end)

	UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if not popOpen or not isPress(input) or not State.dock.players.pinned then return end
		if isInside(pop, input.Position) or isInside(plPanel, input.Position) then return end
		closePop()
	end)

	task.spawn(function()
		local n = 0
		while gui.Parent do
			task.wait(0.2)
			n += 1
			local c = workspace.CurrentCamera
			if V.view then
				local h = hum(V.view)
				if h and c and c.CameraSubject ~= h then c.CameraSubject = h end
			end
			for p, on in pairs(V.hlOn) do
				if on and p.Parent == Players and not (V.hl[p] and V.hl[p].Parent) then setHL(p, true) end
			end
			if popOpen and not holder.Visible and not State.dock.players.pinned then closePop() end
			if n % 3 == 0 then
				local mine = root(player)
				for p, r in pairs(rows) do
					if p ~= player then
						local th = root(p)
						r.dist.Text = (mine and th) and (math.floor((mine.Position - th.Position).Magnitude) .. "m") or "-"
					end
				end
				if popOpen then updateInfo() end
			end
		end
	end)

	State.extCards = {}
	local EXTK = { "appearance", "players" }
	local EXTC = { appearance = apC, players = plC }
	local function extGone(k) local d = State.dock[k] return d.off or d.pinned end
	function State.extHome(key)
		return UDim2.fromOffset(key == "players" and 245 or 0, State.extY or 720)
	end
	function State.extPlace(h)
		local n = 0
		for _, k in ipairs(EXTK) do if not extGone(k) or State.dock[k].hold then n += 1 end end
		State.extH = n > 0 and (CH + GAP) or 0
		State.extY = h + GAP
		for _, k in ipairs(EXTK) do
			local c = EXTC[k]
			if not extGone(k) and c.Parent == layoutFrame and not c:FindFirstChild("PopScale") then c.Position = State.extHome(k) end
		end
		State.plPlace()
		return n > 0 and (State.extY + CH) or h
	end
end)
if not State.extOk then warn("[Nauru Hub] appearance/players widgets failed: " .. tostring(State.extErr)) end

do
	local PAGE_TITLES = { main = "main", visuals = "visuals", effects = "effects", player = "player", config = "config", themes = "themes", settings = "settings" }
	local PAGE_ORDER = { "main", "visuals", "effects", "player", "config", "themes", "settings" }
	local MAX_RESULTS, ROW_H = 6, 26

	local resBox = createFrame(content, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(383 - 16 - 180, 82), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(180, 0), BackgroundColor3 = C.field, ClipsDescendants = true, Visible = false, ZIndex = 40 })
	corner(resBox, 6)
	stroke(resBox, C.accent, 1, 0.55)
	local resRows = {}

	local hideTok = 0
	local function hideResults()
		hideTok += 1
		local t = hideTok
		if not resBox.Visible then return end
		tween(resBox, 0.16, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(180, 0) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
		task.delay(0.17, function()
			if hideTok == t then
				resBox.Visible = false
				for _, r in ipairs(resRows) do r:Destroy() end
				resRows = {}
			end
		end)
	end

	local function collect()
		local out, seen = {}, {}
		for _, pn in ipairs(PAGE_ORDER) do
			local pg = pages[pn]
			if pg then
				for _, d in ipairs(pg:GetDescendants()) do
					if (d:IsA("TextLabel") or d:IsA("TextButton")) and not d.RichText and d.TextXAlignment ~= Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
						and not d:GetAttribute("NoTranslate") then
						local t = d.Text
						if type(t) == "string" and #t >= 3 and #t <= 40 and t:find("%a%a") then
							local key = pn .. "|" .. t:lower()
							if not seen[key] then seen[key] = true table.insert(out, { text = t, page = pn, inst = d }) end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
		return out
	end

	local function goTo(item)
		local pn, inst = item.page, item.inst
		for i, sec in ipairs(sections) do
			for _, n in ipairs(sec.pages) do
				if n == pn then
					if #sec.pages > 1 then currentTab = pn end
					setSection(i)
				end
			end
		end

		task.delay(0.05, function()
			if not inst or not inst.Parent then return end
			local top, sf = inst, nil
			while top.Parent and top.Parent ~= pages[pn] do
				if top.Parent:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then sf = top.Parent break end
				top = top.Parent
			end
			if not sf then return end
			local y = top.Position.Y.Offset
			local target = math.clamp(y - 60, 0, math.max(0, sf.CanvasSize.Y.Offset - sf.AbsoluteWindowSize.Y / math.max(responsiveScale.Scale, 0.01)))
			tween(sf, 0.25, { CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, target) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
			local w = top.Size.X.Offset > 0 and top.Size.X.Offset or 140
			local h = math.max(top.Size.Y.Offset, 18)
			local hl = createFrame(sf, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(top.Position.X.Offset - 6, y), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w + 12, h + 4), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent, BackgroundTransparency = 0.7, ZIndex = 5 })
			corner(hl, 5)
			tween(hl, 1.4, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
			task.delay(1.5, function() hl:Destroy() end)
		end)

		searchBox.Text = ""
		hideResults()
		if searchOpen then
			searchOpen = false
			tween(searchBar, 0.18, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(180, 0) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
			task.delay(0.19, function() if not searchOpen then searchBar.Visible = false end end)
		end
	end

	local function refresh()
		local q = searchBox.Text:lower():match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
		if q == "" or not searchOpen then hideResults() return end
		hideTok += 1
		for _, r in ipairs(resRows) do r:Destroy() end
		resRows = {}

		local hits = {}
		for _, it in ipairs(collect()) do
			local at = it.text:lower():find(q, 1, true)
			if at then it.rank = at table.insert(hits, it) end
		end
		table.sort(hits, function(a, b) if a.rank ~= b.rank then return a.rank < b.rank end return a.text < b.text end)

		local n = math.min(#hits, MAX_RESULTS)
		resBox.Visible = true
		if n == 0 then
			local l = createLabel(resBox, "no results", 11, C.dim, FONT, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, ROW_H), ZIndex = 41 })
			table.insert(resRows, l)
			tween(resBox, 0.12, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(180, ROW_H) })
			return
		end
		for i = 1, n do
			local it = hits[i]
			local row = createButton(resBox, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, (i - 1) * ROW_H), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, ROW_H), BackgroundColor3 = C.hoverField, ZIndex = 41 })
			createLabel(row, it.text, 12, C.text, FONT, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -66, 1, 0), ZIndex = 42, NoTitle = true })
			createLabel(row, PAGE_TITLES[it.page] or it.page, 10, C.dim, FONT, { Position = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 0), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(52, ROW_H), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, ZIndex = 42 })
			row.MouseEnter:Connect(function() tween(row, 0.08, { BackgroundTransparency = 0 }) end)
			row.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tween(row, 0.08, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }) end)
			row.Activated:Connect(function() goTo(it) end)
			table.insert(resRows, row)
		end
		resRows.first = hits[1]
		tween(resBox, 0.12, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(180, n * ROW_H) })
	end

	searchBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(refresh)
	searchBox.FocusLost:Connect(function(enter)
		if enter and resRows.first then goTo(resRows.first) end
	end)
	searchBtn.Activated:Connect(function()
		task.defer(function()
			if not searchOpen then searchBox.Text = "" hideResults() else refresh() end
		end)
	end)
end

State.phoneInit = function()

	local FONT, FONT_BOLD = FONT, FONT_BOLD
	pcall(function()
		local m, b = Enum.Font.BuilderSansMedium, Enum.Font.BuilderSansBold
		FONT, FONT_BOLD = m, b
	end)
	local PW, PH, GAP = 216, 430, 12
	local SW, SH = PW - 14, PH - 14
	local AW = 186
	local CENTER, RIGHT = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
	local BEZEL = Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 12)
	local conns = {}
	local isOpen, userOpen, side, current, notesBox = false, false, "left", nil, nil

	local function nl(o) o:SetAttribute("NoTranslate", true) return o end
	local function nt(o) o:SetAttribute("NoTheme", true) return o end
	local function pd(o) o:SetAttribute("NoCap", true) return o end

	local dock = createFrame(gui, { Name = "PhoneDock", Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ClipsDescendants = true, Visible = false, ZIndex = 4 })
	local phone = nt(create("CanvasGroup", { Name = "Phone", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(PW, PH), BackgroundColor3 = BEZEL, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, dock))
	corner(phone, 36)
	local pStroke = stroke(phone, C.accent, 1.5, 0.5)
	local pscale = create("UIScale", {}, phone)
	local ps2 = create("UIScale", {}, phone)

	for _, b in ipairs({ { 0, 96, 3, 26 }, { 0, 134, 3, 44 }, { PW - 3, 120, 3, 58 } }) do
		corner(nt(createFrame(phone, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(b[1], b[2]), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(b[3], b[4]), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(38, 38, 44) })), 2)
	end

	do
		local function capFix(o)
			if o:GetAttribute("NoCap") or o.RichText then return end
			local te = State.lang and State.lang.tracked[o]
			if te and o.Text == te.tr then return end
			local t = o.Text
			if t == "" or t:find("%d%a") or t:lower():find("%d%s*[ap]m$") or t == "ms" or t == "s" then return end
			local n = State.titleCase(t)
			if n ~= t then o.Text = n end
		end
		local function watch(o)
			if o:IsA("TextLabel") or o:IsA("TextButton") then
				capFix(o)
				o:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function() capFix(o) end)
			end
		end
		phone.DescendantAdded:Connect(watch)
		task.defer(function() for _, d in ipairs(phone:GetDescendants()) do watch(d) end end)
		State.langRoots = State.langRoots or {}
		table.insert(State.langRoots, phone)
		local function adopt(d) if State.lang and State.lang.adopt then State.lang.adopt(d) end end
		phone.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d) task.defer(adopt, d) end)
