		task.defer(function() for _, d in ipairs(phone:GetDescendants()) do adopt(d) end end)
	end

	local screen = create("CanvasGroup", { Name = "Screen", Position = UDim2.fromOffset(7, 7), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(SW, SH), BackgroundColor3 = C.bg, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, phone)
	corner(screen, 30)

	local wall = nt(createFrame(screen, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = C.white }))
	local wallGrad = create("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90 }, wall)
	local function paintWall()
		wallGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, C.bg:Lerp(C.accent, 0.08)),
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.55, C.bg),
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, C.bg:Lerp(C.accent, 0.4)),
		})
	end
	paintWall()
	table.insert(C.hooks, paintWall)
	local glow = createFrame(screen, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0.82, 0, 0.8, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(190, 190), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent, BackgroundTransparency = 0.9 })
	corner(glow, 95)

	local bar = createFrame(screen, { Name = "StatusBar", Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28), ZIndex = 10 })
	local timeL = nl(createLabel(bar, "", 11, C.text, FONT_BOLD, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(60, 28), NoTitle = true }))
	corner(nt(createFrame(bar, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 7), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(58, 15), BackgroundColor3 = BEZEL })), 8)
	local batt = createFrame(bar, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 10) })
	corner(batt, 3)
	stroke(batt, C.sub, 1)
	corner(createFrame(batt, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(2, 2), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 6), BackgroundColor3 = C.green }), 1)
	createFrame(bar, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, -17, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(2, 4), BackgroundColor3 = C.sub })
	local wifi = newIcon(bar, 12, "wifi")
	wifi.Holder.Position = UDim2.new(1, -48, 0.5, 0)
	wifi.tint(C.text, 0)

	do
		local GRAY, OFFC = Color3.fromRGB(58, 58, 66), Color3.fromRGB(92, 92, 100)
		local WHITE, DARK, SUB = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), Color3.fromRGB(28, 28, 32), Color3.fromRGB(172, 172, 180)
		local ORANGE, GREEN, BLUE, PURPLE, RED = Color3.fromRGB(255, 149, 0), Color3.fromRGB(52, 199, 89), Color3.fromRGB(10, 132, 255), Color3.fromRGB(94, 92, 230), Color3.fromRGB(255, 69, 58)
		local U, G = 38, 8
		local MX, Y0 = math.floor((SW - (4 * U + 3 * G)) / 2), 46
		local function cx(c) return MX + c * (U + G) end
		local function cy(r) return Y0 + r * (U + G) end

		local brightness, volume = 1, 1
		local ccOpen, drag, draggingB, draggingV = false, nil, false, false

		local dimLayer = nt(createFrame(screen, { Name = "BrightnessDim", Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ZIndex = 200 }))
		dimLayer.Active = false
		pcall(function() dimLayer.Interactable = false end)

		local prog = Instance.new("NumberValue")
		local cc = nt(create("CanvasGroup", { Name = "ControlCenter", Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, GroupTransparency = 1, Visible = false, ZIndex = 20 }, screen))
		local ccBg = nt(createButton(cc, { Name = "Backdrop", Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 12), BackgroundTransparency = 0.18, ZIndex = 1 }))
		local body = nt(createFrame(cc, { Name = "Modules", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 2 }))
		local bodyScale = create("UIScale", {}, body)

		local function render(p)
			p = math.clamp(p, 0, 1)
			cc.Visible = p > 0.003
			cc.GroupTransparency = 1 - p
			body.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, -(1 - p) * 34)
			bodyScale.Scale = 0.93 + 0.07 * p
		end
		prog.Changed:Connect(function() render(prog.Value) end)

		local function round(parent, x, y, w, h, r)
			local f = nt(createFrame(parent, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, h), BackgroundColor3 = GRAY, BackgroundTransparency = 0.1 }))
			corner(f, r)
			return f
		end
		local function mkIcon(parent, size, name, fb, z)
			local ic = newIcon(parent, size, name)
			table.remove(State.icons)
			ic.Holder.ZIndex = z or 4
			local fbL
			if not ic.img and #ic.inks == 0 then
				fbL = nl(createLabel(ic.Holder, fb or "?", size - 2, WHITE, FONT_BOLD, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), TextXAlignment = CENTER, NoTitle = true, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None }))
			end
			local o = { Holder = ic.Holder }
			function o.color(c, t) ic.tint(c, t or 0.12) if fbL then fbL.TextColor3 = c end end
			function o.set(n, fbText) if ic.setIcon then ic.setIcon(n) end if fbL and fbText then fbL.Text = fbText end end
			o.color(WHITE, 0)
			return o
		end
		local function pressFx(b)
			local sc = create("UIScale", {}, b)
			b.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function() tween(sc, 0.08, { Scale = 0.92 }) end)
			b.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function() tween(sc, 0.2, { Scale = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back) end)
			b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tween(sc, 0.12, { Scale = 1 }) end)
		end

		local function toggle(parent, x, y, w, h, name, fb, onColor, onIcon, init, cb, label)
			local b = nt(createButton(parent, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, h), BackgroundColor3 = OFFC, BackgroundTransparency = 0.1, ZIndex = 3 }))
			corner(b, math.min(w, h) / 2)
			pressFx(b)
			local ic = mkIcon(b, 18, name, fb)
			ic.Holder.Position = label and UDim2.fromOffset(h / 2 + 1, h / 2) or UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
			local lb
			if label then lb = nl(createLabel(b, label, 10, WHITE, FONT_BOLD, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(h - 3, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -(h + 2), 1, 0), NoTitle = true, ZIndex = 4 })) end
			local on = init and true or false
			local t = {}
			local function paint(instant)
				local d = instant and 0 or 0.16
				tween(b, d, { BackgroundColor3 = on and onColor or OFFC, BackgroundTransparency = on and 0 or 0.1 })
				local c = on and (onIcon or WHITE) or WHITE
				ic.color(c, d)
				if lb then lb.TextColor3 = c end
			end
			function t.set(v, instant) on = v and true or false paint(instant) end
			function t.get() return on end
			b.Activated:Connect(function() on = not on paint() if cb then cb(on) end end)
			paint(true)
			return t
		end
		local function setCC(open, instant)
			ccOpen = open
			draggingB, draggingV = false, false
			State.stopTweens(prog)
			if instant then
				prog.Value = open and 1 or 0
				render(prog.Value)
			else
				tween(prog, open and 0.42 or 0.3, { Value = open and 1 or 0 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
			end
		end
		State.phoneCC = { open = function() setCC(true) end, close = function() setCC(false) end }

		local conn = round(body, cx(0), cy(0), 2 * U + G, 2 * U + G, 22)
		local airplane, cell, wifiT, bt
		local function radios() wifi.Holder.Visible = wifiT.get() end
		airplane = toggle(conn, 6, 6, 34, 34, "plane", "A", ORANGE, WHITE, false, function(on)
			cell.set(not on) wifiT.set(not on) bt.set(not on) radios()
		end)
		cell = toggle(conn, 44, 6, 34, 34, "signal", "S", GREEN, WHITE, true)
		wifiT = toggle(conn, 6, 44, 34, 34, "wifi", "W", BLUE, WHITE, true, radios)
		bt = toggle(conn, 44, 44, 34, 34, "bluetooth", "B", BLUE, WHITE, true)

		local np = round(body, cx(2), cy(0), 2 * U + G, 2 * U + G, 22)
		np.ClipsDescendants = true

		local npArt = nt(create("ImageLabel", { Name = "NpArt", Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop, ImageTransparency = 0.12, Visible = false, ZIndex = 1 }, np))
		corner(npArt, 22)
		create("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.3, 0.1), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.78, 0.82), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1) }) }, npArt)
		local npTitle = pd(nl(createLabel(np, "Not Playing", 10, WHITE, FONT_BOLD, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 11), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(64, 14), NoTitle = true, ZIndex = 3 })))
		local npSub = pd(nl(createLabel(np, "Music", 9, SUB, FONT, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 25), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(64, 12), NoTitle = true, ZIndex = 3 })))
		npTitle.TextStrokeTransparency, npSub.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.75, 0.85
		local function transport(x, name, fb, size, fn)
			local b = nt(createButton(np, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, 62), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(24, 24), ZIndex = 3 }))
			pressFx(b)
			local ic = mkIcon(b, size, name, fb)
			ic.Holder.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
			b.Activated:Connect(function() pcall(fn) end)
			return ic
		end
		npTitle.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
		npSub.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd

		local function media() return State.music end
		transport(17, "skip-back", "<", 14, function()
			local M = media()
			if M and M.now().id then M.prev() else ympPrevious() end
		end)
		local playIc = transport(42, "play", ">", 18, function()
			local M = media()
			if M then M.toggle()
			elseif ympSound.IsPlaying then ympSound:Pause() elseif ympSound.IsPaused then ympSound:Resume() end
		end)
		transport(67, "skip-forward", ">", 14, function()
			local M = media()
			if M and M.now().id then M.next() else ympAdvance(false) end
		end)
		local lastNpTitle, lastNpSub, lastNpIcon, lastNpArtId
		local function refreshMedia()
			local playing, paused, loading = false, false, false
			local title, artist = nil, nil
			pcall(function()
				local M = State.music
				local n = M and M.now()
				playing, paused, loading = ympSound.IsPlaying, ympSound.IsPaused, (n and n.busy) or false
				if n and n.id and n.title ~= "" then title, artist = n.title, n.author end
			end)
			if not title then
				local ok, t = pcall(function() return titleLabel.Text end)
				if ok and type(t) == "string" and t ~= "" and t ~= "not playing" and (playing or paused or loading) then title = t end
			end
			local sub
			if loading then sub = "Loading..."
			elseif title and artist and artist ~= "" then sub = artist
			elseif playing then sub = "Playing"
			elseif paused then sub = "Paused"
			else sub = "Music" end
			local artId
			pcall(function() local n = State.music and State.music.now() if n and n.id then artId = n.id end end)
			if artId ~= lastNpArtId then
				local img
				if artId then pcall(function() img = State.music.thumb(artId) end) end
				if img or not artId then lastNpArtId = artId end
				npArt.Image = img or ""
				npArt.Visible = img ~= nil
			end
			local nt2 = title or "Not Playing"
			if nt2 ~= lastNpTitle then lastNpTitle = nt2 npTitle.Text = nt2 end
			if sub ~= lastNpSub then lastNpSub = sub npSub.Text = sub end
			local icn = playing and "pause" or "play"
			if icn ~= lastNpIcon then lastNpIcon = icn playIc.set(icn, playing and "||" or ">") end
		end

		toggle(body, cx(0), cy(2), U, U, "lock", "L", WHITE, RED, false)
		toggle(body, cx(1), cy(2), U, U, "airplay", "M", WHITE, DARK, false)
		toggle(body, cx(0), cy(3), 2 * U + G, U, "moon", "F", PURPLE, WHITE, false, nil, "Focus")

		local function slider(col, name, fb)
			local card = nt(create("CanvasGroup", { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(cx(col), cy(2)), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(U, 2 * U + G), BackgroundColor3 = GRAY, BackgroundTransparency = 0.1, BorderSizePixel = 0 }, body))
			corner(card, 19)
			local fill = nt(createFrame(card, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = WHITE }))
			local hit = nt(createButton(card, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = 3 }))
			local ic = mkIcon(card, 16, name, fb, 4)
			ic.Holder.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -19)
			return card, fill, ic, hit
		end
		local bCard, bFill, bIc, bHit = slider(2, "sun", "B")
		local vCard, vFill, vIc, vHit = slider(3, "volume-2", "V")

		local function setBrightness(v)
			brightness = math.clamp(v, 0.15, 1)
			bFill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, brightness)
			dimLayer.BackgroundTransparency = 1 - (1 - brightness) * 0.6
			bIc.color(brightness < 0.3 and WHITE or DARK, 0)
		end
		local function setVolume(v)
			volume = math.clamp(v, 0, 1)
			pcall(function() ympSound.Volume = volume end)
			vFill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, volume)
			vIc.color(volume < 0.3 and WHITE or DARK, 0)
			vIc.set(volume <= 0.001 and "volume-x" or (volume > 0.5 and "volume-2" or "volume-1"))
		end
		local function ratioFrom(card, input)
			local a, s = card.AbsolutePosition, card.AbsoluteSize
			return math.clamp(1 - (input.Position.Y - a.Y) / math.max(s.Y, 1), 0, 1)
		end
		bHit.InputBegan:Connect(function(input) if isPress(input) then draggingB = true setBrightness(ratioFrom(bCard, input)) end end)
		vHit.InputBegan:Connect(function(input) if isPress(input) then draggingV = true setVolume(ratioFrom(vCard, input)) end end)
		setBrightness(1)
		pcall(function() volume = math.clamp(ympSound.Volume, 0, 1) end)
		setVolume(volume)

		toggle(body, cx(0), cy(4), U, U, "flashlight", "T", WHITE, DARK, false)
		local function quick(col, name, fb, appId)
			local b = nt(createButton(body, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(cx(col), cy(4)), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(U, U), BackgroundColor3 = GRAY, BackgroundTransparency = 0.1, ZIndex = 3 }))
			corner(b, U / 2)
			pressFx(b)
			local ic = mkIcon(b, 18, name, fb)
			ic.Holder.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
			b.Activated:Connect(function()
				setCC(false)
				task.delay(0.2, function() if State.phoneOpenApp then State.phoneOpenApp(appId) end end)
			end)
		end
		quick(1, "timer", "T", "timer")
		quick(2, "calculator", "C", "calc")
		quick(3, "camera", "O", "camera")

		local function k() return math.max(pscale.Scale * ps2.Scale, 0.01) end
		local function beginDrag(input)
			if not isPress(input) then return end
			State.stopTweens(prog)
			drag = { y0 = input.Position.Y, from = prog.Value, moved = false }
		end
		local pullZone = nt(createButton(screen, { Name = "CCPull", Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28), ZIndex = 55 }))
		pullZone.InputBegan:Connect(beginDrag)
		ccBg.InputBegan:Connect(beginDrag)
		table.insert(conns, UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
			if not isMove(input) then return end
			if drag then
				local dy = (input.Position.Y - drag.y0) / k()
				if math.abs(dy) > 5 then drag.moved = true end
				if drag.moved then prog.Value = math.clamp(drag.from + dy / 130, 0, 1) end
			end
			if draggingB then setBrightness(ratioFrom(bCard, input)) end
			if draggingV then setVolume(ratioFrom(vCard, input)) end
		end))
		table.insert(conns, UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
			if not isPress(input) then return end
			draggingB, draggingV = false, false
			if drag then
				local d = drag
				drag = nil
				if not d.moved then
					setCC(false)
				elseif d.from < 0.5 then
					setCC(prog.Value > 0.3)
				else
					setCC(prog.Value > 0.7)
				end
			end
		end))

		local acc = 0
		table.insert(conns, RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
			if not dock.Visible then
				if ccOpen or prog.Value > 0 then drag = nil setCC(false, true) end
				return
			end
			if prog.Value <= 0 then return end
			acc += dt
			if acc < 0.25 then return end
			acc = 0
			refreshMedia()
			if not draggingV then
				local ok, v = pcall(function() return math.clamp(ympSound.Volume, 0, 1) end)
				if ok and math.abs(v - volume) > 0.01 then setVolume(v) end
			end
		end))
		refreshMedia()
	end

	local function pl(parent, text, size, color, x, y, w, h, align, font)
		return createLabel(parent, text, size, color, font or FONT, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, h), TextXAlignment = align or Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, NoTitle = true })
	end
	local b64enc, b64dec
	do
	local B64C, B64R = {}, {}
	do
		local al = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/"
		for i = 1, 64 do B64C[i - 1] = al:sub(i, i) B64R[al:byte(i)] = i - 1 end
	end
	b64enc = function(raw)
		local b, n = buffer.fromstring(raw), #raw
		local out = {}
		for i = 0, n - 1, 3 do
			local x = buffer.readu8(b, i)
			local y = i + 1 < n and buffer.readu8(b, i + 1) or 0
			local z = i + 2 < n and buffer.readu8(b, i + 2) or 0
			local v = x * 65536 + y * 256 + z
			local c3 = i + 1 < n and B64C[math.floor(v / 64) % 64] or "="
			local c4 = i + 2 < n and B64C[v % 64] or "="
			out[#out + 1] = B64C[math.floor(v / 262144) % 64] .. B64C[math.floor(v / 4096) % 64] .. c3 .. c4
			if i > 0 and i % 90000 == 0 then task.wait() end
		end
		return table.concat(out)
	end
	b64dec = function(str)
		local n = #str
		local pad = (str:sub(-2) == "==") and 2 or (str:sub(-1) == "=" and 1 or 0)
		local size = math.floor(n / 4 * 3) - pad
		local out = buffer.create(size)
		local o = 0
		for i = 1, n, 4 do
			local a, b, c, d = str:byte(i, i + 3)
			local v = B64R[a] * 262144 + B64R[b] * 4096 + (B64R[c] or 0) * 64 + (B64R[d] or 0)
			if o < size then buffer.writeu8(out, o, math.floor(v / 65536)) end
			if o + 1 < size then buffer.writeu8(out, o + 1, math.floor(v / 256) % 256) end
			if o + 2 < size then buffer.writeu8(out, o + 2, v % 256) end
			o += 3
			if i % 120000 == 1 then task.wait() end
		end
		return out
	end
	end

	local CamStore = { IDX = "NauruHub/camera_photos.json", photos = {} }
	State.phonePhotos = CamStore.photos
	function CamStore.loadEi(p)
		if p.ei then return p.ei end
		local ei = game:GetService("AssetService"):CreateEditableImage({ Size = Vector2.new(p.w, p.h) })
		ei:WritePixelsBuffer(Vector2.zero, Vector2.new(p.w, p.h), b64dec(readfile(p.file)))
		p.ei = ei
		return ei
	end
	function CamStore.save()
		pcall(function()
			if not (writefile and isfolder and makefolder) then return end
			if not isfolder("NauruHub") then makefolder("NauruHub") end
			local out = {}
			for _, p in ipairs(CamStore.photos) do
				if p.file or p.png then out[#out + 1] = { file = p.file, png = p.png, w = p.w, h = p.h, time = p.time, fov = p.fov } end
			end
			writefile(CamStore.IDX, game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONEncode(out))
		end)
	end

	function CamStore.rayShot(cf, fov, aspect)
		local W, H = 400, math.max(100, math.floor(400 / math.max(0.5, aspect or 1.6)))
		local buf = buffer.create(W * H * 4)
		local params = RaycastParams.new()
		pcall(function() local ch = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer.Character if ch then params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { ch } end end)
		local L = game:GetService("Lighting")
		local sun = Vector3.new(0.4, 0.8, 0.3).Unit
		pcall(function() sun = L:GetSunDirection() end)
		local tanH = math.tan(math.rad(fov or 70) / 2)
		local ar = W / H
		local origin, look, right, up = cf.Position, cf.LookVector, cf.RightVector, cf.UpVector
		local terrain = workspace.Terrain
		local n = 0
		for y = 0, H - 1 do
			local v = 1 - (y + 0.5) / H * 2
			local sky = Color3.fromRGB(110, 160, 235):Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(205, 225, 245), math.clamp(1 - (v + 1) / 2 + 0.2, 0, 1))
			for x = 0, W - 1 do
				local u = (x + 0.5) / W * 2 - 1
				local dir = (look + right * (u * tanH * ar) + up * (v * tanH)).Unit
				local col = sky
				local from = origin
				for _ = 1, 4 do
					local r = workspace:Raycast(from, dir * 900, params)
					if not r then break end
					local inst = r.Instance
					if inst:IsA("BasePart") and inst ~= terrain and inst.Transparency > 0.85 then
						from = r.Position + dir * 0.05
					else
						local base = inst.Color
						if inst == terrain then pcall(function() base = terrain:GetMaterialColor(r.Material) end) end
						local lit = 0.5 + 0.5 * math.max(0, r.Normal:Dot(sun))
						col = base:Lerp(sky, math.clamp((r.Position - origin).Magnitude / 900, 0, 0.85) * 0.6)
						col = Color3.new(col.R * lit, col.G * lit, col.B * lit)
						break
					end
				end
				local o = (y * W + x) * 4
				buffer.writeu8(buf, o, math.floor(col.R * 255 + 0.5))
				buffer.writeu8(buf, o + 1, math.floor(col.G * 255 + 0.5))
				buffer.writeu8(buf, o + 2, math.floor(col.B * 255 + 0.5))
				buffer.writeu8(buf, o + 3, 255)
				n += 1
				if n % 700 == 0 then task.wait() end
			end
		end
		local ei = game:GetService("AssetService"):CreateEditableImage({ Size = Vector2.new(W, H) })
		ei:WritePixelsBuffer(Vector2.zero, Vector2.new(W, H), buf)
		return ei
	end

	function CamStore.pngEncode(px, w, h)
		local band, bxor, rshift = bit32.band, bit32.bxor, bit32.rshift
		if not CamStore.crcT then
			local t = {}
			for i = 0, 255 do
				local c = i
				for _ = 1, 8 do
					if band(c, 1) == 1 then c = bxor(0xEDB88320, rshift(c, 1)) else c = rshift(c, 1) end
				end
				t[i] = c
			end
			CamStore.crcT = t
		end
		local T = CamStore.crcT
		local function be32(b, o, v)
			buffer.writeu8(b, o, math.floor(v / 16777216) % 256)
			buffer.writeu8(b, o + 1, math.floor(v / 65536) % 256)
			buffer.writeu8(b, o + 2, math.floor(v / 256) % 256)
			buffer.writeu8(b, o + 3, v % 256)
		end
		local function crc(b, s0, n)
			local c = 0xFFFFFFFF
			for i = s0, s0 + n - 1 do
				c = bxor(T[band(bxor(c, buffer.readu8(b, i)), 255)], rshift(c, 8))
				if i % 150000 == 0 then task.wait() end
			end
			return bxor(c, 0xFFFFFFFF)
		end
		local rowB = w * 4
		local rawLen = (rowB + 1) * h
		local raw = buffer.create(rawLen)
		for y = 0, h - 1 do
			local base = y * (rowB + 1)
			buffer.copy(raw, base + 1, px, y * rowB, rowB)
			for x = 0, w - 1 do buffer.writeu8(raw, base + 1 + x * 4 + 3, 255) end
		end
		task.wait()
		local nBlk = math.ceil(rawLen / 65535)
		local zLen = 2 + nBlk * 5 + rawLen + 4
		local z = buffer.create(zLen)
		buffer.writeu8(z, 0, 0x78)
		buffer.writeu8(z, 1, 0x01)
		local zo, ro = 2, 0
		for _ = 1, nBlk do
			local n = math.min(65535, rawLen - ro)
			buffer.writeu8(z, zo, (ro + n >= rawLen) and 1 or 0)
			buffer.writeu16(z, zo + 1, n)
			buffer.writeu16(z, zo + 3, bxor(n, 0xFFFF))
			buffer.copy(z, zo + 5, raw, ro, n)
			zo += 5 + n
			ro += n
		end
		local s1, s2 = 1, 0
		for i = 0, rawLen - 1 do
			s1 = (s1 + buffer.readu8(raw, i)) % 65521
			s2 = (s2 + s1) % 65521
			if i % 150000 == 0 and i > 0 then task.wait() end
		end
		be32(z, zo, s2 * 65536 + s1)
		local out = buffer.create(57 + zLen)
		buffer.writestring(out, 0, "\137PNG\r\n\26\n")
		be32(out, 8, 13)
		buffer.writestring(out, 12, "IHDR")
		be32(out, 16, w)
		be32(out, 20, h)
		buffer.writeu8(out, 24, 8)
		buffer.writeu8(out, 25, 6)
		be32(out, 29, crc(out, 12, 17))
		be32(out, 33, zLen)
		buffer.writestring(out, 37, "IDAT")
		buffer.copy(out, 41, z, 0, zLen)
		be32(out, 41 + zLen, crc(out, 37, 4 + zLen))
		local e = 45 + zLen
		be32(out, e, 0)
		buffer.writestring(out, e + 4, "IEND")
		be32(out, e + 8, 0xAE426082)
		return buffer.tostring(out)
	end
	function CamStore.persist(p, done)
		task.spawn(function()
			local ok, err = pcall(function()
				if not (writefile and buffer) then error("no writefile / buffer here") end

				local diag = {}
				p.diag = diag
				local function viaFile()
					if p.v == nil or p.kind == "world" then return false end
					local okS, cs = pcall(function() return game:GetService("CaptureService") end)
					if not (okS and cs) then diag[#diag + 1] = "no CaptureService" return false end
					local okC, content = pcall(function() if p.kind == "capture" then return Content.fromObject(p.v) end return Content.fromUri(p.v) end)
					if not okC then diag[#diag + 1] = "content: " .. tostring(content):sub(1, 40) return false end
					local okP, path = pcall(function() return cs:GetCaptureFilePathAsync(content) end)
					if not (okP and type(path) == "string" and path ~= "") then diag[#diag + 1] = "path: " .. tostring(path):sub(1, 60) path = nil end
					local bytes
					local function isImg(v) return type(v) == "string" and #v > 200 and (v:sub(2, 4) == "PNG" or v:sub(1, 3) == "\255\216\255") end
					local function tryRead(pth, label)
						local okR, d = pcall(readfile, pth)
						if okR and isImg(d) then bytes = d return true end
						diag[#diag + 1] = label .. ": " .. tostring(d):sub(1, 40)
						return false
					end
					if path then
						tryRead(path, "readfile")
						if not bytes then tryRead((path:gsub("\\", "/")), "readfile/") end
						if not bytes then local rel = path:match("[Rr]oblox[/\\](.+)$") if rel then tryRead(rel, "readfile rel") end end
					end
					if not bytes then
						for _, arg in ipairs({ content, path }) do
							if bytes then break end
							local okU, ud = pcall(function() return cs:GetCaptureUploadDataAsync(arg) end)
							if okU and type(ud) == "table" then
								local keys = {}
								for k, v in pairs(ud) do
									keys[#keys + 1] = tostring(k) .. ":" .. typeof(v)
									if not bytes and type(v) == "string" then
										if isImg(v) then bytes = v
										elseif v:sub(1, 5) == "iVBOR" or v:sub(1, 4) == "/9j/" then pcall(function() bytes = buffer.tostring(b64dec(v)) end)
										elseif #v < 300 and v:lower():match("%.[pj][np]e?g$") then tryRead(v, "upload path") end
									end
								end
								if not bytes then diag[#diag + 1] = "upload keys " .. table.concat(keys, ",") end
							else
								diag[#diag + 1] = "upload: " .. tostring(ud):sub(1, 40)
							end
						end
					end
					if not bytes then return false end
					if not isfolder("NauruHub") then makefolder("NauruHub") end
					local ext = (bytes:sub(1, 3) == "\255\216\255") and ".jpg" or ".png"
					local fp = "NauruHub/cam_" .. os.time() .. math.random(1000, 9999) .. ext
					writefile(fp, bytes)
					if not (isfile and isfile(fp) and getcustomasset) then diag[#diag + 1] = "cannot show saved file" return false end
					getcustomasset(fp)
					p.png = fp
					return true
				end
				local okF, gotF = pcall(viaFile)
				if not okF then diag[#diag + 1] = "file: " .. tostring(gotF):sub(1, 50) end
				if okF and gotF then return end

				local cands = {}
				if p.kind == "capture" then
					cands[#cands + 1] = function() return Content.fromObject(p.v) end
					cands[#cands + 1] = function() return Content.fromUri(p.v.ContentId) end
				else
					cands[#cands + 1] = function() return Content.fromUri(p.v) end
				end
				local ei, errs = nil, {}
				for _, mk in ipairs(cands) do
					local okc, res = pcall(function() return game:GetService("AssetService"):CreateEditableImageAsync(mk()) end)
					if okc and res then ei = res break end
					errs[#errs + 1] = tostring(res)
				end
				if not ei then
					if not p.cf then error(tostring(errs[1] or "no editable image") .. " [" .. table.concat(diag, "; ") .. "]", 0) end
					local okr, res = pcall(CamStore.rayShot, p.cf, p.fov, p.aspect)
					if not okr then error(tostring(errs[1]) .. " | render: " .. tostring(res) .. " [" .. table.concat(diag, "; ") .. "]", 0) end
					ei, p.approx = res, true
				end
				local sz = ei.Size
				local w, h = sz.X, sz.Y
				if w > 420 then
					local nw, nh = 420, math.max(1, math.floor(sz.Y * 420 / sz.X))
					if pcall(function() ei:Resize(Vector2.new(nw, nh)) end) then w, h = nw, nh end
				end
				local pxb = ei:ReadPixelsBuffer(Vector2.zero, Vector2.new(w, h))
				if not isfolder("NauruHub") then makefolder("NauruHub") end
				local okPng, pngErr = pcall(function()
					local data = CamStore.pngEncode(pxb, w, h)
					local fp = "NauruHub/cam_" .. os.time() .. math.random(1000, 9999) .. ".png"
					writefile(fp, data)
					if not (isfile and isfile(fp) and getcustomasset) then error("png not written") end
					getcustomasset(fp)
					p.png, p.w, p.h = fp, w, h
				end)
				if okPng then return end
				diag[#diag + 1] = "png: " .. tostring(pngErr):sub(1, 50)
				local txt = b64enc(buffer.tostring(pxb))
				local path = "NauruHub/cam_" .. os.time() .. math.random(1000, 9999) .. ".txt"
				writefile(path, txt)
				if not (isfile and isfile(path)) then error("writefile did not create the file") end
				p.file, p.w, p.h, p.ei = path, w, h, ei
			end)
			if ok then CamStore.save() end
			if done then done(ok, err) end
		end)
	end
	pcall(function()
		if false and isfile and isfile(CamStore.IDX) then
			for _, e in ipairs(game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(readfile(CamStore.IDX))) do
				local fp = e.png or e.file
				if type(e) == "table" and fp and isfile(fp) and #CamStore.photos < 5 then
					table.insert(CamStore.photos, { kind = e.png and "png" or "px", file = (not e.png) and e.file or nil, png = e.png, w = e.w, h = e.h, time = e.time or "", fov = e.fov or 70 })
				end
			end
		end
	end)

	function State.glassStyle(o, tint, radius)
		o.BackgroundColor3 = tint or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
		o.BackgroundTransparency = tint and 0.6 or 0.86
		local cr = o:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner")
		if cr and radius then cr.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, radius) end
		local st = o:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke") or stroke(o, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 1.2, 0)
		st.Color, st.Thickness, st.Transparency = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 1.2, 0
		create("UIGradient", { Rotation = 40, Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.08), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.35, 0.72), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.65, 0.8), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.22) }) }, st)
		local hi = nt(createFrame(o, { Name = "Spec", Position = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 2), Size = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0.5, -2), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1) }))
		corner(hi, radius or 8)
		create("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0.72, 1) }, hi)
		o:SetAttribute("GlassBase", o.BackgroundTransparency)
		return st
	end
	local function pbtn(parent, x, y, w, h, text, cb, filled, glass)
		local b = createButton(parent, { Sound = true, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, h), BackgroundColor3 = filled and C.accent or C.field, BackgroundTransparency = 0, TextSize = 12, Font = FONT_BOLD, TextColor3 = (glass ~= false) and Color3.new(1, 1, 1) or (filled and C.bg or C.text) })
		b.Text = text
		corner(b, 8)
		if glass ~= false then
			b.AutoButtonColor = false
			State.glassStyle(b, filled and C.accent or nil, math.floor(h / 2))

			local WHITE3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
			local function syncGlass()
				local c = b.BackgroundColor3
				local want
				if c == C.field or c == C.hoverField or c == WHITE3 then
					if c ~= WHITE3 then b.BackgroundColor3 = WHITE3 end
					want = 0.86
				else
					want = 0.55
				end
				b:SetAttribute("GlassBase", want)
				if math.abs(b.BackgroundTransparency - want) > 0.01 then tween(b, 0.15, { BackgroundTransparency = want }) end
			end
			b:GetPropertyChangedSignal("BackgroundColor3"):Connect(syncGlass)
			b:GetPropertyChangedSignal("TextColor3"):Connect(function()
				if b.TextColor3 == C.text or b.TextColor3 == C.bg then b.TextColor3 = WHITE3 end
			end)
			local us = create("UIScale", {}, b)
			b.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
				tween(us, 0.08, { Scale = 0.94 })
				tween(b, 0.08, { BackgroundTransparency = math.max(0, (b:GetAttribute("GlassBase") or 0.86) - 0.16) })
			end)
			local function rel()
				tween(us, 0.22, { Scale = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
				tween(b, 0.2, { BackgroundTransparency = b:GetAttribute("GlassBase") or 0.86 })
			end
			b.MouseButton1Up:Connect(rel)
			b.MouseLeave:Connect(rel)
		elseif not filled then
			b.MouseEnter:Connect(function() tween(b, 0.1, { BackgroundColor3 = C.hoverField }) end)
			b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tween(b, 0.1, { BackgroundColor3 = C.field }) end)
		end
		b.Activated:Connect(cb)
		return b
	end

	function State.pIconBtn(parent, x, y, w, h, text, cb, icon0, filled, glass)
		local b = pbtn(parent, x, y, w, h, "", cb, filled, glass)
		local row = nt(createFrame(b, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ZIndex = b.ZIndex + 1 }))
		create("UIListLayout", { FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal, HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center, VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center, SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, Padding = UDim.new(0, 6) }, row)
		local slot = nt(createFrame(row, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 12), BackgroundTransparency = 1, LayoutOrder = 1, ZIndex = b.ZIndex + 1 }))
		local lbl = (createLabel(row, text, 10, b.TextColor3, FONT_BOLD, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, h), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, LayoutOrder = 2, ZIndex = b.ZIndex + 1, NoTitle = true }))
		local o = { btn = b, slot = slot }
		local ic, paintFn
		function o.set(t, icon)
			lbl.Text = t
			if icon then
				if ic then ic.Holder:Destroy() ic = nil end
				paintFn = nil
				for _, c in ipairs(slot:GetChildren()) do c:Destroy() end
				if type(icon) == "function" then
					paintFn = icon(slot, b.TextColor3)
				else
					ic = newIcon(slot, 12, icon)
					table.remove(State.icons)
					ic.Holder.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
					ic.paint(b.TextColor3)
				end
			end
		end
		b:GetPropertyChangedSignal("TextColor3"):Connect(function()
			lbl.TextColor3 = b.TextColor3
			if ic then ic.paint(b.TextColor3) end
			if paintFn then paintFn(b.TextColor3) end
		end)
		o.set(text, icon0)
		return o
	end

	local scores = {}
	pcall(function()
		if isfile and isfile("NauruHub/phone_scores.txt") then
			local d = game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(readfile("NauruHub/phone_scores.txt"))
			if type(d) == "table" then scores = d end
		end
	end)
	local function record(key, value, lowerIsBetter)
		local old = scores[key]
		if old ~= nil and ((lowerIsBetter and value >= old) or (not lowerIsBetter and value <= old)) then return false end
		scores[key] = value
		pcall(function()
			if writefile then writefile("NauruHub/phone_scores.txt", game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONEncode(scores)) end
		end)
		return true
	end

	local function glyph(parent, kind, size, color)
		local holder = nt(createFrame(parent, { Name = "Mark", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size) }))
		local img = create("ImageLabel", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ImageColor3 = color }, holder)
		if State.setLucide(img, kind, color) then return holder end
		img:Destroy()
		local function box(x, y, w, h, r)
			local b = nt(createFrame(holder, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x * size, y * size), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(1, w * size), math.max(1, h * size)), Rotation = r or 0, BackgroundColor3 = color }))
			corner(b, 99)
			return b
		end
		if kind == "flag" then
			box(0.3, 0.5, 0.12, 0.8)
			box(0.6, 0.3, 0.5, 0.36)
		elseif kind == "bomb" then
			box(0.5, 0.56, 0.62, 0.62)
			box(0.5, 0.56, 0.95, 0.1, 0) box(0.5, 0.56, 0.95, 0.1, 90) box(0.5, 0.56, 0.95, 0.1, 45) box(0.5, 0.56, 0.95, 0.1, -45)
		else
			nl(createLabel(holder, kind:sub(1, 1):upper(), math.floor(size * 0.9), color, FONT_BOLD, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), TextXAlignment = CENTER, NoTitle = true }))
		end
		return holder
	end

	function State.drawBomb(parent, size, lit)
		local k = size / 40
		local holder = nt(createFrame(parent, { Name = "Mark", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size) }))
		local function bx(x, y, w, h, col, r, rot, tr)
			local b = nt(createFrame(holder, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x * k, y * k), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w * k, h * k), BackgroundColor3 = col, BackgroundTransparency = tr or 0, Rotation = rot or 0 }))
			corner(b, r * k)
			return b
		end
		bx(8, 34, 22, 4, Color3.new(0, 0, 0), 2, 0, 0.7)
		local body = bx(5, 12, 26, 26, Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 38), 13)
		create("UIGradient", { Rotation = 45, Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(96, 98, 120), Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 14)) }, body)
		stroke(body, Color3.fromRGB(150, 154, 176), 1, 0.6)
		bx(9, 17, 9, 5, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 2.5, -35, 0.45)
		bx(10.5, 18.5, 3, 3, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 1.5, 0, 0.1)
		bx(22, 12, 9, 5, Color3.fromRGB(58, 58, 68), 2, -45)
		local fuse = bx(28.2, 4.5, 2.2, 8.5, Color3.fromRGB(222, 184, 126), 1, 33)
		local sp = nt(createFrame(holder, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(33 * k, 5 * k), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(2, 2), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }))
		local glow = nt(createFrame(sp, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(9 * k, 9 * k), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 170, 50), BackgroundTransparency = 0.6 }))
		corner(glow, 99)
		local rays = {}
		for i, rot in ipairs({ 0, 45, 90, 135 }) do
			local ray = nt(createFrame(sp, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(1, 1.2 * k), 7 * k), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 240, 130), BackgroundTransparency = 0.2, Rotation = rot }))
			corner(ray, 1)
			rays[i] = ray
		end
		local core = nt(createFrame(sp, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(4 * k, 4 * k), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 226, 70) }))
		corner(core, 99)
		local api = { lit = lit ~= false, body = body, spark = sp }
		local t0 = os.clock() + math.random()
		local conn
		conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			if not holder.Parent then conn:Disconnect() return end
			sp.Visible = api.lit
			fuse.BackgroundColor3 = api.lit and Color3.fromRGB(222, 184, 126) or Color3.fromRGB(90, 70, 55)
			if not api.lit then return end
			local n = os.clock() - t0
			for i, r in ipairs(rays) do
				r.Rotation = (i - 1) * 45 + n * 140 + math.sin(n * 20 + i) * 14
				r.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(1, 1.2 * k), (4 + 4 * (0.5 + 0.5 * math.sin(n * 18 + i * 1.7))) * k)
			end
			local c = (3 + 1.6 * (0.5 + 0.5 * math.sin(n * 26))) * k
			core.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(c, c)
			glow.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5 + 0.2 * math.sin(n * 15)
		end)
		return holder, api
	end

	function State.drawFlag(parent, size, col)
		local k = size / 20
		local holder = nt(createFrame(parent, { Name = "Mark", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size) }))
		local function bx(x, y, w, h, c, r, tr)
			local b = nt(createFrame(holder, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x * k, y * k), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(1, w * k), math.max(1, h * k)), BackgroundColor3 = c, BackgroundTransparency = tr or 0 }))
			corner(b, r * k)
			return b
		end
		local metal = Color3.fromRGB(205, 210, 224)
		bx(4, 17.2, 12, 2.4, Color3.fromRGB(120, 124, 140), 1.2)
		bx(6.5, 15.4, 7, 2.4, Color3.fromRGB(150, 154, 170), 1.2)
		bx(9, 2.4, 1.8, 14, metal, 0.9)
		bx(8.3, 0.6, 3.2, 3.2, Color3.fromRGB(255, 205, 70), 1.6)
		for i = 0, 4 do
			bx(10.8, 2.6 + i * 2.15, 8.2 - i * 1.6, 2.4, col, 0.6)
		end
		bx(10.8, 2.6, 8.2, 1.2, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.6, 0.65)
		return holder
	end

	function State.drawShovel(parent, size, col)
		local k = size / 20
		local holder = nt(createFrame(parent, { Name = "Mark", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size), Rotation = 40 }))
		local dim = col:Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(90, 90, 100), 0.45)
		local function bx(x, y, w, h, r, c, rot)
			local b = nt(createFrame(holder, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x * k, y * k), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(1, w * k), math.max(1, h * k)), BackgroundColor3 = c or col, Rotation = rot or 0, ZIndex = 52 }))
			corner(b, r * k)
			return b
		end
		bx(5.5, 0.4, 9, 2.2, 1.1, dim)
		bx(5.5, 0.4, 2.2, 5, 1.1, dim)
		bx(12.3, 0.4, 2.2, 5, 1.1, dim)
		bx(9, 2, 2, 8.5, 0.9)
		bx(8, 9, 4, 2.4, 0.8, dim)
		local blade = bx(5.6, 10.6, 8.8, 9, 4.4)
		bx(8.6, 11.6, 2.8, 5.8, 1.4, dim, 0).BackgroundTransparency = 0.7
		return holder
	end
	function State.drawMine(parent, size, hot)
		local k = size / 20
		local holder = nt(createFrame(parent, { Name = "Mark", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size) }))
		local function bx(x, y, w, h, c, r, rot, tr)
			local b = nt(createFrame(holder, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x * k, y * k), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(1, w * k), math.max(1, h * k)), BackgroundColor3 = c, BackgroundTransparency = tr or 0, Rotation = rot or 0 }))
			corner(b, r * k)
			return b
		end
		local dark = Color3.fromRGB(22, 22, 30)
		for _, rot in ipairs({ 0, 45, 90, 135 }) do bx(0.6, 9, 18.8, 2, dark, 1, rot) end
		for _, a in ipairs({ { 0.2, 9.1 }, { 17.9, 9.1 }, { 9.1, 0.2 }, { 9.1, 17.9 } }) do bx(a[1], a[2], 1.9, 1.9, dark, 1) end
		local body = bx(3.2, 3.2, 13.6, 13.6, dark, 7)
		create("UIGradient", { Rotation = 45, Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(110, 112, 134), Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 16)) }, body)
		bx(5.6, 5.4, 4.2, 2.4, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 1.2, -35, 0.35)
		bx(5.3, 5.2, 1.6, 1.6, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.8, 0, 0.05)
		if hot then bx(8.2, 8.2, 3.6, 3.6, Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 60), 1.8, 0, 0.1) end
		return holder
	end

	local function sym(parent, kind, color, s)
		local rot = ({ left = 0, up = 90, right = 180, down = 270 })[kind] or 0
		local h = nt(createFrame(parent, { Name = "Sym", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(s, s), Rotation = rot }))
		local t = math.max(2.5, s * 0.15)
		local function bar(x, y, w, r)
			local b = nt(createFrame(h, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x * s, y * s), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w * s, t), Rotation = r or 0, BackgroundColor3 = color }))
			corner(b, t)
		end
		local function dot(x, y)
			local d = nt(createFrame(h, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x * s, y * s), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(t * 1.7, t * 1.7), BackgroundColor3 = color }))
			corner(d, t)
		end
		if kind == "+" then bar(0.5, 0.5, 0.95) bar(0.5, 0.5, 0.95, 90)
		elseif kind == "-" then bar(0.5, 0.5, 0.95)
		elseif kind == "×" then bar(0.5, 0.5, 1.2, 45) bar(0.5, 0.5, 1.2, -45)
		elseif kind == "÷" then bar(0.5, 0.5, 0.95) dot(0.5, 0.17) dot(0.5, 0.83)
		elseif kind == "=" then bar(0.5, 0.32, 0.95) bar(0.5, 0.68, 0.95)
		elseif kind == "del" then bar(0.56, 0.5, 0.82) bar(0.22, 0.34, 0.42, -45) bar(0.22, 0.66, 0.42, 45)
		else bar(0.5, 0.34, 0.5, -45) bar(0.5, 0.66, 0.5, 45) end
		return h
	end

	local function trashIcon(parent, color, s)
		local h = nt(createFrame(parent, { Name = "TrashIcon", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(s, s) }))
		h:SetAttribute("Trash", true)
		local t = math.max(1.5, s * 0.11)
		local function bar(x, y, w, hh, r)
			local b = nt(createFrame(h, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x * s, y * s), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(1, w * s), math.max(1, hh)), BackgroundColor3 = color }))
			corner(b, r or 1)
			return b
		end
		bar(0.5, 0.2, 0.34, t)
		bar(0.5, 0.3, 0.92, t)
		local body = nt(createFrame(h, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0.5 * s, 0.38 * s), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0.68 * s, 0.56 * s), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }))
		corner(body, math.max(2, s * 0.14))
		stroke(body, color, t)
		bar(0.38, 0.66, 0.0, s * 0.26, 1).Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(1.2, s * 0.08), s * 0.26)
		bar(0.62, 0.66, 0.0, s * 0.26, 1).Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(1.2, s * 0.08), s * 0.26)
		return h
	end
	local function arrowBtn(parent, x, y, w, h, dir, cb)
		local b = pbtn(parent, x, y, w, h, "", cb)
		sym(b, dir, C.text, 14)
		return b
	end

	local function buildMines(f)
		local N, CS = 8, 22
		local LEVELS = { { "easy", 8 }, { "medium", 10 }, { "hard", 14 } }
		local level, M = 2, 10
		local FLAGC = Color3.fromRGB(255, 100, 100)
		local NUMC = { Color3.fromRGB(90, 160, 255), Color3.fromRGB(60, 200, 110), Color3.fromRGB(255, 90, 90), Color3.fromRGB(170, 110, 255), Color3.fromRGB(255, 170, 60), Color3.fromRGB(60, 200, 200), Color3.fromRGB(230, 230, 230), Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150) }
		local nb = {}
		for i = 1, N * N do
			local r, c = math.floor((i - 1) / N) + 1, (i - 1) % N + 1
			local list = {}
			for dr = -1, 1 do
				for dc = -1, 1 do
					local rr, cc = r + dr, c + dc
					if (dr ~= 0 or dc ~= 0) and rr >= 1 and rr <= N and cc >= 1 and cc <= N then table.insert(list, (rr - 1) * N + cc) end
				end
			end
			nb[i] = list
		end

		local mine, adj, rev, flag = {}, {}, {}, {}
		local started, over, flagMode, revealed, flags, t0 = false, false, false, 0, 0, 0
		local cells, downAt = {}, {}

		local flagHold = nt(createFrame(f, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(2, 1), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18) }))
		State.drawFlag(flagHold, 16, FLAGC)
		local counter = pl(f, "", 13, C.text, 24, 0, 70, 20, nil, FONT_BOLD)
		local timeLbl = pl(f, "0s", 12, C.sub, AW - 94, 0, 94, 20, RIGHT, FONT_BOLD)
		local msg = pl(f, "", 11, C.sub, 0, 250, AW, 18, CENTER)
		local bestL = pl(f, "", 10, C.dim, 0, 270, AW, 14, CENTER)
		pl(f, "hold a tile or use flag mode to flag", 10, C.dim, 0, 286, AW, 14, CENTER)

		local function showBest()
			local b = scores["mines_" .. LEVELS[level][1]]
			bestL.Text = LEVELS[level][1] .. "  -  best " .. (b and (b .. "s") or "-")
		end
		local gen = 0
		local function setMark(i, kind, color, hot)
			local old = cells[i]:FindFirstChild("Mark")
			if old then old:Destroy() end
			if kind then
				local h = (kind == "flag") and State.drawFlag(cells[i], 17, color) or State.drawMine(cells[i], 17, hot)
				local sc = create("UIScale", { Scale = 0.2 }, h)
				tween(sc, 0.25, { Scale = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
			end
		end
		local function paint(k, delay)
			local c = cells[k]
			c.Text = adj[k] > 0 and tostring(adj[k]) or ""
			c.TextColor3 = NUMC[adj[k]] or C.text
			c.TextTransparency = 1
			local g, pop = gen, c:FindFirstChild("Pop")
			task.delay(delay or 0, function()
				if g ~= gen or not pop then return end
				State.stopTweens(c)
				State.stopTweens(pop)
				c.BackgroundColor3 = C.accent:Lerp(C.line, 0.3)
				pop.Scale = 0.5
				tween(pop, 0.34, { Scale = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
				tween(c, 0.5, { BackgroundColor3 = C.panel, TextTransparency = 0 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
			end)
		end
		local function reset()
			mine, adj, rev, flag = {}, {}, {}, {}
			started, over, revealed, flags = false, false, 0, 0
			gen += 1
			table.clear(downAt)
			M = LEVELS[level][2]
			for i = 1, N * N do
				State.stopTweens(cells[i])
				local pop = cells[i]:FindFirstChild("Pop")
				if pop then State.stopTweens(pop) pop.Scale = 1 end
				cells[i].TextTransparency = 0
				cells[i].Text, cells[i].BackgroundColor3, cells[i].TextColor3 = "", C.line, C.text
				setMark(i, nil)
			end
			counter.Text = tostring(M)
			timeLbl.Text = "0s"
			msg.Text = "tap a tile to start"
			showBest()
		end
		local function plant(safe)
			local ban = { [safe] = true }
			for _, j in ipairs(nb[safe]) do ban[j] = true end
			local pool = {}
			for i = 1, N * N do if not ban[i] then table.insert(pool, i) end end
			mine = {}
			for _ = 1, M do mine[table.remove(pool, math.random(#pool))] = true end
			for i = 1, N * N do
				local n = 0
				for _, j in ipairs(nb[i]) do if mine[j] then n += 1 end end
				adj[i] = n
			end
		end
		local function reveal(i)
			local queue, head, depth = { i }, 1, { [i] = 0 }
			while head <= #queue do
				local k = queue[head]
				head += 1
				if not rev[k] and not flag[k] then
					rev[k] = true
					revealed += 1
					paint(k, depth[k] * 0.05)
					if adj[k] == 0 then
						for _, j in ipairs(nb[k]) do
							if not rev[j] and not flag[j] and depth[j] == nil then
								depth[j] = depth[k] + 1
								table.insert(queue, j)
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
		local function lose(hit)
			over = true
			local g = gen
			local hr, hc = math.floor((hit - 1) / N), (hit - 1) % N
			for k = 1, N * N do
				if mine[k] and not flag[k] then
					local d = math.sqrt((math.floor((k - 1) / N) - hr) ^ 2 + ((k - 1) % N - hc) ^ 2)
					task.delay(d * 0.06, function()
						if g ~= gen then return end
						cells[k].BackgroundColor3 = (k == hit) and C.red or C.red:Lerp(C.field, 0.55)
						setMark(k, "bomb", C.white, k == hit)
					end)
				elseif flag[k] and not mine[k] then
					cells[k].BackgroundColor3 = C.red:Lerp(C.field, 0.6)
					setMark(k, nil)
					cells[k].Text, cells[k].TextColor3 = "X", C.white
				end
			end
			timeLbl.Text = math.floor(os.clock() - t0) .. "s"
			msg.Text = "boom! you hit a mine"
		end
		local function win()
			over = true
			for k = 1, N * N do
				if mine[k] then
					local g = gen
					task.delay((k % N + math.floor(k / N)) * 0.025, function() if g == gen then setMark(k, "flag", C.green) end end)
				end
			end
			counter.Text = "0"
			local t = math.floor(os.clock() - t0)
			timeLbl.Text = t .. "s"
			local fresh = record("mines_" .. LEVELS[level][1], t, true)
			msg.Text = "cleared in " .. t .. "s" .. (fresh and " - new best!" or "")
			showBest()
		end
		local function toggleFlag(i)
			if over or rev[i] then return end
			if flag[i] then
				flag[i] = nil
				flags -= 1
				setMark(i, nil)
			else
				flag[i] = true
				flags += 1
				setMark(i, "flag", FLAGC)
			end
			counter.Text = tostring(M - flags)
		end
		local function tap(i)
			if over then return end
			if flagMode then toggleFlag(i) return end
			if flag[i] then return end
			if not started then
				started, t0 = true, os.clock()
				plant(i)
			end
			if rev[i] then
				if adj[i] > 0 then
					local fl = 0
					for _, j in ipairs(nb[i]) do if flag[j] then fl += 1 end end
					if fl == adj[i] then
						local hit
						for _, j in ipairs(nb[i]) do
							if not flag[j] and not rev[j] then
								if mine[j] then hit = hit or j else reveal(j) end
							end
						end
						if hit then lose(hit) return end
					end
				end
			else
				if mine[i] then lose(i) return end
				reveal(i)
			end
			if revealed == N * N - M then win() end
		end

		local grid = createFrame(f, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(1, 58), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(N * (CS + 1) - 1, N * (CS + 1) - 1) })
		local longDone = {}
		for i = 1, N * N do
			local r, c = math.floor((i - 1) / N) + 1, (i - 1) % N + 1
			local x0, y0 = (c - 1) * (CS + 1), (r - 1) * (CS + 1)
			local cell = nl(createButton(grid, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x0, y0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(CS, CS), BackgroundColor3 = C.line, BackgroundTransparency = 0, TextSize = 13, Font = FONT_BOLD, TextColor3 = C.text, Active = false, AutoButtonColor = false }))
			corner(cell, 5)
			create("UIScale", { Name = "Pop" }, cell)
			cells[i] = cell

			local hb = nt(createButton(grid, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x0, y0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(CS + 1, CS + 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", ZIndex = 5 }))
			hb.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function()
				longDone[i] = false
				local stamp = os.clock()
				downAt[i] = stamp
				task.delay(0.45, function()
					if downAt[i] == stamp and not over and not rev[i] then
						longDone[i] = true
						toggleFlag(i)
					end
				end)
			end)
			hb.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function() downAt[i] = nil end)
			hb.MouseLeave:Connect(function() downAt[i] = nil end)
			hb.Activated:Connect(function()
				if longDone[i] then longDone[i] = false return end
				tap(i)
			end)
			hb.MouseButton2Click:Connect(function() toggleFlag(i) end)
		end

		local modeBtn, lvlBtn

		local modeIcons = {}
		local thumb
		local function paintMode()
			if thumb then tween(thumb, 0.22, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(flagMode and 30 or 2, 2), BackgroundColor3 = flagMode and FLAGC or C.accent }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out) end
			for i, ic in ipairs(modeIcons) do
				local on = (i == 2) == flagMode
				tween(ic.UIScale, 0.18, { Scale = on and 1.1 or 0.85 })
			end
		end
		modeBtn = pbtn(f, 0, 24, 58, 26, "", function()
			flagMode = not flagMode
			paintMode()
		end)
		thumb = nt(createFrame(modeBtn, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(2, 2), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(26, 22), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent, BackgroundTransparency = 0.45, ZIndex = 50 }))
		corner(thumb, 11)
		for i = 1, 2 do
			local slot = nt(createFrame(modeBtn, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(i == 1 and 15 or 43, 13), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18), ZIndex = 51 }))
			create("UIScale", {}, slot)
			if i == 1 then State.drawShovel(slot, 18, Color3.new(1, 1, 1)) else State.drawFlag(slot, 16, Color3.new(1, 1, 1)) end
			modeIcons[i] = slot
		end
		paintMode()
		lvlBtn = pbtn(f, 64, 24, 58, 26, "medium", function()
			level = level % #LEVELS + 1
			lvlBtn.Text = LEVELS[level][1]
			reset()
		end)
		pbtn(f, 128, 24, 58, 26, "new", reset, true)
		task.spawn(function()
			while gui.Parent do
				task.wait(0.5)
				if started and not over then timeLbl.Text = math.floor(os.clock() - t0) .. "s" end
			end
		end)
		reset()
	end

	local function buildBomb(f)
		local COLS, ROWS, BOMBS, SAFE = 3, 4, 3, 9
		local BW, BH = 56, 52
		local bombs, opened, picks, roundPts, over, gen = {}, {}, 0, 0, false, 0
		local banked, best = 0, scores.bomb or 0
		local boxes, strokes = {}, {}
		local BACK, OUT = Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out
		local scoreL = pl(f, "", 12, C.text, 0, 0, AW, 18, nil, FONT_BOLD)
		local roundL = pl(f, "", 11, C.sub, 0, 20, AW - 64, 16)
		local riskL = pl(f, "", 10, C.sub, AW - 64, 20, 64, 16, RIGHT)
		local riskTrack = nt(createFrame(f, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 38), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(AW, 4), BackgroundColor3 = C.line }))
		corner(riskTrack, 2)
		local riskFill = nt(createFrame(riskTrack, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), BackgroundColor3 = C.green }))
		corner(riskFill, 2)
		local gridF = createFrame(f, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 44), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(AW, 232) })
		local flash = nt(createFrame(gridF, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 150), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ZIndex = 20 }))
		corner(flash, 10)
		local msg = pl(f, "", 11, C.text, 0, 284, AW, 18, CENTER)
		local cashBtn
		local riskNow = 0

		local function center(i) local r, c = math.floor((i - 1) / COLS), (i - 1) % COLS return c * 65 + BW / 2, r * 60 + BH / 2 end
		local function riskColor(r) return C.green:Lerp(C.red, math.clamp(r * 2, 0, 1)) end
		local function refresh()
			scoreL.Text = ("banked %d   best %d"):format(banked, best)
			roundL.Text = ("round %d pts  -  %d/%d safe"):format(roundPts, picks, SAFE)
			riskNow = (not over and picks < SAFE) and BOMBS / (ROWS * COLS - picks) or 0
			riskL.Text = over and "" or ("risk %d%%"):format(math.floor(riskNow * 100 + 0.5))
			tween(riskFill, 0.25, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(riskNow, 1), BackgroundColor3 = riskColor(riskNow) })
		end
		local function burst(x, y, cols, n, spread)
			for _ = 1, n do
				local p = nt(createFrame(gridF, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 4), BackgroundColor3 = cols[math.random(#cols)], ZIndex = 18, Rotation = math.random(0, 90) }))
				corner(p, 1)
				local a, d = math.random() * math.pi * 2, spread * (0.5 + math.random() * 0.7)
				tween(p, 0.55, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x + math.cos(a) * d, y + math.sin(a) * d + 8), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Rotation = math.random(-200, 200), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(1, 1) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, OUT)
				task.delay(0.6, function() p:Destroy() end)
			end
		end
		local function floatText(i, text, col)
			local x, y = center(i)
			local l = nl(createLabel(gridF, text, 13, col, FONT_BOLD, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(BW, 16), TextXAlignment = CENTER, NoTitle = true, ZIndex = 15 }))
			tween(l, 0.7, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y - 26), TextTransparency = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, OUT)
			task.delay(0.75, function() l:Destroy() end)
		end
		local function shake(power)
			task.spawn(function()
				local base = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 44)
				for n = 1, 12 do
					local a = power * (13 - n) / 12
					gridF.Position = base + UDim2.fromOffset((math.random() * 2 - 1) * a, (math.random() * 2 - 1) * a)
					task.wait(0.03)
				end
				gridF.Position = base
			end)
		end
		local function showBomb(k, lit, delay)
			task.delay(delay, function()
				local b = boxes[k]
				if not b.Parent or not bombs[k] then return end
				b.Text, b.TextTransparency, b.BackgroundTransparency = "", 0, 0
				tween(b, 0.15, { BackgroundColor3 = lit and C.red or Color3.fromRGB(72, 30, 36) })
				local h = State.drawBomb(b, 32, lit)
				local sc = create("UIScale", { Scale = 0.2 }, h)
				tween(sc, 0.3, { Scale = 1 }, BACK, OUT)
				if not lit then local cx, cy = center(k) burst(cx, cy, { Color3.fromRGB(255, 160, 60), Color3.fromRGB(90, 90, 100) }, 5, 16) end
			end)
		end
		local function flip(i, apply)
			local b = boxes[i]
			tween(b, 0.07, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, BH) })
			task.delay(0.08, function()
				if not b.Parent then return end
				apply()
				tween(b, 0.22, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(BW, BH) }, BACK, OUT)
			end)
		end

		local function newRound()
			gen += 1
			local g = gen
			bombs, opened, picks, roundPts, over = {}, {}, 0, 0, false
			local pool = {}
			for i = 1, ROWS * COLS do pool[i] = i end
			for _ = 1, BOMBS do bombs[table.remove(pool, math.random(#pool))] = true end
			for i = 1, ROWS * COLS do
				local b = boxes[i]
				b.Text, b.BackgroundColor3, b.TextColor3, b.TextTransparency, b.BackgroundTransparency = "?", C.field, C.text, 0, 0
				strokes[i].Transparency = 1
				local mk = b:FindFirstChild("Mark")
				if mk then mk:Destroy() end
				b.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0)
				task.delay((i - 1) * 0.03, function()
					if g == gen and b.Parent then tween(b, 0.28, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(BW, BH) }, BACK, OUT) end
				end)
			end
			cashBtn.Text = "cash out"
			msg.Text = "don't pick the bomb!"
			refresh()
		end
		local function pick(i)
			if over or opened[i] then return end
			opened[i] = true
			local x, y = center(i)
			if bombs[i] then
				over = true
				showBomb(i, true, 0.08)
				task.delay(0.08, function()
					flash.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
					tween(flash, 0.4, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
					local ring = nt(createFrame(gridF, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 10), ZIndex = 16 }))
					corner(ring, 999)
					local rs = stroke(ring, Color3.fromRGB(255, 190, 80), 3)
					tween(ring, 0.45, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(150, 150) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, OUT)
					tween(rs, 0.45, { Transparency = 1, Thickness = 1 })
					task.delay(0.5, function() ring:Destroy() end)
					burst(x, y, { Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 90), Color3.fromRGB(255, 130, 40), Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 60), Color3.new(1, 1, 1) }, 22, 70)
					shake(7)
				end)
				local n = 0
				for k = 1, ROWS * COLS do
					if bombs[k] and k ~= i then n += 1 showBomb(k, false, 0.2 + n * 0.14) end
					if not bombs[k] and not opened[k] then tween(boxes[k], 0.3, { TextTransparency = 0.75, BackgroundTransparency = 0.5 }) end
				end
				msg.Text = "boom! lost " .. roundPts .. " pts"
				cashBtn.Text = "new round"
			else
				picks += 1
				roundPts += picks * 10
				local pts = picks * 10
				flip(i, function()
					boxes[i].Text, boxes[i].BackgroundColor3, boxes[i].TextColor3 = "+" .. pts, C.panel, C.green
					strokes[i].Transparency = 0
					tween(strokes[i], 0.6, { Transparency = 1 })
				end)
				task.delay(0.1, function() floatText(i, "+" .. pts, C.green) burst(x, y, { C.green, Color3.new(1, 1, 1) }, 7, 30) end)
				msg.Text = "safe! cash out or keep going"
				if picks == SAFE then
					local gain = roundPts + 50
					banked += gain
					best = math.max(best, banked) record("bomb", best)
					over = true
					msg.Text = "perfect round! +" .. gain
					cashBtn.Text = "new round"
					task.delay(0.15, function()
						for _ = 1, 4 do burst(AW / 2, 110, { C.green, C.accent, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Color3.fromRGB(255, 214, 10) }, 10, 110) end
					end)
				end
			end
			refresh()
		end

		for i = 1, ROWS * COLS do
			local r, c = math.floor((i - 1) / COLS), (i - 1) % COLS
			local b = nl(createButton(gridF, { Sound = true, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(c * 65 + BW / 2, r * 60 + BH / 2), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(BW, BH), BackgroundColor3 = C.field, BackgroundTransparency = 0, TextSize = 14, Font = FONT_BOLD, TextColor3 = C.text }))
			corner(b, 10)
			strokes[i] = stroke(b, C.green, 2, 1)
			boxes[i] = b
			b.Activated:Connect(function() pick(i) end)
		end
		cashBtn = pbtn(f, 0, 308, AW, 30, "cash out", function()
			if over then newRound() return end
			if picks == 0 then msg.Text = "pick a box first" return end
			local got = roundPts
			banked += got
			best = math.max(best, banked) record("bomb", best)
			burst(AW / 2, 220, { Color3.fromRGB(255, 214, 10), Color3.fromRGB(255, 240, 150), Color3.new(1, 1, 1) }, 14, 80)
			newRound()
			msg.Text = "banked " .. got .. " pts"
			refresh()
			scoreL.TextColor3 = C.green
			tween(scoreL, 0.7, { TextColor3 = C.text })
		end, true)

		local pulse
		pulse = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			if not f.Parent then pulse:Disconnect() return end
			riskFill.BackgroundTransparency = (not over and riskNow >= 0.4) and (0.5 + 0.5 * math.sin(os.clock() * 8)) * 0.5 or 0
		end)
		table.insert(conns, pulse)
		newRound()
	end

	local function buildTic(f)
		local LINES = { { 1, 2, 3 }, { 4, 5, 6 }, { 7, 8, 9 }, { 1, 4, 7 }, { 2, 5, 8 }, { 3, 6, 9 }, { 1, 5, 9 }, { 3, 5, 7 } }
		local board, over, gen, cells, cellStroke, marks = {}, false, 0, {}, {}, {}
		local wins, losses, draws, streak = 0, 0, 0, 0
		local diff, twoP, human = 3, false, "X"
		local turn, busy = "X", false
		local XC, OC = C.accent, C.green
		local WHITE = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
		local function markColor(m) return m == "X" and XC or OC end

		local function drawMark(parent, kind, size, col, glow)
			local box = nt(createFrame(parent, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }))
			local th = math.max(2, size * 0.17)
			if glow then
				local g = nt(createFrame(box, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size * 1.35, size * 1.35), BackgroundColor3 = col, BackgroundTransparency = 0.86 }))
				corner(g, size * 0.675)
			end
			if kind == "X" then
				for _, rot in ipairs({ 45, -45 }) do
					local bar = nt(createFrame(box, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(th, size * 1.08), BackgroundColor3 = col, Rotation = rot }))
					corner(bar, th / 2)
					create("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Color = ColorSequence.new(col:Lerp(WHITE, 0.35), col) }, bar)
				end
			else
				local ring = nt(createFrame(box, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size * 0.92, size * 0.92), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }))
				corner(ring, size)
				stroke(ring, col, th)
			end
			return box
		end
		local iconBtn = State.pIconBtn

		local boards = {}
		for k = 1, 3 do
			local card = createFrame(f, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset((k - 1) * 64, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(58, 34), BackgroundColor3 = C.field, BackgroundTransparency = 0 })
			corner(card, 9)
			local chip = nt(createFrame(card, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(9, 8), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }))
			boards[k] = { name = pl(card, "", 9, C.sub, 19, 3, 37, 11), num = pl(card, "0", 16, C.text, 0, 14, 58, 19, CENTER, FONT_BOLD), card = card, chip = chip }
			create("UIStroke", { Color = C.line, Thickness = 1, Transparency = 0.4 }, card)
		end
		local function chipMark(k, kind)
			local ch = boards[k].chip
			for _, c in ipairs(ch:GetChildren()) do c:Destroy() end
			if kind == "D" then
				for dy = -2, 2, 4 do
					local bar = nt(createFrame(ch, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, dy), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(9, 2), BackgroundColor3 = C.sub }))
					corner(bar, 1)
				end
			else
				drawMark(ch, kind, 9, markColor(kind))
			end
		end
		local function score()
			local mine = twoP and "X" or human
			local theirs = mine == "X" and "O" or "X"
			boards[1].name.Text = twoP and "Player" or "You"
			boards[2].name.Text = "Draw"
			boards[3].name.Text = twoP and "Player" or "Phone"
			boards[1].num.Text, boards[2].num.Text, boards[3].num.Text = tostring(wins), tostring(draws), tostring(losses)
			boards[1].num.TextColor3, boards[3].num.TextColor3 = markColor(mine), markColor(theirs)
			chipMark(1, mine)
			chipMark(2, "D")
			chipMark(3, theirs)
		end

		local diffBtns = {}
		local function paintDiff()
			for k, b in ipairs(diffBtns) do
				local on = (k == diff) and not twoP
				b.BackgroundColor3 = on and C.accent or C.field
				b.TextColor3 = on and C.bg or (twoP and C.dim or C.text)
			end
		end
		local status = pl(f, "", 12, C.sub, 0, 70, AW, 16, CENTER)

		local function winnerOf(b)
			for _, l in ipairs(LINES) do
				local a = b[l[1]]
				if a and a == b[l[2]] and a == b[l[3]] then return a, l end
			end
		end
		local function empties(b)
			local e = {}
			for i = 1, 9 do if not b[i] then table.insert(e, i) end end
			return e
		end
		local function findMove(b, mark)
			for _, l in ipairs(LINES) do
				local cnt, empty = 0, nil
				for _, i in ipairs(l) do
					if b[i] == mark then cnt += 1 elseif not b[i] then empty = i end
				end
				if cnt == 2 and empty then return empty end
			end
		end
		local function minimax(b, who, ai, hu, depth)
			local w = winnerOf(b)
			if w == ai then return 10 - depth end
			if w == hu then return depth - 10 end
			local e = empties(b)
			if #e == 0 then return 0 end
			local best = (who == ai) and -100 or 100
			for _, i in ipairs(e) do
				b[i] = who
				local v = minimax(b, who == "X" and "O" or "X", ai, hu, depth + 1)
				b[i] = nil
				if who == ai then best = math.max(best, v) else best = math.min(best, v) end
			end
			return best
		end
		local function bestMove(ai)
			local hu = ai == "X" and "O" or "X"
			local e = empties(board)
			if #e == 9 then return ({ 1, 3, 5, 7, 9 })[math.random(5)] end
			local top, picks = -1000, {}
			for _, i in ipairs(e) do
				board[i] = ai
				local v = minimax(board, hu, ai, hu, 1)
				board[i] = nil
				if v > top then top, picks = v, { i } elseif v == top then table.insert(picks, i) end
			end
			return picks[math.random(#picks)]
		end
		local function heuristicMove(ai)
			local hu = ai == "X" and "O" or "X"
			local e = empties(board)
			local m = findMove(board, ai) or findMove(board, hu)
			if m then return m end
			if not board[5] then return 5 end
			local corners = {}
			for _, i in ipairs({ 1, 3, 7, 9 }) do if not board[i] then table.insert(corners, i) end end
			if #corners > 0 and math.random() < 0.7 then return corners[math.random(#corners)] end
			return e[math.random(#e)]
		end
		local function aiMove(ai)
			local e = empties(board)
			if diff == 1 then
				return (math.random() < 0.25 and findMove(board, ai)) or e[math.random(#e)]
			elseif diff == 2 then
				if math.random() < 0.2 then return e[math.random(#e)] end
				return heuristicMove(ai)
			end
			return bestMove(ai)
		end

		local lastMove
		local strike, gridFrame
		local function place(i, mark)
			board[i] = mark
			local c = cells[i]
			if marks[i] then marks[i]:Destroy() end
			local mk = drawMark(c, mark, 30, markColor(mark), true)
			local sc = create("UIScale", { Scale = 0.1 }, mk)
			tween(sc, 0.32, { Scale = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
			marks[i] = mk
			if lastMove and lastMove ~= i then tween(cellStroke[lastMove], 0.2, { Color = C.line, Thickness = 1 }) end
			lastMove = i
			tween(cellStroke[i], 0.2, { Color = markColor(mark), Thickness = 1.5 })
		end
		local function turnText()
			if twoP then return turn .. "'s Turn" end
			return turn == human and ("Your Turn (" .. human .. ")") or "Phone Is Thinking..."
		end
		local function check()
			local w, l = winnerOf(board)
			if w then
				over = true
				local col = markColor(w)
				if twoP then
					if w == "X" then wins += 1 else losses += 1 end
					status.Text = w .. " Wins!"
				elseif w == human then
					wins += 1 streak += 1
					record("tic_streak", streak)
					status.Text = "You Win! Streak " .. streak .. "  (Best " .. (scores.tic_streak or streak) .. ")"
				else
					losses += 1 streak = 0
					status.Text = "Phone Wins"
				end
				for _, i in ipairs(l) do
					tween(cellStroke[i], 0.2, { Color = col, Thickness = 2.5 })
					tween(cells[i], 0.3, { BackgroundColor3 = col:Lerp(C.field, 0.72) })
				end
				local function cen(i) return Vector2.new(((i - 1) % 3) * 64 + 29, math.floor((i - 1) / 3) * 64 + 29) end
				local a, z = cen(l[1]), cen(l[3])
				local d = z - a
				strike = nt(createFrame(gridFrame, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset((a.X + z.X) / 2, (a.Y + z.Y) / 2), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 5), BackgroundColor3 = col, Rotation = math.deg(math.atan2(d.Y, d.X)), ZIndex = 6 }))
				corner(strike, 3)
				tween(strike, 0.35, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(d.Magnitude + 34, 5) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
			elseif #empties(board) == 0 then
				over = true
				draws += 1
				if not twoP then streak = 0 end
				status.Text = "Draw"
			end
			if over then
				status.TextColor3 = C.text
				score()
			end
			return over
		end

		local function phoneTurn(my)
			busy = true
			status.Text = "Phone Is Thinking..."
			task.delay(0.3 + math.random() * 0.25, function()
				if over or gen ~= my then return end
				local ai = human == "X" and "O" or "X"
				place(aiMove(ai), ai)
				busy = false
				if not check() then
					turn = human
					status.Text = turnText()
				end
			end)
		end
		local function newGame()
			gen += 1
			board, over, busy, lastMove, turn = {}, false, false, nil, "X"
			status.TextColor3 = C.sub
			if strike then strike:Destroy() strike = nil end
			for i = 1, 9 do
				if marks[i] then marks[i]:Destroy() marks[i] = nil end
				tween(cellStroke[i], 0.15, { Color = C.line, Thickness = 1 })
				tween(cells[i], 0.15, { BackgroundColor3 = C.field })
			end
			score()
			paintDiff()
			status.Text = turnText()
			if not twoP and human == "O" then phoneTurn(gen) end
		end

		for k, nm in ipairs({ "Easy", "Medium", "Hard" }) do
			local ib = iconBtn(f, (k - 1) * 64, 40, 58, 24, nm, function()
				if twoP then return end
				diff = k
				wins, draws, losses, streak = 0, 0, 0, 0
				newGame()
			end, ({ "smile", "meh", "flame" })[k], true)
			diffBtns[k] = ib.btn
		end

		local grid = createFrame(f, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 92), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(186, 186) })
		gridFrame = grid
		for i = 1, 9 do
			local r, c = math.floor((i - 1) / 3), (i - 1) % 3
			local b = nl(createButton(grid, { Sound = true, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(c * 64, r * 64), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(58, 58), BackgroundColor3 = C.field, BackgroundTransparency = 0, TextSize = 30, Font = FONT_BOLD, TextColor3 = C.text }))
			corner(b, 10)
			create("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Color = ColorSequence.new(WHITE, Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 205)) }, b)
			cellStroke[i] = stroke(b, C.line, 1)
			cells[i] = b
			b.MouseEnter:Connect(function() if not over and not board[i] and not busy then tween(cellStroke[i], 0.1, { Color = C.dim }) end end)
			b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() if not board[i] and not over then tween(cellStroke[i], 0.1, { Color = C.line }) end end)
			b.Activated:Connect(function()
				if over or board[i] or busy then return end
				if twoP then
					place(i, turn)
					if check() then return end
					turn = turn == "X" and "O" or "X"
					status.Text = turnText()
				else
					if turn ~= human then return end
					place(i, human)
					if check() then return end
					turn = human == "X" and "O" or "X"
					phoneTurn(gen)
				end
			end)
		end

		local modeO, sideO
		iconBtn(f, 0, 286, 46, 26, "New", function() newGame() end, "rotate-ccw")
		modeO = iconBtn(f, 52, 286, 82, 26, "vs Phone", function()
			twoP = not twoP
			modeO.set(twoP and "2 Players" or "vs Phone", twoP and "users" or "user")
			sideO.btn.Visible = not twoP
			wins, draws, losses, streak = 0, 0, 0, 0
			newGame()
		end, "user")
		local function sideIcon()
			for _, c in ipairs(sideO.slot:GetChildren()) do c:Destroy() end
			drawMark(sideO.slot, human, 10, markColor(human))
		end
		sideO = iconBtn(f, 140, 286, 46, 26, "You", function()
			human = human == "X" and "O" or "X"
			sideIcon()
			wins, draws, losses, streak = 0, 0, 0, 0
			newGame()
		end)
		sideIcon()
		newGame()
	end

	local function buildReflex(f)
		local state, t0, token = "idle", 0, 0
		local results = {}
		local panel = nl(createButton(f, { Sound = true, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(AW, 170), BackgroundColor3 = C.field, BackgroundTransparency = 0, TextSize = 18, Font = FONT_BOLD, TextColor3 = C.text, TextWrapped = true }))
		corner(panel, 16)
		panel.Text = "tap to start"
		local lastL = pl(f, "last: -", 12, C.text, 0, 184, AW, 18, nil, FONT_BOLD)
		local bestL = pl(f, "best: " .. (scores.reflex and (scores.reflex .. " ms") or "-"), 12, C.sub, 0, 204, AW, 18)
		local avgL = pl(f, "average (last 5): -", 12, C.sub, 0, 224, AW, 18)
		local function stats()
			local last, best, sum, n = results[#results], scores.reflex, 0, 0
			for _, v in ipairs(results) do best = best and math.min(best, v) or v end
			for i = math.max(1, #results - 4), #results do sum += results[i] n += 1 end
			if best then record("reflex", best, true) end
			lastL.Text = "last: " .. last .. " ms"
			bestL.Text = "best: " .. best .. " ms"
			avgL.Text = "average (last 5): " .. math.floor(sum / n) .. " ms"
		end
		panel.Activated:Connect(function()
			if state == "idle" then
				state, token = "wait", token + 1
				local my = token
				panel.Text = "wait for green..."
				panel.TextColor3 = C.text
				tween(panel, 0.15, { BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 45, 45) })
				task.delay(1 + math.random() * 3, function()
					if state == "wait" and token == my then
						state, t0 = "go", os.clock()
						panel.Text = "TAP!"
						panel.TextColor3 = C.bg
						panel.BackgroundColor3 = C.green
					end
				end)
			elseif state == "wait" then
				state, token = "idle", token + 1
				panel.Text = "too early!\ntap to retry"
				tween(panel, 0.15, { BackgroundColor3 = C.field })
			else
				state = "idle"
				local ms = math.floor((os.clock() - t0) * 1000)
				table.insert(results, ms)
				panel.Text = ms .. " ms\ntap to retry"
				panel.TextColor3 = C.text
				tween(panel, 0.15, { BackgroundColor3 = C.field })
				stats()
			end
		end)
	end

	local function buildNotes(f)
		local FILE, OLD = "NauruHub/phone_notes.json", "NauruHub/phone_notes.txt"
		local HS = game:GetService("HttpService")
		local YEL = Color3.fromRGB(255, 214, 10)
		local RED = Color3.fromRGB(255, 69, 58)
		local M = 12
		local W = SW - M * 2
		local FONTS = {}
		for _, nm in ipairs({ "GothamMedium", "GothamBold", "Arial", "SourceSans", "Roboto", "Nunito", "Ubuntu", "Oswald", "Merriweather", "Code", "RobotoMono", "Cartoon", "Fantasy", "Antique", "SciFi", "Highway", "Arcade", "Bangers", "IndieFlower", "PatrickHand", "Kalam", "PermanentMarker", "SpecialElite", "AmaticSC", "Fondamento", "Creepster", "Michroma", "Jura" }) do
			pcall(function() FONTS[#FONTS + 1] = { name = nm, label = (nm:gsub("Medium", "")), font = Enum.Font[nm] } end)
		end
		local COLS = { false, Color3.fromRGB(255, 110, 110), Color3.fromRGB(255, 170, 80), Color3.fromRGB(245, 215, 90), Color3.fromRGB(110, 210, 130), Color3.fromRGB(90, 200, 220), Color3.fromRGB(110, 150, 250), Color3.fromRGB(190, 130, 250), Color3.fromRGB(245, 130, 190) }
		local function fontOf(name) for _, ft in ipairs(FONTS) do if ft.name == name then return ft end end return FONTS[1] end

		local data, cur = { notes = {} }, nil
		pcall(function()
			if isfile and isfile(FILE) then
				local d = HS:JSONDecode(readfile(FILE))
				for _, n in ipairs(d.notes or {}) do
					n.images, n.font, n.size, n.color, n.t, n.text = n.images or {}, n.font or "GothamMedium", n.size or 13, n.color or 1, n.t or os.time(), n.text or ""
					table.insert(data.notes, n)
				end
			elseif isfile and isfile(OLD) then
				local txt = readfile(OLD)
				if txt ~= "" then table.insert(data.notes, { id = "old", text = txt, font = "GothamMedium", size = 13, color = 1, t = os.time(), images = {} }) end
			end
		end)

		local saveTok = 0
		local function writeNow()
			pcall(function()
				if not writefile then return end
				if not isfolder("NauruHub") then makefolder("NauruHub") end
				local out = { notes = {} }
				for _, n in ipairs(data.notes) do
					local imgs = {}
					for _, im in ipairs(n.images) do
						if im.t ~= "temp" then imgs[#imgs + 1] = { t = im.t, v = im.v, w = im.w, h = im.h } end
					end
					out.notes[#out.notes + 1] = { id = n.id, text = n.text, font = n.font, size = n.size, color = n.color, t = n.t, images = imgs }
				end
				writefile(FILE, HS:JSONEncode(out))
			end)
		end
		local function save(now)
			saveTok += 1
			local my = saveTok
			if now then writeNow() else task.delay(1.2, function() if my == saveTok then writeNow() end end) end
		end

		local root = createFrame(f, { Name = "NotesRoot", Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-8, -62), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(SW, SH) })
		local lv = createFrame(root, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = C.bg })
		local ev = createFrame(root, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = C.bg, Visible = false })

		local function navBtn(parent, x, w, text, cb, back, bold, alignRight)
			local b = nt(createButton(parent, { Sound = true, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, 31), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, 22) }))
			local tx = 0
			if back then
				tx = 14
				local h = nt(createFrame(b, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 22) }))
				sym(h, "left", YEL, 12)
			end
			nt(pl(b, text, 14, YEL, tx, 0, w - tx, 22, alignRight and RIGHT or nil, bold and FONT_BOLD or FONT))
			b.Activated:Connect(cb)
			return b
		end

		navBtn(lv, 6, 70, "Home", function() if State.phoneCloseApp then State.phoneCloseApp() end end, true)
		nl(createLabel(lv, "Notes", 26, C.text, FONT_BOLD, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(M + 2, 56), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W, 32), NoTitle = true }))

		local sbar = createFrame(lv, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(M, 92), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W, 28), BackgroundColor3 = C.field })
		corner(sbar, 9)
		local ring = nt(createFrame(sbar, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(9, 8), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(9, 9), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }))
		corner(ring, 99)
		stroke(ring, C.sub, 1.5)
		local handle = nt(createFrame(sbar, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(19, 18), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 2), Rotation = 45, BackgroundColor3 = C.sub }))
		corner(handle, 2)
		local searchBox = nl(create("TextBox", { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(26, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W - 34, 28), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", PlaceholderText = "Search", PlaceholderColor3 = C.sub, TextColor3 = C.text, Font = FONT, TextSize = 12, ClearTextOnFocus = false, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left }, sbar))

		local sStroke = stroke(sbar, YEL, 1.2, 1)
		searchBox.Focused:Connect(function() tween(sStroke, 0.15, { Transparency = 0.2 }) end)
		searchBox.FocusLost:Connect(function() tween(sStroke, 0.2, { Transparency = 1 }) end)

		local listSc = create("ScrollingFrame", { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 128), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(SW, 244), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ScrollBarThickness = 2, ScrollBarImageColor3 = C.dim, CanvasSize = UDim2.new(), AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y }, lv)
		create("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 3), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, listSc)
		create("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, M), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, M) }, listSc)

		createFrame(lv, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 374), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(SW, 1), BackgroundColor3 = C.line })
		local countL = nl(createLabel(lv, "", 10, C.sub, FONT, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 384), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(SW, 14), TextXAlignment = CENTER, NoTitle = true }))
		local comp = nt(createButton(lv, { Sound = true, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(SW - 48, 379), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(32, 26) }))
		local sq = nt(createFrame(comp, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -1, 0.5, 1), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }))
		corner(sq, 3)
