		stroke(sq, YEL, 1.6)
		local pen = nt(createFrame(comp, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 3, 0.5, -3), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 3), Rotation = -45, BackgroundColor3 = YEL }))
		corner(pen, 2)
		local compSc = create("UIScale", {}, comp)
		comp.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function() tween(compSc, 0.07, { Scale = 0.82 }) end)
		comp.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function()
			tween(compSc, 0.25, { Scale = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
			pen.Rotation = -70
			tween(pen, 0.4, { Rotation = -45 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Elastic, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
		end)
		comp.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tween(compSc, 0.1, { Scale = 1 }) end)

		local listRefresh
		local function dateText(n) return os.date("%d %B %Y at %H:%M", n and n.t or os.time()) end
		local dateL = nl(createLabel(ev, "", 10, C.sub, FONT, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 58), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(SW, 14), TextXAlignment = CENTER, NoTitle = true }))

		local sc = create("ScrollingFrame", { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(M, 78), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W, 256), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ScrollBarThickness = 2, ScrollBarImageColor3 = C.dim, CanvasSize = UDim2.new(), AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y }, ev)
		create("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 6), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, sc)
		local ed = nl(create("TextBox", {
			LayoutOrder = 0, BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 120), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, Text = "",
			PlaceholderText = "Start typing...", PlaceholderColor3 = C.sub, TextColor3 = C.text, Font = FONT, TextSize = 13,
			MultiLine = true, TextWrapped = true, ClearTextOnFocus = false,
			TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top,
		}, sc))
		notesBox = ed
		local status = nl(createLabel(ev, "", 9, C.dim, FONT, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 338), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(SW, 14), TextXAlignment = CENTER, NoTitle = true }))

		local loading = false
		local function refreshStatus(extra)
			if not cur then return end
			status.Text = extra or (#ed.Text .. " characters  -  " .. #cur.images .. " image" .. (#cur.images == 1 and "" or "s"))
		end

		local function loadPx(im)
			local ei = game:GetService("AssetService"):CreateEditableImage({ Size = Vector2.new(im.w, im.h) })
			ei:WritePixelsBuffer(Vector2.zero, Vector2.new(im.w, im.h), b64dec(readfile(im.v)))
			return ei
		end
		local function setImage(label, im)
			return (pcall(function()
				if im.t == "asset" then label.Image = im.v
				elseif im.t == "file" then label.Image = getcustomasset(im.v)
				elseif im.t == "px" then
					if im.ei then
						label.ImageContent = Content.fromObject(im.ei)
					else
						task.spawn(function()
							local ok = pcall(function()
								local ei = loadPx(im)
								im.ei = ei
								if label.Parent then label.ImageContent = Content.fromObject(ei) end
							end)
							if not ok and label.Parent then pl(label.Parent, "image unavailable", 10, C.dim, 0, 46, W, 16, CENTER) end
						end)
					end
				elseif im.kind == "capture" then label.ImageContent = Content.fromObject(im.v)
				else label.Image = im.v end
			end))
		end
		local function renderImages(animLast)
			for _, c in ipairs(sc:GetChildren()) do if c:GetAttribute("NoteImg") then c:Destroy() end end
			if not cur then return end
			for i, im in ipairs(cur.images) do
				local row = createFrame(sc, { LayoutOrder = i, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 112), BackgroundColor3 = C.field })
				row:SetAttribute("NoteImg", true)
				corner(row, 10)
				if animLast and i == #cur.images then
					local us = create("UIScale", { Scale = 0.8 }, row)
					tween(us, 0.4, { Scale = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
				end
				local il = nt(create("ImageLabel", { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit }, row))
				if not setImage(il, im) then pl(row, "image unavailable", 10, C.dim, 0, 46, W, 16, CENTER) end
				if im.t == "temp" then
					local tg = pl(row, "session only - not saved", 8, C.white, 6, 98, 130, 12)
					tg.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.4
				end
				local x = createButton(row, { Sound = true, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 4), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 20), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 0.35, TextSize = 11, Font = FONT_BOLD, TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1) })
				nt(x)
				x.Text = "x"
				corner(x, 10)
				x.Activated:Connect(function()
					local gone = table.remove(cur.images, i)
					if gone and gone.t == "px" and delfile then pcall(delfile, gone.v) end
					cur.t = os.time()
					renderImages()
					refreshStatus()
					save()
				end)
			end
		end
		local function attach(im)
			if not cur then return end
			table.insert(cur.images, im)
			cur.t = os.time()
			renderImages(true)
			refreshStatus()
			save()
			task.defer(function() sc.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 1e5) end)
		end

		local function persistPhoto(im)
			if not (writefile and buffer) then return false, "no writefile / buffer here" end
			return pcall(function()
				local content = (im.kind == "capture") and Content.fromObject(im.v) or Content.fromUri(im.v)
				local ei = game:GetService("AssetService"):CreateEditableImageAsync(content)
				local sz = ei.Size
				local w, h = sz.X, sz.Y
				if w > 300 then
					local nw, nh = 300, math.max(1, math.floor(sz.Y * 300 / sz.X))
					if pcall(function() ei:Resize(Vector2.new(nw, nh)) end) then w, h = nw, nh end
				end
				local txt = b64enc(buffer.tostring(ei:ReadPixelsBuffer(Vector2.zero, Vector2.new(w, h))))
				if not isfolder("NauruHub") then makefolder("NauruHub") end
				local path = "NauruHub/note_photo_" .. os.time() .. math.random(100, 999) .. ".txt"
				writefile(path, txt)
				if not (isfile and isfile(path)) then error("writefile did not create the file") end
				im.t, im.v, im.w, im.h, im.ei, im.kind = "px", path, w, h, ei, nil
			end)
		end

		local function parseAndAttach(raw)
			raw = (raw or ""):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
			if raw == "" then return end
			local id = raw:match("^%d+$")
			if id then attach({ t = "asset", v = "rbxassetid://" .. id }) return end
			if raw:match("^rbxassetid://%d+") or raw:match("^rbxasset") then attach({ t = "asset", v = raw }) return end
			if raw:find("roblox%.com") then
				local n = raw:match("[?&]id=(%d+)") or raw:match("asset/(%d+)") or raw:match("/(%d+)")
				if n then attach({ t = "asset", v = "rbxassetid://" .. n }) return end
			end
			if raw:match("^https?://") then
				local rq = request or http_request or (syn and syn.request)
				if not (rq and writefile and getcustomasset) then refreshStatus("urls need request + writefile + getcustomasset") return end
				refreshStatus("downloading...")
				task.spawn(function()
					local ok, res = pcall(rq, { Url = raw, Method = "GET" })
					local body = ok and res and res.Body
					if not body or #body < 16 then refreshStatus("download failed") return end
					local ext = (body:sub(1, 4) == "\137PNG") and ".png" or ((body:sub(1, 2) == "\255\216") and ".jpg" or nil)
					if not ext then refreshStatus("not a png / jpg image") return end
					pcall(function() if not isfolder("NauruHub") then makefolder("NauruHub") end end)
					local path = "NauruHub/note_img_" .. os.time() .. math.random(100, 999) .. ext
					local w = pcall(writefile, path, body)
					if not w then refreshStatus("could not save the image") return end
					attach({ t = "file", v = path })
				end)
				return
			end

			local cand = { "NauruHub/" .. raw, raw }
			for _, c in ipairs(cand) do
				local ok, has = pcall(function() return isfile and isfile(c) end)
				if ok and has then
					if getcustomasset then attach({ t = "file", v = c }) else refreshStatus("getcustomasset is not available here") end
					return
				end
			end
			refreshStatus("not found - use an asset id, url or file name")
		end

		local function applyStyle()
			if not cur then return end
			local ft = fontOf(cur.font)
			ed.Font = ft.font
			ed.TextSize = cur.size
			ed.TextColor3 = COLS[cur.color] or C.text
		end

		local function lift(fr) for _, d in ipairs(fr:GetDescendants()) do if d:IsA("GuiObject") then d.ZIndex = 21 end end end
		local PANEL_HIDE = 356
		local panelClip = createFrame(ev, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(M, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W, PANEL_HIDE), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ClipsDescendants = true, ZIndex = 20 })
		local function newPanel(y, h)
			local p = createFrame(panelClip, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, PANEL_HIDE), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W, h), BackgroundColor3 = C.panel, Visible = false, ZIndex = 20 })
			corner(p, 12)
			stroke(p, C.line, 1)
			p:SetAttribute("by", y)
			return p
		end
		local fmtPanel = newPanel(196, 158)
		local imgPanel = newPanel(278, 74)
		local photoPanel = newPanel(284, 68)
		local panels = { fmtPanel, imgPanel, photoPanel }
		local function isOpen(p) return p:GetAttribute("open") == true end
		local function popPanel(p, on)
			if isOpen(p) == on and (on or not p.Visible) then return end
			p:SetAttribute("open", on)
			local tok = (p:GetAttribute("tok") or 0) + 1
			p:SetAttribute("tok", tok)
			State.stopTweens(p)
			if on then
				p.Visible = true
				local by = p:GetAttribute("by") or 0
				if p.Position.Y.Offset < by then p.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, PANEL_HIDE) end
				tween(p, 0.32, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, by) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
			else
				tween(p, 0.24, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, PANEL_HIDE) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
				task.delay(0.26, function()
					if p:GetAttribute("tok") == tok then p.Visible = false end
				end)
			end
		end
		local function closePanels() for _, p in ipairs(panels) do popPanel(p, false) end end

		local sizeL
		local dotStrokes, fontBtns = {}, {}
		local function paintFmt()
			if not cur then return end
			sizeL.Text = tostring(cur.size)
			for k, s in pairs(dotStrokes) do s.Transparency = (cur.color == k) and 0 or 1 end
			for name, b in pairs(fontBtns) do b.TextColor3 = (cur.font == name) and YEL or C.text end
		end
		local function sizeStep(d)
			if not cur then return end
			cur.size = math.clamp(cur.size + d, 9, 26)
			applyStyle()
			paintFmt()
			save()
		end
		pbtn(fmtPanel, 8, 8, 44, 26, "A-", function() sizeStep(-1) end).TextSize = 11
		sizeL = pl(fmtPanel, "13", 12, C.text, 56, 8, W - 112, 26, CENTER, FONT_BOLD)
		pbtn(fmtPanel, W - 52, 8, 44, 26, "A+", function() sizeStep(1) end).TextSize = 13
		local dstep = (W - 16 - 16) / (#COLS - 1)
		for k = 1, #COLS do
			local d = nt(createButton(fmtPanel, { Sound = true, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8 + (k - 1) * dstep, 42), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16), BackgroundColor3 = COLS[k] or C.text, BackgroundTransparency = 0 }))
			corner(d, 99)
			dotStrokes[k] = stroke(d, YEL, 2, 1)
			d.Activated:Connect(function()
				if not cur then return end
				cur.color = k
				applyStyle()
				paintFmt()
				save()
			end)
		end
		local fontSc = create("ScrollingFrame", { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 66), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W - 16, 84), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ScrollBarThickness = 2, ScrollBarImageColor3 = C.dim, CanvasSize = UDim2.new(), AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y }, fmtPanel)
		create("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 2), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, fontSc)
		for i, ft in ipairs(FONTS) do
			local b = nl(createButton(fontSc, { Sound = true, LayoutOrder = i, Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 24), BackgroundColor3 = C.field, BackgroundTransparency = 0.4, TextSize = 14, Font = ft.font, TextColor3 = C.text }))
			b.Text = ft.label
			corner(b, 7)
			fontBtns[ft.name] = b
			b.Activated:Connect(function()
				if not cur then return end
				cur.font = ft.name
				applyStyle()
				paintFmt()
				save()
			end)
		end
		lift(fmtPanel)

		local inBox = nl(create("TextBox", { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 8), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W - 16, 28), BackgroundColor3 = C.field, BorderSizePixel = 0, Text = "", PlaceholderText = "asset id, url or file name", PlaceholderColor3 = C.dim, TextColor3 = C.text, Font = FONT, TextSize = 11, ClearTextOnFocus = false, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left }, imgPanel))
		corner(inBox, 8)
		create("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8) }, inBox)
		pbtn(imgPanel, 8, 42, 80, 26, "Add", function()
			local t = inBox.Text
			inBox.Text = ""
			popPanel(imgPanel, false)
			parseAndAttach(t)
		end, true)
		pbtn(imgPanel, 94, 42, 80, 26, "Cancel", function() inBox.Text = "" popPanel(imgPanel, false) end)
		lift(imgPanel)

		local pnote = pl(photoPanel, "", 9, C.dim, 8, 4, W - 16, 12, Enum.TextXAlignment.Left)
		local pthumbs = {}
		for k = 1, 5 do
			local tb = createButton(photoPanel, { Sound = true, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8 + (k - 1) * 33, 24), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(30, 30), BackgroundColor3 = C.field, BackgroundTransparency = 0.4 })
			corner(tb, 7)
			local tim = nt(create("ImageLabel", { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop, Visible = false }, tb))
			corner(tim, 7)
			pthumbs[k] = { b = tb, im = tim }
			tb.Activated:Connect(function()
				local shots = State.phonePhotos or {}
				local ph = shots[k]
				if not ph then return end
				if ph.kind == "world" and not ph.file and not ph.png then pnote.Text = "3D renders can't be attached" return end
				popPanel(photoPanel, false)
				if ph.png then
					attach({ t = "file", v = ph.png })
					save(true)
					renderImages()
					return
				end
				if ph.file then
					attach({ t = "px", v = ph.file, w = ph.w, h = ph.h, ei = ph.ei })
					save(true)
					renderImages()
					return
				end
				local im = { t = "temp", kind = ph.kind, v = ph.v }
				attach(im)
				task.spawn(function()
					refreshStatus("saving photo...")
					local ok, err = persistPhoto(im)
					if ok then save(true) renderImages() refreshStatus("photo saved")
					else renderImages() refreshStatus("not saved: " .. tostring(err):sub(1, 70)) end
				end)
			end)
		end
		lift(photoPanel)
		local function openPhotos()
			local shots = State.phonePhotos or {}
			pnote.Text = #shots == 0 and "take a photo in Camera first" or "pick a photo from the Camera gallery"
			for k, t in ipairs(pthumbs) do
				local ph = shots[k]
				t.im.Visible = false
				if ph and (ph.kind ~= "world" or ph.file or ph.png) then
					t.im.Visible = (pcall(function()
						if ph.png then
							t.im.Image = getcustomasset(ph.png)
						elseif ph.kind == "px" or ph.kind == "world" then
							if not ph.ei then CamStore.loadEi(ph) end
							t.im.ImageContent = Content.fromObject(ph.ei)
						elseif ph.kind == "capture" then t.im.ImageContent = Content.fromObject(ph.v) else t.im.Image = ph.v end
					end))
				end
			end
			local was = isOpen(photoPanel)
			closePanels()
			popPanel(photoPanel, not was)
		end

		local viewTok = 0
		local function showEditor(on)
			viewTok += 1
			local my = viewTok
			local ei, eo = Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out
			local off = UDim2.fromOffset(-math.floor(SW * 0.3), 0)
			State.stopTweens(ev)
			State.stopTweens(lv)
			if on then
				ev.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(SW, 0)
				ev.Visible, lv.Visible = true, true
				tween(ev, 0.36, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0) }, ei, eo)
				tween(lv, 0.36, { Position = off }, ei, eo)
				task.delay(0.38, function() if my == viewTok then lv.Visible = false end end)
			else
				lv.Position = lv.Visible and lv.Position or off
				ev.Visible, lv.Visible = true, true
				tween(lv, 0.32, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0) }, ei, eo)
				tween(ev, 0.32, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(SW, 0) }, ei, eo)
				task.delay(0.34, function() if my == viewTok then ev.Visible = false end end)
			end
		end
		local function leaveEditor()
			if cur and cur.text:match("^%s*$") and #cur.images == 0 then
				for i, n in ipairs(data.notes) do if n == cur then table.remove(data.notes, i) break end end
			end
			cur = nil
			ed:ReleaseFocus()
			closePanels()
			showEditor(false)
			save(true)
			listRefresh(true)
		end
		local function openNote(n)
			cur = n
			loading = true
			ed.Text = n.text
			loading = false
			applyStyle()
			renderImages()
			sc.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 0)
			dateL.Text = dateText(n)
			closePanels()
			paintFmt()
			showEditor(true)
			refreshStatus()
		end
		navBtn(ev, 6, 80, "Notes", leaveEditor, true)
		navBtn(ev, SW - M - 56, 56, "Done", leaveEditor, false, true, true)

		createFrame(ev, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 356), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(SW, 1), BackgroundColor3 = C.line })
		local function tbBtn(i, kind, fallback, cb)
			local b = nt(createButton(ev, { Sound = true, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(math.floor(SW * (2 * i - 1) / 8), 362), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(40, 28) }))
			local obj
			if kind == "Aa" then
				obj = nl(createLabel(b, "Aa", 15, YEL, FONT_BOLD, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), TextXAlignment = CENTER, NoTitle = true }))
			elseif kind == "trash-2" then
				obj = trashIcon(b, YEL, 18)
			else
				local img = nt(create("ImageLabel", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ImageColor3 = YEL }, b))
				if State.setLucide(img, kind, YEL) then
					obj = img
				else
					img:Destroy()
					obj = nl(createLabel(b, fallback, 10, YEL, FONT_BOLD, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), TextXAlignment = CENTER, NoTitle = true }))
				end
			end
			local tsc = create("UIScale", {}, b)
			b.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function() tween(tsc, 0.07, { Scale = 0.8 }) end)
			b.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function() tween(tsc, 0.25, { Scale = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back) end)
			b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tween(tsc, 0.1, { Scale = 1 }) end)
			b.Activated:Connect(cb)
			return b, obj
		end
		local function tint(obj, col)
			if obj:GetAttribute("Trash") then
				for _, d in ipairs(obj:GetDescendants()) do
					if d:IsA("UIStroke") then d.Color = col elseif d:IsA("Frame") and d.BackgroundTransparency < 1 then d.BackgroundColor3 = col end
				end
			elseif obj:IsA("ImageLabel") then obj.ImageColor3 = col else obj.TextColor3 = col end
		end

		tbBtn(1, "Aa", "Aa", function()
			local was = isOpen(fmtPanel)
			closePanels()
			popPanel(fmtPanel, not was)
			paintFmt()
		end)
		tbBtn(2, "image", "IMG", function()
			local was = isOpen(imgPanel)
			closePanels()
			popPanel(imgPanel, not was)
			if isOpen(imgPanel) then inBox:CaptureFocus() end
		end)
		tbBtn(3, "camera", "CAM", openPhotos)
		local delObj, armedDel
		local _, delIcon = tbBtn(4, "trash-2", "DEL", function()
			if not armedDel then
				armedDel = true
				tint(delObj, RED)
				task.delay(2, function() if armedDel then armedDel = false tint(delObj, YEL) end end)
				return
			end
			armedDel = false
			tint(delObj, YEL)
			if cur then
				for i, n in ipairs(data.notes) do if n == cur then table.remove(data.notes, i) break end end
				cur = nil
			end
			ed:ReleaseFocus()
			closePanels()
			showEditor(false)
			save(true)
			listRefresh(true)
		end)
		delObj = delIcon

		ed:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
			if loading or not cur then return end
			cur.text = ed.Text
			cur.t = os.time()
			dateL.Text = dateText(cur)
			refreshStatus()
			save()
		end)
		ed.Focused:Connect(function() closePanels() end)
		ed.FocusLost:Connect(function() save(true) end)
		table.insert(C.hooks, function() if cur then applyStyle() paintFmt() end end)

		local function titleOf(n)
			local first, rest
			for line in n.text:gmatch("[^\n]+") do
				if not first then first = line elseif not rest then rest = line break end
			end
			return first or "New Note", rest
		end
		local function animateRow(row, y, k)
			local items = {}
			for _, d in ipairs(row:GetDescendants()) do if d:IsA("TextLabel") then items[#items + 1] = d end end
			row.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(28, y)
			for _, l in ipairs(items) do l.TextTransparency = 1 end
			task.delay(math.min(k, 12) * 0.04, function()
				if not row.Parent then return end
				tween(row, 0.34, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, y) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
				for _, l in ipairs(items) do if l.Parent then tween(l, 0.3, { TextTransparency = 0 }) end end
			end)
		end
		local function sectionOf(t, t0)
			if t >= t0 then return 1, "Today" end
			if t >= t0 - 86400 then return 2, "Yesterday" end
			if t >= t0 - 7 * 86400 then return 3, "Previous 7 Days" end
			if t >= t0 - 30 * 86400 then return 4, "Previous 30 Days" end
			return 5, "Older"
		end
		local function stampOf(t, t0)
			if t >= t0 then return os.date("%H:%M", t) end
			if t >= t0 - 86400 then return "Yesterday" end
			if t >= t0 - 6 * 86400 then return os.date("%A", t) end
			return os.date("%d/%m/%Y", t)
		end
		function listRefresh(anim)
			for _, c in ipairs(listSc:GetChildren()) do if c:IsA("GuiObject") then c:Destroy() end end
			table.sort(data.notes, function(x, y) return (x.t or 0) > (y.t or 0) end)
			countL.Text = #data.notes .. (#data.notes == 1 and " Note" or " Notes")
			local q = searchBox.Text:lower()
			local shown = {}
			for _, n in ipairs(data.notes) do
				if q == "" or n.text:lower():find(q, 1, true) then shown[#shown + 1] = n end
			end
			if #shown == 0 then
				local e = nl(createLabel(listSc, q == "" and "No notes yet\ntap the pencil to write one" or "No results", 11, C.sub, FONT, { LayoutOrder = 1, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 60), TextXAlignment = CENTER, TextWrapped = true, NoTitle = true }))
				return
			end
			local now = os.time()
			local d = os.date("*t", now)
			local t0 = now - (d.hour * 3600 + d.min * 60 + d.sec)
			local order, last, group, rows, kAnim = 0, nil, nil, 0, 0
			for _, n in ipairs(shown) do
				local t = n.t or now
				local sec, name = sectionOf(t, t0)
				if sec ~= last then
					last = sec
					order += 1
					nl(createLabel(listSc, name, 12, C.text, FONT_BOLD, { LayoutOrder = order, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24), NoTitle = true }))
					order += 1
					group = createFrame(listSc, { LayoutOrder = order, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), BackgroundColor3 = C.field, ClipsDescendants = true })
					corner(group, 10)
					rows = 0
				end
				rows += 1
				group.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, rows * 50)
				local row = nl(createButton(group, { Sound = true, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, (rows - 1) * 50), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 50), BackgroundColor3 = C.hoverField, BackgroundTransparency = 1 }))
				local title, sub = titleOf(n)
				nl(pl(row, title, 12, C.text, 12, 7, W - 24, 16, nil, FONT_BOLD))
				local prev = sub or ((#n.images > 0) and (#n.images .. (#n.images == 1 and " image" or " images"))) or "No additional text"
				nl(pl(row, stampOf(t, t0) .. "   " .. prev, 11, C.sub, 12, 27, W - 24, 15))
				if rows > 1 then createFrame(group, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, (rows - 1) * 50), Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 1), BackgroundColor3 = C.line }) end
				row.MouseEnter:Connect(function() tween(row, 0.1, { BackgroundTransparency = 0 }) end)
				row.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tween(row, 0.1, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }) end)
				row.Activated:Connect(function() openNote(n) end)
				if anim then kAnim += 1 animateRow(row, (rows - 1) * 50, kAnim) end
			end
			createFrame(listSc, { LayoutOrder = 1e6, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 8) })
		end
		searchBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(listRefresh)
		table.insert(C.hooks, function() if lv.Visible then listRefresh() end end)

		comp.Activated:Connect(function()
			local n = { id = tostring(os.time()) .. math.random(100, 999), text = "", font = "GothamMedium", size = 13, color = 1, t = os.time(), images = {} }
			table.insert(data.notes, 1, n)
			openNote(n)
			ed:CaptureFocus()
		end)
		listRefresh(true)

		table.insert(conns, RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			if cur and (not isOpen or not current or current.id ~= "notes") and ed:IsFocused() then ed:ReleaseFocus() end
		end))
	end

	local function buildDraw(f)

		local CW, CH = AW, 178
		local canvas = createFrame(f, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(CW, CH), BackgroundColor3 = C.field, ClipsDescendants = true })
		corner(canvas, 12)
		stroke(canvas, C.line, 1)
		local gridF = nt(createFrame(canvas, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Visible = false }))
		for x = 20, CW - 1, 20 do nt(createFrame(gridF, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(1, CH), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 0.9 })) end
		for y = 20, CH - 1, 20 do nt(createFrame(gridF, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, y), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(CW, 1), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 0.9 })) end
		local vGuide = nt(createFrame(canvas, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(CW / 2 - 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(1, CH), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent, BackgroundTransparency = 0.55, Visible = false }))
		local hGuide = nt(createFrame(canvas, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, CH / 2 - 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(CW, 1), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent, BackgroundTransparency = 0.55, Visible = false }))
		local layer = nt(createFrame(canvas, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }))

		local MAXD = 2500
		local tool, colorVal, bs, hue = "pen", "accent", 5, 0
		local fillShapes, mirror = false, 0
		local items, warned = 0, false
		local strokes, redoStack, erasers = {}, {}, {}
		local drawing, curStroke, lastPts, chans = false, nil, {}, nil
		local updatePreview

		local function brushColor()
			if colorVal == "accent" then return C.accent end
			if colorVal == "rainbow" then return Color3.fromHSV(hue % 1, 0.85, 1) end
			return colorVal
		end
		local function curColor() return tool == "erase" and canvas.BackgroundColor3 or brushColor() end
		local function variants(p)
			if mirror == 0 then return { p } end
			local m = Vector2.new(CW - p.X, p.Y)
			if mirror == 1 then return { p, m } end
			return { p, m, Vector2.new(p.X, CH - p.Y), Vector2.new(CW - p.X, CH - p.Y) }
		end
		local function full()
			if items >= MAXD then
				if not warned then warned = true notify("Phone", "canvas full - tap clear") end
				return true
			end
			return false
		end

		local function dotAt(p)
			if full() then return end
			if colorVal == "rainbow" and tool ~= "erase" then hue += 0.006 end
			local d = nt(createFrame(layer, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(p.X - bs / 2, p.Y - bs / 2), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(bs, bs), BackgroundColor3 = curColor() }))
			if bs >= 4 then corner(d, bs / 2) end
			items += 1
			table.insert(curStroke, d)
			if tool == "erase" then table.insert(erasers, d) end
		end
		local function penTo(p)
			for k, v in ipairs(variants(p)) do
				local l = lastPts[k]
				if l then
					local n = math.floor((v - l).Magnitude / math.max(1.5, bs * 0.5))
					for j = 1, n do dotAt(l:Lerp(v, j / n)) end
				end
				dotAt(v)
				lastPts[k] = v
			end
		end

		local function makeShape(kind)
			local col = brushColor()
			local fr = nt(createFrame(layer, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), BackgroundColor3 = col, BackgroundTransparency = (kind == "line" or fillShapes) and 0 or 1 }))
			if kind == "line" then
				corner(fr, bs / 2)
			elseif kind == "oval" then
				create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0.5, 0) }, fr)
			end
			if (kind == "rect" or kind == "oval") and not fillShapes then
				create("UIStroke", { Color = col, Thickness = bs, ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border }, fr)
			end
			items += 1
			table.insert(curStroke, fr)
			return fr
		end
		local function place(kind, fr, a, b)
			if kind == "line" then
				local d = b - a
				fr.Position = UDim2.fromOffset((a.X + b.X) / 2, (a.Y + b.Y) / 2)
				fr.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(d.Magnitude, bs), bs)
				fr.Rotation = math.deg(math.atan2(d.Y, d.X))
			else
				local w, h = math.max(math.abs(b.X - a.X), 1), math.max(math.abs(b.Y - a.Y), 1)
				fr.Position = UDim2.fromOffset((a.X + b.X) / 2, (a.Y + b.Y) / 2)
				fr.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, h)
			end
		end

		local function rel(v3)
			local ap, sz = canvas.AbsolutePosition, canvas.AbsoluteSize
			return Vector2.new(math.clamp((v3.X - ap.X) / math.max(sz.X, 1) * CW, 0, CW), math.clamp((v3.Y - ap.Y) / math.max(sz.Y, 1) * CH, 0, CH))
		end
		canvas.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
			if not isPress(input) or full() then return end
			local p = rel(input.Position)
			for _, st in ipairs(redoStack) do for _, d in ipairs(st) do d:Destroy() end end
			redoStack = {}
			drawing, curStroke, lastPts = true, {}, {}
			table.insert(strokes, curStroke)
			if #strokes > 400 then table.remove(strokes, 1) end
			if tool == "pen" or tool == "erase" then
				penTo(p)
			else
				chans = {}
				for k, v in ipairs(variants(p)) do
					chans[k] = { a = v, f = makeShape(tool) }
					place(tool, chans[k].f, v, v)
				end
			end
		end)
		table.insert(conns, UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
			if not drawing or not isMove(input) then return end
			local p = rel(input.Position)
			if tool == "pen" or tool == "erase" then
				penTo(p)
			elseif chans then
				for k, v in ipairs(variants(p)) do
					if chans[k] then place(tool, chans[k].f, chans[k].a, v) end
				end
			end
		end))
		table.insert(conns, UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
			if not isPress(input) or not drawing then return end
			drawing, lastPts, chans = false, {}, nil
			if curStroke and #curStroke == 0 and strokes[#strokes] == curStroke then table.remove(strokes) end
		end))

		local outline = {}
		local function mark(btn, on) local st = outline[btn] if st then tween(st, 0.12, { Color = on and C.accent or C.line, Thickness = on and 2 or 1 }) end end
		local function iconBtn(x, y, w, h, kind, letter, cb)
			local b = pbtn(f, x, y, w, h, "", cb)
			outline[b] = stroke(b, C.line, 1)
			local holder = glyph(b, kind, 14, C.text)
			if #holder:GetChildren() == 0 then b.Text = letter b.TextSize = 11 end
			return b
		end

		local toolBtns, TOOLS = {}, { { "pen", "pencil", "P" }, { "line", "slash", "L" }, { "rect", "square", "R" }, { "oval", "circle", "O" }, { "erase", "eraser", "E" } }
		local swatches = {}
		local function setTool(t)
			tool = t
			for name, b in pairs(toolBtns) do mark(b, name == t) end
			if t == "erase" then for _, sw in ipairs(swatches) do mark(sw.btn, false) end end
			if updatePreview then updatePreview() end
		end
		local TW = (CW - 4 * 4) / 5
		for i, t in ipairs(TOOLS) do
			toolBtns[t[1]] = iconBtn((i - 1) * (TW + 4), 184, TW, 24, t[2], t[3], function() setTool(t[1]) end)
		end
		mark(toolBtns.pen, true)

		local function C3(r, g, b) return Color3.fromRGB(r, g, b) end
		local palette = {
			"accent", C3(255, 69, 58), C3(255, 159, 10), C3(255, 214, 10), C3(48, 209, 88), C3(10, 132, 255), C3(191, 90, 242), C3(245, 245, 245), C3(20, 20, 22),
			C3(255, 105, 180), C3(64, 200, 224), C3(160, 110, 70), C3(142, 142, 147), C3(166, 226, 46), C3(30, 50, 120), C3(120, 25, 40), C3(255, 205, 160), "rainbow",
		}
		local function pickColor(i)
			colorVal = palette[i]
			if tool == "erase" then setTool("pen") end
			for j, sw in ipairs(swatches) do tween(sw.st, 0.12, { Color = (j == i) and C.text or C.line, Thickness = (j == i) and 2 or 1 }) end
			updatePreview()
		end
		for i, col in ipairs(palette) do
			local r, c = math.floor((i - 1) / 9), (i - 1) % 9
			local btn = createButton(f, { Sound = true, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(c * 21, 213 + r * 21), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18), BackgroundColor3 = (col == "accent") and C.accent or (col == "rainbow" and Color3.new(1, 1, 1) or col), BackgroundTransparency = 0 })
			if col ~= "accent" then nt(btn) end
			corner(btn, 9)
			if col == "rainbow" then
				local seq = {}
				for k = 0, 6 do table.insert(seq, ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(k / 6, Color3.fromHSV(k / 7, 0.85, 1))) end
				create("UIGradient", { Rotation = 45, Color = ColorSequence.new(seq) }, btn)
			end
			local st = stroke(btn, i == 1 and C.text or C.line, i == 1 and 2 or 1)
			swatches[i] = { btn = btn, st = st }
			btn.Activated:Connect(function() pickColor(i) end)
		end

		local SY = 258
		local track = nt(createFrame(f, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(2, SY + 9), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(122, 4), BackgroundColor3 = C.line }))
		corner(track, 2)
		local fillF = nt(createFrame(track, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.15, 1), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent }))
		corner(fillF, 2)
		local knob = nt(createFrame(track, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.15, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 14), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1) }))
		corner(knob, 7)
		stroke(knob, C.line, 1)
		local hit = createButton(f, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-4, SY), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(134, 22), BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
		local prevBox = createFrame(f, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(138, SY), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(48, 20), BackgroundColor3 = C.field })
		corner(prevBox, 8)
		local prevDot = nt(createFrame(prevBox, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(5, 5), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent }))
		corner(prevDot, 12)
		local prevGrad = create("UIGradient", { Enabled = false, Rotation = 45, Color = ColorSequence.new({ ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromHSV(0, 0.85, 1)), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.33, Color3.fromHSV(0.33, 0.85, 1)), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.66, Color3.fromHSV(0.66, 0.85, 1)), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromHSV(0.95, 0.85, 1)) }) }, prevDot)
		local prevStroke = stroke(prevDot, C.line, 1, 1)
		function updatePreview()
			local frac = (bs - 1) / 27
			fillF.Size = UDim2.fromScale(frac, 1)
			knob.Position = UDim2.fromScale(frac, 0.5)
			local d = math.min(bs, 18)
			prevDot.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(d, d)
			if tool == "erase" then
				prevDot.BackgroundColor3 = canvas.BackgroundColor3
				prevStroke.Transparency = 0
				prevGrad.Enabled = false
			else
				prevStroke.Transparency = 1
				prevGrad.Enabled = (colorVal == "rainbow")
				prevDot.BackgroundColor3 = (colorVal == "rainbow") and Color3.new(1, 1, 1) or brushColor()
			end
		end
		local sliding = false
		local function slideTo(x)
			local frac = math.clamp((x - track.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(track.AbsoluteSize.X, 1), 0, 1)
			bs = math.clamp(math.floor(frac * 27 + 1.5), 1, 28)
			updatePreview()
		end
		hit.InputBegan:Connect(function(input) if isPress(input) then sliding = true slideTo(input.Position.X) end end)
		table.insert(conns, UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input) if sliding and isMove(input) then slideTo(input.Position.X) end end))
		table.insert(conns, UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input) if isPress(input) then sliding = false end end))
		updatePreview()

		local function setVis(st, v) for _, d in ipairs(st) do if d.Parent then d.Visible = v end end end
		local uBtn = iconBtn(0, 287, 43, 22, "undo-2", "U", function()
			local st = table.remove(strokes)
			if not st then return end
			setVis(st, false)
			items -= #st
			warned = false
			table.insert(redoStack, st)
		end)
		iconBtn(47, 287, 43, 22, "redo-2", "R", function()
			local st = table.remove(redoStack)
			if not st then return end
			setVis(st, true)
			items += #st
			table.insert(strokes, st)
		end)
		iconBtn(94, 287, 43, 22, "trash-2", "C", function()
			for _, d in ipairs(layer:GetChildren()) do d:Destroy() end
			items, warned, strokes, redoStack, erasers, curStroke = 0, false, {}, {}, {}, nil
		end)
		iconBtn(141, 287, 45, 22, "paint-bucket", "B", function()
			local c = brushColor()
			tween(canvas, 0.2, { BackgroundColor3 = c })
			for _, d in ipairs(erasers) do if d.Parent then d.BackgroundColor3 = c end end
			task.delay(0.21, updatePreview)
		end)

		local mBtn, gBtn, sBtn
		local W3 = (CW - 8) / 3
		mBtn = pbtn(f, 0, 314, W3, 22, "Mirror", function()
			mirror = (mirror + 1) % 3
			mBtn.Text = mirror == 0 and "Mirror" or (mirror == 1 and "Mirror 2" or "Mirror 4")
			mark(mBtn, mirror > 0)
			vGuide.Visible, hGuide.Visible = mirror >= 1, mirror == 2
			notify("Phone", mirror == 0 and "mirror off" or (mirror == 1 and "mirror: left / right" or "mirror: four-way"))
		end)
		outline[mBtn] = stroke(mBtn, C.line, 1)
		gBtn = pbtn(f, W3 + 4, 314, W3, 22, "Grid", function()
			gridF.Visible = not gridF.Visible
			mark(gBtn, gridF.Visible)
		end)
		outline[gBtn] = stroke(gBtn, C.line, 1)
		sBtn = pbtn(f, (W3 + 4) * 2, 314, W3, 22, "Solid", function()
			fillShapes = not fillShapes
			mark(sBtn, fillShapes)
		end)
		outline[sBtn] = stroke(sBtn, C.line, 1)
		for _, b in ipairs({ mBtn, gBtn, sBtn }) do b.TextSize = 10 end
	end

	local function buildCalc(f)
		local cur, acc, op, fresh, pcted, rep = "0", nil, nil, true, false, nil
		local hist = {}
		local disp = createFrame(f, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(AW, 86), BackgroundColor3 = C.field })
		corner(disp, 14)
		local histHint = pl(disp, "History", 9, C.dim, 12, 6, 80, 12)
		local expr = pl(disp, "", 12, C.sub, 12, 19, AW - 24, 16, RIGHT)
		local main = nl(createLabel(disp, "0", 34, C.text, FONT_BOLD, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 38), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(AW - 20, 42), TextXAlignment = RIGHT, NoTitle = true, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None }))

		local function fmt(n)
			if n ~= n or math.abs(n) == math.huge then return "error" end
			if n == 0 then return "0" end
			local a = math.abs(n)
			if a >= 1e12 or a < 1e-9 then
				local s = string.format("%.6g", n)
				s = s:gsub("e([%+%-])0*(%d)", function(sg, d) return "e" .. (sg == "-" and "-" or "") .. d end)
				return s
			end
			local s = string.format("%.12g", n)
			if s:find("e") then
				s = string.format("%.12f", n)
				s = s:gsub("0+$", "")
				s = s:gsub("%.$", "")
			end
			return s
		end
		local function show()
			main.Text = cur
			local n = #cur
			main.TextSize = n > 12 and 17 or (n > 10 and 20 or (n > 8 and 24 or (n > 6 and 28 or 34)))
			histHint.Text = #hist > 0 and ("History (" .. #hist .. ")") or "History"
			if op and acc and not fresh then expr.Text = fmt(acc) .. " " .. op .. " " .. cur end
		end
		local function calc(a, o, b)
			if o == "+" then return a + b elseif o == "-" then return a - b elseif o == "×" then return a * b
			elseif o == "÷" then if b == 0 then return nil end return a / b end
			return b
		end
		local function val() return tonumber(cur) or 0 end
		local function setError(msg)
			cur, acc, op, fresh, pcted, rep = "error", nil, nil, true, false, nil
			expr.Text = msg or ""
			show()
		end
		local function digit(d)
			if cur == "error" or fresh then
				cur, fresh = d, false
				if not op then rep = nil end
			elseif cur == "0" then cur = d
			elseif cur == "-0" then cur = "-" .. d
			elseif #(cur:gsub("[^%d]", "")) < 12 then cur ..= d end
			pcted = false
			show()
		end
		local function operator(o)
			if cur == "error" then return end
			if op and fresh and not pcted then
				op = o
				expr.Text = fmt(acc) .. " " .. o
				return
			end
			if op then
				local r = calc(acc, op, val())
				if r == nil then setError("can't divide by 0") return end
				acc = r
				cur = fmt(r)
			else
				acc = val()
			end
			op, fresh, pcted, rep = o, true, false, nil
			expr.Text = fmt(acc) .. " " .. o
			show()
		end
		local function equals()
			if cur == "error" then return end
			local a, b, o
			if op then a, b, o = acc, val(), op
			elseif rep then a, b, o = val(), rep[2], rep[1]
			else return end
			local r = calc(a, o, b)
			if r == nil then setError("can't divide by 0") return end
			rep = { o, b }
			expr.Text = fmt(a) .. " " .. o .. " " .. fmt(b) .. " ="
			cur = fmt(r)
			table.insert(hist, 1, { expr.Text, cur })
			if #hist > 12 then table.remove(hist) end
			acc, op, fresh, pcted = nil, nil, true, false
			show()
		end
		local function percent()
			if cur == "error" then return end
			if op and acc then
				local v = val()
				cur = fmt((op == "+" or op == "-") and acc * v / 100 or v / 100)
				fresh, pcted = true, true
			else
				cur = fmt(val() / 100)
				fresh = true
			end
			show()
		end
		local function key(k)
			if k:match("^%d$") then digit(k)
			elseif k == "." then
				if cur == "error" or fresh then cur, fresh = "0.", false elseif not cur:find("%.") and #cur < 13 then cur ..= "." end
				pcted = false
				show()
			elseif k == "C" then
				cur, acc, op, fresh, pcted, rep = "0", nil, nil, true, false, nil
				expr.Text = ""
				show()
			elseif k == "±" then
				if cur == "error" then return end
				if fresh and op then cur, fresh = "-0", false
				elseif cur ~= "0" then cur = (cur:sub(1, 1) == "-") and cur:sub(2) or ("-" .. cur) end
				show()
			elseif k == "%" then percent()
			elseif k == "del" then
				if fresh or cur == "error" then return end
				cur = cur:sub(1, -2)
				if cur == "" or cur == "-" or cur == "-0" then cur = "0" end
				show()
			elseif k == "=" then equals()
			else operator(k) end
		end

		local KEYS = {
			{ "C", "±", "%", "÷" },
			{ "7", "8", "9", "×" },
			{ "4", "5", "6", "-" },
			{ "1", "2", "3", "+" },
			{ "0", ".", "del", "=" },
		}
		local SYMS = { ["÷"] = true, ["×"] = true, ["-"] = true, ["+"] = true, ["="] = true, ["del"] = true }
		for r, row in ipairs(KEYS) do
			for c, k in ipairs(row) do
				local isOp = (c == 4)
				local b = pbtn(f, (c - 1) * 48, 94 + (r - 1) * 49, 42, 44, SYMS[k] and "" or k, function() key(k) end, isOp)
				local kc = b:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner")
				if kc then kc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 21) end
				if SYMS[k] then
					sym(b, k, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), k == "del" and 20 or 18)
				else
					b.TextSize = (k == "C" or k == "±" or k == "%") and 20 or 24
					if k == "C" then b.TextColor3 = C.red end
				end
			end
		end

		local panel = createFrame(f, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 94), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(AW, 240), BackgroundColor3 = C.field, Visible = false })
		corner(panel, 14)
		pl(panel, "history", 11, C.sub, 12, 8, 100, 16, nil, FONT_BOLD)
		local rows = {}
		local function closePanel() panel.Visible = false end
		local function openPanel()
			for _, r in ipairs(rows) do r:Destroy() end
			rows = {}
			if #hist == 0 then
				local e = pl(panel, "nothing yet - press = after a sum", 10, C.dim, 12, 40, AW - 24, 14)
				table.insert(rows, e)
			end
			for i, h in ipairs(hist) do
				if i > 7 then break end
				local b = createButton(panel, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 28 + (i - 1) * 27), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(AW - 12, 25), BackgroundColor3 = C.bg, BackgroundTransparency = 0.4, TextSize = 10, Font = FONT, TextColor3 = C.text })
				b.Text = h[1] .. " " .. h[2]
				nl(b)
				corner(b, 8)
				b.Activated:Connect(function()
					cur, fresh, acc, op, pcted = h[2], true, nil, nil, false
					expr.Text = h[1]
					show()
					closePanel()
				end)
				table.insert(rows, b)
			end
			panel.Visible = true
		end
		local clearH = pbtn(panel, 6, 212, 84, 22, "clear all", function() hist = {} show() openPanel() end)
		clearH.TextSize = 10
		local closeH = pbtn(panel, AW - 90, 212, 84, 22, "close", closePanel, true)
		closeH.TextSize = 10
		local dispBtn = createButton(disp, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "" })
		dispBtn.ZIndex = 5
		dispBtn.Activated:Connect(function() if panel.Visible then closePanel() else openPanel() end end)

		local MAP = { [Enum.KeyCode.Period] = ".", [Enum.KeyCode.KeypadPeriod] = ".", [Enum.KeyCode.Return] = "=", [Enum.KeyCode.KeypadEnter] = "=", [Enum.KeyCode.Backspace] = "del", [Enum.KeyCode.Delete] = "C", [Enum.KeyCode.Escape] = "C", [Enum.KeyCode.C] = "C", [Enum.KeyCode.KeypadPlus] = "+", [Enum.KeyCode.Minus] = "-", [Enum.KeyCode.KeypadMinus] = "-", [Enum.KeyCode.Asterisk] = "×", [Enum.KeyCode.KeypadMultiply] = "×", [Enum.KeyCode.X] = "×", [Enum.KeyCode.Slash] = "÷", [Enum.KeyCode.KeypadDivide] = "÷" }
		local NAMES = { "Zero", "One", "Two", "Three", "Four", "Five", "Six", "Seven", "Eight", "Nine" }
		local DIG, PAD = {}, {}
		for n = 0, 9 do
			DIG[Enum.KeyCode[NAMES[n + 1]]] = tostring(n)
			PAD[Enum.KeyCode["Keypad" .. NAMES[n + 1]]] = tostring(n)
		end
		table.insert(conns, UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
			if processed or not isOpen or not current or current.id ~= "calc" then return end
			local kc = input.KeyCode
			local shift = UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) or UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.RightShift)
			local k
			if kc == Enum.KeyCode.Equals then k = shift and "+" or "="
			elseif DIG[kc] then
				if not shift then k = DIG[kc]
				elseif kc == Enum.KeyCode.Eight then k = "×"
				elseif kc == Enum.KeyCode.Five then k = "%" end
			elseif PAD[kc] then k = PAD[kc]
			else k = MAP[kc] end
			if k then
				if panel.Visible then closePanel() end
				key(k)
			end
		end))
		show()
	end

	local function buildTimer(f)
		local tabs, pages = {}, {}
		local function pick(i)
			for j, p in ipairs(pages) do p.Visible = (j == i) end
			for j, t in ipairs(tabs) do
				local on = (j == i)
				t.BackgroundColor3 = on and C.accent or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
				t:SetAttribute("GlassBase", on and 0.5 or 0.86)
				tween(t, 0.15, { BackgroundTransparency = on and 0.5 or 0.86 })
				t.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
			end
		end
		for i, name in ipairs({ "stopwatch", "countdown" }) do
			tabs[i] = pbtn(f, (i - 1) * 96, 0, 90, 26, name, function() pick(i) end, false, true)
			tabs[i].TextSize = 10
			pages[i] = createFrame(f, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 34), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(AW, 300) })
		end

		local sw = pages[1]
		local running, base, startClock, laps = false, 0, 0, {}
		local big = nl(createLabel(sw, "00:00.00", 34, C.text, FONT_BOLD, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 4), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(AW, 50), TextXAlignment = CENTER, NoTitle = true, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None }))
		local function now() return running and (base + os.clock() - startClock) or base end
		local function fmt(t) return string.format("%02d:%05.2f", math.floor(t / 60), t % 60) end
		local lapText = createLabel(sw, "", 11, C.sub, FONT, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 128), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(AW - 8, 166), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top, TextWrapped = true, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None, NoTitle = true })
		nl(lapText)
		local function drawLaps()
			local lines = {}
			local best, worst
			for i = 2, #laps do
				local d = laps[i] - laps[i - 1]
				if not best or d < best then best = d end
				if not worst or d > worst then worst = d end
			end
			for i = #laps, math.max(1, #laps - 7), -1 do
				local split = laps[i] - (laps[i - 1] or 0)
				local mark = (#laps > 2 and split == best) and "  fastest" or ((#laps > 2 and split == worst) and "  slowest" or "")
				table.insert(lines, ("lap %d   %s   +%s%s"):format(i, fmt(laps[i]), fmt(split), mark))
			end
			lapText.Text = table.concat(lines, "\n")
		end
		local startBtn
		startBtn = pbtn(sw, 0, 62, 90, 34, "start", function()
			if running then
				base = now()
				running = false
				startBtn.Text = "start"
			else
				startClock, running = os.clock(), true
				startBtn.Text = "stop"
			end
		end, true, true)
		pbtn(sw, 96, 62, 90, 34, "lap", function()
			if running or base > 0 then table.insert(laps, now()) drawLaps() end
		end, false, true)
		pbtn(sw, 0, 100, AW, 24, "reset", function()
			running, base, laps = false, 0, {}
			startBtn.Text = "start"
			big.Text = "00:00.00"
			drawLaps()
		end, false, true)

		local cd = pages[2]
		local total, remaining, cdRunning, endAt, done = 300, 300, false, 0, false
		local cbig = nl(createLabel(cd, "05:00", 40, C.text, FONT_BOLD, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 4), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(AW, 52), TextXAlignment = CENTER, NoTitle = true, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None }))
		local bar = createFrame(cd, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 60), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(AW - 16, 6), BackgroundColor3 = C.field })
		corner(bar, 3)
		local fill = createFrame(bar, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent })
		corner(fill, 3)
		local cmsg = pl(cd, "pick a time, then start", 10, C.dim, 0, 72, AW, 14, CENTER)
		local cStart
		local function cfmt(t) t = math.ceil(t) return string.format("%02d:%02d", math.floor(t / 60), t % 60) end
		local function cshow()
			cbig.Text = cfmt(remaining)
			fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(total > 0 and math.clamp(remaining / total, 0, 1) or 0, 1)
		end
		local function setTime(s)
			if cdRunning then return end
			total, remaining, done = s, s, false
			cbig.TextColor3 = C.text
			cmsg.Text = "pick a time, then start"
			cshow()
		end
		for i, m in ipairs({ 1, 3, 5, 10 }) do
			pbtn(cd, (i - 1) * 47, 94, 44, 26, m .. "m", function() setTime(m * 60) end, false, true).TextSize = 11
		end
		pbtn(cd, 0, 124, 90, 24, "+30s", function()
			if cdRunning then endAt += 30 else total += 30 remaining += 30 done = false end
			total = math.max(total, remaining)
			cshow()
		end, false, true).TextSize = 10
		pbtn(cd, 96, 124, 90, 24, "-30s", function()
			if cdRunning then endAt -= 30 remaining = math.max(0, endAt - os.clock()) else remaining = math.max(0, remaining - 30) end
			cshow()
		end, false, true).TextSize = 10
		cStart = pbtn(cd, 0, 154, 90, 34, "start", function()
			if cdRunning then
				remaining = math.max(0, endAt - os.clock())
				cdRunning = false
				cStart.Text = "start"
				cmsg.Text = "paused"
			else
				if remaining <= 0 then remaining = total done = false end
				if remaining <= 0 then return end
				endAt, cdRunning = os.clock() + remaining, true
				done = false
				cbig.TextColor3 = C.text
				cStart.Text = "pause"
				cmsg.Text = "running"
			end
		end, true, true)
		pbtn(cd, 96, 154, 90, 34, "reset", function()
			cdRunning, done = false, false
			remaining = total
			cStart.Text = "start"
			cbig.TextColor3 = C.text
			cmsg.Text = "pick a time, then start"
			cshow()
		end, false, true)

		local flashT = 0
		table.insert(conns, RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
			local visible = isOpen and current ~= nil and current.id == "timer"
			if cdRunning then
				remaining = endAt - os.clock()
				if remaining <= 0 then
					remaining, cdRunning, done = 0, false, true
					cStart.Text = "start"
					cmsg.Text = "time is up!"
					pcall(notify, "Timer", "time is up!")
				end
				if visible then cshow() end
			end
			if not visible then return end
			if running then big.Text = fmt(now()) end
			if done then
				flashT += dt
				cbig.TextColor3 = (math.floor(flashT * 2) % 2 == 0) and C.red or C.text
			end
		end))
		pick(1)
		cshow()
	end

	local function buildSnake(f)
		local N, CS = 12, 15
		local G = N * CS
		local iconBtn = State.pIconBtn
		local GOLD = Color3.fromRGB(255, 205, 70)
		local scoreL = pl(f, "", 12, C.text, 0, 0, 74, 20, nil, FONT_BOLD)
		local lenL = pl(f, "", 10, C.dim, 62, 0, 62, 20, CENTER)
		local bestL = pl(f, "", 12, C.sub, AW - 74, 0, 74, 20, RIGHT, FONT_BOLD)
		local grid = createFrame(f, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 24), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(G, G), BackgroundColor3 = C.field, ClipsDescendants = true })
		corner(grid, 8)
		local gStroke = stroke(grid, C.line, 1)
		for i = 1, N - 1 do
			createFrame(grid, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(i * CS, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(1, G), BackgroundColor3 = C.line, BackgroundTransparency = 0.75 })
			createFrame(grid, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, i * CS), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(G, 1), BackgroundColor3 = C.line, BackgroundTransparency = 0.75 })
		end
		local edge = nt(createFrame(grid, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }))
		local msg = pl(f, "", 10, C.dim, 0, 207, AW, 14, CENTER)

		local ICON = {}
		local function ibar(par, x, y, w, h, col)
			local q = nt(createFrame(par, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, h), BackgroundColor3 = col }))
			corner(q, 1)
			return q
		end
		local function paintAll(list) return function(c) for _, q in ipairs(list) do q.BackgroundColor3 = c end end end
		function ICON.plus(par, col)
			return paintAll({ ibar(par, 5, 1, 2, 10, col), ibar(par, 1, 5, 10, 2, col) })
		end
		function ICON.pause(par, col)
			return paintAll({ ibar(par, 2, 1, 3, 10, col), ibar(par, 7, 1, 3, 10, col) })
		end
		function ICON.play(par, col)
			local t = nl(createLabel(par, "▶", 10, col, FONT_BOLD, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), TextXAlignment = CENTER, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None, NoTitle = true }))
			return function(c) t.TextColor3 = c end
		end
		function ICON.walls(par, col)
			local box = nt(createFrame(par, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 1), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 10), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }))
			corner(box, 2)
			local st = stroke(box, col, 1)
			local list = {
				ibar(par, 1, 4, 10, 1, col), ibar(par, 1, 7, 10, 1, col),
				ibar(par, 6, 2, 1, 2, col), ibar(par, 3, 5, 1, 2, col), ibar(par, 9, 5, 1, 2, col), ibar(par, 6, 8, 1, 2, col),
			}
			return function(c) st.Color = c for _, q in ipairs(list) do q.BackgroundColor3 = c end end
		end
		function ICON.style(par, col)
			return paintAll({ ibar(par, 1, 1, 4, 4, col), ibar(par, 7, 1, 4, 4, col), ibar(par, 1, 7, 4, 4, col), ibar(par, 7, 7, 4, 4, col) })
		end
		function ICON.wrap(par, col)
			local arrow = nl(createLabel(par, "▶", 6, col, FONT_BOLD, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 12), TextXAlignment = CENTER, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None, NoTitle = true }))
			local list = { ibar(par, 0, 1, 2, 10, col), ibar(par, 10, 1, 2, 10, col), ibar(par, 3, 5, 4, 2, col) }
			return function(c) arrow.TextColor3 = c for _, q in ipairs(list) do q.BackgroundColor3 = c end end
		end

		local headFx = nt(createFrame(grid, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(CS, CS), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ZIndex = 4, Visible = false }))
		local fxZ = 4
		local function fxPart(par, cx, cy, w, h, col, rot, circ, anchor)
			local q = nt(createFrame(par, { AnchorPoint = anchor or Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(cx, cy), Size = UDim2.fromScale(w, h), BackgroundColor3 = col, Rotation = rot or 0, ZIndex = fxZ }))
			if circ then corner(q, 99) end
			return q
		end
		local tongue

		local GREEN, DARK, DOT, HEADC = Color3.fromRGB(104, 196, 84), Color3.fromRGB(72, 154, 64), Color3.fromRGB(176, 230, 124), Color3.fromRGB(46, 124, 54)

		local SKINS = {
			{ "Classic", GREEN, DARK, DOT, HEADC },
			{ "Ocean", Color3.fromRGB(70, 160, 230), Color3.fromRGB(46, 112, 190), Color3.fromRGB(150, 210, 255), Color3.fromRGB(28, 80, 150) },
			{ "Violet", Color3.fromRGB(168, 110, 236), Color3.fromRGB(120, 72, 190), Color3.fromRGB(214, 180, 255), Color3.fromRGB(84, 46, 150) },
			{ "Sunset", Color3.fromRGB(255, 150, 70), Color3.fromRGB(226, 96, 54), Color3.fromRGB(255, 214, 150), Color3.fromRGB(180, 60, 40) },
			{ "Rose", Color3.fromRGB(255, 130, 176), Color3.fromRGB(220, 84, 134), Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 224), Color3.fromRGB(170, 44, 96) },
			{ "Gold", Color3.fromRGB(255, 205, 70), Color3.fromRGB(214, 160, 40), Color3.fromRGB(255, 240, 170), Color3.fromRGB(160, 110, 20) },
			{ "Ghost", Color3.fromRGB(236, 238, 245), Color3.fromRGB(190, 194, 208), Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), Color3.fromRGB(130, 136, 156) },
			{ "Lava", Color3.fromRGB(230, 60, 50), Color3.fromRGB(150, 30, 30), Color3.fromRGB(255, 160, 80), Color3.fromRGB(255, 120, 30) },
			{ "Mint", Color3.fromRGB(80, 230, 200), Color3.fromRGB(44, 170, 156), Color3.fromRGB(190, 255, 240), Color3.fromRGB(20, 110, 110) },
			{ "Rainbow", Color3.fromRGB(255, 120, 120), Color3.fromRGB(120, 200, 255), Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Color3.fromRGB(150, 90, 230), rainbow = true },
			{ "Frog", Color3.fromRGB(110, 200, 80), Color3.fromRGB(70, 150, 60), Color3.fromRGB(214, 240, 120), Color3.fromRGB(84, 176, 70) },
			{ "Tabby", Color3.fromRGB(176, 176, 188), Color3.fromRGB(112, 112, 128), Color3.fromRGB(226, 226, 236), Color3.fromRGB(146, 146, 160) },
			{ "Cocoa", Color3.fromRGB(176, 124, 84), Color3.fromRGB(130, 86, 54), Color3.fromRGB(232, 202, 162), Color3.fromRGB(150, 100, 64) },
			{ "Panda", Color3.fromRGB(246, 246, 249), Color3.fromRGB(66, 66, 74), Color3.fromRGB(110, 110, 120), Color3.fromRGB(250, 250, 252) },
			{ "Fox", Color3.fromRGB(238, 132, 52), Color3.fromRGB(192, 92, 36), Color3.fromRGB(255, 238, 212), Color3.fromRGB(216, 102, 30) },
			{ "Bee", Color3.fromRGB(255, 210, 50), Color3.fromRGB(52, 46, 42), Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Color3.fromRGB(255, 196, 40) },
			{ "Night", Color3.fromRGB(46, 50, 68), Color3.fromRGB(30, 32, 46), Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Color3.fromRGB(30, 32, 46) },
		}
		local function skinIndex(name)
			for i, k in ipairs(SKINS) do if k[1] == name then return i end end
			return 1
		end

		local ANIMALS = {
			{ key = "snake", name = "Snake", skin = "Classic", dot = 0, dotTr = 0, tongue = "fork" },
			{ key = "frog", name = "Frog", skin = "Frog", dot = 0, dotTr = 0, tongue = "frog" },
			{ key = "cat", stripe = true, name = "Cat", skin = "Tabby", dot = 0, dotTr = 0, tongue = nil },
			{ key = "dog", name = "Dog", skin = "Cocoa", dot = 0, dotTr = 0, tongue = "dog", always = true },
			{ key = "panda", stripe = true, name = "Panda", skin = "Panda", dot = 0, dotTr = 0, tongue = nil },
			{ key = "bunny", name = "Bunny", skin = "Ghost", dot = 0, dotTr = 0, tongue = nil },
			{ key = "fox", name = "Fox", skin = "Fox", dot = 0, dotTr = 0, tongue = nil },
			{ key = "dragon", name = "Dragon", skin = "Lava", dot = 0, dotTr = 0, tongue = "flame" },
			{ key = "bee", stripe = true, name = "Bee", skin = "Bee", dot = 0, dotTr = 0, tongue = nil },
			{ key = "penguin", name = "Penguin", skin = "Night", dot = 0, dotTr = 0, tongue = nil },
		}
		local APPLES = {
			{ "Apple", Color3.fromRGB(214, 44, 46), Color3.fromRGB(168, 28, 36), Color3.fromRGB(70, 170, 70) },
			{ "Green", Color3.fromRGB(130, 206, 64), Color3.fromRGB(86, 152, 44), Color3.fromRGB(60, 150, 60) },
			{ "Peach", Color3.fromRGB(255, 160, 136), Color3.fromRGB(226, 108, 96), Color3.fromRGB(90, 180, 80) },
			{ "Orange", Color3.fromRGB(255, 150, 30), Color3.fromRGB(214, 100, 20), Color3.fromRGB(70, 170, 70), round = true },
			{ "Berry", Color3.fromRGB(96, 116, 232), Color3.fromRGB(58, 72, 176), Color3.fromRGB(70, 200, 170), round = true },
			{ "Plum", Color3.fromRGB(168, 74, 208), Color3.fromRGB(114, 42, 150), Color3.fromRGB(70, 170, 70), round = true },
			{ "Strawberry", Color3.fromRGB(228, 40, 64), Color3.fromRGB(176, 24, 48), Color3.fromRGB(70, 180, 70), kind = "strawberry" },
			{ "Cherry", Color3.fromRGB(196, 24, 52), Color3.fromRGB(140, 14, 38), Color3.fromRGB(70, 170, 70), kind = "cherry" },
			{ "Watermelon", Color3.fromRGB(240, 70, 90), Color3.fromRGB(46, 130, 60), Color3.fromRGB(170, 226, 130), kind = "melon" },
			{ "Lemon", Color3.fromRGB(252, 226, 70), Color3.fromRGB(214, 184, 40), Color3.fromRGB(80, 176, 70), kind = "lemon" },
			{ "Pear", Color3.fromRGB(190, 214, 70), Color3.fromRGB(140, 176, 48), Color3.fromRGB(70, 170, 70), kind = "pear" },
			{ "Grapes", Color3.fromRGB(150, 80, 204), Color3.fromRGB(100, 50, 150), Color3.fromRGB(70, 170, 70), kind = "grapes" },
			{ "Pineapple", Color3.fromRGB(250, 192, 50), Color3.fromRGB(204, 132, 30), Color3.fromRGB(70, 170, 70), kind = "pine" },
		}
		local skinI, appleI = math.clamp(tonumber(scores.snake_skin) or 1, 1, #SKINS), math.clamp(tonumber(scores.snake_apple) or 1, 1, #APPLES)
		local animalI = math.clamp(tonumber(scores.snake_animal) or 1, 1, #ANIMALS)
		local rainbow = SKINS[skinI].rainbow == true

		local function buildDeco(par, A, z)
			for _, c in ipairs(par:GetChildren()) do c:Destroy() end
			local W, K = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Color3.fromRGB(14, 14, 18)
			local PINK = Color3.fromRGB(255, 150, 170)
			local sk = SKINS[skinIndex(A.skin)]
			local body, dark = sk[2], sk[3]
			local function eyes(style, x, ys, size, col)
				for _, ey in ipairs(ys) do
					if style == "snake" then
						local e = fxPart(par, x, ey, size, size, W, 0, true)
						fxPart(e, 0.66, 0.5, 0.52, 0.52, K, 0, true)
					elseif style == "cute" then
						local e = fxPart(par, x, ey, size, size, W, 0, true)
						fxPart(e, 0.58, 0.5, 0.56, 0.56, K, 0, true)
					elseif style == "dot" then
						local e = fxPart(par, x, ey, size, size, K, 0, true)
						fxPart(e, 0.64, 0.3, 0.34, 0.34, W, 0, true)
					elseif style == "slit" then
						local e = fxPart(par, x, ey, size, size, col or Color3.fromRGB(240, 205, 70), 0, true)
						fxPart(e, 0.5, 0.5, 0.22, 0.82, K, 0, true)
					end
				end
			end
			local k = A.key
			fxZ = z
			if k == "cat" then
				for _, ey in ipairs({ 0.08, 0.92 }) do
					local ear = fxPart(par, 0.3, ey, 0.34, 0.34, dark, 45, false)
					fxPart(ear, 0.5, 0.5, 0.5, 0.5, PINK, 0, false)
				end
			elseif k == "dog" then
				for s, ey in ipairs({ 0.0, 1.0 }) do fxPart(par, 0.4, ey, 0.3, 0.46, dark, s == 1 and 14 or -14, true) end
			elseif k == "panda" then
				for _, ey in ipairs({ 0.0, 1.0 }) do fxPart(par, 0.28, ey, 0.34, 0.34, K, 0, true) end
				for s, ey in ipairs({ 0.27, 0.73 }) do fxPart(par, 0.64, ey, 0.34, 0.44, K, s == 1 and 18 or -18, true) end
			elseif k == "bunny" then
				for s, ey in ipairs({ 0.24, 0.76 }) do
					local ear = fxPart(par, -0.12, ey, 0.82, 0.26, body, s == 1 and 9 or -9, true)
					fxPart(ear, 0.5, 0.5, 0.74, 0.5, PINK, 0, true)
				end
			elseif k == "fox" then
				for _, ey in ipairs({ 0.06, 0.94 }) do
					local ear = fxPart(par, 0.3, ey, 0.34, 0.34, dark, 45, false)
					fxPart(ear, 0.5, 0.5, 0.46, 0.46, K, 0, false)
				end
				fxPart(par, 0.88, 0.5, 0.42, 0.62, Color3.fromRGB(255, 238, 212), 0, true)
			elseif k == "dragon" then
				for s, ey in ipairs({ 0.1, 0.9 }) do fxPart(par, 0.14, ey, 0.55, 0.14, Color3.fromRGB(240, 228, 196), s == 1 and 25 or -25, true) end
			end
			fxZ = z + 1
			if k == "snake" then
				eyes("snake", 0.68, { 0.26, 0.74 }, 0.34)
			elseif k == "frog" then
				for _, ey in ipairs({ 0.05, 0.95 }) do
					local ring = fxPart(par, 0.55, ey, 0.5, 0.5, dark, 0, true)
					local wh = fxPart(ring, 0.5, 0.5, 0.72, 0.72, W, 0, true)
					fxPart(wh, 0.58, 0.5, 0.5, 0.5, K, 0, true)
				end
				for _, ny in ipairs({ 0.4, 0.6 }) do fxPart(par, 0.92, ny, 0.07, 0.07, dark, 0, true) end
			elseif k == "cat" then
				eyes("slit", 0.66, { 0.27, 0.73 }, 0.3)
				fxPart(par, 0.94, 0.5, 0.16, 0.13, PINK, 0, true)
				for s = -1, 1, 2 do
					for n = 0, 1 do
						local w = fxPart(par, 1.04, 0.5 + s * (0.2 + n * 0.1), 0.46, 0.04, Color3.fromRGB(240, 240, 246), s * (8 + n * 12), false)
						w.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
					end
				end
			elseif k == "dog" then
				eyes("dot", 0.64, { 0.27, 0.73 }, 0.26)
				local nose = fxPart(par, 0.95, 0.5, 0.22, 0.22, K, 0, true)
				fxPart(nose, 0.35, 0.3, 0.3, 0.3, W, 0, true).BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
			elseif k == "panda" then
				eyes("cute", 0.66, { 0.27, 0.73 }, 0.22)
				fxPart(par, 0.95, 0.5, 0.17, 0.13, K, 0, true)
			elseif k == "bunny" then
				eyes("dot", 0.64, { 0.27, 0.73 }, 0.26)
				fxPart(par, 0.94, 0.5, 0.15, 0.13, PINK, 0, true)
			elseif k == "fox" then
				eyes("dot", 0.64, { 0.25, 0.75 }, 0.24)
				fxPart(par, 1.0, 0.5, 0.17, 0.16, K, 0, true)
			elseif k == "dragon" then
				eyes("slit", 0.64, { 0.26, 0.74 }, 0.32, Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 60))
				for _, ny in ipairs({ 0.38, 0.62 }) do fxPart(par, 0.94, ny, 0.08, 0.08, K, 0, true) end
			elseif k == "bee" then
				eyes("cute", 0.62, { 0.27, 0.73 }, 0.3)
				for s = -1, 1, 2 do
					fxPart(par, 1.02, 0.5 + s * 0.3, 0.5, 0.06, K, s * 35, false)
					fxPart(par, 1.225, 0.5 + s * 0.443, 0.15, 0.15, K, 0, true)
				end
			elseif k == "penguin" then
				eyes("cute", 0.6, { 0.27, 0.73 }, 0.3)
				fxPart(par, 1.02, 0.5, 0.34, 0.3, Color3.fromRGB(255, 150, 40), 45, false)
			end
			local tg = nt(createFrame(par, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ZIndex = z }))
			if A.tongue == "fork" then
				local TC = Color3.fromRGB(226, 52, 70)
				fxPart(tg, 1.12, 0.5, 0.38, 0.1, TC, 0, false)
				fxPart(tg, 1.36, 0.4, 0.2, 0.1, TC, -28, false)
				fxPart(tg, 1.36, 0.6, 0.2, 0.1, TC, 28, false)
			elseif A.tongue == "frog" then
				fxPart(tg, 1.25, 0.5, 0.6, 0.1, Color3.fromRGB(240, 90, 120), 0, true)
				fxPart(tg, 1.55, 0.5, 0.16, 0.2, Color3.fromRGB(240, 90, 120), 0, true)
			elseif A.tongue == "dog" then
				fxPart(tg, 1.16, 0.5, 0.3, 0.24, Color3.fromRGB(255, 120, 144), 0, true)
				fxPart(tg, 1.16, 0.5, 0.22, 0.04, Color3.fromRGB(214, 80, 106), 0, false)
			elseif A.tongue == "flame" then
				fxPart(tg, 1.14, 0.5, 0.42, 0.2, Color3.fromRGB(255, 150, 40), 0, true)
				fxPart(tg, 1.36, 0.5, 0.26, 0.12, Color3.fromRGB(255, 230, 90), 0, true)
			end
			fxZ = 4
			return tg
		end
		tongue = buildDeco(headFx, ANIMALS[animalI], 4)
		GREEN, DARK, DOT, HEADC = SKINS[skinI][2], SKINS[skinI][3], SKINS[skinI][4], SKINS[skinI][5]
		local snake, occ, queue, food = {}, {}, {}, nil
		local pool, shrink = {}, nil
		local dx, dy = 1, 0
		local score, eaten, interval = 0, 0, 0.2
		local running, over, paused, wrap = false, false, false, false
		local acc = 0
		local pauseBtn, wrapBtn, newBtn

		local function pos(x, y) return UDim2.fromScale(x * CS / G, y * CS / G) end
		local CELL = UDim2.fromScale(CS / G, CS / G)
		local function newSeg(x, y, col, round)
			local s = nt(createFrame(grid, { Position = pos(x, y), Size = CELL, BackgroundColor3 = col }))
			corner(s, 0)
			local fill = nt(createFrame(s, { Name = "Fill", BackgroundColor3 = col, ZIndex = 3, Visible = false }))
			s:GetPropertyChangedSignal("BackgroundColor3"):Connect(function() fill.BackgroundColor3 = s.BackgroundColor3 end)
			local d = nt(createFrame(s, { Name = "Dot", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.34, 0.34), BackgroundColor3 = DOT, BackgroundTransparency = 0.3 }))
			corner(d, 99)
			return s
		end
		local ENDR = 6
		local function setFill(fr, bx, by)
			local fill = fr:FindFirstChild("Fill")
			if not fill then return end
			if not bx then fill.Visible = false return end
			fill.BackgroundColor3 = fr.BackgroundColor3
			fill.Visible = true
			if bx ~= 0 then
				fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 1)
				fill.Position = UDim2.fromScale(bx > 0 and 0.5 or 0, 0)
			else
				fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 0.5)
				fill.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, by > 0 and 0.5 or 0)
			end
		end
		local function dirTo(a, b)
			local ddx, ddy = b.x - a.x, b.y - a.y
			if ddx > 1 then ddx = -1 elseif ddx < -1 then ddx = 1 end
			if ddy > 1 then ddy = -1 elseif ddy < -1 then ddy = 1 end
			return ddx, ddy
		end
		local function styleSeg(fr, x, y, role, bx, by)
			local c, dot = fr:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner"), fr:FindFirstChild("Dot")
			if role == "head" then
				fr.BackgroundColor3 = HEADC
				if c then c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, ENDR) end
				setFill(fr, bx, by)
				if dot then dot.Visible = false end
			else
				fr.BackgroundColor3 = rainbow and Color3.fromHSV(((x * 3 + y * 5) % 12) / 12, 0.55, 1) or ((ANIMALS[animalI].stripe and (x + y) % 2 ~= 0) and DARK or GREEN)
				if c then c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, role == "tail" and ENDR or 0) end
				if role == "tail" then setFill(fr, bx, by) else setFill(fr) end
				if dot then dot.Visible = role ~= "tail" and (ANIMALS[animalI].dot or 0) > 0 end
			end
			if dot then
				local A = ANIMALS[animalI]
				dot.BackgroundColor3 = DOT
				dot.BackgroundTransparency = A.dotTr or 0.3
				if (A.dot or 0) > 0 then dot.Size = UDim2.fromScale(A.dot, A.dot) end
			end
		end
		local function applySkin(i)
			skinI = i
			local k = SKINS[i]
			rainbow = k.rainbow == true
			GREEN, DARK, DOT, HEADC = k[2], k[3], k[4], k[5]
			for n, sg in ipairs(snake) do
				if sg.f.Parent then
					local bx, by
					if n == 1 and snake[2] then bx, by = dirTo(sg, snake[2]) elseif n == #snake and n > 1 then bx, by = dirTo(sg, snake[n - 1]) end
					styleSeg(sg.f, sg.x, sg.y, n == 1 and "head" or (n == #snake and "tail" or "body"), bx, by)
				end
			end
		end
		local function applyAnimal(i, keepSkin)
			animalI = i
			tongue = buildDeco(headFx, ANIMALS[i], 4)
			applySkin(keepSkin and skinI or skinIndex(ANIMALS[i].skin))
		end

		local function setRect(fr, x, y, ax, ay, len)
			local w, h, ox, oy = CS, CS, 0, 0
			if ax ~= 0 then w = len ox = ax > 0 and (CS - len) or 0 end
			if ay ~= 0 then h = len oy = ay > 0 and (CS - len) or 0 end
			fr.Position = UDim2.fromScale((x * CS + ox - 0.5) / G, (y * CS + oy - 0.5) / G)
			fr.Size = UDim2.fromScale((w + 1) / G, (h + 1) / G)
		end
		local function full(fr, x, y)
			fr.Position = UDim2.fromScale((x * CS - 0.5) / G, (y * CS - 0.5) / G)
			fr.Size = UDim2.fromScale((CS + 1) / G, (CS + 1) / G)
		end
		local function bestKey() return wrap and "snake_wrap" or "snake" end
		local function header()
			scoreL.Text = "score " .. score
			lenL.Text = "len " .. #snake
			bestL.Text = "best " .. (scores[bestKey()] or 0)
		end
		local function release()
			if shrink then
				shrink.f.Visible = false
				pool[#pool + 1] = shrink.f
				shrink = nil
			end
		end
		local function getFrame(x, y, col)
			local fr = table.remove(pool)
			if fr then
				fr.Visible = true
				fr.BackgroundColor3 = col
			else
				fr = newSeg(x, y, col)
			end
			return fr
		end
		local function clearBoard()
			for _, s in ipairs(snake) do s.f:Destroy() end
			if shrink then shrink.f:Destroy() shrink = nil end
			for _, fr in ipairs(pool) do fr:Destroy() end
			pool = {}
			if food then food.f:Destroy() end
			snake, occ, queue, food = {}, {}, {}, nil
		end
		local FS = 1.4
		local function buildApple(x, y, gold, parent, px, idx)
			local ap = APPLES[idx or appleI] or APPLES[1]
			local body = gold and GOLD or ap[2]
			local shade = gold and Color3.fromRGB(226, 160, 40) or ap[3]
			local root = nt(createFrame(parent or grid, px and { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(px, px), BackgroundTransparency = 1 } or { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale((x + 0.5) * CS / G, (y + 0.5) * CS / G), Size = CELL, BackgroundTransparency = 1, ZIndex = 1 }))
			local function part(cx, cy, w, h, col, rot, tr, circ)
				local q = nt(createFrame(root, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(cx, cy), Size = UDim2.fromScale(w, h), BackgroundColor3 = col, BackgroundTransparency = tr or 0, Rotation = rot or 0 }))
				if circ ~= false then corner(q, 99) end
				return q
			end
			local kd, leafC, WH, BR = ap.kind, ap[4], Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Color3.fromRGB(104, 70, 40)
			if kd then
				if kd == "strawberry" then
					part(0.5, 0.42, 0.86, 0.6, body)
					part(0.5, 0.62, 0.6, 0.62, body)
					part(0.5, 0.8, 0.3, 0.32, shade)
					for _, sd in ipairs({ { .3, .4 }, { .5, .33 }, { .7, .4 }, { .4, .56 }, { .6, .56 }, { .5, .72 } }) do part(sd[1], sd[2], 0.08, 0.1, Color3.fromRGB(255, 232, 120)) end
					part(0.36, 0.17, 0.32, 0.14, leafC, -25) part(0.64, 0.17, 0.32, 0.14, leafC, 25) part(0.5, 0.13, 0.14, 0.24, leafC, 0)
				elseif kd == "cherry" then
					part(0.4, 0.34, 0.05, 0.5, BR, -18, 0, false) part(0.6, 0.34, 0.05, 0.5, BR, 18, 0, false)
					part(0.3, 0.7, 0.46, 0.46, shade) part(0.3, 0.68, 0.42, 0.42, body)
					part(0.7, 0.72, 0.46, 0.46, shade) part(0.7, 0.7, 0.42, 0.42, body)
					part(0.22, 0.6, 0.1, 0.12, WH, -25, 0.45) part(0.62, 0.62, 0.1, 0.12, WH, -25, 0.45)
					part(0.62, 0.1, 0.34, 0.16, leafC, -20)
				elseif kd == "melon" then
					part(0.5, 0.55, 0.88, 0.84, shade)
					part(0.5, 0.55, 0.76, 0.72, leafC)
					part(0.5, 0.55, 0.66, 0.62, body)
					for _, sd in ipairs({ { .38, .46 }, { .62, .46 }, { .5, .6 }, { .36, .66 }, { .64, .66 } }) do part(sd[1], sd[2], 0.07, 0.11, Color3.fromRGB(30, 24, 24), 0) end
				elseif kd == "lemon" then
					part(0.5, 0.58, 0.9, 0.62, shade, -15)
					part(0.5, 0.56, 0.86, 0.58, body, -15)
					part(0.07, 0.64, 0.14, 0.14, body, 0) part(0.93, 0.5, 0.14, 0.14, body, 0)
					part(0.34, 0.5, 0.18, 0.12, WH, -20, 0.45)
					part(0.68, 0.2, 0.34, 0.16, leafC, -28)
				elseif kd == "pear" then
					part(0.5, 0.7, 0.78, 0.6, shade) part(0.5, 0.68, 0.74, 0.56, body)
					part(0.5, 0.4, 0.5, 0.5, shade) part(0.5, 0.39, 0.46, 0.46, body)
					part(0.3, 0.62, 0.14, 0.2, WH, -20, 0.5)
					part(0.5, 0.14, 0.07, 0.22, BR, 12, 0, false) part(0.7, 0.14, 0.32, 0.15, leafC, -25)
				elseif kd == "grapes" then
					part(0.5, 0.14, 0.07, 0.22, BR, 0, 0, false) part(0.7, 0.16, 0.3, 0.15, leafC, -25)
					for _, g in ipairs({ { .28, .4 }, { .5, .4 }, { .72, .4 }, { .39, .6 }, { .61, .6 }, { .5, .8 } }) do
						part(g[1], g[2], 0.34, 0.34, shade) part(g[1], g[2] - 0.01, 0.3, 0.3, body)
						part(g[1] - 0.06, g[2] - 0.07, 0.08, 0.08, WH, 0, 0.45)
					end
				elseif kd == "pine" then
					part(0.5, 0.62, 0.78, 0.8, shade) part(0.5, 0.61, 0.74, 0.76, body)
					for _, sd in ipairs({ { .3, .5 }, { .5, .46 }, { .7, .5 }, { .4, .64 }, { .6, .64 }, { .3, .78 }, { .5, .8 }, { .7, .78 } }) do part(sd[1], sd[2], 0.11, 0.11, shade, 45, 0.15, false) end
					part(0.5, 0.14, 0.14, 0.4, leafC, 0, 0, false) part(0.34, 0.18, 0.14, 0.34, leafC, -28, 0, false) part(0.66, 0.18, 0.14, 0.34, leafC, 28, 0, false)
				end
				return root
			end
			if ap.round then
				part(0.5, 0.63, 0.8, 0.76, shade)
				part(0.5, 0.58, 0.76, 0.72, body)
			else
				part(0.5, 0.66, 0.78, 0.68, shade)
				part(0.35, 0.55, 0.6, 0.62, body)
				part(0.65, 0.55, 0.6, 0.62, body)
				part(0.5, 0.64, 0.64, 0.6, body)
			end
			part(0.3, 0.47, 0.16, 0.22, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), -25, 0.45)
			part(0.5, 0.24, 0.1, 0.28, Color3.fromRGB(104, 70, 40), 14, 0, false)
			part(0.72, 0.16, 0.36, 0.18, ap[4], -28)
			return root
		end
		local function applyApple(i)
			appleI = i
			if food and food.f and food.f.Parent then
				local old = food.f
				local fr = buildApple(food.x, food.y, food.gold)
				create("UIScale", { Scale = FS }, fr)
				food.f = fr
				old:Destroy()
			end
		end
		local function placeFood(forceNormal, drop)
			if food then
				local o = food.f
				food = nil
				local osc = o:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIScale")
				if osc then tween(osc, 0.15, { Scale = 0 }) end
				task.delay(0.16, function() o:Destroy() end)
			end
			local free = {}
			for i = 0, N * N - 1 do
				if not occ[i] then free[#free + 1] = i end
			end
			if #free == 0 then return false end
			local k = free[math.random(#free)]
			local gold = (not forceNormal) and eaten > 0 and eaten % 5 == 0
			local x, y = k % N, math.floor(k / N)
			local fr = buildApple(x, y, gold)
			local sc = create("UIScale", { Scale = drop and FS or 0 }, fr)
			if drop then
				local landed = fr.Position
				fr.Visible = false
				fr.Position = UDim2.fromScale((x + 0.5) * CS / G, -0.3)
				fr.Rotation = -28
				task.delay(0.38, function()
					if not fr.Parent then return end
					fr.Visible = true
					tween(fr, 0.85, { Position = landed }, Enum.EasingStyle.Bounce, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
					tween(fr, 0.6, { Rotation = 0 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
				end)
			else
				tween(sc, 0.3, { Scale = FS }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
			end
			food = { x = x, y = y, f = fr, gold = gold, ttl = gold and 45 or nil }
			return true
		end
		local function placeHead(p)
			local h = snake[1]
			if not h then headFx.Visible = false return end
			local mx, my = h.mx or dx, h.my or dy
			local off = (1 - p) * CS
			headFx.Visible = true
			headFx.Position = UDim2.fromScale((h.x * CS + CS / 2 - mx * off) / G, (h.y * CS + CS / 2 - my * off) / G)
			headFx.Rotation = math.deg(math.atan2(my, mx))
		end
		local function newGame()
			clearBoard()
			dx, dy = 1, 0
			score, eaten, interval, acc = 0, 0, 0.2, 0
			running, over, paused = false, false, false
			for j = 1, 3 do
				local x = 3 + j
				table.insert(snake, 1, { x = x, y = 6, f = newSeg(x, 6, GREEN) })
				occ[6 * N + x] = true
			end
			for i, sg in ipairs(snake) do
				local bx, by
				if i == 1 and snake[2] then bx, by = dirTo(sg, snake[2]) elseif i == #snake and i > 1 then bx, by = dirTo(sg, snake[i - 1]) end
				styleSeg(sg.f, sg.x, sg.y, i == 1 and "head" or (i == #snake and "tail" or "body"), bx, by)
			end
			snake[1].f.ZIndex = 2
			placeHead(1)
			placeFood(true, true)
			header()
			msg.Text = "press a direction to start"
			if pauseBtn then pauseBtn.set("Pause", ICON.pause) end
		end
		local function die(note)
			over, running = true, false
			if snake[1] then tween(snake[1].f, 0.2, { BackgroundColor3 = C.red }) placeHead(1) end
			local fresh = score > 0 and record(bestKey(), score)
			header()
			msg.Text = (note or "game over") .. " - " .. score .. (fresh and " - new best!" or "")
		end
		local function step()
			local h = snake[1]
			full(h.f, h.x, h.y)
			release()
			if queue[1] then
				dx, dy = queue[1][1], queue[1][2]
				table.remove(queue, 1)
			end
			local nx, ny = h.x + dx, h.y + dy
			if wrap then
				nx, ny = nx % N, ny % N
			elseif nx < 0 or ny < 0 or nx >= N or ny >= N then
				die("hit the wall") return
			end
			local eat = food ~= nil and food.x == nx and food.y == ny
			local tail = snake[#snake]
			if occ[ny * N + nx] and (eat or not (tail.x == nx and tail.y == ny)) then
				die("bit yourself") return
			end
			if not eat then
				table.remove(snake)
				occ[tail.y * N + tail.x] = nil
				local nxt = snake[#snake]
				local tdx, tdy = nxt.x - tail.x, nxt.y - tail.y
				if tdx > 1 then tdx = -1 elseif tdx < -1 then tdx = 1 end
				if tdy > 1 then tdy = -1 elseif tdy < -1 then tdy = 1 end
				tail.f.ZIndex = 1
				local pv = snake[#snake - 1]
				local tbx, tby
				if pv then tbx, tby = dirTo(nxt, pv) end
				styleSeg(nxt.f, nxt.x, nxt.y, "tail", tbx, tby)
				shrink = { f = tail.f, x = tail.x, y = tail.y, dx = tdx, dy = tdy }
			end
			local fr = getFrame(nx, ny, HEADC)
			styleSeg(fr, nx, ny, "head", -dx, -dy)
			styleSeg(h.f, h.x, h.y, "body")
			h.f.ZIndex = 1
			fr.ZIndex = 2
			setRect(fr, nx, ny, -dx, -dy, 0)
			table.insert(snake, 1, { x = nx, y = ny, f = fr, mx = dx, my = dy })
			occ[ny * N + nx] = true
			if eat then
				local gain = food.gold and 3 or 1
				score += gain
				eaten += 1
				interval = math.max(0.09, 0.2 - score * 0.003)
				if not placeFood() then die("board full - perfect") return end
				header()
				msg.Text = food.gold and "golden food - worth 3, hurry!" or ("+" .. gain)
			elseif food and food.ttl then
				food.ttl -= 1
				if food.ttl <= 0 then placeFood(true) end
			end
		end
		local function render(p)
			local h = snake[1]
			if h and h.mx then setRect(h.f, h.x, h.y, -h.mx, -h.my, p * CS) end
			if shrink then setRect(shrink.f, shrink.x, shrink.y, shrink.dx, shrink.dy, (1 - p) * CS) end
			placeHead(p)
		end
		local function steer(ax, ay)
			if over or paused then return end
			local lx, ly = dx, dy
			local q = queue[#queue]
			if q then lx, ly = q[1], q[2] end
			if ax == -lx and ay == -ly then return end
			if not (ax == lx and ay == ly) and #queue < 2 then table.insert(queue, { ax, ay }) end
			if not running then
				running = true
				acc = 0
				msg.Text = wrap and "wrap-around mode" or "walls are deadly"
			end
		end
		local function togglePause()
			if over or not running then return end
			paused = not paused
			if paused then pauseBtn.set("Resume", ICON.play) else pauseBtn.set("Pause", ICON.pause) end
			msg.Text = paused and "paused" or ""
		end

		local KEYS = { [Enum.KeyCode.Left] = { -1, 0 }, [Enum.KeyCode.A] = { -1, 0 }, [Enum.KeyCode.Right] = { 1, 0 }, [Enum.KeyCode.D] = { 1, 0 }, [Enum.KeyCode.Up] = { 0, -1 }, [Enum.KeyCode.W] = { 0, -1 }, [Enum.KeyCode.Down] = { 0, 1 }, [Enum.KeyCode.S] = { 0, 1 } }
		table.insert(conns, UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, processed)
			if processed or not isOpen or not current or current.id ~= "snake" then return end
			local d = KEYS[input.KeyCode]
			if d then steer(d[1], d[2])
			elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Space or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.P then togglePause()
			elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.R then newGame() end
		end))

		local swipeStart
		grid.InputBegan:Connect(function(input) if isPress(input) then swipeStart = input.Position end end)
		table.insert(conns, UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
			if swipeStart and isMove(input) then
				local d = input.Position - swipeStart
				if math.max(math.abs(d.X), math.abs(d.Y)) > 16 then
					swipeStart = input.Position
