					if math.abs(d.X) > math.abs(d.Y) then steer(d.X > 0 and 1 or -1, 0) else steer(0, d.Y > 0 and 1 or -1) end
				end
			end
		end))
		table.insert(conns, UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
			if swipeStart and isPress(input) then swipeStart = nil end
		end))

		do
			local PS, KS = 112, 32
			local pad = nt(createFrame(f, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(AW - PS, 226), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(PS, PS), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }))
			local baseC = nt(createFrame(pad, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1) }))
			corner(baseC, PS / 2)
			State.glassStyle(baseC, nil, PS / 2)
			baseC.BackgroundTransparency = 0.93
			local hub = nt(createFrame(pad, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(22, 22), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1) }))
			corner(hub, 11)
			State.glassStyle(hub, C.accent, 11)
			local hubS = create("UIScale", {}, hub)
			local function key(x, y, dir, ax, ay)
				local b = pbtn(pad, x, y, KS, KS, "", function() end, false, true)
				sym(b, dir, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 13)
				b.InputBegan:Connect(function(input) if isPress(input) then steer(ax, ay) tween(hubS, 0.08, { Scale = 1.25 }) end end)
				b.InputEnded:Connect(function(input) if isPress(input) then tween(hubS, 0.22, { Scale = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out) end end)
			end
			local m = (PS - KS) / 2
			key(m, 5, "up", 0, -1)
			key(5, m, "left", -1, 0)
			key(m, PS - KS - 5, "down", 0, 1)
			key(PS - KS - 5, m, "right", 1, 0)
		end

		local BRICK, MORTAR, PORTAL = Color3.fromRGB(176, 84, 70), Color3.fromRGB(104, 44, 38), Color3.fromRGB(90, 200, 235)
		local function paintEdges()
			edge:ClearAllChildren()
			local T = 4
			if wrap then
				gStroke.Color, gStroke.Thickness, gStroke.Transparency = PORTAL, 1.5, 0.35
				local function dash(x, y, w, h) nt(createFrame(edge, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, h), BackgroundColor3 = PORTAL, BackgroundTransparency = 0.45 })) end
				for x = 3, G - 8, 10 do dash(x, 0, 5, 2) dash(x, G - 2, 5, 2) end
				for y = 3, G - 8, 10 do dash(0, y, 2, 5) dash(G - 2, y, 2, 5) end
			else
				gStroke.Color, gStroke.Thickness, gStroke.Transparency = BRICK, 1.5, 0.3
				local function strip(x, y, w, h) nt(createFrame(edge, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, h), BackgroundColor3 = BRICK, BackgroundTransparency = 0.45 })) end
				local function joint(x, y, w, h) nt(createFrame(edge, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, h), BackgroundColor3 = MORTAR, BackgroundTransparency = 0.35 })) end
				strip(0, 0, G, T) strip(0, G - T, G, T) strip(0, T, T, G - 2 * T) strip(G - T, T, T, G - 2 * T)
				for x = 9, G - 4, 12 do joint(x, 0, 1, T) joint(x + 6, G - T, 1, T) end
				for y = 7, G - 8, 8 do joint(0, y, T, 1) joint(G - T, y, T, 1) end
				joint(0, T - 1, G, 1) joint(0, G - T, G, 1)
			end
		end

		newBtn = iconBtn(f, 0, 232, 68, 24, "New", newGame, ICON.plus, true, true)
		pauseBtn = iconBtn(f, 0, 259, 68, 24, "Pause", togglePause, ICON.pause, false, true)
		wrapBtn = iconBtn(f, 0, 286, 68, 24, "Walls", function()
			if running and not over then msg.Text = "change the mode between games" return end
			wrap = not wrap
			wrapBtn.set(wrap and "Wrap" or "Walls", wrap and ICON.wrap or ICON.walls)
			paintEdges()
			header()
			msg.Text = wrap and "mode: wrap-around" or "mode: walls"
		end, nil, false, true)
		wrapBtn.set("Walls", ICON.walls)
		paintEdges()

		do
			local GREY = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 160)
			local panel = nt(createFrame(f, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(AW, 228), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 26), ZIndex = 30, Visible = false, Active = true }))
			corner(panel, 12)
			stroke(panel, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 1, 0.85)
			local function lab(par, text, y, size, col, bold) local l = pl(par, text, size, col, 0, y, AW, 14, CENTER, bold and FONT_BOLD or FONT) l.ZIndex = 32 return l end
			lab(panel, "Customize", 4, 12, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), true)

			local strip = nt(createFrame(panel, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 24), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(AW - 16, 34), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 0.93, ZIndex = 31 }))
			corner(strip, 8)
			local pSegs, pApple = {}, nil
			for i = 1, 5 do
				local q = nt(createFrame(strip, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8 + (i - 1) * 20, 7), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(21, 20), BackgroundColor3 = GREEN, ZIndex = 32 }))
				corner(q, 0)
				local d = nt(createFrame(q, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.34, 0.34), BackgroundColor3 = DOT, BackgroundTransparency = 0.3, ZIndex = 33 }))
				corner(d, 99)
				pSegs[i] = { q, d }
			end
			local pFace = nt(createFrame(strip, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8 + 4 * 20, 7), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 20), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ZIndex = 33 }))
			local function paintPreview()
				local A = ANIMALS[animalI]
				for i, sg in ipairs(pSegs) do
					local isHead = i == 5
					sg[1].BackgroundColor3 = isHead and HEADC or (rainbow and Color3.fromHSV(((i * 2) % 12) / 12, 0.55, 1) or ((A.stripe and i % 2 == 0) and DARK or GREEN))
					sg[2].BackgroundColor3 = DOT
					sg[2].BackgroundTransparency = A.dotTr or 0.3
					sg[2].Size = UDim2.fromScale(math.max(A.dot or 0.34, 0.1), math.max(A.dot or 0.34, 0.1))
					sg[2].Visible = not isHead and i ~= 1 and (A.dot or 0) > 0
				end
				local tg = buildDeco(pFace, A, 34)
				tg.Visible = A.tongue ~= nil
				if pApple then pApple:Destroy() end
				pApple = buildApple(0, 0, false, strip, 26)
				pApple.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(AW - 16 - 38, 4)
				for _, d in ipairs(pApple:GetDescendants()) do if d:IsA("GuiObject") then d.ZIndex = 33 end end
				pApple.ZIndex = 33
			end

			local function saveStyle()
				scores.snake_skin, scores.snake_apple, scores.snake_animal = skinI, appleI, animalI
				pcall(function() if writefile then writefile("NauruHub/phone_scores.txt", game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONEncode(scores)) end end)
			end

			local pages, tabBtns = {}, {}
			local function newPage() return nt(createFrame(panel, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 88), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(AW, 106), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ZIndex = 31, Visible = false })) end
			local pgAnimal, pgColor, pgApple = newPage(), newPage(), newPage()
			pages = { pgAnimal, pgColor, pgApple }
			local nameL = {}
			nameL[1], nameL[2], nameL[3] = lab(pgAnimal, "", 84, 10, GREY), lab(pgColor, "", 98, 10, GREY), lab(pgApple, "", 74, 10, GREY)

			local animalBtns, skinStrokes, appleStrokes = {}, {}, {}
			local function refreshSel()
				for i, b in ipairs(animalBtns) do b.BackgroundColor3 = i == animalI and C.accent or C.field end
				for i, st in ipairs(skinStrokes) do st.Transparency = i == skinI and 0 or 1 end
				for i, st in ipairs(appleStrokes) do st.Transparency = i == appleI and 0 or 1 end
				nameL[1].Text = ANIMALS[animalI].name
				nameL[2].Text = SKINS[skinI][1]
				nameL[3].Text = APPLES[appleI][1]
			end
			local function swatch(parent, x, y, size, fill, inner, onPick, rainbowFill)
				local b = nt(createButton(parent, { Sound = true, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size), BackgroundColor3 = fill, BackgroundTransparency = 0, ZIndex = 32, AutoButtonColor = false }))
				corner(b, 999)
				if rainbowFill then
					local kps = {}
					for i = 0, 6 do table.insert(kps, ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(i / 6, Color3.fromHSV(i / 6 % 1, 0.6, 1))) end
					b.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
					nt(create("UIGradient", { Color = ColorSequence.new(kps), Rotation = 45 }, b))
				end
				local d = nt(createFrame(b, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.36, 0.36), BackgroundColor3 = inner, ZIndex = 33 }))
				corner(d, 99)
				local st = stroke(b, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 2, 1)
				nt(st)
				local sc = create("UIScale", {}, b)
				b.MouseEnter:Connect(function() tween(sc, 0.12, { Scale = 1.1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back) end)
				b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tween(sc, 0.12, { Scale = 1 }) end)
				b.Activated:Connect(function()
					tween(sc, 0.08, { Scale = 0.88 })
					task.delay(0.08, function() tween(sc, 0.2, { Scale = 1.1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back) end)
					onPick()
				end)
				return st
			end

			local function fruitSwatch(parent, x, y, size, idx, onPick)
				local b = nt(createButton(parent, { Sound = true, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 0.92, ZIndex = 32, AutoButtonColor = false }))
				corner(b, 8)
				local root = buildApple(0, 0, false, b, size - 4, idx)
				root.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(2, 2)
				root.ZIndex = 33
				for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do if d:IsA("GuiObject") then d.ZIndex = 33 end end
				local st = stroke(b, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 2, 1)
				nt(st)
				local sc = create("UIScale", {}, b)
				b.MouseEnter:Connect(function() tween(sc, 0.12, { Scale = 1.1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back) end)
				b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tween(sc, 0.12, { Scale = 1 }) end)
				b.Activated:Connect(function()
					tween(sc, 0.08, { Scale = 0.88 })
					task.delay(0.08, function() tween(sc, 0.2, { Scale = 1.1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back) end)
					onPick()
				end)
				return st
			end

			do
				local bw, gap = 40, 3
				local x0 = (AW - (4 * bw + 3 * gap)) / 2
				for i, A in ipairs(ANIMALS) do
					local col, row = (i - 1) % 4, (i - 1) // 4
					local b = pbtn(pgAnimal, x0 + col * (bw + gap), 2 + row * 26, bw, 22, A.name, function()
						applyAnimal(i) paintPreview() refreshSel() saveStyle()
					end, false)
					b.TextSize = 9
					b.ZIndex = 32
					animalBtns[i] = b
				end
			end

			for i, k in ipairs(SKINS) do
				local col, row = (i - 1) % 6, (i - 1) // 6
				skinStrokes[i] = swatch(pgColor, col * ((AW - 26) / 5), 2 + row * 31, 26, k[2], k[5], function()
					applySkin(i) paintPreview() refreshSel() saveStyle()
				end, k.rainbow)
			end

			for i, k in ipairs(APPLES) do
				appleStrokes[i] = fruitSwatch(pgApple, ((i - 1) % 7) * ((AW - 24) / 6), 2 + ((i - 1) // 7) * 34, 24, i, function()
					applyApple(i) paintPreview() refreshSel() saveStyle()
				end)
			end

			local function showTab(n)
				for i, pg in ipairs(pages) do pg.Visible = i == n end
				for i, b in ipairs(tabBtns) do b.BackgroundColor3 = i == n and C.accent or C.field end
			end
			for i, name in ipairs({ "Animal", "Color", "Fruit" }) do
				local b = pbtn(panel, 8 + (i - 1) * 58, 62, 54, 20, name, function() showTab(i) end, false)
				b.TextSize = 10
				b.ZIndex = 32
				tabBtns[i] = b
			end

			local function closePanel()
				panel.Visible = false
			end
			local done = pbtn(panel, 0, 198, AW, 24, "Done", closePanel, true)
			done.ZIndex = 32
			paintPreview() refreshSel() showTab(1)
			local styleBtn
			styleBtn = iconBtn(f, 0, 313, 68, 24, "Style", function()
				if running and not over and not paused then togglePause() end
				paintPreview() refreshSel()
				panel.Visible = true
			end, ICON.style, false, true)

			table.insert(conns, RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
				if panel.Visible and (not isOpen or not current or current.id ~= "snake") then panel.Visible = false end
			end))
		end

		table.insert(conns, RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
			if not isOpen or not current or current.id ~= "snake" then return end
			if tongue then tongue.Visible = (not over) and (ANIMALS[animalI].always or (os.clock() % 1.5) < 0.4) end
			if not running or over or paused then return end
			acc += math.min(dt, 0.05)
			if acc >= interval then
				acc -= interval
				step()
				if over then return end
				acc = math.min(acc, interval)
			end
			render(acc / interval)
		end))

		newGame()
	end

	local function buildMemory(f)
		local RGBc = Color3.fromRGB
		local FACES = {
			{ "heart", RGBc(235, 90, 110) }, { "star", RGBc(245, 195, 60) }, { "moon", RGBc(150, 150, 245) }, { "sun", RGBc(250, 155, 45) },
			{ "cloud", RGBc(100, 175, 240) }, { "bolt", RGBc(185, 115, 240) }, { "flame", RGBc(240, 105, 60) }, { "gem", RGBc(60, 200, 190) },
		}
		local FW, FH = 42, 56
		local ART = 24
		local PAPER, EDGE = Color3.fromRGB(250, 246, 238), Color3.fromRGB(196, 190, 178)
		local DUR = 0.3
		local cards, up, matched, moves, lock, over, gen = {}, {}, 0, 0, false, false, 0
		local started, t0 = false, 0
		local movesL = pl(f, "", 12, C.text, 0, 0, 80, 20, nil, FONT_BOLD)
		local timeL = pl(f, "0:00", 12, C.sub, AW / 2 - 30, 0, 60, 20, CENTER, FONT_BOLD)
		local bestL = pl(f, "", 12, C.sub, AW - 84, 0, 84, 20, RIGHT, FONT_BOLD)
		local msg = pl(f, "", 11, C.sub, 0, 266, AW, 16, CENTER)
		local pips = {}
		for k = 1, #FACES do
			local p = nt(createFrame(f, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset((AW - (#FACES * 15 - 5)) / 2 + (k - 1) * 15, 288), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 10), BackgroundColor3 = C.line, BackgroundTransparency = 0.2 }))
			corner(p, 5)
			pips[k] = p
		end

		local function bestText() bestL.Text = "best " .. (scores.memory and (scores.memory .. " mv") or "-") end

		local function paintFace(h, kind, col)
			local white, black = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
			local light, dark = col:Lerp(white, 0.45), col:Lerp(black, 0.3)
			local function bx(x, y, w, hh, c, r, tr, round)
				local b = nt(createFrame(h, { Position = UDim2.fromScale(x / 20, y / 20), Size = UDim2.fromScale(w / 20, hh / 20), BackgroundColor3 = c, BackgroundTransparency = tr or 0 }))
				create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(round and 0.5 or (r or 0.25), 0) }, b)
				return b
			end
			if kind == "heart" then
				bx(1.6, 2.2, 8.8, 8.8, col, 0, 0, true)
				bx(9.6, 2.2, 8.8, 8.8, col, 0, 0, true)
				bx(2.2, 6.4, 15.6, 5, col, 0.3)
				bx(4.2, 10.6, 11.6, 3.4, col, 0.3)
				bx(6.4, 13.4, 7.2, 3, col, 0.3)
				bx(8.4, 15.8, 3.2, 2.6, col, 0.4)
				bx(4, 4, 3.4, 3.4, white, 0, 0.55, true)
			elseif kind == "star" then
				bx(8.8, 0.8, 2.4, 4, col, 0.35)
				bx(7, 3.6, 6, 3.2, col, 0.3)
				bx(1.2, 6.4, 17.6, 3.4, col, 0.3)
				bx(3.6, 9.4, 12.8, 3.4, col, 0.3)
				bx(4.2, 12.4, 4.2, 5.8, col, 0.3)
				bx(11.6, 12.4, 4.2, 5.8, col, 0.3)
				bx(8.8, 2, 2.4, 3, white, 0.4, 0.5)
				bx(8.2, 8, 3.6, 3.6, light, 0, 0.1, true)
			elseif kind == "moon" then
				bx(2, 2, 16, 16, col, 0, 0, true)
				bx(5, 5, 4, 4, dark, 0, 0.35, true)
				bx(10.6, 9, 5, 5, dark, 0, 0.35, true)
				bx(6.4, 12.6, 2.6, 2.6, dark, 0, 0.35, true)
				bx(4, 3.4, 3.2, 2, white, 0.5, 0.55)
			elseif kind == "sun" then
				bx(9, 0.8, 2, 3.4, col, 0.5)
				bx(9, 15.8, 2, 3.4, col, 0.5)
				bx(0.8, 9, 3.4, 2, col, 0.5)
				bx(15.8, 9, 3.4, 2, col, 0.5)
				for _, a in ipairs({ { 3.2, 3.2 }, { 14.4, 3.2 }, { 3.2, 14.4 }, { 14.4, 14.4 } }) do bx(a[1], a[2], 2.4, 2.4, col, 0, 0, true) end
				bx(5, 5, 10, 10, col, 0, 0, true)
				bx(7, 6.8, 3, 3, white, 0, 0.55, true)
			elseif kind == "cloud" then
				bx(1.6, 8.4, 7.4, 7.4, col, 0, 0, true)
				bx(5.2, 3.8, 9, 9, col, 0, 0, true)
				bx(10.8, 7, 7.6, 7.6, col, 0, 0, true)
				bx(3.4, 10.4, 13.4, 5.6, col, 0.5)
				bx(7.4, 5.4, 3.6, 2, white, 0.5, 0.5)
				bx(5.6, 16.8, 1.6, 2.6, light, 0.5)
				bx(10.4, 16.8, 1.6, 2.6, light, 0.5)
			elseif kind == "bolt" then
				bx(9, 0.8, 6.4, 4.4, col, 0.25)
				bx(7, 4.6, 6.4, 4, col, 0.25)
				bx(3.8, 8.2, 11.2, 3.4, col, 0.25)
				bx(6.6, 11.2, 6.4, 4.2, col, 0.25)
				bx(6.6, 14.8, 3.4, 4.2, col, 0.4)
				bx(10, 1.6, 2.4, 1.4, white, 0.5, 0.5)
			elseif kind == "flame" then
				bx(4.2, 8, 11.6, 11, col, 0, 0, true)
				bx(6.6, 3.6, 6.4, 8, col, 0.45)
				bx(8.6, 0.8, 3, 5, col, 0.5)
				bx(6.4, 10.6, 7.2, 8, RGBc(255, 190, 70), 0, 0, true)
				bx(8.2, 13.6, 3.6, 5, RGBc(255, 240, 170), 0, 0, true)
			else
				bx(5, 1.6, 10, 4, light, 0.3)
				bx(1.4, 5.4, 17.2, 4.2, col, 0.25)
				bx(3.6, 9.2, 12.8, 3.6, col, 0.3)
				bx(6, 12.4, 8, 3.2, dark, 0.3)
				bx(8.2, 15.2, 3.6, 3, dark, 0.4)
				bx(6.2, 2.6, 2.4, 1.6, white, 0.5, 0.5)
				bx(3.2, 6.4, 2.8, 1.8, white, 0.5, 0.6)
			end
		end
		local function paintBack(h)
			local function bp(x, y, w, hh, c, tr, round, r)
				local b = nt(createFrame(h, { Position = UDim2.fromScale(x / FW, y / FH), Size = UDim2.fromScale(w / FW, hh / FH), BackgroundColor3 = c, BackgroundTransparency = tr }))
				create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(round and 0.5 or (r or 0.1), 0) }, b)
				return b
			end
			bp(3.5, 3.5, FW - 7, FH - 7, C.accent, 0, false, 0.07)
			for i = 0, 4 do
				for j = 0, 6 do
					local x, y = 9.5 + i * 5.6, 9.5 + j * 6.2
					if not (math.abs(x - FW / 2) < 8 and math.abs(y - FH / 2) < 8) then bp(x - 1, y - 1, 2, 2, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.62, true) end
				end
			end
			local fr = bp(6.5, 6.5, FW - 13, FH - 13, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 1, false, 0.06)
			stroke(fr, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 1, 0.45)
			bp(FW / 2 - 8, FH / 2 - 8, 16, 16, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.3, true)
			bp(FW / 2 - 5.5, FH / 2 - 5.5, 11, 11, C.accent, 0, true)
			bp(FW / 2 - 2.2, FH / 2 - 2.2, 4.4, 4.4, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.15, true)
		end

		local function paintCard(h, kind, col)
			local function holder(cx, cy, size, rot)
				return nt(createFrame(h, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(cx / FW, cy / FH), Size = UDim2.fromScale(size / FW, size / FH), Rotation = rot or 0, BackgroundTransparency = 1 }))
			end
			paintFace(holder(FW / 2, FH / 2, ART), kind, col)
			paintFace(holder(8.5, 8.5, 9), kind, col)
			paintFace(holder(FW - 8.5, FH - 8.5, 9, 180), kind, col)
		end

		local function smooth(p) return p * p * (3 - 2 * p) end
		local function applyFlip(c, p)
			local e = smooth(p)
			local w = math.abs(math.cos(math.pi * e))
			if e >= 0.5 and not c.swapped then
				c.swapped = true
				c.shown = c.anim.to
				c.face.Visible = c.shown
				c.backA.Visible = not c.shown
			end
			c.btn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(FW * w, FH)
			c.sc.Scale = 1 + 0.12 * math.sin(math.pi * p)
		end
		local function settle(c)
			c.btn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(FW, FH)
			c.sc.Scale = 1
		end
		local function startFlip(c, to, now)
			c.anim = { t0 = now or os.clock(), to = to }
			c.swapped = false
		end
		local function mmss(x) return string.format("%d:%02d", math.floor(x / 60), x % 60) end
		local lastSec = -1
		table.insert(conns, RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			local now = os.clock()
			for _, c in ipairs(cards) do
				if c.anim then
					local p = math.clamp((now - c.anim.t0) / DUR, 0, 1)
					applyFlip(c, p)
					if p >= 1 then
						c.anim = nil
						settle(c)
					end
				elseif c.pop then
					local p = math.clamp((now - c.pop) / 0.32, 0, 1)
					c.sc.Scale = 1 + 0.16 * math.sin(math.pi * p)
					if p >= 1 then c.pop = nil c.sc.Scale = 1 end
				end
				if c.shake then
					local p = math.clamp((now - c.shake) / 0.36, 0, 1)
					c.btn.Rotation = 7 * math.sin(p * math.pi * 5) * (1 - p)
					if p >= 1 then c.shake = nil c.btn.Rotation = 0 end
				end
			end
			if started and not over and current and current.id == "memory" then
				local s = math.floor(now - t0)
				if s ~= lastSec then
					lastSec = s
					timeL.Text = mmss(s)
				end
			end
		end))

		local function lightPip(k) pips[k].BackgroundColor3, pips[k].BackgroundTransparency = FACES[k][2], 0 end
		local function addBadge(c)
			local b = nt(createFrame(c.btn, { Name = "Badge", Position = UDim2.fromOffset(FW - 14, 3), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(11, 11), BackgroundColor3 = RGBc(60, 200, 110) }))
			corner(b, 6)
			local function arm(x, y, w, rot)
				local a = nt(createFrame(b, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, 1.5), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Rotation = rot }))
				corner(a, 1)
			end
			arm(3.9, 6.3, 3.2, 45)
			arm(6.9, 5, 5.6, -50)
			c.badge = b
		end
		local function flip(i)
			local c = cards[i]
			if lock or over or c.up or c.matched or c.anim then return end
			if not started then
				started, t0, lastSec = true, os.clock(), -1
			end
			c.up = true
			startFlip(c, true)
			table.insert(up, i)
			if #up < 2 then return end
			moves += 1
			movesL.Text = "moves " .. moves
			local a, b = up[1], up[2]
			local ca, cb = cards[a], cards[b]
			local my = gen
			if ca.id == cb.id then
				matched += 1
				ca.matched, cb.matched = true, true
				up = {}
				lightPip(ca.id)
				task.delay(DUR + 0.02, function()
					if gen ~= my then return end
					local now = os.clock()
					for _, c2 in ipairs({ ca, cb }) do
						c2.base.Color, c2.base.Thickness = FACES[c2.id][2], 2
						c2.btn.BackgroundColor3 = PAPER:Lerp(FACES[c2.id][2], 0.14)
						addBadge(c2)
						c2.pop = now
					end
				end)
				if matched == #FACES then
					over = true
					local fresh = record("memory", moves, true)
					timeL.Text = mmss(math.floor(os.clock() - t0))
					msg.Text = "all pairs in " .. moves .. " moves" .. (fresh and " - new best!" or "")
					bestText()
				else
					msg.Text = matched .. " / " .. #FACES .. " pairs"
				end
			else
				lock = true
				task.delay(DUR + 0.04, function()
					if gen ~= my then return end
					local now = os.clock()
					ca.shake, cb.shake = now, now
				end)
				task.delay(1.0, function()
					if gen ~= my then return end
					local now = os.clock()
					ca.up, cb.up = false, false
					startFlip(ca, false, now)
					startFlip(cb, false, now)
					task.delay(DUR + 0.04, function()
						if gen ~= my then return end
						up, lock = {}, false
					end)
				end)
			end
		end
		local function newGame()
			gen += 1
			local ids = {}
			for k = 1, #FACES do ids[#ids + 1] = k ids[#ids + 1] = k end
			for i = #ids, 2, -1 do
				local j = math.random(i)
				ids[i], ids[j] = ids[j], ids[i]
			end
			up, matched, moves, lock, over = {}, 0, 0, false, false
			started, lastSec = false, -1
			for i, c in ipairs(cards) do
				c.id, c.up, c.matched, c.shown, c.anim, c.pop, c.shake, c.swapped = ids[i], false, false, false, nil, nil, nil, false
				if c.face then c.face:Destroy() end
				c.base.Color, c.base.Thickness = EDGE, 1
				if c.badge then c.badge:Destroy() c.badge = nil end
				local face = FACES[c.id]
				c.face = nt(createFrame(c.btn, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Visible = false }))
				paintCard(c.face, face[1], face[2])
				c.backA.Visible = true
				c.btn.Rotation = 0
				c.btn.BackgroundColor3 = PAPER
				settle(c)
			end
			for k = 1, #FACES do pips[k].BackgroundColor3, pips[k].BackgroundTransparency = C.line, 0.2 end
			movesL.Text = "moves 0"
			timeL.Text = "0:00"
			msg.Text = "tap two cards to flip them"
			bestText()
		end
		for i = 1, 16 do
			local r, c = math.floor((i - 1) / 4), (i - 1) % 4
			local btn = nl(createButton(f, { Sound = true, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(c * 48 + FW / 2, 26 + r * 60 + FH / 2), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(FW, FH), BackgroundColor3 = PAPER, BackgroundTransparency = 0, ClipsDescendants = true, AutoButtonColor = false }))
			corner(btn, 6)
			local base = stroke(btn, EDGE, 1, 0)
			local backA = nt(createFrame(btn, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1) }))
			paintBack(backA)
			local sc = create("UIScale", {}, btn)
			cards[i] = { btn = btn, backA = backA, base = base, sc = sc, shown = false }
			btn.Activated:Connect(function() flip(i) end)
		end
		pbtn(f, 0, 306, AW, 30, "new game", newGame, true)
		newGame()
	end

	local function buildSpotifyInner(f)
		local M = State.music
		if not M then
			pl(f, "the music player is not loaded", 11, C.sub, 0, 130, AW, 16, CENTER)
			return
		end
		local Player = Players.LocalPlayer
		local LEFT = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		local GREEN = Color3.fromRGB(30, 215, 96)
		local BG, SURF, SURF2 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 18), Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40), Color3.fromRGB(62, 62, 62)
		local WHITE, GRAY, DIM, BLACK = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Color3.fromRGB(179, 179, 179), Color3.fromRGB(115, 115, 115), Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
		local HS = game:GetService("HttpService")
		local FILE, LIKES = "NauruHub/phone_playlists.txt", "NauruHub/phone_liked.txt"
		local W, H = SW, SH
		local PADX = 10
		local IW = W - PADX * 2
		local NAV_Y = H - 62
		local MINI_Y = NAV_Y - 46

		local lists, liked = {}, {}
		pcall(function()
			if isfile and isfile(FILE) then
				local d = HS:JSONDecode(readfile(FILE))
				if type(d) == "table" then
					for _, P in ipairs(d) do
						if type(P) == "table" and type(P.name) == "string" and type(P.ids) == "table" then table.insert(lists, P) end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
		pcall(function()
			if isfile and isfile(LIKES) then
				local d = HS:JSONDecode(readfile(LIKES))
				if type(d) == "table" then
					for _, id in ipairs(d) do if type(id) == "string" then liked[id] = true end end
				end
			end
		end)
		local function ensureFolder()
			if isfolder and makefolder and not isfolder("NauruHub") then makefolder("NauruHub") end
		end
		local function save()
			pcall(function()
				if not writefile then return end
				ensureFolder()
				writefile(FILE, HS:JSONEncode(lists))
			end)
		end
		local function saveLikes()
			pcall(function()
				if not writefile then return end
				ensureFolder()
				local a = {}
				for id in pairs(liked) do a[#a + 1] = id end
				table.sort(a)
				writefile(LIKES, HS:JSONEncode(a))
			end)
		end
		local songs, byId = {}, {}
		local function refreshSongs()
			songs = M.downloads()
			byId = {}
			for _, e in ipairs(songs) do byId[e.id] = e end
		end
		refreshSongs()
		local function trim(t) return (tostring(t or ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")) end

		local function hue(s)
			local h = 0
			for i = 1, #s do h = (h * 31 + s:byte(i)) % 360 end
			return h / 360
		end
		local function tint(s, v, sat) return Color3.fromHSV(hue(tostring(s)), sat or 0.5, v or 0.4) end
		local function mmss(x)
			x = math.max(0, math.floor(x or 0))
			return string.format("%d:%02d", math.floor(x / 60), x % 60)
		end
		local function mk(parent, props) return nt(createFrame(parent, props)) end
		local function T(parent, text, size, color, x, y, w, h, align, bold)
			return pd(nt(createLabel(parent, text, size, color, bold and FONT_BOLD or FONT, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, h), TextXAlignment = align or LEFT, NoTitle = true })))
		end
		local function hit(parent, cb, z)
			local b = nt(createButton(parent, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ZIndex = z or 5 }))
			if cb then b.Activated:Connect(cb) end
			return b
		end
		local function clear(sc) for _, c in ipairs(sc:GetChildren()) do if c:IsA("GuiObject") then c:Destroy() end end end
		local function mkScroll(parent, y)
			local sc = nt(create("ScrollingFrame", {
				Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, y), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W, H - y), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ScrollBarThickness = 2,
				ScrollBarImageColor3 = GRAY, CanvasSize = UDim2.new(), AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y,
				ElasticBehavior = Enum.ElasticBehavior.Never,
			}, parent))
			create("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 0), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, sc)
			create("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, PADX), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, PADX) }, sc)
			return sc
		end
		local function spacer(parent, order, h) return mk(parent, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, h or 112), LayoutOrder = order }) end

		local ALT = { house = "home", ["circle-arrow-down"] = "arrow-down-circle", ["circle-plus"] = "plus-circle", ["circle-minus"] = "minus-circle", ["circle-check"] = "check-circle", ellipsis = "more-horizontal", ["layout-grid"] = "grid" }
		local function ic(parent, name, size, color, x, y, fb)
			local api = { color = color, name = name, fb = fb }
			local h = mk(parent, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size) })
			local img = nt(create("ImageLabel", { BackgroundTransparency = 1, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), ImageColor3 = color }, h))
			local lb = pd(nt(createLabel(h, fb or "", math.max(8, math.floor(size * 0.8)), color, FONT_BOLD, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, NoTitle = true, Visible = false, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None })))
			api.h = h
			local function apply()
				local ok = State.setLucide(img, api.name, api.color)
				if not ok and ALT[api.name] then ok = State.setLucide(img, ALT[api.name], api.color) end
				img.Visible = ok and true or false
				lb.Visible = (not ok) and api.fb ~= nil
				img.ImageColor3 = api.color
				lb.TextColor3 = api.color
			end
			function api.set(n, col, fbText)
				if n then api.name = n end
				if col then api.color = col end
				if fbText then api.fb = fbText lb.Text = fbText end
				apply()
			end
			apply()
			return api
		end
		local function heart(parent, s, x, y, on, offColor, onColor)
			local api = { on = on }
			local h = mk(parent, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(s, s) })
			local outl = ic(h, "heart", s, offColor or GRAY, s / 2, s / 2, "♡")
			local fill = mk(h, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1) })
			local col = onColor or GREEN
			local a = s / 1.707
			local d = a * 1.4142
			local cx, cy = s / 2, s * 0.04 + a * 0.854
			mk(fill, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(cx, cy), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(a, a), Rotation = 45, BackgroundColor3 = col })
			for _, sx in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
				local c = mk(fill, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(cx + sx * d / 4, cy - d / 4), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(a, a), BackgroundColor3 = col })
				corner(c, 999)
			end
			api.h = h
			function api.set(v)
				api.on = v and true or false
				fill.Visible = api.on
				outl.h.Visible = not api.on
			end
			api.set(on)
			return api
		end
		local function playGlyph(parent, s, color, x, y)
			local api = {}
			local h = mk(parent, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(s, s) })
			local tri = pd(nt(createLabel(h, "▶", math.floor(s * 0.95), color, FONT_BOLD, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(s * 0.06, 0), Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, NoTitle = true, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None })))
			local bars = mk(h, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Visible = false })
			for _, bx in ipairs({ 0.18, 0.58 }) do
				local b = mk(bars, { Position = UDim2.fromScale(bx, 0.14), Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.24, 0.72), BackgroundColor3 = color })
				corner(b, 1)
			end
			api.h = h
			function api.set(playing)
				tri.Visible = not playing
				bars.Visible = playing and true or false
			end
			return api
		end
		local function bump(h)
			local us = h:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIScale") or create("UIScale", {}, h)
			us.Scale = 0.65
			tween(us, 0.32, { Scale = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
		end
		local function cover(parent, id, s, x, y, r, fill)
			local fr = mk(parent, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(s, s), BackgroundColor3 = fill or (id and tint(id, 0.32) or SURF2), ClipsDescendants = true })
			corner(fr, r or 4)
			local img = id and M.thumb(id)
			if img then
				nt(create("ImageLabel", { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = img, ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop }, fr))
			else
				ic(fr, "music", math.max(10, math.floor(s * 0.42)), GRAY, s / 2, s / 2, "♪")
			end
			return fr
		end
		local function avatar(parent, x, y, s)
			local a = mk(parent, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(s, s), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromHSV(hue(Player.Name), 0.6, 0.78) })
			corner(a, 999)
			T(a, string.upper(Player.Name:sub(1, 1)), math.floor(s * 0.5), WHITE, 0, 0, s, s, Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, true)
			return a
		end

		local function likedIds()
			local out = {}
			for _, e in ipairs(songs) do if liked[e.id] then out[#out + 1] = e.id end end
			return out
		end
		local function allIds()
			local out = {}
			for _, e in ipairs(songs) do out[#out + 1] = e.id end
			return out
		end
		local function getList(kind, idx)
			if kind == "liked" then return { name = "Liked Songs", ids = likedIds(), kind = kind } end
			if kind == "downloaded" then return { name = "Downloaded", ids = allIds(), kind = kind } end
			local P = lists[idx]
			if not P then return nil end
			return { name = P.name, ids = P.ids, kind = "user", idx = idx }
		end
		local function listArt(parent, Ls, s, x, y, r)
			if Ls.kind == "liked" or Ls.kind == "downloaded" then
				local fr = mk(parent, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(s, s), BackgroundColor3 = WHITE })
				corner(fr, r or 4)
				if Ls.kind == "liked" then
					create("UIGradient", { Rotation = 45, Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(70, 40, 230), Color3.fromRGB(170, 200, 235)) }, fr)
					heart(fr, math.floor(s * 0.46), s / 2, s / 2, true, WHITE, WHITE)
				else
					create("UIGradient", { Rotation = 45, Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(20, 120, 60), Color3.fromRGB(30, 215, 96)) }, fr)
					ic(fr, "arrow-down", math.floor(s * 0.46), WHITE, s / 2, s / 2, "↓")
				end
				return fr
			end
			local first
			for _, id in ipairs(Ls.ids) do if byId[id] then first = id break end end
			return cover(parent, first, s, x, y, r, (not first) and tint(Ls.name, 0.3) or nil)
		end

		local ctxName, ctxKey = "Your Library", ""
		local baseOrder = {}
		local shuffleOn, repeatOn = false, false
		local lastId
		local function entriesOf(ids)
			local out = {}
			for _, id in ipairs(ids) do if byId[id] then out[#out + 1] = byId[id] end end
			return out
		end
		local function playEntries(es, i, name, key)
			if #es == 0 or not es[i] then return end
			ctxName, ctxKey = name or ctxName, key or ctxKey
			baseOrder = es
			if shuffleOn then
				local rest = {}
				for k, e in ipairs(es) do if k ~= i then rest[#rest + 1] = e end end
				for k = #rest, 2, -1 do
					local j = math.random(k)
					rest[k], rest[j] = rest[j], rest[k]
				end
				local q = { es[i] }
				for _, e in ipairs(rest) do q[#q + 1] = e end
				M.play(q, 1)
			else
				M.play(es, i)
			end
		end
		local function applyShuffle(on)
			shuffleOn = on
			local q = M.queueRef
			local n = M.now()
			if not q or #q == 0 or not n.id then return end
			local cur
			for i, x in ipairs(q) do if x.id == n.id then cur = i break end end
			if not cur then return end
			local others = {}
			for i, x in ipairs(q) do if i ~= cur then others[#others + 1] = x end end
			if on then
				for i = #others, 2, -1 do
					local j = math.random(i)
					others[i], others[j] = others[j], others[i]
				end
			else
				local pos = {}
				for i, e in ipairs(baseOrder) do pos[e.id] = i end
				table.sort(others, function(a, b) return (pos[a.id] or 1e9) < (pos[b.id] or 1e9) end)
			end
			local k = 0
			for i = 1, #q do
				if i ~= cur then
					k += 1
					q[i] = others[k]
				end
			end
		end

		local root = mk(f, { Name = "SpotifyRoot", Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-8, -62), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W, H), BackgroundColor3 = BG, ClipsDescendants = true })
		local pages = {}
		local function mkPage(z, color)
			return mk(root, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W, H), BackgroundColor3 = color or BG, ZIndex = z, Visible = false })
		end
		local reg = {}
		local function paintNow(id)
			for i = #reg, 1, -1 do
				local r = reg[i]
				if not r.title.Parent then
					table.remove(reg, i)
				else
					local on = id ~= nil and r.id == id
					r.title.TextColor3 = on and GREEN or WHITE
					if r.spk then r.spk.Visible = on end
				end
			end
		end
		local toastF = mk(root, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(W / 2, MINI_Y - 6), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(160, 26), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 235), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ZIndex = 40, Visible = false })
		corner(toastF, 6)
		local toastL = T(toastF, "", 9, BLACK, 0, 0, 160, 26, Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, true)
		local tTok = 0
		local function toast(text)
			tTok += 1
			local my = tTok
			toastL.Text = text
			toastF.Visible = true
			toastF.BackgroundTransparency, toastL.TextTransparency = 0.04, 0
			task.delay(1.8, function()
				if my ~= tTok then return end
				tween(toastF, 0.25, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
				tween(toastL, 0.25, { TextTransparency = 1 })
				task.delay(0.27, function() if my == tTok then toastF.Visible = false end end)
			end)
		end

		local go, openList, openCreate, openSheet, openPlayer, closePlayer
		local B = {}
		local curKind, curIdx, listBack = "liked", nil, "home"
		local sheetSong, pendingAdd
		local mainPage, lastTab = "home", "home"
		local Mini, Nav, Pl = {}, {}, {}
		local force = false

		local function songRow(parent, order, e, o)
			o = o or {}
			local row = nt(createButton(parent, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 46), BackgroundTransparency = 1, LayoutOrder = order }))
			cover(row, e.id, 38, 0, 4, 3)
			local rw = IW - 46 - (o.right and 30 or 4)
			local t1 = nl(T(row, e.title, 11, WHITE, 46, 6, rw - 14, 15, nil, true))
			local sx = 46
			if o.dl ~= false then
				ic(row, "circle-arrow-down", 10, GREEN, 51, 31, "↓")
				sx = 59
			end
			nl(T(row, o.sub or (e.author ~= "" and e.author or "Saved track"), 9, GRAY, sx, 24, rw - (sx - 46), 13))
			local spk = ic(row, "volume-2", 11, GREEN, IW - (o.right and 40 or 12), 14, "♪")
			spk.h.Visible = false
			reg[#reg + 1] = { id = e.id, title = t1, spk = spk.h }
			if o.tap then row.Activated:Connect(o.tap) end
			local ri
			if o.right then
				local rb = nt(createButton(row, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(30, 40), ZIndex = 3 }))
				if o.right == "heart" then
					ri = heart(rb, 14, 15, 20, liked[e.id] and true or false, GRAY)
				else
					local nm = ({ more = "ellipsis", remove = "circle-minus", add = "circle-plus", on = "circle-check" })[o.right] or "ellipsis"
					local fb = ({ more = "...", remove = "-", add = "+", on = "✓" })[o.right] or "..."
					ri = ic(rb, nm, 16, o.right == "on" and GREEN or GRAY, 15, 20, fb)
				end
				if o.rightCb then rb.Activated:Connect(function()
					if o.right == "more" and ri and ri.h then
						bump(ri.h)
						ri.h.Rotation = -90
						tween(ri.h, 0.35, { Rotation = 0 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
					end
					o.rightCb(ri)
				end) end
			end
			return row, ri
		end

		local function listRow(parent, order, Ls, back)
			local row = nt(createButton(parent, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 54), BackgroundTransparency = 1, LayoutOrder = order }))
			listArt(row, Ls, 46, 0, 4, 3)
			nl(T(row, Ls.name, 11, WHITE, 54, 9, IW - 60, 15, nil, true))
			local n, sx = #Ls.ids, 54
			if Ls.kind == "liked" then
				ic(row, "pin", 10, GREEN, 59, 33, "•")
				sx = 68
			end
			T(row, "Playlist • " .. n .. (n == 1 and " song" or " songs"), 9, GRAY, sx, 26, IW - sx - 6, 13)
			row.Activated:Connect(function() openList(Ls.kind, Ls.idx, back or "lib") end)
			return row
		end

		do
			local pHome = mkPage(1)
			pages.home = pHome
			avatar(pHome, PADX, 34, 26)
			local greetL = T(pHome, "Good evening", 15, WHITE, PADX + 34, 34, 140, 26, nil, true)
			local sc = mkScroll(pHome, 66)
			local picks, picksN = {}, -1
			local function section(order, text)
				local fr = mk(sc, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32), LayoutOrder = order })
				T(fr, text, 13, WHITE, 0, 10, IW, 18, nil, true)
			end
			function B.home()
				clear(sc)
				refreshSongs()
				local h = tonumber(os.date("%H")) or 12
				greetL.Text = h < 5 and "Good night" or h < 12 and "Good morning" or h < 18 and "Good afternoon" or "Good evening"
				local n = 0
				local tiles = { getList("liked"), getList("downloaded") }
				for i = 1, math.min(#lists, 4) do tiles[#tiles + 1] = getList("user", i) end
				local rows = math.ceil(#tiles / 2)
				n += 1
				local grid = mk(sc, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, rows * 40 - 4), LayoutOrder = n })
				local tw = (IW - 6) / 2
				for i, Ls in ipairs(tiles) do
					local col, row = (i - 1) % 2, math.floor((i - 1) / 2)
					local b = nt(createButton(grid, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(col * (tw + 6), row * 40), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(tw, 36), BackgroundColor3 = SURF, BackgroundTransparency = 0 }))
					corner(b, 4)
					listArt(b, Ls, 36, 0, 0, 4)
					local nm = nl(T(b, Ls.name, 9, WHITE, 42, 0, tw - 46, 36, nil, true))
					nm.TextWrapped = true
					b.Activated:Connect(function() openList(Ls.kind, Ls.idx, "home") end)
				end
				if #songs == 0 then
					n += 1
					spacer(sc, n, 14)
					n += 1
					local card = mk(sc, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 112), LayoutOrder = n, BackgroundColor3 = SURF })
					corner(card, 8)
					T(card, "Your library is empty", 12, WHITE, 12, 12, IW - 24, 16, nil, true)
					local s = T(card, "Search for a song and tap its download button. Everything you download shows up here.", 9, GRAY, 12, 32, IW - 24, 36)
					s.TextWrapped = true
					s.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
					local pill = mk(card, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(12, 76), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(96, 26), BackgroundColor3 = WHITE })
					corner(pill, 13)
					T(pill, "Find songs", 10, BLACK, 0, 0, 96, 26, Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, true)
					hit(pill, function() go("search") end)
				else
					n += 1
					section(n, "Your downloads")
					n += 1
					local hr = mk(sc, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 126), LayoutOrder = n })
					local hs = nt(create("ScrollingFrame", { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ScrollBarThickness = 0, CanvasSize = UDim2.new(), AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.X, ElasticBehavior = Enum.ElasticBehavior.Never }, hr))
					create("UIListLayout", { FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal, Padding = UDim.new(0, 8), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, hs)
					for i, e in ipairs(songs) do
						if i > 12 then break end
						local card = nt(createButton(hs, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(84, 124), LayoutOrder = i }))
						cover(card, e.id, 84, 0, 0, 4)
						local t1 = nl(T(card, e.title, 10, WHITE, 0, 89, 84, 14, nil, true))
						nl(T(card, e.author ~= "" and e.author or "Saved track", 9, GRAY, 0, 103, 84, 12))
						reg[#reg + 1] = { id = e.id, title = t1 }
						card.Activated:Connect(function() playEntries(songs, i, "Downloaded", "downloaded") end)
					end
					if picksN ~= #songs then
						picksN = #songs
						picks = {}
						local pool = {}
						for _, e in ipairs(songs) do pool[#pool + 1] = e end
						for i = 1, math.min(5, #pool) do picks[#picks + 1] = table.remove(pool, math.random(#pool)) end
					end
					n += 1
					section(n, "Quick picks")
					for i, e in ipairs(picks) do
						n += 1
						songRow(sc, n, e, { tap = function() playEntries(picks, i, "Quick picks", "picks") end, right = "heart", rightCb = function(hr2)
							liked[e.id] = (not liked[e.id]) or nil
							saveLikes()
							hr2.set(liked[e.id] and true or false)
							toast(liked[e.id] and "Added to Liked Songs" or "Removed from Liked Songs")
						end })
					end
				end
				n += 1
				spacer(sc, n)
				paintNow(lastId)
			end
		end

		do
			local pSearch = mkPage(1)
			pages.search = pSearch
			T(pSearch, "Search", 18, WHITE, PADX, 32, 120, 26, nil, true)
			local bar = mk(pSearch, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(PADX, 64), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(IW, 30), BackgroundColor3 = WHITE })
			corner(bar, 6)
			ic(bar, "search", 14, BLACK, 16, 15, "?")
			local sBox = pd(nt(create("TextBox", { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(30, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(IW - 38, 30), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", PlaceholderText = "What do you want to listen to?", PlaceholderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(100, 100, 100), TextColor3 = BLACK, Font = FONT_BOLD, TextSize = 10, TextXAlignment = LEFT, ClearTextOnFocus = false, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd }, bar)))
			local cancelB = nt(createButton(pSearch, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(W - PADX - 44, 64), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(44, 30), Visible = false }))
			T(cancelB, "Cancel", 10, WHITE, 0, 0, 44, 30, Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, false)
			local browse = mkScroll(pSearch, 102)
			local results = mkScroll(pSearch, 102)
			results.Visible = false
			local note = T(pSearch, "", 10, GRAY, PADX, 140, IW, 40, Enum.TextXAlignment.Center)
			note.TextWrapped = true
			note.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
			local searchTok = 0
			local function barState()
				local active = sBox:IsFocused() or sBox.Text ~= ""
				cancelB.Visible = active
				tween(bar, 0.18, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(active and IW - 50 or IW, 30) })
			end
			sBox.Focused:Connect(barState)
			sBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(barState)
			local function showBrowse()
				searchTok += 1
				results.Visible, browse.Visible = false, true
				note.Text = ""
				clear(results)
			end
			cancelB.Activated:Connect(function()
				sBox.Text = ""
				sBox:ReleaseFocus()
				showBrowse()
				barState()
			end)
			local function resultRow(order, r)
				local row = nt(createButton(results, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 50), BackgroundTransparency = 1, LayoutOrder = order }))
				local cv = cover(row, nil, 40, 0, 5, 3)
				M.cover(r.id, r.thumb, function(img)
					if cv.Parent then
						cv:ClearAllChildren()
						corner(cv, 3)
						nt(create("ImageLabel", { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = img, ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop }, cv))
					end
				end)
				nl(T(row, r.title, 11, WHITE, 48, 7, IW - 48 - 34, 15, nil, true))
				nl(T(row, "Song • " .. (r.author ~= "" and r.author or "Unknown artist"), 9, GRAY, 48, 25, IW - 48 - 34, 13))
				local rb = nt(createButton(row, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(32, 40), ZIndex = 3 }))
				local ring = mk(rb, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 20), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 20), BackgroundColor3 = GREEN, BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
				corner(ring, 999)
				local rs = stroke(ring, GRAY, 1.3, 0)
				local ico = ic(ring, "arrow-down", 11, GRAY, 10, 10, "↓")
				local state = "idle"
				local function paint(st)
					state = st
					if st == "idle" then
						ring.BackgroundTransparency, rs.Color, rs.Transparency = 1, GRAY, 0
						ico.set("arrow-down", GRAY, "↓")
					elseif st == "busy" then
						ring.BackgroundTransparency, rs.Color, rs.Transparency = 1, GREEN, 0
						ico.set("loader", GREEN, "...")
					elseif st == "done" then
						ring.BackgroundTransparency, rs.Transparency = 0, 1
						ico.set("arrow-down", BLACK, "↓")
					else
						ring.BackgroundTransparency, rs.Color, rs.Transparency = 1, Color3.fromRGB(235, 87, 87), 0
						ico.set("x", Color3.fromRGB(235, 87, 87), "!")
					end
				end
				local function act()
					if state == "busy" then return end
					if state == "done" or M.has(r.id) then
						playEntries({ { id = r.id, title = r.title, author = r.author } }, 1, "Search results", "search")
						return
					end
					paint("busy")
					local started = M.download(r.id, r.title, r.author, function(ok)
						if rb.Parent then paint(ok and "done" or "fail") end
						if ok then
							refreshSongs()
							toast("Added to Downloaded")
						else
							toast("Download failed")
						end
					end)
					if started == false then
						paint("idle")
						toast("Another song is downloading")
					end
				end
				row.Activated:Connect(act)
				rb.Activated:Connect(act)
				paint(M.has(r.id) and "done" or "idle")
			end
			local function runSearch(q)
				q = trim(q or sBox.Text)
				if q == "" then showBrowse() return end
				sBox.Text = q
				sBox:ReleaseFocus()
				searchTok += 1
				local my = searchTok
				clear(results)
				browse.Visible, results.Visible = false, true
				note.Text = "Searching..."
				M.search(q, function(res, err)
					if my ~= searchTok then return end
					clear(results)
					if not res then note.Text = err or "Search failed" return end
					if #res == 0 then note.Text = "Couldn't find \"" .. q .. "\"" return end
					note.Text = ""
					local sh = mk(results, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26), LayoutOrder = 0 })
					T(sh, "Songs", 13, WHITE, 0, 6, IW, 18, nil, true)
					for i, r in ipairs(res) do resultRow(i, r) end
					spacer(results, 9999)
				end)
			end
			sBox.FocusLost:Connect(function(enter) if enter then runSearch() end end)

			local GENRES = {
				{ "Pop", 141, 103, 171 }, { "Hip-Hop", 186, 93, 7 }, { "Rock", 230, 17, 91 }, { "Chill", 71, 125, 149 },
				{ "Party", 175, 40, 150 }, { "Workout", 119, 119, 119 }, { "Focus", 80, 55, 80 }, { "Gaming", 39, 133, 106 },
				{ "Anime", 220, 20, 140 }, { "Phonk", 30, 50, 100 }, { "Lo-fi", 96, 129, 8 }, { "Latin", 220, 20, 60 },
				{ "Dance", 216, 64, 0 }, { "Metal", 13, 115, 236 },
			}
			function B.search()
				if browse.Visible then
					clear(browse)
					local hd = mk(browse, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26), LayoutOrder = 0 })
					T(hd, "Browse all", 13, WHITE, 0, 6, IW, 18, nil, true)
					local rows = math.ceil(#GENRES / 2)
					local grid = mk(browse, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, rows * 56), LayoutOrder = 1 })
					local tw = (IW - 8) / 2
					for i, g in ipairs(GENRES) do
						local col, row = (i - 1) % 2, math.floor((i - 1) / 2)
						local base = Color3.fromRGB(g[2], g[3], g[4])
						local t = nt(createButton(grid, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(col * (tw + 8), row * 56), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(tw, 48), BackgroundColor3 = base, BackgroundTransparency = 0, ClipsDescendants = true }))
						corner(t, 6)
						local art = mk(t, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(tw - 12, 38), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(34, 34), BackgroundColor3 = base:Lerp(BLACK, 0.28), Rotation = 24 })
						corner(art, 4)
						ic(art, "music", 14, base:Lerp(WHITE, 0.55), 17, 17, "♪")
						local nm = T(t, g[1], 11, WHITE, 8, 6, tw - 16, 30, nil, true)
						nm.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
						t.Activated:Connect(function() runSearch(g[1] .. " music") end)
					end
					spacer(browse, 9999)
				end
			end
		end

		do
			local pLib = mkPage(1)
			pages.lib = pLib
			avatar(pLib, PADX, 34, 26)
			T(pLib, "Your Library", 15, WHITE, PADX + 34, 34, 100, 26, nil, true)
			local plus = nt(createButton(pLib, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(W - 6, 32), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(30, 30) }))
			ic(plus, "plus", 18, WHITE, 15, 15, "+")
			plus.Activated:Connect(function() openCreate(nil) end)
			local chipKind = "playlists"
			local chips = {}
			local function paintChips()
				for k, c in pairs(chips) do
					c.fr.BackgroundColor3 = (k == chipKind) and GREEN or SURF
					c.lb.TextColor3 = (k == chipKind) and BLACK or WHITE
				end
			end
			local function makeChip(x, w, text, key)
				local fr = mk(pLib, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, 68), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, 22), BackgroundColor3 = SURF })
				corner(fr, 11)
				local lb = T(fr, text, 9, WHITE, 0, 0, w, 22, Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, true)
				chips[key] = { fr = fr, lb = lb }
				hit(fr, function() chipKind = key paintChips() B.lib() end)
			end
			makeChip(PADX, 58, "Playlists", "playlists")
			makeChip(PADX + 64, 46, "Songs", "songs")
			ic(pLib, "arrow-up-down", 10, WHITE, PADX + 6, 106, "↕")
			T(pLib, "Recents", 9, WHITE, PADX + 16, 99, 80, 14, nil, true)
			ic(pLib, "layout-grid", 12, GRAY, W - PADX - 6, 106, "▦")
			local sc = mkScroll(pLib, 120)
			function B.lib()
				clear(sc)
				refreshSongs()
				paintChips()
				local n = 0
				if chipKind == "playlists" then
					local items = { getList("liked"), getList("downloaded") }
					for i = 1, #lists do items[#items + 1] = getList("user", i) end
					for _, Ls in ipairs(items) do
						n += 1
						listRow(sc, n, Ls, "lib")
					end
					if #lists == 0 then
						n += 1
						local hint = mk(sc, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 74), LayoutOrder = n })
						local s = T(hint, "Create your first playlist and fill it with the songs you downloaded.", 9, GRAY, 0, 10, IW, 30)
						s.TextWrapped = true
						s.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
						local pill = mk(hint, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 44), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(104, 26), BackgroundColor3 = WHITE })
						corner(pill, 13)
						T(pill, "Create playlist", 9, BLACK, 0, 0, 104, 26, Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, true)
						hit(pill, function() openCreate(nil) end)
					end
				else
					if #songs == 0 then
						n += 1
						local s = T(mk(sc, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40), LayoutOrder = n }), "No songs yet. Use Search to download some.", 9, GRAY, 0, 12, IW, 26)
						s.TextWrapped = true
					end
					for i, e in ipairs(songs) do
						n += 1
						songRow(sc, n, e, { tap = function() playEntries(songs, i, "Downloaded", "downloaded") end, right = "more", rightCb = function() openSheet(e) end })
					end
				end
				n += 1
				spacer(sc, n)
				paintNow(lastId)
			end
		end

		do
			local pList = mkPage(3)
			pages.list = pList
			local sc = mkScroll(pList, 0)
			local backB = nt(createButton(pList, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 30), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(34, 32), ZIndex = 6 }))
			local backBg = mk(backB, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(17, 16), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(24, 24), BackgroundColor3 = BLACK, BackgroundTransparency = 0.55 })
			corner(backBg, 999)
			ic(backB, "chevron-left", 16, WHITE, 17, 16, "<")
			backB.Activated:Connect(function() go(listBack) end)
			local armedTok = 0
			function B.list()
				clear(sc)
				refreshSongs()
				local Ls = getList(curKind, curIdx)
				if not Ls then go(listBack) return end
				local isUser = Ls.kind == "user"
				local key = Ls.kind .. tostring(Ls.idx or "")
				local top
				if Ls.kind == "liked" then top = Color3.fromRGB(86, 66, 200)
				elseif Ls.kind == "downloaded" then top = Color3.fromRGB(24, 120, 62)
				else
					local first
					for _, id in ipairs(Ls.ids) do if byId[id] then first = id break end end
					top = tint(first or Ls.name, 0.5, 0.55)
				end
				local hb = mk(sc, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 258), LayoutOrder = 0 })
				local bg = mk(hb, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-PADX, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W, 258), BackgroundColor3 = WHITE })
				create("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Color = ColorSequence.new(top, BG) }, bg)
				local shadow = mk(hb, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset((IW - 112) / 2 + 2, 52), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(112, 112), BackgroundColor3 = BLACK, BackgroundTransparency = 0.7 })
				corner(shadow, 6)
				listArt(hb, Ls, 112, (IW - 112) / 2, 46, 4)
				nl(T(hb, Ls.name, 16, WHITE, 0, 168, IW, 22, nil, true))
				local n = #Ls.ids
				local av = avatar(hb, 0, 193, 14)
				T(hb, Player.Name, 9, WHITE, 19, 192, IW - 19, 16, nil, true)
				T(hb, "Playlist • " .. n .. (n == 1 and " song" or " songs"), 9, GRAY, 0, 208, IW, 13)

				local ay = 238
				if isUser then
					local addB = nt(createButton(hb, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, ay - 16), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(30, 32) }))
					ic(addB, "plus", 18, GRAY, 15, 16, "+")
					addB.Activated:Connect(function() go("pick") end)
					local moreB = nt(createButton(hb, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(30, ay - 16), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(30, 32) }))
					ic(moreB, "ellipsis", 18, GRAY, 15, 16, "...")
					local del = mk(hb, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(62, ay - 11), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(98, 22), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(205, 55, 55), Visible = false })
					corner(del, 11)
					T(del, "Delete playlist", 9, WHITE, 0, 0, 98, 22, Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, true)
					hit(del, function()
						table.remove(lists, curIdx)
						save()
						toast("Playlist deleted")
						go(listBack)
					end)
					moreB.Activated:Connect(function()
						armedTok += 1
						local my = armedTok
						del.Visible = not del.Visible
						task.delay(3, function() if my == armedTok and del.Parent then del.Visible = false end end)
					end)
				else
					local d = mk(hb, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, ay), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18), BackgroundColor3 = GREEN })
					corner(d, 999)
					ic(d, "arrow-down", 11, BLACK, 9, 9, "↓")
				end
				local shufB = nt(createButton(hb, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(IW - 84, ay - 16), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(32, 32) }))
				local shufI = ic(shufB, "shuffle", 18, shuffleOn and GREEN or GRAY, 16, 16, "⇄")
				shufB.Activated:Connect(function()
					applyShuffle(not shuffleOn)
					shufI.set(nil, shuffleOn and GREEN or GRAY)
					toast(shuffleOn and "Shuffle on" or "Shuffle off")
				end)
				local playB = nt(createButton(hb, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(IW, ay), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(40, 40), BackgroundColor3 = GREEN, BackgroundTransparency = 0 }))
				corner(playB, 999)
				local pg = playGlyph(playB, 18, BLACK, 20, 20)
				local nowp = M.now()
				pg.set(nowp.playing and ctxKey == key)
				playB.Activated:Connect(function()
					local cn = M.now()
					if cn.id and ctxKey == key then
						M.toggle()
						force = true
					else
						local es = entriesOf(Ls.ids)
						if #es == 0 then toast(isUser and "Add some songs first" or "Nothing here yet") return end
						playEntries(es, shuffleOn and math.random(#es) or 1, Ls.name, key)
					end
				end)

				local es = entriesOf(Ls.ids)
				if #Ls.ids == 0 then
					local e2 = mk(sc, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 90), LayoutOrder = 1 })
					local s = T(e2, isUser and "Let's find something for your playlist" or "Nothing here yet", 12, WHITE, 0, 14, IW, 18, Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, true)
					if isUser then
						local pill = mk(e2, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset((IW - 96) / 2, 44), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(96, 28), BackgroundColor3 = WHITE })
						corner(pill, 14)
						T(pill, "Add songs", 10, BLACK, 0, 0, 96, 28, Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, true)
						hit(pill, function() go("pick") end)
					else
						local s2 = T(e2, Ls.kind == "liked" and "Tap the heart on a song to save it here." or "Songs you download show up here.", 9, GRAY, 0, 38, IW, 26, Enum.TextXAlignment.Center)
						s2.TextWrapped = true
					end
				end
				local k = 0
				for i, id in ipairs(Ls.ids) do
					local e = byId[id]
					if e then
						k += 1
						local at = k
						local o = { tap = function() playEntries(es, at, Ls.name, key) end }
						if isUser then
							o.right = "remove"
							o.rightCb = function() table.remove(Ls.ids, i) save() B.list() end
						elseif Ls.kind == "liked" then
							o.right = "heart"
							o.rightCb = function(hr)
								liked[e.id] = nil
								saveLikes()
								B.list()
							end
						else
							o.right = "more"
							o.rightCb = function() openSheet(e) end
						end
						songRow(sc, i, e, o)
					elseif isUser then
						local row = mk(sc, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40), LayoutOrder = i })
						T(row, "Unavailable song", 11, DIM, 46, 4, IW - 80, 15, nil, true)
						T(row, "Download it again to play it", 9, DIM, 46, 20, IW - 80, 13)
						local rb = nt(createButton(row, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(30, 36) }))
						ic(rb, "circle-minus", 16, GRAY, 15, 18, "-")
						rb.Activated:Connect(function() table.remove(Ls.ids, i) save() B.list() end)
					end
				end
				spacer(sc, 99999)
				paintNow(lastId)
			end
		end

		do
			local pPick = mkPage(3)
			pages.pick = pPick
			local backB = nt(createButton(pPick, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 30), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(34, 32) }))
			ic(backB, "chevron-left", 18, WHITE, 17, 16, "<")
			backB.Activated:Connect(function() go("list") end)
			local title = T(pPick, "Add songs", 13, WHITE, 42, 32, IW - 40, 18, nil, true)
			local cnt = T(pPick, "", 9, GRAY, 42, 49, IW - 40, 12)
			local sc = mkScroll(pPick, 70)
			function B.pick()
				clear(sc)
				refreshSongs()
				local P = lists[curIdx]
				if not P then go("lib") return end
				title.Text = P.name
				local function where(id) for at, x in ipairs(P.ids) do if x == id then return at end end end
				local function count() cnt.Text = #P.ids .. (#P.ids == 1 and " song" or " songs") .. " in this playlist" end
				count()
				if #songs == 0 then
					local s = T(mk(sc, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 50), LayoutOrder = 1 }), "No downloaded songs yet. Use Search to download some.", 9, GRAY, 0, 14, IW, 30)
					s.TextWrapped = true
				end
				for i, e in ipairs(songs) do
					songRow(sc, i, e, { right = where(e.id) and "on" or "add", tap = function() end, rightCb = function(ri)
						local at = where(e.id)
						if at then table.remove(P.ids, at) ri.set("circle-plus", GRAY, "+") else table.insert(P.ids, e.id) ri.set("circle-check", GREEN, "✓") end
						save()
						count()
					end })
				end
				spacer(sc, 99999, 60)
				paintNow(lastId)
			end
		end

		local pCreate = mkPage(36)
		do
			T(pCreate, "Give your playlist a name", 14, WHITE, 0, 120, W, 24, Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, true)
			local box = pd(nt(create("TextBox", { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 168), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W - 40, 36), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", PlaceholderText = "My playlist", PlaceholderColor3 = DIM, TextColor3 = WHITE, Font = FONT_BOLD, TextSize = 18, ClearTextOnFocus = false, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center }, pCreate)))
			mk(pCreate, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 206), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W - 40, 1), BackgroundColor3 = GRAY })
			local pill = mk(pCreate, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset((W - 92) / 2, 238), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(92, 34), BackgroundColor3 = GREEN })
			corner(pill, 17)
			T(pill, "Create", 11, BLACK, 0, 0, 92, 34, Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, true)
			local cancel = nt(createButton(pCreate, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset((W - 92) / 2, 280), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(92, 28) }))
			T(cancel, "Cancel", 10, WHITE, 0, 0, 92, 28, Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, true)
			local function finish()
				local name = trim(box.Text):sub(1, 22)
				if name == "" then name = "My playlist #" .. (#lists + 1) end
				table.insert(lists, { name = name, ids = {} })
				save()
				box.Text = ""
				pCreate.Visible = false
				local idx = #lists
				if pendingAdd then
					table.insert(lists[idx].ids, pendingAdd.id)
					save()
					toast("Added to " .. name)
					pendingAdd = nil
					B.refreshChrome()
					if mainPage == "lib" then B.lib() end
				else
					openList("user", idx, mainPage == "list" and "lib" or mainPage)
				end
			end
			hit(pill, finish)
			box.FocusLost:Connect(function(enter) if enter then finish() end end)
			cancel.Activated:Connect(function()
				box.Text = ""
				pendingAdd = nil
				pCreate.Visible = false
				B.refreshChrome()
			end)
			function openCreate(songToAdd)
				pendingAdd = songToAdd
				pCreate.Visible = true
				B.refreshChrome()
				box:CaptureFocus()
			end
		end

		local pSheet = mkPage(35, BLACK)
		do
			pSheet.BackgroundTransparency = 0.45
			local sheetTok, closeSheet = 0, nil
			local scrim = nt(createButton(pSheet, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1) }))
			scrim.Activated:Connect(function() closeSheet() end)
			local panel = mk(pSheet, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, H), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W, 250), BackgroundColor3 = SURF, ZIndex = 2 })
			corner(panel, 14)
			local body = mk(panel, {})
			body.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
			function closeSheet()
				if not pSheet.Visible then return end
				sheetTok += 1
				local my = sheetTok
				tween(pSheet, 0.2, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
				tween(panel, 0.22, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, H + panel.Size.Y.Offset + 6) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
				task.delay(0.24, function() if my == sheetTok then pSheet.Visible = false end end)
			end
			function openSheet(e)
				sheetSong = e
				clear(body)
				local rows = math.min(#lists + 2, 6)
				local ph = 92 + rows * 40 + 24
				panel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W, ph)
				mk(body, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(W / 2, 6), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(32, 3), BackgroundColor3 = GRAY })
				cover(body, e.id, 40, PADX, 18, 4)
				nl(T(body, e.title, 11, WHITE, PADX + 48, 20, W - 80, 15, nil, true))
				nl(T(body, e.author ~= "" and e.author or "Saved track", 9, GRAY, PADX + 48, 36, W - 80, 13))
				mk(body, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(PADX, 66), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(IW, 1), BackgroundColor3 = SURF2 })
				local sc = nt(create("ScrollingFrame", { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 72), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W, rows * 40 + 8), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0, ScrollBarThickness = 0, CanvasSize = UDim2.new(), AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, ElasticBehavior = Enum.ElasticBehavior.Never }, body))
				create("UIListLayout", { SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, sc)
				create("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, PADX), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, PADX) }, sc)
				local order = 0
				local function item(art, text, cb)
					order += 1
					local b = nt(createButton(sc, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40), BackgroundTransparency = 1, LayoutOrder = order }))
					art(b)
					T(b, text, 11, WHITE, 44, 0, IW - 50, 40, nil, true)
					b.Activated:Connect(function() closeSheet() cb() end)
				end
				item(function(b)
					local sq = mk(b, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 4), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(32, 32), BackgroundColor3 = SURF2 })
					corner(sq, 3)
					ic(sq, "plus", 16, WHITE, 16, 16, "+")
				end, "New playlist", function() openCreate(e) end)
				item(function(b) listArt(b, { kind = "liked", ids = {}, name = "" }, 32, 0, 4, 3) end, liked[e.id] and "Remove from Liked Songs" or "Liked Songs", function()
					liked[e.id] = (not liked[e.id]) or nil
					saveLikes()
					toast(liked[e.id] and "Added to Liked Songs" or "Removed from Liked Songs")
				end)
				for i, P in ipairs(lists) do
					item(function(b) listArt(b, getList("user", i), 32, 0, 4, 3) end, P.name, function()
						for _, id in ipairs(P.ids) do
							if id == e.id then toast("Already in " .. P.name) return end
						end
						table.insert(P.ids, e.id)
						save()
						toast("Added to " .. P.name)
					end)
				end
				sheetTok += 1
				State.stopTweens(pSheet)
				State.stopTweens(panel)
				pSheet.BackgroundTransparency = 1
				panel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, H + ph + 6)
				pSheet.Visible = true

				tween(pSheet, 0.25, { BackgroundTransparency = 0.45 })
				tween(panel, 0.42, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, H) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
				for i, b in ipairs(sc:GetChildren()) do
					if b:IsA("GuiButton") then
						local us = create("UIScale", { Scale = 0.85 }, b)
						task.delay(0.08 + i * 0.04, function() if us.Parent then tween(us, 0.28, { Scale = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out) end end)
					end
				end
			end
		end

		local fade = mk(root, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, NAV_Y - 18), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W, 18), BackgroundColor3 = BG, ZIndex = 7 })
		create("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Transparency = NumberSequence.new(1, 0) }, fade)
		local nav = mk(root, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, NAV_Y), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W, 62), BackgroundColor3 = BG, ZIndex = 8 })
		do
			local items = { { "home", "Home", "house", "⌂" }, { "search", "Search", "search", "?" }, { "lib", "Your Library", "library", "▥" } }
			for i, it in ipairs(items) do
				local x = (i - 1) * (W / 3)
				local b = nt(createButton(nav, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W / 3, 40) }))
				local icn = ic(b, it[3], 17, GRAY, W / 6, 14, it[4])
				local lb = T(b, it[2], 8, GRAY, 0, 25, W / 3, 11, Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, true)
				Nav[it[1]] = { icn = icn, lb = lb }
				b.Activated:Connect(function() go(it[1]) end)
			end
		end
		local mini = mk(root, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, MINI_Y), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W - 8, 42), BackgroundColor3 = SURF, ZIndex = 9, Visible = false })
		do
			corner(mini, 7)
			Mini.bg = mini
			Mini.cover = mk(mini, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(5, 5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(32, 32), BackgroundColor3 = SURF2, ClipsDescendants = true })
			corner(Mini.cover, 4)
			Mini.title = nl(T(mini, "", 10, WHITE, 44, 6, W - 8 - 44 - 66, 14, nil, true))
			Mini.artist = nl(T(mini, "", 9, GRAY, 44, 20, W - 8 - 44 - 66, 12))
			local openB = nt(createButton(mini, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W - 8 - 66, 42) }))
			openB.Activated:Connect(function() openPlayer() end)
			local hb = nt(createButton(mini, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(W - 8 - 66, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(32, 42), ZIndex = 2 }))
			Mini.heart = heart(hb, 15, 16, 21, false, WHITE)
			hb.Activated:Connect(function()
				local n = M.now()
				if not n.id then return end
				liked[n.id] = (not liked[n.id]) or nil
				saveLikes()
				bump(Mini.heart.h)
				toast(liked[n.id] and "Added to Liked Songs" or "Removed from Liked Songs")
				force = true
			end)
			local pb = nt(createButton(mini, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(W - 8 - 34, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(34, 42), ZIndex = 2 }))
			Mini.play = playGlyph(pb, 16, WHITE, 17, 21)
			pb.Activated:Connect(function() bump(Mini.play.h) M.toggle() force = true end)
			local track = mk(mini, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 39), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W - 8 - 12, 2), BackgroundColor3 = WHITE, BackgroundTransparency = 0.75 })
			corner(track, 1)
			Mini.fill = mk(track, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), BackgroundColor3 = WHITE })
			corner(Mini.fill, 1)
		end

		local pPlayer = mkPage(30)
		pPlayer.Visible = false
		do
			local g = mk(pPlayer, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = WHITE })
			Pl.grad = create("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 70), BG) }, g)
			local dn = nt(createButton(pPlayer, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 30), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(36, 32) }))
			ic(dn, "chevron-down", 20, WHITE, 18, 16, "v")
			dn.Activated:Connect(function() closePlayer() end)
			T(pPlayer, "PLAYING FROM", 7, GRAY, 40, 32, W - 80, 10, Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, true)
			Pl.ctx = nl(T(pPlayer, "", 9, WHITE, 40, 43, W - 80, 14, Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, true))
			local mo = nt(createButton(pPlayer, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(W - 6, 30), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(36, 32) }))
			ic(mo, "ellipsis", 18, WHITE, 18, 16, "...")
			mo.Activated:Connect(function()
				local n = M.now()
				if n.id and byId[n.id] then
					local dots = mo:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("GuiObject")
					if dots then
						bump(dots)
						dots.Rotation = -90
						tween(dots, 0.35, { Rotation = 0 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
					end
					openSheet(byId[n.id])
				end
			end)
			local sh = mk(pPlayer, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(28, 78), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(150, 150), BackgroundColor3 = BLACK, BackgroundTransparency = 0.7 })
			corner(sh, 7)
			Pl.cover = mk(pPlayer, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(26, 72), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(150, 150), BackgroundColor3 = SURF2, ClipsDescendants = true })
			corner(Pl.cover, 6)
			Pl.title = nl(T(pPlayer, "Nothing playing", 14, WHITE, 20, 238, W - 70, 20, nil, true))
			Pl.artist = nl(T(pPlayer, "", 10, GRAY, 20, 258, W - 70, 14))
			local hb = nt(createButton(pPlayer, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(W - 50, 238), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(34, 34) }))
			Pl.heart = heart(hb, 18, 17, 17, false, WHITE)
			hb.Activated:Connect(function()
				local n = M.now()
				if not n.id then return end
				liked[n.id] = (not liked[n.id]) or nil
				saveLikes()
				bump(Pl.heart.h)
				toast(liked[n.id] and "Added to Liked Songs" or "Removed from Liked Songs")
				force = true
			end)
			local tr = mk(pPlayer, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 288), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W - 40, 3), BackgroundColor3 = WHITE, BackgroundTransparency = 0.7 })
			corner(tr, 2)
			Pl.fill = mk(tr, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), BackgroundColor3 = WHITE })
			corner(Pl.fill, 2)
			Pl.knob = mk(tr, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(9, 9), BackgroundColor3 = WHITE })
			corner(Pl.knob, 5)
			Pl.pos = T(pPlayer, "0:00", 8, GRAY, 20, 296, 50, 11)
			Pl.len = T(pPlayer, "0:00", 8, GRAY, W - 70, 296, 50, 11, Enum.TextXAlignment.Right)
			local sk = nt(createButton(pPlayer, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(14, 280), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W - 28, 18) }))
			bindDrag(sk, function(x) M.seek(x) force = true end)

			local cy = 340
			local function cbtn(x, name, fb, cb, size)
				local b = nt(createButton(pPlayer, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, cy), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(34, 34) }))
				local i = ic(b, name, size or 18, WHITE, 17, 17, fb)
				b.Activated:Connect(function() bump(i.h) cb(i) force = true end)
				return i
			end
			Pl.shuf = cbtn(26, "shuffle", "⇄", function(i)
				applyShuffle(not shuffleOn)
				i.set(nil, shuffleOn and GREEN or WHITE)
			end, 17)
			cbtn(62, "skip-back", "|◀", function() M.prev() end, 22)
			local pbig = nt(createButton(pPlayer, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(W / 2, cy), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(46, 46), BackgroundColor3 = WHITE, BackgroundTransparency = 0 }))
			corner(pbig, 999)
			Pl.play = playGlyph(pbig, 20, BLACK, 23, 23)
			pbig.Activated:Connect(function() bump(Pl.play.h) M.toggle() force = true end)
			cbtn(W - 62, "skip-forward", "▶|", function() M.next() end, 22)
			Pl.rep = cbtn(W - 26, "repeat", "↻", function(i)
				repeatOn = not repeatOn
				i.set(nil, repeatOn and GREEN or WHITE)
				toast(repeatOn and "Repeat on" or "Repeat off")
			end, 17)
			ic(pPlayer, "speaker", 12, GREEN, 26, 378, "♪")
			T(pPlayer, "This phone", 8, GREEN, 38, 372, 90, 12, nil, true)
			ic(pPlayer, "list-music", 14, WHITE, W - 24, 378, "≡")
			local closeTok = 0
			function openPlayer()
				closeTok += 1
				pPlayer.Visible = true
				pPlayer.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 26)
				tween(pPlayer, 0.28, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
				lastId = "force"
				force = true
				B.refreshChrome()
			end
			function closePlayer()
				closeTok += 1
				local my = closeTok
				tween(pPlayer, 0.22, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 60) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
				task.delay(0.2, function()
					if my ~= closeTok then return end
					pPlayer.Visible = false
					B.refreshChrome()
				end)
			end
		end

		function B.refreshChrome()
			local overlay = pPlayer.Visible or pCreate.Visible
			local showNav = mainPage ~= "pick" and not overlay
			nav.Visible, fade.Visible = showNav, showNav
			local showMini = (M.now().id ~= nil) and not overlay
			if showMini and not mini.Visible then
				mini.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, MINI_Y + 26)
				mini.Visible = true
				tween(mini, 0.42, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(4, MINI_Y) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
			elseif not showMini then
				mini.Visible = false
			end
			local tab = (mainPage == "home" or mainPage == "search" or mainPage == "lib") and mainPage or lastTab
			for k, n in pairs(Nav) do
				local on = k == tab
				n.icn.set(nil, on and WHITE or GRAY)
				n.lb.TextColor3 = on and WHITE or GRAY
			end
		end
		function go(name)
			mainPage = name
			if name == "home" or name == "search" or name == "lib" then lastTab = name end
			for k, p in pairs(pages) do p.Visible = (k == name) end
			if B[name] then
				local okb, errb = pcall(B[name])
				if not okb then
					warn("[Nauru Hub] Spotify page '" .. name .. "' failed: " .. tostring(errb))
					toast("Error: " .. tostring(errb):sub(1, 60))
				end
			end
			local p = pages[name]
			if name == "list" or name == "pick" then
				p.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 0)
				tween(p, 0.24, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
			end
			B.refreshChrome()
		end
		function openList(kind, idx, back)
			curKind, curIdx, listBack = kind, idx, back or mainPage
			if listBack == "list" or listBack == "pick" then listBack = lastTab end
			go("list")
		end

		local acc, lastShuf, lastRep = 0, nil, nil
		local function tick()
			local n = M.now()
			if n.id ~= lastId then
				lastId = n.id
				local img = n.id and M.thumb(n.id)
				for _, spec in ipairs({ { Mini.cover, 32, 4, 14 }, { Pl.cover, 150, 6, 54 } }) do
					local cv = spec[1]
					cv:ClearAllChildren()
					corner(cv, spec[3])
					if img then
						nt(create("ImageLabel", { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Image = img, ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop }, cv))
					else
						ic(cv, "music", spec[4], GRAY, spec[2] / 2, spec[2] / 2, "♪")
					end
				end
				if n.id then
					for _, spec in ipairs({ { Mini.cover, 0.9 }, { Pl.cover, 0.94 } }) do
						local us = create("UIScale", { Scale = spec[2] }, spec[1])
						tween(us, 0.3, { Scale = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
					end
					for _, lb in ipairs({ Mini.title, Pl.title, Mini.artist, Pl.artist }) do
						lb.TextTransparency = 1
						tween(lb, 0.35, { TextTransparency = 0 })
					end
					tween(Mini.bg, 0.5, { BackgroundColor3 = tint(n.id, 0.3, 0.5) })
				end
				local base = n.id and tint(n.id, 0.3, 0.5) or SURF
				Pl.grad.Color = ColorSequence.new({ ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, n.id and tint(n.id, 0.55, 0.55) or Color3.fromRGB(70, 70, 70)), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.8, BG), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, BG) })
				paintNow(n.id)
				B.refreshChrome()
			end
			local title = n.id and n.title or "Nothing playing"
			local artist = n.busy and ("Loading" .. string.rep(".", math.floor(os.clock() * 3) % 4)) or (n.id and (n.author ~= "" and n.author or "Saved track") or "Pick a song")
			Mini.title.Text, Pl.title.Text = title, title
			Mini.artist.Text, Pl.artist.Text = artist, artist
			Pl.ctx.Text = ctxName
			local isLiked = n.id and liked[n.id] and true or false
			Mini.heart.set(isLiked)
			Pl.heart.set(isLiked)
			if lastShuf ~= shuffleOn then
				lastShuf = shuffleOn
				Pl.shuf.set(nil, shuffleOn and GREEN or WHITE)
			end
			if lastRep ~= repeatOn then
				lastRep = repeatOn
				Pl.rep.set(nil, repeatOn and GREEN or WHITE)
			end
			local playing = n.playing and true or false
			Mini.play.set(playing)
			Pl.play.set(playing)
			local frac = n.len > 0 and math.clamp(n.pos / n.len, 0, 1) or 0
			Mini.fill.Size = UDim2.fromScale(frac, 1)
			if pPlayer.Visible then
				tween(Pl.fill, 0.2, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(frac, 1) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear)
				tween(Pl.knob, 0.2, { Position = UDim2.fromScale(frac, 0.5) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear)
				Pl.pos.Text = mmss(n.pos)
				Pl.len.Text = n.len > 0 and mmss(n.len) or "0:00"
			end
			if repeatOn and n.id and playing and n.len > 2 and n.len - n.pos < 0.45 then M.seek(0) end
		end
		table.insert(conns, RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
			if not isOpen or not current or current.id ~= "spotify" then return end
			acc += dt
			if acc < 0.2 and not force then return end
			acc, force = 0, false
			pcall(tick)
		end))

		local function refreshView()
			refreshSongs()
			if B[mainPage] then B[mainPage]() end
			B.refreshChrome()
		end
		f:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(function() if f.Visible then refreshView() end end)
		go("home")
		pcall(tick)
	end

	local function buildSpotify(f)
		local ok, err = pcall(buildSpotifyInner, f)
		if not ok then
			warn("[Nauru Hub] Spotify failed: " .. tostring(err))
			local l = pl(f, "Spotify error:\n" .. tostring(err), 8, Color3.fromRGB(255, 110, 110), -4, 20, AW + 8, 300, nil)
			l.TextWrapped = true
			l.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
			l.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None
		end
	end

	local function buildInfo(f)
		local fps = 60
		table.insert(conns, RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt) if dt > 0 then fps = fps * 0.9 + (1 / dt) * 0.1 end end))
		local WARN = Color3.fromRGB(255, 170, 60)
		local BAD = Color3.fromRGB(255, 90, 90)
		local ROWS = { "fps", "ping", "players", "server age", "place id", "job id", "you" }
		local vals, rows = {}, {}
		for i, name in ipairs(ROWS) do
			local y = (i - 1) * 30
			local row = createFrame(f, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, y), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(AW, 26), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 0.94 })
			nt(row)
			corner(row, 10)
			local accentBar = nt(createFrame(row, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 6), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 14), BackgroundColor3 = C.accent }))
