			corner(accentBar, 2)
			local nameL = pl(row, name, 11, C.sub, 16, 0, 74, 26)
			vals[name] = pl(row, "-", 11, C.text, 80, 0, AW - 90, 26, RIGHT, FONT_BOLD)
			rows[i] = { row = row, y = y, a = nameL, b = vals[name], bt = row.BackgroundTransparency, bar = accentBar }
		end
		local introTok = 0
		local function intro()
			introTok += 1
			local my = introTok
			for i, r in ipairs(rows) do
				State.stopTweens(r.row)
				r.row.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-18, r.y)
				r.row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
				r.a.TextTransparency, r.b.TextTransparency = 1, 1
				task.delay((i - 1) * 0.05, function()
					if my ~= introTok then return end
					tween(r.row, 0.35, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, r.y), BackgroundTransparency = r.bt }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint)
					tween(r.a, 0.35, { TextTransparency = 0 })
					tween(r.b, 0.35, { TextTransparency = 0 })
				end)
			end
		end

		local BARS, CH = 30, 34
		local step = AW / BARS
		pl(f, "fps history", 10, C.dim, 0, 288, AW, 12)
		local chart = createFrame(f, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 302), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(AW, CH), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 0.94 })
		nt(chart)
		corner(chart, 10)
		stroke(chart, C.line, 1, 0.5)
		nt(createFrame(chart, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, CH * (1 - 60 / 90)), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(AW, 1), BackgroundColor3 = C.sub, BackgroundTransparency = 0.8, ZIndex = 2 }))
		local bars, hist = {}, {}
		for i = 1, BARS do
			hist[i] = 0
			local bar = nt(createFrame(chart, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1), Position = UDim2.fromOffset((i - 1) * step + 0.5, CH), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(2, step - 1), 0), BackgroundColor3 = C.green, BackgroundTransparency = 0.15 }))
			corner(bar, 2)
			bars[i] = bar
		end
		local function fpsColor(v) return v >= 50 and C.green or (v >= 30 and WARN or BAD) end
		local function pingColor(v) return v < 80 and C.green or (v < 160 and WARN or BAD) end
		local function pushHist(v)
			table.remove(hist, 1)
			table.insert(hist, v)
			for i = 1, BARS do
				local h = math.clamp(hist[i] / 90, 0.04, 1) * (CH - 2)
				if hist[i] <= 0 then h = 0 end
				tween(bars[i], 0.3, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(2, step - 1), h), BackgroundColor3 = fpsColor(hist[i]) })
			end
		end

		local function age(sec)
			sec = math.floor(sec)
			return string.format("%d:%02d:%02d", math.floor(sec / 3600), math.floor(sec / 60) % 60, sec % 60)
		end
		local function setVal(lbl, text, color)
			if lbl.Text ~= text then
				lbl.Text = text
			end
			if color then tween(lbl, 0.25, { TextColor3 = color }) end
		end
		local function refresh()
			local f0 = math.floor(fps + 0.5)
			setVal(vals.fps, tostring(f0), fpsColor(f0))
			tween(rows[1].bar, 0.25, { BackgroundColor3 = fpsColor(f0) })
			pcall(pushHist, f0)
			local pingN
			pcall(function() pingN = math.floor(game:GetService("Stats").Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue()) end)
			setVal(vals.ping, pingN and (pingN .. " ms") or "-", pingN and pingColor(pingN) or C.text)
			tween(rows[2].bar, 0.25, { BackgroundColor3 = pingN and pingColor(pingN) or C.accent })
			setVal(vals.players, #Players:GetPlayers() .. " / " .. Players.MaxPlayers)
			setVal(vals["server age"], age(workspace.DistributedGameTime))
			vals["place id"].Text = tostring(game.PlaceId)
			vals["job id"].Text = #game.JobId > 0 and (game.JobId:sub(1, 8) .. "...") or "studio"
			vals.you:SetAttribute("NoTranslate", true) vals.you.Text = player.Name
		end
		local function copyBtn(y, label, getText, filled)
			local btn
			btn = pbtn(f, 0, y, AW, 30, label, function()
				if setclipboard then
					setclipboard(getText())
					btn.Text = "copied!"
					task.delay(1.2, function() if btn.Parent then btn.Text = label end end)
					notify("Phone", label:gsub("^copy ", "") .. " copied")
				else
					btn.Text = "not supported here"
					task.delay(1.2, function() if btn.Parent then btn.Text = label end end)
				end
			end, filled)
			return btn
		end
		copyBtn(214, "copy job id", function() return game.JobId end, true)
		copyBtn(250, "copy place id", function() return tostring(game.PlaceId) end)
		task.spawn(function()
			local was, acc = false, 0
			while gui.Parent do
				local shown = isOpen and current ~= nil and current.id == "info"
				if shown and not was then intro() pcall(refresh) acc = 0 end
				was = shown
				if shown then
					acc += 0.1
					if acc >= 0.5 then acc = 0 pcall(refresh) end
				end
				task.wait(0.1)
			end
		end)
		pcall(refresh)
	end

	local function spotifyLogo(parent, size)
		local GREEN, INK = Color3.fromRGB(30, 215, 96), Color3.fromRGB(10, 12, 10)
		local root = nt(createFrame(parent, { Name = "SpotifyLogo", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(size, size), BackgroundColor3 = GREEN }))
		corner(root, 999)
		local function arc(wFrac, topFrac, thickFrac)
			local w, t = size * wFrac, math.max(1.2, size * thickFrac)
			local sag = w * 0.17
			local R = (w * w / 4 + sag * sag) / (2 * sag)
			local box = nt(createFrame(root, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, topFrac, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, sag + t + 1), ClipsDescendants = true }))
			local outer = nt(createFrame(box, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(R * 2, R * 2), BackgroundColor3 = INK }))
			corner(outer, 999)
			local inner = nt(createFrame(box, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(w / 2, t), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(R * 2 - t * 2, R * 2 - t * 2), BackgroundColor3 = GREEN }))
			corner(inner, 999)
		end
		arc(0.66, 0.285, 0.085)
		arc(0.54, 0.435, 0.072)
		arc(0.42, 0.575, 0.06)
		return root
	end

	local function spotifyDeco(parent)
		local h = nt(createFrame(parent, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Visible = false }))
		local bg = nt(createFrame(h, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1) }))
		local bgGrad = create("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Color = ColorSequence.new({
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(34, 128, 66)),
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.3, Color3.fromRGB(20, 62, 38)),
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.7, Color3.fromRGB(14, 18, 16)),
			ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 10)),
		}) }, bg)
		local wm = nt(create("CanvasGroup", { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(1, 34, 0, 96), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(170, 170), BackgroundTransparency = 1, GroupTransparency = 0.88 }, h))
		spotifyLogo(wm, 170)
		local sm = nt(createFrame(h, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(SW / 2 - 56, 30), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18) }))
		spotifyLogo(sm, 18)
		table.insert(conns, RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			if not h.Visible then return end
			local t = os.clock()
			wm.Position = UDim2.new(1, 34, 0, 96 + math.sin(t * 0.8) * 6)
			wm.Rotation = math.sin(t * 0.5) * 5
			bgGrad.Offset = Vector2.new(0, math.sin(t * 0.4) * 0.05)
		end))
		return h
	end

	local function buildCamera(f)
		local VW, VH = AW, 196
		local photos = CamStore.photos
		local shownIdx, busy, timerOn = 0, false, false
		local KIND = { capture = "screen", id = "screen", world = "3D render", px = "saved", png = "saved" }
		local vf = createFrame(f, { Size = UDim2.fromOffset(VW, VH), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 10), ClipsDescendants = true })
		nt(vf)
		corner(vf, 14)
		local img = nt(create("ImageLabel", { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit, Visible = false, ZIndex = 2 }, vf))
		for i = 1, 2 do
			nt(createFrame(vf, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(math.floor(VW * i / 3), 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(1, VH), BackgroundColor3 = C.white, BackgroundTransparency = 0.88, ZIndex = 3 }))
			nt(createFrame(vf, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, math.floor(VH * i / 3)), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(VW, 1), BackgroundColor3 = C.white, BackgroundTransparency = 0.88, ZIndex = 3 }))
		end
		for _, b in ipairs({ { 8, 8 }, { VW - 24, 8 }, { 8, VH - 9 }, { VW - 24, VH - 9 } }) do
			nt(createFrame(vf, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(b[1], b[2]), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 1), BackgroundColor3 = C.white, BackgroundTransparency = 0.35, ZIndex = 4 }))
		end
		for _, b in ipairs({ { 8, 8 }, { VW - 9, 8 }, { 8, VH - 24 }, { VW - 9, VH - 24 } }) do
			nt(createFrame(vf, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(b[1], b[2]), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(1, 16), BackgroundColor3 = C.white, BackgroundTransparency = 0.35, ZIndex = 4 }))
		end
		local hint = pl(vf, "tap the shutter to photograph\nwhat you are looking at", 10, C.dim, 0, VH / 2 - 14, VW, 28, CENTER)
		hint.TextWrapped = true
		hint.ZIndex = 3
		local fovL = pl(vf, "", 9, C.white, 12, 12, 100, 12, Enum.TextXAlignment.Left, FONT_BOLD)
		fovL.ZIndex = 5
		local countL = nl(createLabel(vf, "", 54, C.white, FONT_BOLD, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), TextXAlignment = CENTER, NoTitle = true, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None, ZIndex = 6 }))
		local flash = nt(createFrame(vf, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = C.white, BackgroundTransparency = 1, ZIndex = 8 }))
		local info = pl(vf, "", 9, C.white, 0, VH - 24, VW, 12, CENTER, FONT_BOLD)
		info.ZIndex = 5

		local shutter
		local busyOv = nt(createFrame(vf, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 0.45, Visible = false, ZIndex = 7 }))
		local busyDots = {}
		for i = 1, 8 do
			local a = (i - 1) / 8 * math.pi * 2
			local d = nt(createFrame(busyOv, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, math.sin(a) * 15, 0.5, -math.cos(a) * 15 - 8), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 6), BackgroundColor3 = C.white, ZIndex = 8 }))
			corner(d, 3)
			busyDots[i] = d
		end
		local busyL = nl(createLabel(busyOv, "", 10, C.white, FONT_BOLD, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 20), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 14), TextXAlignment = CENTER, NoTitle = true, ZIndex = 8 }))
		table.insert(conns, RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
			if not busyOv.Visible then return end
			local ph = os.clock() * 9
			for i, d in ipairs(busyDots) do d.BackgroundTransparency = 0.85 * (1 - ((ph - i) % 8) / 8) end
		end))
		local function setBusy(on, text)
			busyOv.Visible = on
			busyL.Text = text or ""
			if shutter then shutter.BackgroundTransparency = on and 0.55 or 0 end
		end

		local thumbs = {}
		local function setImg(label, p)
			return pcall(function()
				if p.approx and p.kind == "capture" and p.v then
					label.ImageContent = Content.fromObject(p.v)
				elseif p.approx and p.kind == "id" and p.v then
					label.Image = p.v
				elseif p.png then
					label.Image = getcustomasset(p.png)
				elseif p.kind == "px" then
					if p.ei then
						label.ImageContent = Content.fromObject(p.ei)
					else
						task.spawn(function()
							if pcall(CamStore.loadEi, p) and label.Parent then label.ImageContent = Content.fromObject(p.ei) end
						end)
					end
				elseif p.kind == "capture" then label.ImageContent = Content.fromObject(p.v) else label.Image = p.v end
			end)
		end
		local function showPhoto(i)
			shownIdx = i
			img.Visible = false
			for _, q in ipairs(photos) do if q.vpf then q.vpf.Visible = false end end
			local p = photos[i]
			if p then
				if p.kind == "world" then
					p.vpf.Visible = true
				else
					img.Visible = setImg(img, p)
				end
				hint.Visible = false
				info.Text = ("%d/%d  %s  %s  fov %d"):format(i, #photos, p.time, KIND[p.kind], p.fov)
			else
				hint.Visible = true
				info.Text = ""
			end
			for k, t in ipairs(thumbs) do t.stroke.Transparency = (k == i) and 0 or 1 end
		end
		local function paintThumbs()
			for k, t in ipairs(thumbs) do
				local p = photos[k]
				t.btn.BackgroundTransparency = p and 0 or 0.5
				t.lbl.Visible = p ~= nil and p.kind == "world"
				t.img.Visible = false
				if p and p.kind ~= "world" then t.img.Visible = setImg(t.img, p) end
			end
		end
		for k = 1, 5 do
			local btn = createButton(f, { Sound = true, Position = UDim2.fromOffset((k - 1) * 38, 296), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(34, 34), BackgroundColor3 = C.field, BackgroundTransparency = 0.5 })
			corner(btn, 8)
			local im = nt(create("ImageLabel", { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop, Visible = false }, btn))
			corner(im, 8)
			local lbl = pl(btn, "3D", 10, C.text, 0, 0, 34, 34, CENTER, FONT_BOLD)
			lbl.Visible = false
			local st = stroke(btn, C.accent, 1.5, 1)
			thumbs[k] = { btn = btn, img = im, lbl = lbl, stroke = st }
			btn.Activated:Connect(function() if photos[k] then showPhoto(k) end end)
		end

		local fovNow = 70
		table.insert(conns, RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
			if not f.Visible then return end
			local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
			local v = cam and math.floor(cam.FieldOfView + 0.5) or 70
			if v ~= fovNow or fovL.Text == "" then
				fovNow = v
				fovL.Text = "FOV " .. v .. "\194\176"
			end
		end))

		local function worldShot()
			local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
			if not cam then return nil, 0 end
			local vpf = Instance.new("ViewportFrame")
			vpf.Name = "Photo"
			vpf.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
			vpf.BorderSizePixel = 0
			vpf.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(112, 150, 205)
			vpf.ZIndex = 2
			vpf.Visible = false
			pcall(function()
				local L = game:GetService("Lighting")
				vpf.Ambient = L.Ambient
				vpf.LightColor = L.OutdoorAmbient
				vpf.LightDirection = -L:GetSunDirection()
			end)
			local pcf, fov = cam.CFrame, cam.FieldOfView
			local pos, look = pcf.Position, pcf.LookVector
			local KEEP = { SpecialMesh = true, BlockMesh = true, CylinderMesh = true, FileMesh = true, Decal = true, Texture = true, SurfaceAppearance = true }
			local n = 0
			for i, d in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
				if n >= 700 then break end
				if i % 600 == 0 then task.wait() end
				if d:IsA("BasePart") and d.ClassName ~= "Terrain" and d.Transparency < 0.98 then
					local rel = d.Position - pos
					local dist = rel.Magnitude
					if dist < 300 and (dist < 15 or rel.Unit:Dot(look) > 0.3) then
						local ok, c = pcall(function()
							local cl = d:Clone()
							if not cl then return nil end
							for _, ch in ipairs(cl:GetChildren()) do
								if not KEEP[ch.ClassName] then ch:Destroy() end
							end
							return cl
						end)
						if ok and c then
							c.Anchored = true
							c.Parent = vpf
							n += 1
						end
					end
				end
			end
			local vc = Instance.new("Camera")
			vc.CFrame = pcf
			vc.FieldOfView = fov
			vc.Parent = vpf
			vpf.CurrentCamera = vc
			vpf.Parent = vf
			return vpf, n
		end

		local function take()
			if busy then return end
			busy = true
			if timerOn then
				for n = 3, 1, -1 do
					countL.Text = tostring(n)
					countL.TextTransparency = 0
					tween(countL, 0.85, { TextTransparency = 0.7 })
					task.wait(1)
					if not f.Visible then countL.Text = "" busy = false return end
				end
				countL.Text = ""
			end
			setBusy(true, "capturing...")
			local entry, why = nil, {}
			local wasEnabled = gui.Enabled
			local function hideHub() pcall(function() gui.Enabled = false end) end
			local function showHub() pcall(function() gui.Enabled = wasEnabled end) end
			local function waitUntil(fn, t) local t0 = os.clock() while not fn() and os.clock() - t0 < t do task.wait() end end
			local function nextFrame() if not pcall(function() RunService.PostSimulation:Wait() end) then task.wait() end end

			local okS, cs = pcall(function() return game:GetService("CaptureService") end)
			if okS and cs then

				local params
				pcall(function() params = { UICaptureMode = Enum.UICaptureMode.None } end)
				local res
				hideHub()
				nextFrame()
				task.wait(0.08)
				local ok1 = pcall(function()
					cs:TakeScreenshotCaptureAsync(function(result, capture) res = { capture = capture, result = result } end, params)
				end)
				if ok1 then waitUntil(function() return res ~= nil end, 3) end
				showHub()
				if res and res.capture then
					entry = { kind = "capture", v = res.capture }
				else
					table.insert(why, ok1 and ("new API " .. (res and tostring(res.result) or "timed out")) or "new API missing")
				end

				if not entry then
					local id
					hideHub()
					nextFrame()
					task.wait(0.08)
					local ok2 = pcall(function() cs:CaptureScreenshot(function(cid) id = cid end) end)
					if ok2 then waitUntil(function() return id ~= nil end, 3) end
					showHub()
					if id then
						entry = { kind = "id", v = id }
					else
						table.insert(why, ok2 and "old API timed out" or "old API missing")
					end
				end
			else
				table.insert(why, "no CaptureService")
			end

			if not entry then
				setBusy(true, "rendering 3D view...")
				local ok3, vpf, n = pcall(worldShot)
				if ok3 and vpf then entry = { kind = "world", vpf = vpf } else table.insert(why, "3D render failed") end
			end
			setBusy(false)
			if not entry then
				busy = false
				notify("Camera", "capture failed: " .. table.concat(why, "; "))
				return
			end
			if entry.kind == "world" then notify("Camera", "screen capture blocked (" .. table.concat(why, "; ") .. ") - took a 3D render instead") end

			flash.BackgroundTransparency = 0.05
			tween(flash, 0.35, { BackgroundTransparency = 1 })
			local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
			entry.time = os.date("%H:%M:%S")
			entry.fov = cam and math.floor(cam.FieldOfView + 0.5) or 70
			if cam then
				entry.cf = cam.CFrame
				entry.aspect = cam.ViewportSize.X / math.max(1, cam.ViewportSize.Y)
			end
			table.insert(photos, 1, entry)
			if #photos > 5 then
				local old = table.remove(photos)
				if old and old.vpf then old.vpf:Destroy() end
			end
			if false then
				CamStore.persist(entry, function(ok, err)
					if ok and entry.png then notify("Camera", "photo saved") end
					if ok and entry.approx then
						local lines = { "executor: " .. tostring(pcall(function() return (identifyexecutor and identifyexecutor()) or "?" end) and select(2, pcall(function() return (identifyexecutor and identifyexecutor()) or "?" end)) or "?") }
						for _, d in ipairs(entry.diag or {}) do lines[#lines + 1] = d end
						local report = table.concat(lines, "\n")
						pcall(function() if setclipboard then setclipboard(report) end end)
						pcall(function() if writefile then writefile("NauruHub/camera_diag.txt", report) end end)
						pcall(function() print("[Camera diag]\n" .. report) end)
						notify("Camera", "real screenshot can't be read here, saved low-res. reasons below (also in clipboard + NauruHub/camera_diag.txt)")
						for i = 1, math.min(#lines, 6) do task.delay(i * 0.6, function() notify("Camera " .. i, lines[i]:sub(1, 90)) end) end
					end
					if not ok then notify("Camera", "photo not saved (kept for this session): " .. tostring(err):sub(1, 140)) end
				end)
			end
			paintThumbs()
			showPhoto(1)
			local target = entry.vpf or img
			target.Size = UDim2.fromScale(0.92, 0.92)
			target.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.04, 0.04)
			tween(target, 0.32, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 0) }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
			busy = false
		end
		local function takeSafe()
			local ok, err = pcall(take)
			if not ok then
				busy = false
				setBusy(false)
				countL.Text = ""
				pcall(function() gui.Enabled = true end)
				notify("Camera", "error: " .. tostring(err))
			end
		end

		shutter = nt(createButton(f, { Sound = true, AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(VW / 2, 208), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(56, 56), BackgroundColor3 = C.white, BackgroundTransparency = 0 }))
		corner(shutter, 28)
		stroke(shutter, C.white, 2, 0.4)
		local ssc = create("UIScale", {}, shutter)
		shutter.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function() tween(ssc, 0.07, { Scale = 0.86 }) end)
		shutter.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function() tween(ssc, 0.2, { Scale = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back) end)
		shutter.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tween(ssc, 0.1, { Scale = 1 }) end)
		shutter.Activated:Connect(function() task.spawn(takeSafe) end)

		local timerBtn
		timerBtn = pbtn(f, 0, 220, 54, 32, "timer off", function()
			timerOn = not timerOn
			timerBtn.Text = timerOn and "timer 3s" or "timer off"
			timerBtn.TextColor3 = timerOn and C.accent or C.text
		end)
		timerBtn.TextSize = 10
		local camDel = pbtn(f, VW - 40, 220, 32, 32, "", function()
			local p = photos[shownIdx]
			if not p then return end
			if p.vpf then p.vpf:Destroy() end
			table.remove(photos, shownIdx)
			CamStore.save()
			paintThumbs()
			showPhoto(#photos > 0 and math.min(shownIdx, #photos) or 0)
		end)
		camDel.Text = ""
		trashIcon(camDel, C.red, 16)
		pl(f, "gallery (last 5 this session)", 9, C.dim, 0, 276, AW, 12, Enum.TextXAlignment.Left)
		paintThumbs()
		showPhoto(0)
	end

	local function gloss(bg, h, tr)
		local g = nt(createFrame(bg, { Name = "Gloss", Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, h or 20), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1) }))
		corner(g, 11)
		create("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Transparency = NumberSequence.new(tr or 0.72, 1) }, g)
		return g
	end
	local function shade(bg, h, tr)
		local s = nt(createFrame(bg, { Name = "Shade", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0, 1), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, h or 14), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0) }))
		corner(s, 11)
		create("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Transparency = NumberSequence.new(1, tr or 0.84) }, s)
		return s
	end

	local function cameraLogo(parent, size)

		local INK = Color3.fromRGB(22, 22, 24)

		box(28.5, 14, 3, 3, Color3.fromRGB(255, 214, 10), 2, 0.1)
		box(11.5, 14, 17, 17, Color3.fromRGB(104, 104, 110), 8.5)
		box(13, 15.5, 14, 14, Color3.fromRGB(26, 26, 30), 7)

	local function notesLogo(parent, size)
		local YEL, DYEL = Color3.fromRGB(255, 214, 10), Color3.fromRGB(226, 172, 0)

		corner(body, 11)
		create("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 254), Color3.fromRGB(238, 238, 232)) }, body)
		corner(nt(createFrame(body, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 13), BackgroundColor3 = YEL })), 11)
		nt(createFrame(body, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 7), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 6), BackgroundColor3 = YEL }))
		nt(createFrame(body, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 12.5), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1), BackgroundColor3 = DYEL }))
		for _, hx in ipairs({ 9, 18.5, 28 }) do
			local h = nt(createFrame(body, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(hx, 4.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3.5, 3.5), BackgroundColor3 = DYEL }))
			corner(h, 2)
		end
		local LINE = Color3.fromRGB(204, 204, 210)
		for i, w in ipairs({ 22, 22, 12 }) do
			local y = 18.5 + (i - 1) * 6.5
			local dot = nt(createFrame(body, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(6, y - 1), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(2.5, 2.5), BackgroundColor3 = i == 3 and Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 206) or YEL }))
			corner(dot, 1.5)
			nt(createFrame(body, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(11, y), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, 1), BackgroundColor3 = LINE }))
		end

		local t1 = nt(createFrame(body, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(32, 18), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(1.5, 3), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(52, 199, 89), Rotation = -40 }))
		local t2 = nt(createFrame(body, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(34.2, 16.4), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(1.5, 6), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(52, 199, 89), Rotation = 38 }))
		corner(t1, 1) corner(t2, 1)
		shade(body, 10, 0.9)
		return body
	end

	local function tileLogo(c1, c2, glyphName)
		return function(parent, size)
			local bg = nt(createFrame(parent, { Name = "AppLogo", Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1) }))
			corner(bg, 11)
			create("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Color = ColorSequence.new(c1, c2) }, bg)
			stroke(bg, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 1, 0.82)
			gloss(bg, 20, 0.66)
			shade(bg, 14, 0.82)
			pcall(function()
				local sh = newIcon(bg, 22, glyphName)
				table.remove(State.icons)
				sh.Holder.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 1.5)
				sh.paint(Color3.new(0, 0, 0))
				for _, d in ipairs(sh.Holder:GetDescendants()) do
					if d:IsA("ImageLabel") then d.ImageTransparency = 0.8
					elseif d:IsA("Frame") then d.BackgroundTransparency = math.max(d.BackgroundTransparency, 0.8) end
				end
				if sh.Holder:IsA("ImageLabel") then sh.Holder.ImageTransparency = 0.8 end
			end)
			local ic = newIcon(bg, 22, glyphName)
			table.remove(State.icons)
			ic.Holder.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
			ic.paint(Color3.new(1, 1, 1))
			if not ic.img and #ic.inks == 0 then
				nl(createLabel(bg, glyphName:sub(1, 1):upper(), 18, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), FONT_BOLD, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), TextXAlignment = CENTER, NoTitle = true }))
			end
			return bg
		end
	end
	local function box40(parent, x, y, w, h, col, r)
		local b = nt(createFrame(parent, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, h), BackgroundColor3 = col }))
		corner(b, r)
		return b
	end
	local function calcLogo(parent)
		local bg = nt(createFrame(parent, { Name = "CalcLogo", Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(28, 28, 30) }))
		corner(bg, 11)
		create("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromRGB(58, 58, 62), Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 20)) }, bg)

		local disp = box40(bg, 5, 5, 30, 8.5, Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 12), 3)
		nl(createLabel(disp, "0", 9, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), FONT_BOLD, { Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 1, 0), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, NoTitle = true, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None }))
		for r = 0, 3 do
			for c = 0, 3 do
				local col = c == 3 and Color3.fromRGB(255, 159, 10) or (r == 0 and Color3.fromRGB(165, 165, 170) or Color3.fromRGB(84, 84, 90))
				local key = box40(bg, 5 + c * 7.6, 16 + r * 5.4, 6.4, 4, col, 2)
				local hi = box40(key, 0, 0, 6.4, 1.2, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 1)
				hi.BackgroundTransparency = 0.75
			end
		end
		gloss(bg, 18, 0.82)
		return bg
	end
	local function clockLogo(parent)

		corner(bg, 11)

			local tk = nt(createFrame(bg, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 20), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(1.5, 30), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Rotation = i * 30 }))

		return bg
	end
	local function RGB(r, g, b) return Color3.fromRGB(r, g, b) end
	local WH = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)

		gloss(bg, 20, 0.68)
		shade(bg, 14, 0.8)
		return bg
	end
	local function spin(o, deg) o.Rotation = deg return o end
	local function alpha(o, a) o.BackgroundTransparency = a return o end
	local function ringBox(parent, x, y, w, col, th)
		local r = nt(createFrame(parent, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, w), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }))
		corner(r, w / 2)
		stroke(r, col, th)
		return r
	end
	local function snakeLogo(parent)
		local bg = gameTile(parent, "SnakeLogo", RGB(150, 230, 110), RGB(35, 140, 65))
		box40(bg, 17, 28, 2, 8, RGB(235, 50, 60), 1)
		spin(box40(bg, 14.5, 33, 2, 5, RGB(235, 50, 60), 1), 28)
		spin(box40(bg, 19.5, 33, 2, 5, RGB(235, 50, 60), 1), -28)
		box40(bg, 9, 5, 22, 27, RGB(24, 112, 52), 11)
		box40(bg, 10.5, 6.5, 19, 24, RGB(60, 175, 85), 9.5)
		for _, ex in ipairs({ 12.5, 22.5 }) do
			box40(bg, ex, 12, 6, 7, WH, 3)
			box40(bg, ex + 1.8, 13.5, 2.4, 5, RGB(15, 15, 18), 1.2)
			box40(bg, ex + 3.4, 13, 1.4, 1.4, WH, 0.7)
		end
		box40(bg, 16.5, 24, 1.8, 1.8, RGB(24, 112, 52), 1)
		box40(bg, 21.7, 24, 1.8, 1.8, RGB(24, 112, 52), 1)
		for _, cx in ipairs({ 12.5, 25.5 }) do
			alpha(box40(bg, cx, 21.5, 2, 2, RGB(24, 112, 52), 1), 0.45)
			alpha(box40(bg, cx + (cx < 20 and 1 or -1), 25, 1.6, 1.6, RGB(24, 112, 52), 0.8), 0.55)
		end
		return bg
	end
	local function bombLogo(parent)

		local bg = gameTile(parent, "BombLogo", RGB(255, 170, 70), RGB(215, 50, 40))
		local boom = nt(createFrame(bg, { Name = "Boom", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(20, 22), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(6, 6), BackgroundColor3 = RGB(255, 238, 160), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }))
		corner(boom, 20)
		local art = nt(createFrame(bg, { Name = "BombArt", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }))
		local pop = create("UIScale", {}, art)

		alpha(box40(art, 8, 33, 22, 4, RGB(90, 15, 10), 2), 0.6)
		local body = box40(art, 5, 12, 24, 24, RGB(30, 30, 38), 12)
		create("UIGradient", { Rotation = 45, Color = ColorSequence.new(RGB(84, 86, 104), RGB(10, 10, 16)) }, body)
		local rim = stroke(body, RGB(120, 124, 146), 1, 0.65)
		alpha(box40(art, 11, 30.5, 12, 2.5, RGB(255, 120, 60), 1.2), 0.7)
		alpha(spin(box40(art, 9, 16, 8, 4.5, WH, 2.2), -35), 0.55)
		alpha(box40(art, 10.4, 17.4, 2.4, 2.4, WH, 1.2), 0.2)
		local collar = spin(box40(art, 22, 12, 9, 5, RGB(50, 50, 58), 2), -45)
		alpha(spin(box40(art, 23, 12.6, 7, 1.3, RGB(140, 144, 165), 1), -45), 0.5)

		local FB, FD, FL, FROT = Vector2.new(29.4, 11.6), Vector2.new(0.55, -0.835), 9, 33.4
		local fuse = nt(createFrame(art, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(2.2, FL), BackgroundColor3 = RGB(222, 184, 126), Rotation = FROT }))
		corner(fuse, 1)
		local char = nt(createFrame(fuse, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 2.5), BackgroundColor3 = RGB(70, 40, 25) }))
		corner(char, 1)

		local sp = nt(createFrame(art, { Name = "Spark", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(2, 2), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }))
		create("UIScale", { Scale = 0.72 }, sp)
		local glow = nt(createFrame(sp, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(7, 7), BackgroundColor3 = RGB(255, 170, 50), BackgroundTransparency = 0.6 }))
		corner(glow, 7)
		local rays = {}
		for i, rot in ipairs({ 0, 45, 90, 135 }) do
			local ray = nt(createFrame(sp, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(1.2, 10), BackgroundColor3 = RGB(255, 240, 130), BackgroundTransparency = 0.2, Rotation = rot }))
			corner(ray, 1)
			rays[i] = { f = ray, r0 = rot }
		end
		local core = nt(createFrame(sp, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(5, 5), BackgroundColor3 = RGB(255, 226, 70) }))
		corner(core, 3)
		local heart = nt(createFrame(sp, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(2.4, 2.4), BackgroundColor3 = WH }))
		corner(heart, 2)
		local embers = {}
		for i = 1, 3 do
			local e = nt(createFrame(art, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(1.8, 1.8), BackgroundColor3 = i == 2 and RGB(255, 250, 200) or RGB(255, 190, 60), BackgroundTransparency = 1 }))
			corner(e, 1)
			embers[i] = { f = e, dx = ({ -5, 4, 0.5 })[i], rise = ({ 8, 10, 12 })[i], off = (i - 1) / 3 }
		end

		local T0 = os.clock()
		local CYC, BURN, REGROW = 3.8, 3.1, 0.3
		local conn
		conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
			if not bg.Parent then conn:Disconnect() return end
			if not dock.Visible then return end
			local now = os.clock() - T0
			local t = now % CYC
			local u = math.clamp(t / BURN, 0, 1)
			local booming = t >= BURN

			local len = FL * (1 - 0.45 * u)
			if t < REGROW then len = len * (t / REGROW) end
			local tip = FB + FD * len
			fuse.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(2.2, math.max(len, 0.5))
			local mid = FB + FD * (len / 2)
			fuse.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(mid.X, mid.Y)
			fuse.Visible = not booming
			sp.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(tip.X, tip.Y)
			sp.Visible = not booming

			local fl = now * (14 + u * 14)
			for i, r in ipairs(rays) do
				r.f.Rotation = r.r0 + now * 120 + math.sin(fl + i) * 14
				r.f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(1.2, 4 + 3 * (0.5 + 0.5 * math.sin(fl * 1.3 + i * 1.7)))
			end
			local cs = 4.2 + 1.6 * (0.5 + 0.5 * math.sin(fl * 1.9))
			core.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(cs, cs)
			local gs = 6.5 + 2 * (0.5 + 0.5 * math.sin(fl * 0.9 + 1))
			glow.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(gs, gs)
			glow.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5 + 0.2 * math.sin(fl * 1.1)

			for _, e in ipairs(embers) do
				local k = (now * 1.5 + e.off) % 1
				e.f.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(tip.X + e.dx * k, tip.Y - e.rise * k)
				e.f.BackgroundTransparency = booming and 1 or (0.1 + 0.9 * k)
				local sz = 2.2 * (1 - k * 0.7)
				e.f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(sz, sz)
			end

			if booming then
				local b = (t - BURN) / (CYC - BURN)
				local ease = 1 - (1 - math.min(b * 2.2, 1)) ^ 3
				pop.Scale = 1 + 0.14 * math.sin(math.min(b * 2.2, 1) * math.pi) + 0.03 * (1 - b)
				art.Rotation = math.sin(b * 40) * 3 * (1 - b)
				boom.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(6 + 30 * ease, 6 + 30 * ease)
				boom.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15 + 0.85 * b ^ 0.8
				rim.Color = RGB(255, 190, 90)
				rim.Transparency = 0.1 + 0.6 * b
			else
				local shake = math.sin(now * (9 + u * 34)) * (0.3 + u * u * 3.2)
				art.Rotation = shake
				pop.Scale = 1 + 0.015 * math.sin(now * (6 + u * 20)) * (0.5 + u)
				boom.BackgroundTransparency = 1
				rim.Color = RGB(120, 124, 146):Lerp(RGB(255, 150, 60), u * u)
				rim.Transparency = 0.65 - 0.4 * u * u
			end
		end)
		table.insert(conns, conn)
		return bg
	end
	local function minesLogo(parent)
		local bg = gameTile(parent, "MinesLogo", RGB(120, 180, 255), RGB(40, 95, 210))
		for r = 0, 3 do
			for c = 0, 3 do
				alpha(box40(bg, 4 + c * 8.8, 4 + r * 8.8, 7.6, 7.6, WH, 2), (r + c) % 2 == 0 and 0.88 or 0.93)
			end
		end
		alpha(box40(bg, 9, 32, 24, 4, RGB(10, 25, 70), 2), 0.5)
		box40(bg, 8, 31, 22, 4, RGB(20, 40, 95), 2)
		box40(bg, 13, 27, 12, 4, RGB(20, 40, 95), 1.5)
		alpha(box40(bg, 20, 10, 13, 10, RGB(10, 25, 70), 1), 0.6)
		box40(bg, 18, 8, 2.5, 21, WH, 1)
		spin(box40(bg, 19, 8, 13, 10, RGB(240, 60, 60), 1), 0)
		box40(bg, 19, 8, 13, 3, RGB(255, 120, 110), 1)
		box40(bg, 19, 15.5, 13, 2.5, RGB(195, 35, 45), 1)
		box40(bg, 17.2, 6.2, 4, 4, RGB(255, 220, 90), 2)
		return bg
	end
	local function ticLogo(parent)
		local bg = gameTile(parent, "TicLogo", RGB(95, 220, 145), RGB(25, 145, 90))
		for _, v in ipairs({ 14.5, 25 }) do
			alpha(box40(bg, v, 5, 1.5, 30, WH, 1), 0.35)
			alpha(box40(bg, 5, v, 30, 1.5, WH, 1), 0.35)
		end
		local function x(cx, cy)
			spin(box40(bg, cx - 1.3, cy - 4, 2.6, 8, RGB(235, 50, 55), 1.3), 45)
			spin(box40(bg, cx - 1.3, cy - 4, 2.6, 8, RGB(235, 50, 55), 1.3), -45)
		end
		local function o(cx, cy) ringBox(bg, cx - 3.5, cy - 3.5, 7, RGB(35, 105, 255), 2.2) end
		x(9.75, 9.75)
		o(30.75, 9.75)
		o(20.5, 20.5)
		x(9.75, 30.75)
		x(30.75, 30.75)
		return bg
	end

	local function drawLogo(parent)
		local bg = gameTile(parent, "DrawLogo", RGB(255, 132, 128), RGB(214, 48, 142))
		for i = 0, 15 do
			local x, y = 9 + i * 1.75, 32 + math.sin(i * 0.75) * 2.2
			local d = box40(bg, x - 1.4, y - 1.4, 2.8, 2.8, Color3.fromHSV(i / 17, 0.8, 1), 1.4)
			d.BackgroundTransparency = 0.05
		end
		local pen = nt(createFrame(bg, { Name = "Pencil", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(21, 20), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 31), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Rotation = 45 }))
		alpha(box40(pen, 1.5, 6, 10, 26, RGB(90, 15, 60), 4), 0.65)
		local woodClip = nt(createFrame(pen, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 24), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 8), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ClipsDescendants = true }))
		spin(box40(woodClip, 1.46, -3.54, 7.07, 7.07, RGB(244, 206, 150), 0.5), 45)
		local leadClip = nt(createFrame(pen, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 27), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 5), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ClipsDescendants = true }))
		spin(box40(leadClip, 3.59, -1.41, 2.83, 2.83, RGB(52, 52, 60), 0.5), 45)
		box40(pen, 0, 8, 10, 16, RGB(255, 200, 40), 1)
		box40(pen, 0, 8, 3, 16, RGB(255, 232, 120), 1)
		box40(pen, 7.4, 8, 2.6, 16, RGB(222, 146, 18), 1)
		box40(pen, 4.6, 8, 0.9, 16, RGB(235, 165, 25), 0).BackgroundTransparency = 0.4
		box40(pen, 0, 5, 10, 3.6, RGB(196, 200, 210), 0.5)
		box40(pen, 0, 5.9, 10, 0.8, RGB(130, 134, 146), 0)
		box40(pen, 0, 7.2, 10, 0.8, RGB(130, 134, 146), 0)
		box40(pen, 0, 0, 10, 5.6, RGB(255, 128, 160), 2.4)
		box40(pen, 1, 0.8, 3, 3.4, RGB(255, 190, 205), 1.2).BackgroundTransparency = 0.45
		return bg
	end

	local function gearLogo(parent)
		local bg = nt(createFrame(parent, { Name = "GearLogo", Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1) }))
		corner(bg, 11)
		create("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Color = ColorSequence.new(RGB(178, 180, 190), RGB(96, 98, 108)) }, bg)
		stroke(bg, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 1, 0.8)
		local function gear(ox, oy, col, tr)
			for i = 0, 3 do
				local t = nt(createFrame(bg, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(20 + ox, 20 + oy), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(7, 32), BackgroundColor3 = col, BackgroundTransparency = tr, Rotation = i * 45 }))
				corner(t, 2)
			end
			local body = box40(bg, 8 + ox, 8 + oy, 24, 24, col, 12)
			body.BackgroundTransparency = tr
		end
		gear(0, 1.5, Color3.new(0, 0, 0), 0.72)
		gear(0, 0, RGB(244, 245, 250), 0)
		box40(bg, 12.5, 12.5, 15, 15, RGB(206, 208, 218), 7.5)
		box40(bg, 15.5, 15.5, 9, 9, RGB(118, 120, 130), 4.5)
		gloss(bg, 20, 0.7)
		shade(bg, 14, 0.84)
		return bg
	end

	local home = create("CanvasGroup", { Name = "Home", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ZIndex = 2 }, screen)
	local homeScale = create("UIScale", {}, home)
	local bigTime = nl(createLabel(home, "", 42, C.text, FONT_BOLD, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 36), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(SW, 48), TextXAlignment = CENTER, NoTitle = true, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None }))
	local dateL = pl(home, "", 11, C.sub, 0, 84, SW, 16, CENTER)
	local function updateHome() end
	updateHome()

	local appHost = create("CanvasGroup", { Name = "AppHost", Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = C.bg, BorderSizePixel = 0, GroupTransparency = 1, Visible = false, ZIndex = 5 }, screen)
	local appBg = createFrame(appHost, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1) })
	local decos = {}
	local appBody = createFrame(appHost, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 62), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(AW, 340) })
	local titleL = pl(appHost, "", 13, C.text, 0, 29, SW, 20, CENTER, FONT_BOLD)
	local subL = pl(appHost, "", 9, C.dim, 0, 46, SW, 12, CENTER)
	local appFrames, built = {}, {}
	local appCorner = create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 30) }, appHost)
	local appScale = create("UIScale", { Scale = 1 }, appHost)
	appHost.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
	appHost.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(SW, SH)
	appHost.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(SW / 2, SH / 2)
	local curTile, curC, curS0
	local function tileCenter(btn)
		if not btn or not btn.Parent then return Vector2.new(SW / 2, SH / 2), 40 end
		local k = math.max(pscale.Scale * ps2.Scale * homeScale.Scale, 0.01)
		local c = (btn.AbsolutePosition + btn.AbsoluteSize / 2 - screen.AbsolutePosition) / k
		return c, btn.AbsoluteSize.X / k
	end

	local function subtitle(id)
		local function b(k, unit) local v = scores[k] return v and (tostring(v) .. (unit or "")) or nil end
		local t
		if id == "mines" then
			local p = {}
			for _, lv in ipairs({ "easy", "medium", "hard" }) do local v = b("mines_" .. lv, "s") if v then table.insert(p, lv .. " " .. v) end end
			t = #p > 0 and ("best: " .. table.concat(p, " / ")) or "tap to dig - hold to flag"
		elseif id == "bomb" then t = b("bomb") and ("best bank: " .. b("bomb")) or "bank before it blows"
		elseif id == "tic" then t = b("tic_streak") and ("best streak: " .. b("tic_streak")) or "Easy, Medium, Hard Or 2 Players"
		elseif id == "2048" then t = b("2048") and ("best: " .. b("2048")) or "merge to 2048"
		elseif id == "reflex" then t = b("reflex") and ("best: " .. b("reflex", " ms")) or "wait for green"
		elseif id == "snake" then
			local a, w = b("snake"), b("snake_wrap")
			t = (a or w) and ("best: " .. (a or "-") .. " walls / " .. (w or "-") .. " wrap") or "arrows, WASD or swipe - space pauses"
		elseif id == "memory" then t = b("memory") and ("best: " .. b("memory", " moves")) or "find the 8 pairs"
		elseif id == "calc" then t = "tap the display for history"
		elseif id == "dice" then t = "tap to roll"
		elseif id == "spotify" then t = ""
		elseif id == "notes" then t = "saved as you type"
		elseif id == "draw" then t = "pen, shapes, mirror and more"
		elseif id == "timer" then t = "stopwatch and countdown"
		else t = "" end
		return t
	end

	local backBtn
	local function openApp(app, tile)
		if current then return end
		current, curTile = app, tile
		if not built[app.id] then
			built[app.id] = true
			local fr = createFrame(appBody, { Name = app.id, Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), Visible = false })
			appFrames[app.id] = fr
			local ok, err = pcall(app.build, fr)
			if not ok then warn("[Nauru Hub] phone app '" .. app.id .. "' failed: " .. tostring(err)) end
		end
		for id, fr in pairs(appFrames) do fr.Visible = (id == app.id) end
		if app.deco and not decos[app.id] then
			local ok, d = pcall(app.deco, appBg)
			if ok then decos[app.id] = d else warn("[Nauru Hub] phone background '" .. app.id .. "' failed: " .. tostring(d)) end
		end
		for id, d in pairs(decos) do d.Visible = (id == app.id) end
		titleL.Text = app.bare and "" or app.name
		subL.Text = app.bare and "" or (subtitle(app.id) or "")
		backBtn.Visible = not app.bare

		local c, iconW = tileCenter(tile)
		local s0 = math.clamp(iconW / SW, 0.05, 1)
		curC, curS0 = c, s0
		State.stopTweens(appHost)
		State.stopTweens(appScale)
		State.stopTweens(appCorner)
		State.stopTweens(homeScale)
		State.stopTweens(home)
		appHost.Visible = true
		appHost.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(c.X, c.Y)
		appScale.Scale = s0
		appCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 11 / s0)
		appHost.GroupTransparency = 0.55
		local ei, eo = Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out
		tween(appHost, 0.42, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(SW / 2, SH / 2) }, ei, eo)
		tween(appScale, 0.42, { Scale = 1 }, ei, eo)
		tween(appCorner, 0.42, { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 30) }, ei, eo)
		tween(appHost, 0.16, { GroupTransparency = 0 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine)
		tween(homeScale, 0.42, { Scale = 1.14 }, ei, eo)
		tween(home, 0.3, { GroupTransparency = 0.6 })
	end
	local function closeApp()
		if not current then return end
		current, curTile = nil, nil
		updateHome()
		if notesBox then notesBox:ReleaseFocus() end
		local c, s0 = curC or Vector2.new(SW / 2, SH / 2), curS0 or 0.2
		State.stopTweens(appHost)
		State.stopTweens(appScale)
		State.stopTweens(appCorner)
		State.stopTweens(homeScale)
		State.stopTweens(home)
		local eo = Enum.EasingStyle.Quint
		tween(appHost, 0.36, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(c.X, c.Y) }, eo, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
		tween(appScale, 0.36, { Scale = s0 }, eo, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
		tween(appCorner, 0.36, { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 11 / s0) }, eo, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
		task.delay(0.16, function() if not current then tween(appHost, 0.2, { GroupTransparency = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine) end end)
		tween(homeScale, 0.4, { Scale = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back)
		tween(home, 0.25, { GroupTransparency = 0 })
		task.delay(0.4, function() if not current then appHost.Visible = false end end)
	end

	backBtn = createButton(appHost, { Sound = true, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 32), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(30, 22), BackgroundColor3 = C.field, BackgroundTransparency = 0, TextSize = 14, Font = FONT_BOLD, TextColor3 = C.text })
	backBtn.Text = "<"
	corner(backBtn, 7)
	backBtn.Activated:Connect(closeApp)
	State.phoneCloseApp = closeApp

	local function makeTile(parent, app, x, y, label)
		local btn = nt(createButton(parent, { Sound = true, Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(40, 40), BackgroundColor3 = app.color, BackgroundTransparency = 0 }))
		corner(btn, 11)
		create("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)) }, btn)
		local ic = app.logo and { img = true } or newIcon(btn, 20, app.icon)
		if app.logo then
			app.logo(btn, 32)
		else
			ic.Holder.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
			ic.tint(C.white, 0)
		end
		if not ic.img then
			nl(createLabel(btn, app.name:sub(1, 1), 18, C.white, FONT_BOLD, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), TextXAlignment = CENTER, NoTitle = true }))
		end
		local sc = create("UIScale", {}, btn)
		State.phoneTiles = State.phoneTiles or {}
		table.insert(State.phoneTiles, sc)
		btn.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function() tween(sc, 0.08, { Scale = 0.88 }) end)
		btn.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function() tween(sc, 0.18, { Scale = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back) end)
		btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tween(sc, 0.1, { Scale = 1 }) end)
		btn.Activated:Connect(function() openApp(app, btn) end)
		if label then pl(parent, app.name, 9, C.text, x - 4, y + 43, 48, 12, CENTER) end
	end

	for i = 1, #APPS - 4 do
		local r, c = math.floor((i - 1) / 4), (i - 1) % 4
		makeTile(home, APPS[i], 9 + c * 48, 120 + r * 70, true)
	end
	local dockBar = createFrame(home, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(9, 346), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(SW - 18, 58), BackgroundColor3 = C.field, BackgroundTransparency = 0.35 })
	corner(dockBar, 18)
	for k = #APPS - 3, #APPS do
		makeTile(dockBar, APPS[k], 5 + (k - (#APPS - 3)) * 45, 9, false)
	end

	corner(nt(createFrame(screen, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, -6), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(70, 4), BackgroundColor3 = C.white, BackgroundTransparency = 0.45, ZIndex = 12 })), 2)
	local homeBtn = createButton(screen, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 1), Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(110, 20), ZIndex = 13 })
	homeBtn.Activated:Connect(closeApp)
	State.phoneOpenApp = function(id)
		for _, app in ipairs(APPS) do
			if app.id == id then
				if current then closeApp() task.delay(0.4, function() if not current then openApp(app, nil) end end) else openApp(app, nil) end
				return
			end
		end
	end

	local pv = Instance.new("NumberValue")
	local function windowRect()
		return mainWindow.AbsolutePosition - gui.AbsolutePosition, mainWindow.AbsoluteSize
	end

	local function curScale(sd)
		local s = math.min(responsiveScale.Scale, (gui.AbsoluteSize.Y - 16) / PH, (gui.AbsoluteSize.X - 12) / PW)
		if (sd or side) ~= "over" then

			local wp, ws = windowRect()
			s = math.min(s, (ws.Y - 26 * responsiveScale.Scale) / PH)
			s = math.min(s, math.max((wp.X - GAP - 6) / PW, 0.3))
		end
		return math.clamp(s, 0.45, 1.5)
	end
	local function pickSide()
		local wp = windowRect()
		local ws = math.min(responsiveScale.Scale, (gui.AbsoluteSize.Y - 16) / PH)
		if (wp.X - GAP - 6) / PW >= math.min(ws, 0.62) then return "left" end
		return "over"
	end
	local lastKey
	local function layoutPhone()
		local vp = gui.AbsoluteSize
		local wp, ws = windowRect()
		local s = curScale()
		local p = pv.Value
		local key = table.concat({ vp.X, vp.Y, wp.X, wp.Y, ws.X, ws.Y, s, p, side }, "|")
		if key == lastKey then return end
		lastKey = key
		pscale.Scale = s
		local pw, ph = PW * s, PH * s
		local cy = (side == "over") and (vp.Y / 2) or (wp.Y + ws.Y / 2)
		cy = math.clamp(cy, ph / 2 + 4, math.max(ph / 2 + 4, vp.Y - ph / 2 - 4))
		local x
		dock.Position, dock.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), UDim2.fromOffset(vp.X, vp.Y)
		if side == "left" then
			dock.ZIndex = 4
			dock.Position, dock.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), UDim2.fromOffset(wp.X, vp.Y)
			phone.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
			local closed, open = wp.X + pw, wp.X - GAP
			x = closed + (open - closed) * p
			phone.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, cy)
		else

			dock.ZIndex = 25
			phone.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
			local closed, open = -pw / 2 - 10, pw / 2 + 6
			phone.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(closed + (open - closed) * p, cy)
		end
	end

	do
		local played = false
		function State.phoneIntro()
			if played then return end
			played = true
			local g = create("CanvasGroup", { Name = "HelloIntro", Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 12, 30), BorderSizePixel = 0, ZIndex = 40 }, screen)
			create("UICorner", { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 30) }, g)
			local dismissed = false

			local blobs = {}
			local function bloom(cx, cy, d, col, ph)
				local box = nt(createFrame(g, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(cx, cy), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(d, d), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ZIndex = 41 }))
				for k = 1, 9 do
					local dd = d * (1.05 - k * 0.1)
					local c = nt(createFrame(box, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(dd, dd), BackgroundColor3 = col, BackgroundTransparency = 0.91, ZIndex = 41 }))
					corner(c, dd / 2)
				end
				blobs[#blobs + 1] = { box = box, cx = cx, cy = cy, ph = ph }
			end
			bloom(SW * 0.2, SH * 0.2, 300, Color3.fromRGB(40, 110, 255), 0)
			bloom(SW * 0.9, SH * 0.42, 320, Color3.fromRGB(150, 70, 255), 2)
			bloom(SW * 0.25, SH * 0.78, 300, Color3.fromRGB(255, 70, 150), 4)
			bloom(SW * 0.85, SH * 0.95, 240, Color3.fromRGB(255, 150, 60), 1)
			local sheen = nt(createFrame(g, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), ZIndex = 42 }))
			create("UIGradient", { Rotation = 90, Transparency = NumberSequence.new({ NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.9), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0.97), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.93) }) }, sheen)
			local drift = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
				local t = os.clock()
				for _, b in ipairs(blobs) do
					b.box.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(b.cx + math.sin(t * 0.35 + b.ph) * 22, b.cy + math.cos(t * 0.3 + b.ph) * 26)
				end
			end)

			local skip = nt(createButton(g, { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, ZIndex = 60 }))
			local hint = nl(createLabel(g, "tap to open", 11, Color3.new(1, 1, 1), FONT_BOLD, { Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -62), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(SW, 16), TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, NoTitle = true, TextTransparency = 1, ZIndex = 50 }))
			local function finish()
				if dismissed then return end
				dismissed = true
				tween(g, 0.5, { GroupTransparency = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine)
				task.delay(0.55, function() drift:Disconnect() g:Destroy() end)
			end
			skip.Activated:Connect(finish)

			local HFONT = FONT_BOLD
			for _, nm in ipairs({ "Kalam", "Fondamento", "Cartoon" }) do
				local ok, f = pcall(function() return Enum.Font[nm] end)
				if ok and f then HFONT = f break end
			end
			local function writeWord(word, slow)
				local size = 54
				local tw = size * #word * 0.5
				pcall(function() tw = game:GetService("TextService"):GetTextSize(word, size, HFONT, Vector2.new(1000, 200)).X end)
				if tw > SW - 40 then size = math.max(20, math.floor(size * (SW - 40) / tw)) tw = SW - 40 end
				tw = math.ceil(tw) + 8
				local H = size + 20
				local holder = create("CanvasGroup", { Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ZIndex = 48 }, g)
				local x0, y0 = (SW - tw) / 2, SH * 0.44 - H / 2
				local clip = nt(createFrame(holder, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x0, y0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, H), BackgroundTransparency = 1, ClipsDescendants = true, ZIndex = 49 }))
				local lbl = nl(create("TextLabel", { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(tw, H), BackgroundTransparency = 1, Text = word, Font = HFONT, TextSize = size, TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), RichText = false, TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center, TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center, ZIndex = 50 }, clip))
				lbl:SetAttribute("NoCap", true)
				local tip = nt(createFrame(holder, { AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x0, y0 + H / 2), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(8, 8), BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), ZIndex = 51 }))
				corner(tip, 4)
				create("UIStroke", { Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1), Thickness = 3, Transparency = 0.7 }, tip)
				local dur = (slow and 1.5 or 0.9) * math.clamp(tw / 160, 0.6, 1.4)
				local t0 = os.clock()
				while not dismissed do
					local a = math.min(1, (os.clock() - t0) / dur)
					a = a * a * (3 - 2 * a)
					clip.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(tw * a, H)
					tip.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x0 + tw * a, y0 + H / 2 + math.sin(a * 18) * 6)
					if a >= 1 then break end
					RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
				end
				clip.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(tw, H)
				tip:Destroy()
				return holder
			end

			task.spawn(function()
				task.wait(0.9)
				local words = { "hello", "hola", "bonjour", "ciao", "hallo", "ol\195\161", "hello" }
				for i, w in ipairs(words) do
					if dismissed or not g.Parent then return end
					local holder = writeWord(w, i == 1)
					if dismissed then return end
					task.wait(i == #words and 0.4 or 0.75)
					if i < #words then
						tween(holder, 0.35, { GroupTransparency = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine)
						task.wait(0.4)
						holder:Destroy()
					end
				end
				if dismissed or not g.Parent then return end
				tween(hint, 0.5, { TextTransparency = 0.1 })
				local t0 = os.clock()
				while not dismissed and g.Parent and os.clock() - t0 < 4 do
					tween(hint, 0.8, { TextTransparency = 0.6 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine)
					task.wait(0.8)
					tween(hint, 0.8, { TextTransparency = 0.1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine)
					task.wait(0.8)
				end
				finish()
			end)
		end
	end

	local slideTok, lastShown = 0, false
	local function resetFx()
		State.stopTweens(phone)
		State.stopTweens(ps2)
		phone.GroupTransparency = 0
		ps2.Scale = 1
	end
	local function setActual(v, fromWindow)
		if v == isOpen then return end
		isOpen = v
		slideTok += 1
		local my = slideTok
		State.stopTweens(pv)
		State.stopTweens(pStroke)
		if State.sfx then pcall(State.sfx, v and "on" or "off") end
		local a = ANIMS[State.openAnim] or ANIMS.pop
		if v then
			side = pickSide()
			dock.ZIndex = 4

			resetFx()
			if not dock.Visible then pv.Value = 0 end
			dock.Visible = true
			pStroke.Transparency = 1
			layoutPhone()
			State.phoneIntro()
			task.delay(fromWindow and (0.1 + 6 * 0.07) or 0.1, function()
				if my ~= slideTok or not isOpen then return end
				tween(pv, 0.6, { Value = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
				tween(pStroke, 0.42, { Transparency = 0.5 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine)
				for i, us in ipairs(State.phoneTiles or {}) do
					State.stopTweens(us)
					us.Scale = 0.5
					task.delay(0.12 + i * 0.03, function()
						if my == slideTok and isOpen then tween(us, 0.35, { Scale = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out) else us.Scale = 1 end
					end)
				end
			end)
		else
			if notesBox then notesBox:ReleaseFocus() end
			if fromWindow then

				local ct = math.min(a.close or 0.18, 0.16)
				local es = a.close and Enum.EasingStyle.Sine or nil
				tween(phone, ct, { GroupTransparency = 1 }, es)
				tween(ps2, ct, { Scale = a.s }, es)
				tween(pStroke, ct, { Transparency = 1 }, es)
				task.delay(ct + 0.02, function()
					if my == slideTok and not isOpen then
						dock.Visible = false
						pv.Value = 0
						resetFx()
					end
				end)
			else
				local dur = 0.5
				tween(pv, dur, { Value = 0 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut)
				tween(pStroke, dur * 0.7, { Transparency = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In)
				task.delay(dur + 0.03, function() if my == slideTok and not isOpen then dock.Visible = false end end)
			end
		end
	end

	local lastT = 0
	table.insert(conns, RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
		local want = userOpen and shown
		local fromWindow = (shown ~= lastShown)
		lastShown = shown
		if want ~= isOpen then setActual(want, fromWindow) end
		if dock.Visible then
			if isOpen and pv.Value >= 0.999 and os.clock() - (State.phoneSideT or 0) > 0.8 then
				local ns = pickSide()
				if ns ~= side then
					State.phoneSideT = os.clock()
					side, lastKey = ns, nil
					State.stopTweens(pv)
					pv.Value = 0
					layoutPhone()
					tween(pv, 0.5, { Value = 1 }, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
				end
			end
			layoutPhone()
			local t = os.time()
			if t ~= lastT then
				lastT = t
				local hm = os.date("%H:%M")
				timeL.Text, bigTime.Text, dateL.Text = hm, hm, os.date("%a %d %b")
			end
		end
	end))

	local function changed() if State.phoneChanged then State.phoneChanged(userOpen) end end
	State.phone = {
		open = function() userOpen = true changed() end,
		close = function() userOpen = false changed() end,
		toggle = function() userOpen = not userOpen changed() end,
		isOpen = function() return userOpen end,
	}
	gui.Destroying:Connect(function()
		for _, c in ipairs(conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
	end)
end
function State.buildNauru()
	local W = 344
	local Nauru = { Version = "1.0" }
	local conns = {}
	local customCount = 0
	local tabOrder = 20
	local win

	local function safe(fn, ...)
		if type(fn) ~= "function" then return end
		local ok, err = pcall(fn, ...)
		if not ok then warn("[Nauru] callback error: " .. tostring(err)) end
	end

	local function resolver(methods)
		local lower = {}
		for k, v in pairs(methods) do lower[k:lower()] = v end
		return function(_, key)
			if type(key) ~= "string" then return nil end
			local k = key:lower()
			if lower[k] then return lower[k] end
			for _, pre in ipairs({ "add", "create", "new", "make" }) do
				if k:sub(1, #pre) == pre and lower[k:sub(#pre + 1)] then return lower[k:sub(#pre + 1)] end
			end
			return nil
		end
	end

	local function cfgOf(a, names, ...)
		if type(a) == "table" then return a end
		local vals = table.pack(a, ...)
		local c = {}
		for i, n in ipairs(names) do c[n] = vals[i] end
		return c
	end

	local function bind(obj, name, fn)
		obj[name] = function(a, ...)
			if a == obj then return fn(...) end
			return fn(a, ...)
		end
	end

	local function titleOf(cfg, fallback)
		return cfg.Title or cfg.Name or cfg.Text or cfg.Label or fallback
	end

	local function addRow(tab, h)
		tab._n += 1
		return createFrame(tab._sf, { Name = tab._key, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, h), LayoutOrder = tab._n, ZIndex = math.max(1, 1000 - tab._n) })
	end

	local function finish(row, obj)
		obj.Instance = row
		bind(obj, "Destroy", function() row:Destroy() end)
		bind(obj, "SetVisible", function(v) row.Visible = v and true or false end)
		return obj
	end

	local function findPopup(row)
		for _, ch in ipairs(row:GetChildren()) do
			if ch:IsA("Frame") and ch.ZIndex == 40 then return ch end
		end
	end

	local M = {}

	function M.toggle(tab, a, b, c)
		local W = tab._w
		local cfg = cfgOf(a, { "Title", "Default", "Callback" }, b, c)
		local def = cfg.Default
		if def == nil then def = cfg.Value end
		def = def and true or false
		local row = addRow(tab, 28)
		local cb = createCheckbox(row, 0, 14, W, titleOf(cfg, "toggle"), def, function(v) safe(cfg.Callback, v) end)
		local obj = {}
		bind(obj, "Set", function(v, silent)
			cb.Set(v and true or false, true)
			if not silent then safe(cfg.Callback, v and true or false) end
		end)
		bind(obj, "Get", function() return cb.Get() end)
		if def then task.defer(safe, cfg.Callback, true) end
		return finish(row, obj)
	end

	function M.slider(tab, a, b, c, d, e)
		local W = tab._w
		local cfg = cfgOf(a, { "Title", "Min", "Max", "Default", "Callback" }, b, c, d, e)
		local min, max, def = cfg.Min, cfg.Max, cfg.Default
		local v = cfg.Value
		if type(v) == "table" then
			min = min or v.Min or v[1]
			max = max or v.Max or v[2]
			def = def or v.Default or v[3]
		elseif type(v) == "number" then
			def = def or v
		end
		min = min or 0
		max = max or 100
		if max <= min then max = min + 1 end
		def = math.clamp(def or min, min, max)
		local dec = cfg.Decimals or cfg.Precision
		if not dec then
			local st = cfg.Step or cfg.Increment
			dec = 0
			if st and st > 0 and st < 1 then dec = math.min(4, math.ceil(-math.log10(st) - 1e-9)) end
		end
		local row = addRow(tab, 32)
		local ready = false
		local sl = createSlider(row, 0, 8, W, {
			label = titleOf(cfg, "slider"), min = min, max = max, value = def, decimals = dec, suffix = cfg.Suffix,
			onChange = function(x) if ready then safe(cfg.Callback, x) end end,
		})
		ready = true
		local obj = {}
		bind(obj, "Set", function(x, silent)
			sl.Set(x, true)
			if not silent then safe(cfg.Callback, sl.Get()) end
		end)
		bind(obj, "Get", function() return sl.Get() end)
		task.defer(safe, cfg.Callback, def)
		return finish(row, obj)
	end

	function M.button(tab, a, b)
		local W = tab._w
		local cfg = cfgOf(a, { "Title", "Callback" }, b)
		local row = addRow(tab, 28)
		local btn = createActionButton(row, 0, 14, W, titleOf(cfg, "button"), function() safe(cfg.Callback) end, cfg.Filled or cfg.Primary)
		local obj = {}
		bind(obj, "SetTitle", function(t) btn.Text = t end)
		bind(obj, "Fire", function() safe(cfg.Callback) end)
		return finish(row, obj)
	end

	function M.dropdown(tab, a, b, c, d)
		local W = tab._w
		local cfg = cfgOf(a, { "Title", "Values", "Default", "Callback" }, b, c, d)
		local values = {}
		for _, x in ipairs(cfg.Values or cfg.Options or {}) do table.insert(values, tostring(x)) end
		local def = cfg.Default
		if def == nil then def = cfg.Value end
		if type(def) == "number" then def = values[def] end
		def = def and tostring(def) or values[1] or ""
		local title = cfg.Title or cfg.Name or cfg.Text
		local top = title and 18 or 0
		local base = top + 28
		local row = addRow(tab, base)
		if title then createLabel(row, title, 11, C.text, FONT, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 8), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W, 16) }) end
		local ready = false
		local dd = createDropdown(row, 0, top, W, values, def, function(o) if ready then safe(cfg.Callback, o) end end)
		ready = true
		local list = findPopup(row)
		if list then
			list:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Size"):Connect(function()
				local h = list.Size.Y.Offset
				row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, base + (h > 0 and h + 4 or 0))
			end)
		end
		local obj = {}
		bind(obj, "Set", function(o, silent) dd.Set(tostring(o), true) if not silent then safe(cfg.Callback, dd.Get()) end end)
		bind(obj, "Get", function() return dd.Get() end)
		bind(obj, "Refresh", function(vals, sel)
			local out = {}
			for _, x in ipairs(vals or {}) do table.insert(out, tostring(x)) end
			dd.SetOptions(out, sel and tostring(sel) or out[1] or "")
		end)
		if def ~= "" then task.defer(safe, cfg.Callback, def) end
		return finish(row, obj)
	end

	function M.input(tab, a, b, c)
		local W = tab._w
		local cfg = cfgOf(a, { "Title", "Placeholder", "Callback" }, b, c)
		local title = cfg.Title or cfg.Name or cfg.Text
		local top = title and 16 or 0
		local row = addRow(tab, top + 28)
		if title then createLabel(row, title, 11, C.text, FONT, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 8), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W, 16) }) end
		local box = createTextInput(row, 0, top + 14, W, cfg.Placeholder or "type here...", function(t, enter) safe(cfg.Callback, t, enter) end)
		if cfg.Default ~= nil then box.Text = tostring(cfg.Default) end
		local obj = {}
		bind(obj, "Set", function(t) box.Text = tostring(t) end)
		bind(obj, "Get", function() return box.Text end)
		return finish(row, obj)
	end

	local function toCode(v)
		if typeof(v) == "EnumItem" then return v end
		if type(v) ~= "string" or v == "" then return nil end
		for _, n in ipairs({ v, v:sub(1, 1):upper() .. v:sub(2), v:upper() }) do
			local ok, code = pcall(function() return Enum.KeyCode[n] end)
			if ok and code then return code end
		end
		return nil
	end

	function M.keybind(tab, a, b, c)
		local W = tab._w
		local cfg = cfgOf(a, { "Title", "Default", "Callback" }, b, c)
		local bound = toCode(cfg.Default or cfg.Value or cfg.Key)
		local row = addRow(tab, 28)
		createLabel(row, titleOf(cfg, "keybind"), 12, C.text, FONT, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 14), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W - 46, 16) })
		local btn = createKeybind(row, W - 38, 14, bound and string.sub(string.upper(bound.Name), 1, 5) or "NONE", function(input, name)
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
				bound = name ~= "NONE" and input.KeyCode or nil
			else
				bound = name
			end
			safe(cfg.OnChange or cfg.Changed, bound)
		end)
		conns[#conns + 1] = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gp)
			if gp or capturing or not bound then return end
			local hit
			if typeof(bound) == "EnumItem" then
				hit = input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard and input.KeyCode == bound
			else
				hit = (bound == "M1" and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1)
					or (bound == "M2" and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2)
					or (bound == "M3" and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton3)
			end
			if hit then safe(cfg.Callback, bound) end
		end)
		local obj = {}
		bind(obj, "Set", function(k)
			bound = toCode(k)
			btn.Text = bound and string.sub(string.upper(bound.Name), 1, 5) or "NONE"
		end)
		bind(obj, "Get", function() return bound end)
		return finish(row, obj)
	end

	function M.colorpicker(tab, a, b, c)
		local W = tab._w
		local cfg = cfgOf(a, { "Title", "Default", "Callback" }, b, c)
		local init = cfg.Default or cfg.Value or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
		local row = addRow(tab, 28)
		createLabel(row, titleOf(cfg, "color"), 12, C.text, FONT, { Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 14), AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(W - 34, 16) })
		local ready, mute = false, false
		local cp = createColorPicker(row, W - 24, 14, init, function(col) if ready and not mute then safe(cfg.Callback, col) end end)
		ready = true
		local pop = findPopup(row)
		if pop then
			pop:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Size"):Connect(function()
				row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, math.max(28, 27 + pop.Size.Y.Offset + 6))
			end)
		end
		local obj = {}
		bind(obj, "Set", function(col, silent)
			mute = silent and true or false
			cp.Set(col)
			mute = false
		end)
		bind(obj, "Get", function() return cp.Get() end)
		task.defer(safe, cfg.Callback, init)
		return finish(row, obj)
	end

	function M.header(tab, a)
		local W = tab._w
		local cfg = cfgOf(a, { "Title" })
		local row = addRow(tab, 24)
		createSection(row, 0, 12, W, titleOf(cfg, ""))
		return finish(row, {})
	end

	function M.section(tab, a, b)
		local cfg = cfgOf(a, { "Title", "Side" }, b)
		local side = tostring(cfg.Side or cfg.Position or cfg.Column or ""):lower()
		if side == "left" or side == "l" then return M.left(tab, cfg) end
		if side == "right" or side == "r" then return M.right(tab, cfg) end
		M.header(tab, cfg)
		return tab
	end

	function M.space(tab, a)
		local h = type(a) == "number" and a or (type(a) == "table" and a.Size) or 8
		return finish(addRow(tab, h), {})
	end

	function M.paragraph(tab, a, b)
		local cfg = cfgOf(a, { "Title", "Desc" }, b)
		local title = cfg.Title or cfg.Name or cfg.Text
		local desc = cfg.Desc or cfg.Content or cfg.Description
		tab._n += 1
		local row = createFrame(tab._sf, { Name = tab._key, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, LayoutOrder = tab._n, ZIndex = math.max(1, 1000 - tab._n) })
		create("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 3), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, row)
		create("UIPadding", { PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 2), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 4) }, row)
		local tl, dl
		if title then
			tl = createLabel(row, title, 12, C.text, FONT_BOLD, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, TextWrapped = true, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None, LayoutOrder = 1 })
		end
		if desc then
			dl = createLabel(row, desc, 11, C.sub, FONT, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, TextWrapped = true, TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None, TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top, LayoutOrder = 2, NoTitle = true })
		end
		local obj = {}
		bind(obj, "SetTitle", function(t) if tl then tl.Text = t end end)
		bind(obj, "SetDesc", function(t) if dl then dl.Text = t end end)
		return finish(row, obj)
	end

	local TabMeta
	local function ensureCols(tab)
		if tab._cols then return tab._cols end
		tab._n += 1
		local holder = createFrame(tab._sf, { Name = tab._key, Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, LayoutOrder = tab._n })
		create("UIListLayout", { FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal, Padding = UDim.new(0, 10), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder, VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top }, holder)
		local function col(order)
			local fr = createFrame(holder, { Name = tab._key, Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -5, 0, 0), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y, LayoutOrder = order })
			create("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 6), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, fr)
			return setmetatable({ _sf = fr, _n = 0, _w = math.floor((tab._w - 10) / 2), _key = tab._key }, TabMeta)
		end
		tab._cols = { left = col(1), right = col(2) }
		return tab._cols
	end

	function M.left(tab, a)
		local cfg = cfgOf(a, { "Title" })
		local c = ensureCols(tab).left
		if cfg.Title or cfg.Name then M.header(c, { Title = cfg.Title or cfg.Name }) end
		return c
	end

	function M.right(tab, a)
		local cfg = cfgOf(a, { "Title" })
		local c = ensureCols(tab).right
		if cfg.Title or cfg.Name then M.header(c, { Title = cfg.Title or cfg.Name }) end
		return c
	end

	function M.columns(tab)
		local c = ensureCols(tab)
		return c.left, c.right
	end

	local TabMethods = {
		toggle = M.toggle, slider = M.slider, button = M.button, dropdown = M.dropdown,
		input = M.input, textbox = M.input, textinput = M.input,
		keybind = M.keybind, bind = M.keybind,
		colorpicker = M.colorpicker, color = M.colorpicker, colourpicker = M.colorpicker,
		header = M.header, section = M.section, leftsection = M.left, rightsection = M.right, divider = M.header, separator = M.header, group = M.header, category = M.header,
		paragraph = M.paragraph, label = M.paragraph, text = M.paragraph,
		space = M.space, left = M.left, right = M.right, columns = M.columns,
	}
	TabMeta = { __index = resolver(TabMethods) }

	local function addTab(sec, title, desc)
		title = tostring(title or "tab")
		local key = "nx_" .. sec.name .. "_" .. title .. "_" .. (#sec.pages + 1)
		local page = createPage(key)
		DESCRIPTIONS[key] = desc or ""
		local hasDesc = desc ~= nil and desc ~= "" -- no description line under the tab bar -> pull the content up into that empty row
		local sf = create("ScrollingFrame", {
			Name = "content", Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, hasDesc and 96 or 82), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(376, hasDesc and 293 or 307), BackgroundTransparency = 1, BorderSizePixel = 0,
			ScrollBarThickness = 2, ScrollBarImageColor3 = C.accent, CanvasSize = UDim2.new(), AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
			ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y, ElasticBehavior = Enum.ElasticBehavior.Never,
		}, page)
		create("UIListLayout", { Padding = UDim.new(0, 6), SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder }, sf)
		create("UIPadding", { PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, hasDesc and 6 or 2), PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 14), PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 16), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 16) }, sf)
		table.insert(sec.pages, key)
		tabOrder += 1
		local b = createButton(tabBar, { Sound = true, Text = title, TextSize = 12, Font = FONT_BOLD, TextColor3 = C.sub, Size = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 20), AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X, LayoutOrder = tabOrder, Visible = false })
		create("UIPadding", { PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 5), PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 5) }, b)
		tabs[key] = b
		b.Activated:Connect(function() selectTab(key) end)
		b.MouseEnter:Connect(function() if currentTab ~= key then tween(b, 0.1, { TextColor3 = C.text }) end end)
		b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() if currentTab ~= key then tween(b, 0.1, { TextColor3 = C.sub }) end end)
		local idx = table.find(sections, sec)
		if idx and navs[idx].selected then setSection(idx) end
		local tab = setmetatable({ _sf = sf, _n = 0, _w = W, _key = key, _sec = sec, Title = title, Instance = page }, TabMeta)
		function tab.Select()
			local i = table.find(sections, sec)
			if i then currentTab = key setSection(i) end
		end
		return tab
	end

	local function addSection(title, icon)
		customCount += 1
		if customCount > 5 then
			warn("[Nauru] the sidebar holds 5 custom sections; extra sections are not shown")
		end
		local sec = { name = tostring(title or "section"), icon = icon or Icons.sparkle, y = 34, pages = {} }
		local pos = math.min(customCount, #sections + 1)
		table.insert(sections, pos, sec)
		table.insert(navs, pos, State.makeNav(sec))
		State.layoutNavs(true)
		if customCount == 1 then setSection(pos) end
		return sec
	end

	local SecMethods = {
		tab = function(self, a, b)
			local cfg = cfgOf(a, { "Title", "Desc" }, b)
			return addTab(self._sec, titleOf(cfg, "tab"), cfg.Desc or cfg.Description)
		end,
		select = function(self)
			local i = table.find(sections, self._sec)
			if i then setSection(i) end
		end,
	}
	local SecMeta = { __index = resolver(SecMethods) }

	local function resolveKey(k)
		if typeof(k) == "EnumItem" then return k end
		return toCode(k)
	end

	local WinMethods = {
		section = function(self, a, b)
			local cfg = cfgOf(a, { "Title", "Icon" }, b)
			return setmetatable({ _sec = addSection(titleOf(cfg, "section"), cfg.Icon), Title = titleOf(cfg, "section") }, SecMeta)
		end,
		tab = function(self, a, b)
			local cfg = cfgOf(a, { "Title", "Icon" }, b)
			local sec = addSection(titleOf(cfg, "tab"), cfg.Icon)
			return addTab(sec, titleOf(cfg, "tab"), cfg.Desc or cfg.Description)
		end,
		notify = function(self, a, b)
			if type(a) == "table" then notify(a.Title or "Nauru Hub", a.Content or a.Text or a.Desc or "") else notify(a or "Nauru Hub", b or "") end
		end,
		settheme = function(self, name) pcall(setTheme, tostring(name), true) end,
		settogglekey = function(self, k) CONFIG.toggleKey = resolveKey(k) end,
		settitle = function(self, t) State.setBrand(t) end,
		open = function() setShown(true) end,
		close = function() setShown(false) end,
		toggleui = function() setShown(not shown) end,
		hidesection = function(self, name) return State.hideSection(name) end,
		destroy = function()
			for _, c in ipairs(conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
			pcall(function() State.LA.destroy() end)
			gui:Destroy()
		end,
	}
	local WinMeta = { __index = resolver(WinMethods) }

	function State.setBrand(title, tag)
		local words = {}
		for w in tostring(title):gmatch("%S+") do table.insert(words, w) end
		State.brand.a = (words[1] or ""):upper()
		State.brand.b = table.concat(words, " ", 2):upper()
		State.brand.name = tostring(title)
		State.brand.tag = tag or ""
		refreshBrand()
	end

	local RootMethods = {
		window = function(self, a)
			local opts = type(a) == "table" and a or { Title = a }
			if opts.Title then State.setBrand(opts.Title, opts.Tag or opts.Version or "") end
			if opts.Icon ~= nil and State.logoEmoji then State.logoEmoji.Text = tostring(opts.Icon) end
			if opts.ToggleKey then CONFIG.toggleKey = resolveKey(opts.ToggleKey) end
			if opts.Theme then pcall(setTheme, tostring(opts.Theme), true) end
			if not opts.ShowDemo then State.hideSection("features") end
			win = win or setmetatable({}, WinMeta)
			return win
		end,
		notify = WinMethods.notify,
	}
	local defTab
	local function ensureWin() return win or RootMethods.window(Nauru, {}) end
	local function ensureTab()
		if not defTab then
			local sec = WinMethods.section(ensureWin(), { Title = "main" })
			defTab = SecMethods.tab(sec, { Title = "main" })
		end
		return defTab
	end
	local rootRes, winRes, tabRes = resolver(RootMethods), resolver(WinMethods), resolver(TabMethods)
	setmetatable(Nauru, { __index = function(t, key)
		local f = rootRes(t, key)
		if f then return f end
		f = winRes(t, key)
		if f then return function(_, ...) return f(ensureWin(), ...) end end
		f = tabRes(t, key)
		if f then return function(_, ...) return f(ensureTab(), ...) end end
		return nil
	end })
	Nauru.State = State
	return Nauru
end

State.phoneInit()

do
	local saved
	pcall(function()
		if isfile and isfile("NauruHub/theme_v2.txt") then saved = readfile("NauruHub/theme_v2.txt"):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") end
	end)
	pcall(setTheme, (saved and saved ~= "") and saved or "royal", true)
	if currentTheme == "ember" and saved ~= "ember" then pcall(setTheme, "royal", true) end
end
holder.Visible = false
updateLayout()
view.scale = State.autoScale()
centerView()
setSection(1)
setShown(true)
State.cfg.boot()
pcall(function()
	if isfile and isfile("NauruHub/lang.txt") then
		local c = readfile("NauruHub/lang.txt"):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
		if c ~= "" and c ~= "en" then State.lang.apply(c, true) task.defer(State.lang.reveal) end
	end
end)
task.delay(0.7, function()
	if State.extOk == false then notify("extra widgets failed", tostring(State.extErr):sub(1, 70)) else notify("Nauru Hub", "drag the bottom-right grip to resize") end
end)


State.Nauru = State.buildNauru()
pcall(function()
	getgenv().Nauru = State.Nauru
	getgenv().NauruHub = State.Nauru
end)
return State.Nauru
